Saya akan susun ini sebagai peta kemampuan praktis untuk payment domain + integration pattern, bukan sekadar daftar gateway. Saya akan cek sumber resmi terlebih dahulu untuk bagian yang mudah berubah seperti PCI DSS, idempotency, webhook, dan standar API/error handling.

Iya, kita fokuskan ini sebagai **payment-specific decomposition**, bukan mengulang “integration pattern” umum. Dalam payment system, problem utamanya bukan sekadar “call payment gateway”, tetapi **menjaga state uang tetap benar** ketika terjadi retry, timeout, duplicate webhook, partial failure, settlement delay, refund, dispute, reconciliation, dan audit.

Beberapa baseline yang perlu dipegang: PCI DSS versi terbaru yang relevan saat ini adalah **PCI DSS v4.0.1**, dipublikasikan oleh PCI SSC sebagai revisi terbatas tanpa penambahan/penghapusan requirement dibanding v4.0. ([PCI Perspectives](https://blog.pcisecuritystandards.org/just-published-pci-dss-v4-0-1?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk integrasi API modern, Jakarta REST/JAX-RS memakai resource class dan annotation seperti `@Path`, `@GET`, `@POST`, dan lain-lain untuk membangun endpoint REST. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Dalam payment integration, idempotency dan webhook verification adalah fondasi: Stripe mendokumentasikan idempotent request untuk retry aman, sedangkan webhook dari PSP seperti Stripe, PayPal, dan Adyen perlu diverifikasi signature/HMAC/certificate sesuai mekanisme masing-masing. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# Decomposition Map - Payment Management & Integration Pattern

## 1. Introduction

**Payment management** dalam Java Jersey/JAX-RS based microservice biasanya mencakup:

- menerima payment request dari order/billing/subscription service,
- membuat payment intent/order/authorization ke PSP,
- menerima webhook/callback asynchronous,
- menjaga payment state machine,
- melakukan capture, void, refund, retry, reconciliation,
- mencatat ledger/audit trail,
- memenuhi security/compliance,
- menyediakan observability dan operational tooling.

Hal yang sering salah dipahami: **payment bukan CRUD**. Payment lebih tepat dipandang sebagai **distributed financial workflow**. Satu transaksi bisa melewati banyak state, melibatkan pihak eksternal, dan hasil final sering datang lewat webhook, bukan dari response HTTP awal.

Core design principle:

> **Internal source of truth harus berupa payment state + ledger + audit event yang deterministik, bukan response terakhir dari gateway.**

---

## 2. Diagram

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Client / Order Service] --> B[Jersey Payment API]
    B --> C[Payment Application Service]

    C --> D[Payment State Machine]
    C --> E[Idempotency Service]
    C --> F[Payment Gateway Router]

    F --> G1[Stripe Adapter]
    F --> G2[Adyen Adapter]
    F --> G3[PayPal / Bank Adapter]
    F --> G4[Internal / Offline Payment Adapter]

    G1 --> H[External PSP / Acquirer]
    G2 --> H
    G3 --> H
    G4 --> H

    H --> I[Webhook Endpoint]
    I --> J[Signature Verification]
    J --> K[Inbox / Dedup Store]
    K --> L[Webhook Event Processor]
    L --> D

    D --> M[(PostgreSQL)]
    M --> M1[payments]
    M --> M2[payment_attempts]
    M --> M3[payment_events]
    M --> M4[ledger_entries]
    M --> M5[idempotency_keys]
    M --> M6[outbox_events]

    C --> N[Camunda Process / Saga]
    N --> O[Order / Billing / Fulfillment Service]

    M6 --> P[Outbox Publisher]
    P --> Q[Message Broker / Event Bus]
    Q --> O

    R[Reconciliation Job] --> M
    R --> H

    S[Ops / Support Console] --> B
    T[Observability: logs metrics traces alerts] --> B
    T --> C
    T --> I
    T --> P
```

---

## 3. Decomposition Map dalam Tabel

| No | Area / Skill Domain | Fokus Praktis | Implementasi di Java Jersey/JAX-RS Microservice | Risiko yang Harus Dijaga |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Payment Domain Modeling** | Memahami konsep `payment`, `payment attempt`, `authorization`, `capture`, `refund`, `void`, `settlement`, `chargeback`, `dispute`. | Buat entity terpisah: `Payment`, `PaymentAttempt`, `PaymentEvent`, `Refund`, `LedgerEntry`. Jangan campur semua ke satu table. | Model terlalu sederhana → sulit handle retry, partial capture, refund sebagian, dispute, dan audit. |
| 2 | **Payment State Machine** | Mendesain lifecycle payment yang eksplisit. | Enum state seperti `INITIATED`, `REQUIRES_ACTION`, `AUTHORIZED`, `CAPTURED`, `FAILED`, `CANCELLED`, `REFUNDED`, `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED`, `DISPUTED`. | State lompat sembarangan, double capture, refund sebelum capture, status internal berbeda dari PSP. |
| 3 | **Money Representation** | Menyimpan amount secara aman. | Gunakan integer minor unit seperti `amount_minor BIGINT`, `currency CHAR(3)`, bukan `double`. ISO 4217 mendefinisikan currency code dan hubungan minor unit untuk currency yang memilikinya. ([ISO](https://www.iso.org/iso-4217-currency-codes.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Floating point error, salah decimal currency, rounding mismatch, reconciliation gagal. |
| 4 | **Currency & Minor Unit Handling** | Menangani USD, IDR, JPY, currency zero-decimal, gateway-specific decimal rule. | Buat `CurrencyMetadataService`; validasi amount sebelum call PSP. Stripe, misalnya, memakai positive integer dalam smallest currency unit untuk PaymentIntent amount. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/payment_intents/object?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Salah kirim IDR/JPY/ISK minor unit; amount di gateway tidak sama dengan invoice. |
| 5 | **Payment API Design** | Mendesain REST endpoint untuk create/authorize/capture/refund/status. | Jersey resource: `POST /payments`, `POST /payments/{id}/capture`, `POST /payments/{id}/refunds`, `GET /payments/{id}`. Jakarta REST memakai annotation resource/method untuk expose REST endpoint. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Endpoint terlalu gateway-specific; sulit multi-PSP; breaking change ke consumer internal. |
| 6 | **Idempotency Pattern** | Mencegah double charge saat client retry. | Header `Idempotency-Key`; table `idempotency_keys`; simpan request hash, response snapshot, status processing. Stripe mendukung idempotency untuk retry aman tanpa melakukan operasi yang sama dua kali. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Timeout lalu retry menghasilkan dua payment attempt atau dua charge. |
| 7 | **Gateway Adapter Pattern** | Memisahkan domain payment dari detail PSP. | Interface `PaymentGatewayClient`; implementation `StripeGatewayClient`, `AdyenGatewayClient`, `BankTransferGatewayClient`. | Domain service tercampur payload vendor; vendor migration mahal. |
| 8 | **Gateway Routing** | Menentukan PSP berdasarkan merchant, country, currency, payment method, failover. | `PaymentGatewayRouter` membaca config dari DB/feature flag/config map. | Routing tidak deterministic; payment create di PSP A tapi webhook masuk PSP B tidak terhubung. |
| 9 | **Authorization vs Capture** | Memahami perbedaan reserve funds vs settle funds. | Support flow: `authorizeOnly`, `captureLater`, `autoCapture`. Cocok untuk order yang butuh fulfilment dulu. | Capture order yang belum valid; auth expired; double capture. |
| 10 | **Synchronous Call Handling** | Memproses response awal dari PSP. | HTTP client dengan timeout, retry policy terbatas, circuit breaker, structured error mapping. | Menganggap HTTP 200 sebagai payment final padahal status final bisa datang lewat webhook. |
| 11 | **Webhook Endpoint** | Menerima event asynchronous dari PSP. | `POST /webhooks/{provider}`; simpan raw payload; verify signature; dedup event; process async. PayPal menjelaskan webhook sebagai HTTPS POST dari PayPal ke endpoint server saat event terjadi. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/api/rest/webhooks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Duplicate webhook, fake webhook, out-of-order event, processing lambat bikin PSP retry. |
| 12 | **Webhook Signature Verification** | Memastikan event benar dari PSP. | Stripe: raw body + signature header + endpoint secret; Adyen: HMAC key; PayPal: signature verification dengan header/certificate/verify endpoint. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/webhooks?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Webhook spoofing; attacker mengubah status payment menjadi paid. |
| 13 | **Inbox / Dedup Pattern** | Menjamin event eksternal diproses sekali secara efektif. | Table `payment_inbox_events(provider, event_id, received_at, processed_at, status)` dengan unique constraint. | PSP mengirim event sama berkali-kali → ledger/status berubah berulang. |
| 14 | **Transactional Outbox Pattern** | Menjamin perubahan DB dan publish event internal tetap konsisten. | Setelah update payment state, insert `outbox_events` dalam transaksi DB yang sama; worker publish ke broker. AWS menjelaskan outbox untuk mengatasi dual-write antara database write dan message/event notification. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/prescriptive-guidance/latest/cloud-design-patterns/transactional-outbox.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Payment sukses di DB tapi event ke order/billing hilang; atau event terkirim padahal DB rollback. |
| 15 | **Payment Ledger** | Mencatat dampak finansial secara append-only. | `ledger_entries` dengan debit/credit, amount, currency, reference id, event id, created_at. | Hanya menyimpan status akhir tanpa jejak finansial; audit dan reconciliation sulit. |
| 16 | **Reconciliation** | Mencocokkan data internal dengan statement/report PSP/bank. | Scheduled job ambil settlement report, compare by PSP reference, amount, currency, fee, net settlement. | Internal “paid” tetapi PSP gagal settle; refund/dispute tidak tercermin. |
| 17 | **Refund Management** | Full/partial refund, multiple refund, refund status async. | `POST /payments/{id}/refunds`; refund punya state sendiri: `REQUESTED`, `SUCCEEDED`, `FAILED`, `CANCELLED`. | Refund double, refund melebihi captured amount, refund sukses di PSP tapi internal gagal update. |
| 18 | **Dispute / Chargeback Handling** | Menangani lifecycle sengketa pembayaran. | Webhook event mapping ke `DISPUTE_OPENED`, `DISPUTE_WON`, `DISPUTE_LOST`; attach evidence metadata. | Operasional tidak sadar dispute; revenue overstated. |
| 19 | **Error Mapping & Problem Details** | Menyediakan error API yang konsisten. | Mapping PSP error ke internal error code: `PAYMENT_DECLINED`, `INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS`, `THREE_DS_REQUIRED`, `GATEWAY_TIMEOUT`. RFC 9457 mendefinisikan Problem Details untuk membawa error detail machine-readable di HTTP API. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc9457/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Consumer bergantung pada raw vendor error; sulit observability dan retry decision. |
| 20 | **Retry Strategy** | Membedakan retry teknis vs retry bisnis. | Retry network timeout dengan idempotency; jangan retry hard decline; scheduled retry untuk pending/unknown. | Retry asal-asalan → duplicate charge, fraud signal, rate limit, bad customer experience. |
| 21 | **Unknown / Pending State Handling** | Mengelola kondisi “tidak tahu” setelah timeout. | State `UNKNOWN` atau `PENDING_CONFIRMATION`; query PSP by reference; tunggu webhook. | Mengubah timeout menjadi failed padahal payment berhasil. |
| 22 | **Database Transaction & Locking** | Menjaga consistency saat concurrent request/webhook. | PostgreSQL transaction, unique constraint, optimistic locking `version`, `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` untuk update state kritikal. PostgreSQL current docs menjelaskan empat isolation level dapat diminta, tetapi internalnya tiga level berbeda karena MVCC. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Race antara user retry, webhook, refund, dan reconciliation. |
| 23 | **MyBatis Persistence Mapping** | Mapping query payment yang eksplisit dan audit-friendly. | Mapper XML/annotation untuk insert payment, update state with version, insert event, insert ledger. | Hidden ORM behavior; update tanpa version check; sulit trace query. |
| 24 | **Camunda Saga / Process Orchestration** | Mengorkestrasi payment dalam order lifecycle. | BPMN: reserve order → create payment → wait webhook → capture → notify fulfilment → compensate/refund. | Payment logic tersebar di service tanpa workflow visibility. |
| 25 | **3DS / Strong Customer Authentication Flow** | Menangani payment yang butuh customer action. | State `REQUIRES_ACTION`; return redirect/action payload ke frontend; resume via webhook/callback. | Payment dianggap failed padahal menunggu challenge. |
| 26 | **Tokenization & Stored Payment Method** | Menyimpan reference, bukan card data. | Simpan `payment_method_token`, `customer_token`, `provider_customer_id`; hindari PAN/CVV. | Scope PCI membesar; data sensitif bocor. |
| 27 | **PCI DSS & Security Boundary** | Menentukan apakah service menyentuh cardholder data. | Ideal: hosted checkout/payment page/tokenization by PSP; service hanya menerima token/reference. PCI DSS v4.0.1 adalah revisi terkini PCI DSS dari PCI SSC. ([PCI Perspectives](https://blog.pcisecuritystandards.org/just-published-pci-dss-v4-0-1?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | Tanpa sadar menyimpan PAN/CVV di log, DB, APM, atau payload audit. |
| 28 | **Secrets & Key Management** | Mengelola API key, webhook secret, HMAC key. | Kubernetes secret/external secret manager; rotation; separate test/live key; least privilege. | Secret bocor di GitHub, log, container image, config map. |
| 29 | **Observability** | Melacak payment end-to-end. | Correlation ID, payment ID, provider reference, structured logs, metrics: success rate, pending age, webhook lag, reconciliation mismatch. | Incident sulit dianalisis; support tidak bisa menjawab “kenapa order belum paid?”. |
| 30 | **Operational Console / Backoffice** | Membantu support dan finance. | Endpoint/admin UI untuk payment timeline, retry webhook, manual reconcile, refund request, export report. | Engineer harus query DB manual; risiko human error tinggi. |
| 31 | **Testing Strategy** | Menguji happy path, timeout, duplicate webhook, out-of-order event, refund, reconciliation. | Unit test state transition, contract test adapter, Testcontainers PostgreSQL, PSP sandbox, replay webhook fixture. | Test hanya mock happy path; bug muncul di production. |
| 32 | **Local Development & Sandbox** | Simulasi PSP secara aman. | Docker compose: app + PostgreSQL + mock PSP + webhook simulator. | Developer sulit reproduce issue webhook/retry. |
| 33 | **Kubernetes Deployment** | Menjalankan API, worker, scheduler secara terpisah. | Deployment terpisah: `payment-api`, `webhook-consumer`, `outbox-publisher`, `reconciliation-job`. | Worker autoscaling salah; job double-run; webhook endpoint tidak resilient. |
| 34 | **Cloud / On-Prem Connectivity** | Menghubungkan service ke PSP dari environment berbeda. | Egress allowlist, proxy, TLS, DNS, NAT gateway, certificate truststore. | Payment gagal hanya di on-prem karena egress/firewall/cert issue. |
| 35 | **Provider API Versioning** | Mengontrol perubahan kontrak PSP. | Pin API version bila provider mendukung; regression test sebelum upgrade. Stripe menyarankan API versioning untuk menguji versi baru sebelum commit upgrade. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/versioning?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) | PSP upgrade mengubah payload/error/status tanpa terdeteksi. |
| 36 | **Settlement & Fee Accounting** | Menghitung gross, fee, tax, net settlement. | `payment_amount`, `fee_amount`, `net_amount`, `settlement_currency`, `exchange_rate`. | Finance report tidak cocok dengan bank statement. |
| 37 | **Multi-Tenant / Merchant Config** | Mendukung banyak merchant/business unit. | Merchant config: provider account, currency, allowed payment methods, webhook secret, settlement account. | Payment merchant A masuk akun merchant B. |
| 38 | **Fraud & Risk Signal Integration** | Menghubungkan payment dengan risk engine. | Pre-auth risk check, post-payment fraud event, rule engine, manual review queue. | Fraud decision datang terlambat; order sudah fulfilled. |
| 39 | **Data Retention & Audit** | Menentukan data apa disimpan dan berapa lama. | Append-only event, redaction policy, PII masking, immutable audit trail. | Audit gagal; data sensitif tersimpan terlalu lama. |
| 40 | **Incident & Recovery Playbook** | Menangani payment outage. | Playbook: PSP down, webhook backlog, duplicate charge, reconciliation mismatch, secret rotation. | Tim panik saat payment stuck; recovery manual tanpa kontrol. |

## 4. Mental Model Praktis

Payment service sebaiknya dipandang sebagai gabungan dari 5 lapisan:

```text
1. API Layer
   - Jersey resource
   - request validation
   - idempotency key
   - error response

2. Application / Orchestration Layer
   - payment use case
   - state transition
   - gateway routing
   - Camunda saga

3. Integration Layer
   - PSP adapter
   - webhook receiver
   - signature verification
   - retry/circuit breaker

4. Persistence & Financial Truth Layer
   - payment table
   - attempt table
   - event table
   - ledger table
   - inbox/outbox table

5. Operations & Compliance Layer
   - reconciliation
   - audit
   - monitoring
   - support console
   - PCI/security boundary
```

Kunci praktisnya: **jangan membuat payment service yang hanya “proxy ke gateway”**. Payment service harus menjadi **stateful financial control point**.

---

## 5. Recommended Table Design Awal

Minimal schema konseptual:

| Table | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `payments` | Aggregate utama payment internal. |
| `payment_attempts` | Setiap percobaan charge/authorization ke PSP. |
| `payment_events` | Timeline event internal dan external. |
| `payment_inbox_events` | Dedup webhook/event masuk. |
| `payment_outbox_events` | Event keluar untuk order/billing/fulfillment. |
| `refunds` | Refund request dan statusnya. |
| `ledger_entries` | Catatan finansial append-only. |
| `idempotency_keys` | Mencegah duplicate operation dari retry. |
| `provider_references` | Mapping internal ID ke PSP object/reference ID. |
| `reconciliation_runs` | Riwayat proses rekonsiliasi. |
| `reconciliation_items` | Item mismatch/success dari settlement report. |

---

## 6. Payment State Transition Contoh

```text
INITIATED
  -> REQUIRES_ACTION
  -> AUTHORIZED
  -> CAPTURED
  -> PARTIALLY_REFUNDED
  -> REFUNDED

INITIATED
  -> FAILED

AUTHORIZED
  -> VOIDED

CAPTURED
  -> DISPUTED
  -> CHARGEBACK_LOST / CHARGEBACK_WON
```

Invariant penting:

| Invariant | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Captured amount tidak boleh melebihi authorized amount. |
| Refunded amount tidak boleh melebihi captured amount. |
| Satu idempotency key hanya boleh menghasilkan satu logical operation. |
| Webhook duplicate tidak boleh menghasilkan ledger entry duplicate. |
| Payment final state tidak boleh mundur sembarangan. |
| Semua perubahan finansial harus punya event/audit trail. |
| Provider reference harus unik per provider/environment. |

---

## 7. Roadmap Alur Belajar Singkat

| Tahap | Fokus | Target Praktis |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | **Payment Domain Basics** | Pahami authorization, capture, refund, void, settlement, dispute, chargeback. |
| 2 | **Payment State Machine** | Buat state transition table + invariant. |
| 3 | **Jersey API Design** | Implement endpoint create payment, get status, capture, refund. |
| 4 | **Idempotency & Retry** | Implement `Idempotency-Key` dengan PostgreSQL unique constraint. |
| 5 | **Gateway Adapter** | Buat interface PSP adapter + mock provider. |
| 6 | **Webhook Processing** | Implement raw payload storage, signature verification, inbox dedup. |
| 7 | **Outbox & Event Publishing** | Publish `PaymentCaptured`, `PaymentFailed`, `RefundSucceeded` secara reliable. |
| 8 | **Ledger & Reconciliation** | Buat append-only ledger dan job reconcile dari provider report. |
| 9 | **Camunda Saga** | Orchestrate order → payment → fulfillment → compensation. |
| 10 | **Security & Compliance** | Tokenization, PCI boundary, secrets rotation, masking log. |
| 11 | **Production Readiness** | Observability, alert, runbook, replay webhook, support tooling. |

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 1/18 - Payment Domain Model, State Machine, dan Invariant

Seri pertama ini membahas fondasi paling penting: **bagaimana memodelkan payment secara benar sebelum masuk ke gateway integration, webhook, retry, Camunda saga, refund, reconciliation, dan audit**.

Payment bukan CRUD biasa. Payment adalah **stateful financial workflow** yang harus tahan terhadap:

- retry dari client,
- timeout ke payment gateway,
- webhook yang datang berkanda,
- webhook yang datang terlambat,
- status eksternal yang belum final,
- refund sebagian,
- capture sebagian,
- dispute/chargeback,
- mismatch antara internal system dan settlement report.

Dalam Jakarta REST/JAX-RS, endpoint payment tetap bisa diekspos sebagai resource HTTP menggunakan annotation seperti `@Path`, `@GET`, dan `@POST`, tetapi domain internalnya harus dimodelkan sebagai **state machine**, bukan sekadar table `payment_status`. Jakarta REST sendiri memang dirancang untuk membuat RESTful resource berbasis Java annotation. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Payment service sebaiknya dipandang seperti ini:

```mermaid id="k8y6x3"
flowchart TD
    A[Order / Billing Service] --> B[Payment API - Jersey/JAX-RS]
    B --> C[Payment Application Service]

    C --> D[Payment Aggregate]
    D --> E[Payment State Machine]
    D --> F[Payment Invariants]

    C --> G[Payment Attempt]
    C --> H[Provider Reference]
    C --> I[Payment Event Timeline]
    C --> J[Ledger Entry]

    G --> K[Payment Gateway Adapter]
    K --> L[External PSP]

    L --> M[Webhook]
    M --> N[Webhook Event]
    N --> E

    E --> O[(PostgreSQL)]
    F --> O
    I --> O
    J --> O
```

Core idea:

> **Payment aggregate menyimpan niat bisnis internal. Payment attempt menyimpan percobaan teknis ke gateway. Payment event menyimpan timeline. Ledger menyimpan efek finansial.**

Jangan gabungkan semuanya ke satu object/table karena lifecycle-nya berbeda.

---

# 2. Komponen Domain Utama

| Komponen | Fungsi | Contoh Field Penting |
|---|---|---|
| `Payment` | Aggregate utama pembayaran internal. | `payment_id`, `order_id`, `amount`, `currency`, `status`, `version` |
| `PaymentAttempt` | Percobaan melakukan authorization/charge ke provider. | `attempt_id`, `payment_id`, `provider`, `provider_payment_id`, `status` |
| `PaymentEvent` | Timeline event internal/eksternal. | `event_id`, `payment_id`, `event_type`, `source`, `payload`, `created_at` |
| `ProviderReference` | Mapping ID internal ke ID provider. | `provider`, `provider_payment_id`, `provider_charge_id` |
| `Refund` | Refund sebagai lifecycle terpisah. | `refund_id`, `payment_id`, `amount`, `status` |
| `LedgerEntry` | Catatan finansial append-only. | `entry_id`, `payment_id`, `direction`, `amount`, `currency`, `reference_type` |
| `IdempotencyRecord` | Pencegah duplicate request. | `idempotency_key`, `request_hash`, `response_snapshot`, `status` |

---

# 3. Payment Status - Vendor-Neutral Model

Payment provider punya istilah sendiri-sendiri. Contoh: Stripe `PaymentIntent` punya status untuk menunjukkan apakah payment butuh payment method, confirmation, customer action, sedang processing, butuh capture, sukses, atau canceled. Stripe juga menekankan bahwa webhook bisa digunakan untuk memantau perubahan status payment. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/payment-intents/verifying-status))

Tetapi internal system jangan terlalu bergantung pada enum vendor. Buat enum internal yang stabil.

```java
public enum PaymentStatus {
    INITIATED,

    REQUIRES_ACTION,
    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING,
    AUTHORIZED,

    CAPTURE_PENDING,
    CAPTURED,
    PARTIALLY_CAPTURED,

    FAILED,
    CANCELLED,
    EXPIRED,

    REFUND_PENDING,
    PARTIALLY_REFUNDED,
    REFUNDED,

    DISPUTED,
    CHARGEBACK_WON,
    CHARGEBACK_LOST,

    UNKNOWN
}
```

## Kenapa perlu `UNKNOWN`?

Karena dalam payment, **timeout bukan berarti gagal**.

Contoh:

```text
Service call ke PSP timeout.
Kemungkinan sebenarnya:
1. PSP tidak menerima request.
2. PSP menerima request tapi response gagal balik.
3. PSP berhasil charge customer.
4. PSP masih processing.
```

Kalau langsung set `FAILED`, bisa salah. State yang lebih aman adalah:

```text
AUTHORIZATION_PENDING / CAPTURE_PENDING / UNKNOWN
```

Lalu lakukan:

- query by provider reference,
- tunggu webhook,
- jalankan reconciliation job.

---

# 4. Payment State Machine

## 4.1 Main Flow

```mermaid id="9crrtf"
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> INITIATED

    INITIATED --> AUTHORIZATION_PENDING
    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING --> REQUIRES_ACTION
    REQUIRES_ACTION --> AUTHORIZATION_PENDING

    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING --> AUTHORIZED
    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING --> FAILED
    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING --> UNKNOWN

    AUTHORIZED --> CAPTURE_PENDING
    AUTHORIZED --> CANCELLED
    AUTHORIZED --> EXPIRED

    CAPTURE_PENDING --> CAPTURED
    CAPTURE_PENDING --> PARTIALLY_CAPTURED
    CAPTURE_PENDING --> FAILED
    CAPTURE_PENDING --> UNKNOWN

    PARTIALLY_CAPTURED --> CAPTURED
    CAPTURED --> REFUND_PENDING
    REFUND_PENDING --> PARTIALLY_REFUNDED
    REFUND_PENDING --> REFUNDED

    CAPTURED --> DISPUTED
    DISPUTED --> CHARGEBACK_WON
    DISPUTED --> CHARGEBACK_LOST

    FAILED --> [*]
    CANCELLED --> [*]
    REFUNDED --> [*]
    CHARGEBACK_WON --> [*]
    CHARGEBACK_LOST --> [*]
```

## 4.2 Practical Rule

Jangan izinkan update status sembarangan seperti ini:

```sql
UPDATE payments SET status = 'CAPTURED' WHERE payment_id = ?;
```

Lebih aman:

```sql
UPDATE payments
SET status = 'CAPTURED',
    captured_amount_minor = captured_amount_minor + #{capturedAmountMinor},
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
  AND status IN ('CAPTURE_PENDING', 'PARTIALLY_CAPTURED', 'AUTHORIZED')
  AND version = #{version};
```

Kalau `row_count = 0`, berarti ada kemungkinan:

- state sudah berubah oleh webhook,
- request duplicate,
- version stale,
- transition tidak valid.

---

# 5. Amount dan Currency

Untuk payment, hindari `double` dan `float`.

PostgreSQL mendokumentasikan bahwa floating point bersifat inexact, dan untuk exact storage/calculation seperti monetary amount, gunakan `numeric`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/7.2/datatype.html))

Dalam integrasi PSP, umum juga memakai **minor unit integer**. Stripe, misalnya, mendefinisikan `amount` sebagai positive integer dalam smallest currency unit, seperti `100` untuk USD 1.00 atau `100` untuk JPY 100 karena JPY zero-decimal. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/payment_intents/object))

## Rekomendasi Praktis

Untuk payment core table:

```sql
amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL
```

Contoh:

| Currency | Human Amount | Stored Amount |
|---|---:|---:|
| USD | 10.50 | 1050 |
| IDR | 10,000 | 10000 |
| JPY | 100 | 100 |
| EUR | 19.99 | 1999 |

Tambahkan currency metadata service:

```java
public record Money(long amountMinor, String currency) {
    public Money {
        if (amountMinor < 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("amountMinor must not be negative");
        }
        if (currency == null || currency.length() != 3) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("currency must be ISO-4217 alpha-3 code");
        }
        currency = currency.toUpperCase();
    }

    public boolean isZero() {
        return amountMinor == 0;
    }
}
```

---

# 6. Domain Object Praktis

## 6.1 Payment Aggregate

```java
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

public class Payment {

    private UUID paymentId;
    private String orderId;
    private Money authorizedAmount;
    private Money capturedAmount;
    private Money refundedAmount;
    private PaymentStatus status;
    private long version;
    private Instant createdAt;
    private Instant updatedAt;

    public Payment(
            UUID paymentId,
            String orderId,
            Money authorizedAmount,
            PaymentStatus status
    ) {
        this.paymentId = paymentId;
        this.orderId = orderId;
        this.authorizedAmount = authorizedAmount;
        this.capturedAmount = new Money(0, authorizedAmount.currency());
        this.refundedAmount = new Money(0, authorizedAmount.currency());
        this.status = status;
        this.version = 0;
        this.createdAt = Instant.now();
        this.updatedAt = Instant.now();
    }

    public void markAuthorized(Money amount) {
        requireStatus(PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZATION_PENDING, PaymentStatus.REQUIRES_ACTION, PaymentStatus.UNKNOWN);
        requireSameCurrency(amount);

        if (amount.amountMinor() <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("authorized amount must be positive");
        }

        this.authorizedAmount = amount;
        this.status = PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED;
        touch();
    }

    public void markCaptured(Money amount) {
        requireStatus(PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED, PaymentStatus.CAPTURE_PENDING, PaymentStatus.PARTIALLY_CAPTURED, PaymentStatus.UNKNOWN);
        requireSameCurrency(amount);

        long newCaptured = this.capturedAmount.amountMinor() + amount.amountMinor();

        if (newCaptured > this.authorizedAmount.amountMinor()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("captured amount exceeds authorized amount");
        }

        this.capturedAmount = new Money(newCaptured, amount.currency());
        this.status = newCaptured == this.authorizedAmount.amountMinor()
                ? PaymentStatus.CAPTURED
                : PaymentStatus.PARTIALLY_CAPTURED;

        touch();
    }

    public void markRefunded(Money amount) {
        requireStatus(PaymentStatus.CAPTURED, PaymentStatus.PARTIALLY_REFUNDED, PaymentStatus.REFUND_PENDING);
        requireSameCurrency(amount);

        long newRefunded = this.refundedAmount.amountMinor() + amount.amountMinor();

        if (newRefunded > this.capturedAmount.amountMinor()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("refunded amount exceeds captured amount");
        }

        this.refundedAmount = new Money(newRefunded, amount.currency());
        this.status = newRefunded == this.capturedAmount.amountMinor()
                ? PaymentStatus.REFUNDED
                : PaymentStatus.PARTIALLY_REFUNDED;

        touch();
    }

    public void markFailed() {
        requireStatus(PaymentStatus.INITIATED, PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZATION_PENDING, PaymentStatus.CAPTURE_PENDING, PaymentStatus.UNKNOWN);
        this.status = PaymentStatus.FAILED;
        touch();
    }

    private void requireStatus(PaymentStatus... allowed) {
        for (PaymentStatus candidate : allowed) {
            if (this.status == candidate) {
                return;
            }
        }
        throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid payment transition from " + this.status);
    }

    private void requireSameCurrency(Money amount) {
        if (!this.authorizedAmount.currency().equals(amount.currency())) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("currency mismatch");
        }
    }

    private void touch() {
        this.updatedAt = Instant.now();
    }
}
```

---

# 7. Invariant Payment yang Wajib Ada

Invariant adalah aturan yang **tidak boleh dilanggar dalam kondisi apa pun**.

| No | Invariant | Kenapa Penting |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | `captured_amount <= authorized_amount` | Mencegah capture melebihi auth. |
| 2 | `refunded_amount <= captured_amount` | Mencegah refund lebih besar dari uang yang diterima. |
| 3 | `currency` payment tidak berubah setelah dibuat. | Mencegah mismatch accounting. |
| 4 | Satu `provider_payment_id` hanya boleh map ke satu `payment_id`. | Mencegah webhook salah apply ke payment lain. |
| 5 | Satu webhook event hanya boleh diproses sekali secara efektif. | Mencegah double ledger/update. |
| 6 | Final state tidak boleh mundur ke non-final state tanpa proses eksplisit. | Mencegah status korup akibat late webhook. |
| 7 | Semua perubahan finansial harus menghasilkan `payment_event`. | Untuk audit dan debugging. |
| 8 | Semua efek finansial harus menghasilkan `ledger_entry`. | Untuk reconciliation. |
| 9 | Retry harus memakai idempotency key. | Mencegah double charge. |
| 10 | Timeout tidak otomatis berarti failed. | Mencegah salah status ketika PSP sebenarnya sukses. |

---

# 8. PostgreSQL Schema Awal

## 8.1 `payments`

```sql
CREATE TABLE payments (
    payment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    order_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    authorized_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    captured_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    refunded_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    provider VARCHAR(50),
    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),

    version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_amount_non_negative
        CHECK (amount_minor >= 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_authorized_amount_non_negative
        CHECK (authorized_amount_minor >= 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_captured_amount_valid
        CHECK (captured_amount_minor >= 0 AND captured_amount_minor <= authorized_amount_minor),

    CONSTRAINT chk_refunded_amount_valid
        CHECK (refunded_amount_minor >= 0 AND refunded_amount_minor <= captured_amount_minor),

    CONSTRAINT chk_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency))
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payments_provider_payment
ON payments(provider, provider_payment_id)
WHERE provider IS NOT NULL AND provider_payment_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX ix_payments_order_id
ON payments(order_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payments_status
ON payments(status);
```

## 8.2 `payment_attempts`

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_attempts (
    attempt_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_charge_id VARCHAR(200),

    attempt_type VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    request_hash VARCHAR(128),
    failure_code VARCHAR(100),
    failure_message TEXT,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_attempts_payment_id
ON payment_attempts(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_attempts_provider_ref
ON payment_attempts(provider, provider_payment_id);
```

## 8.3 `payment_events`

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_events (
    event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    event_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    source VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    provider VARCHAR(50),
    provider_event_id VARCHAR(200),

    payload JSONB,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_events_provider_event
ON payment_events(provider, provider_event_id)
WHERE provider IS NOT NULL AND provider_event_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_events_payment_id
ON payment_events(payment_id);
```

## 8.4 `ledger_entries`

```sql
CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    entry_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    direction VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,

    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    reference_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    reference_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_amount_positive
        CHECK (amount_minor > 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_direction
        CHECK (direction IN ('DEBIT', 'CREDIT'))
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_ledger_reference
ON ledger_entries(reference_type, reference_id, entry_type);
```

# 9. MyBatis Mapper Praktis

## 9.1 Update State dengan Optimistic Locking

```xml
<update id="markAuthorized">
    UPDATE payments
    SET status = 'AUTHORIZED',
        authorized_amount_minor = #{authorizedAmountMinor},
        provider = #{provider},
        provider_payment_id = #{providerPaymentId},
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
      AND status IN ('INITIATED', 'AUTHORIZATION_PENDING', 'REQUIRES_ACTION', 'UNKNOWN')
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

## 9.2 Capture dengan Guard Amount

```xml
<update id="addCapturedAmount">
    UPDATE payments
    SET captured_amount_minor = captured_amount_minor + #{captureAmountMinor},
        status = CASE
            WHEN captured_amount_minor + #{captureAmountMinor} = authorized_amount_minor
                THEN 'CAPTURED'
            ELSE 'PARTIALLY_CAPTURED'
        END,
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
      AND status IN ('AUTHORIZED', 'CAPTURE_PENDING', 'PARTIALLY_CAPTURED', 'UNKNOWN')
      AND captured_amount_minor + #{captureAmountMinor} <= authorized_amount_minor
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

## 9.3 Insert Event

```xml
<insert id="insertPaymentEvent">
    INSERT INTO payment_events (
        event_id,
        payment_id,
        event_type,
        source,
        provider,
        provider_event_id,
        payload,
        created_at
    ) VALUES (
        #{eventId},
        #{paymentId},
        #{eventType},
        #{source},
        #{provider},
        #{providerEventId},
        CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb),
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

Practical note:

> Untuk operasi finansial, jangan hanya update `payments`. Selalu insert `payment_events` dalam transaksi yang sama.

---

# 10. Jersey/JAX-RS API Skeleton

```java
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import java.net.URI;
import java.util.UUID;

@Path("/payments")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class PaymentResource {

    private final PaymentApplicationService paymentService;

    public PaymentResource(PaymentApplicationService paymentService) {
        this.paymentService = paymentService;
    }

    @POST
    public Response createPayment(
            @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey,
            CreatePaymentRequest request
    ) {
        PaymentResponse response = paymentService.createPayment(idempotencyKey, request);

        return Response
                .created(URI.create("/payments/" + response.paymentId()))
                .entity(response)
                .build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{paymentId}")
    public Response getPayment(@PathParam("paymentId") UUID paymentId) {
        PaymentResponse response = paymentService.getPayment(paymentId);
        return Response.ok(response).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{paymentId}/capture")
    public Response capturePayment(
            @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey,
            @PathParam("paymentId") UUID paymentId,
            CapturePaymentRequest request
    ) {
        PaymentResponse response = paymentService.capturePayment(idempotencyKey, paymentId, request);
        return Response.ok(response).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{paymentId}/refunds")
    public Response refundPayment(
            @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey,
            @PathParam("paymentId") UUID paymentId,
            RefundPaymentRequest request
    ) {
        RefundResponse response = paymentService.refundPayment(idempotencyKey, paymentId, request);
        return Response.accepted(response).build();
    }
}
```

# 11. Request DTO

```java
public record CreatePaymentRequest(
        String orderId,
        long amountMinor,
        String currency,
        String paymentMethodType,
        boolean captureImmediately
) {}
```

```java
public record CapturePaymentRequest(
        long amountMinor
) {}
```

```java
public record RefundPaymentRequest(
        long amountMinor,
        String reason
) {}
```

---

# 12. Application Service Flow

## 12.1 Create Payment

```text
POST /payments
  1. Validate request.
  2. Validate Idempotency-Key.
  3. Check duplicate order/payment rule.
  4. Insert payments with INITIATED.
  5. Insert payment_attempts.
  6. Call gateway adapter.
  7. Map provider response to internal state.
  8. Insert payment_events.
  9. Return response.
```

## 12.2 Capture Payment

```text
POST /payments/{id}/capture
  1. Validate Idempotency-Key.
  2. Load payment with lock/version.
  3. Ensure status AUTHORIZED or PARTIALLY_CAPTURED.
  4. Ensure capture amount <= remaining authorized amount.
  5. Call provider capture API.
  6. If success/pending, update state.
  7. Insert payment_event.
  8. Insert ledger_entry if financially captured.
```

## 12.3 Refund Payment

```text
POST /payments/{id}/refunds
  1. Validate payment captured.
  2. Ensure refund amount <= remaining captured amount.
  3. Create refund record.
  4. Call provider refund API.
  5. Set refund PENDING/SUCCEEDED depending provider response.
  6. Later confirm via webhook/reconciliation.
```

Adyen, misalnya, mendukung capture penuh maupun partial capture, dan multiple partial captures dapat menghasilkan multiple webhook, satu untuk tiap capture request. Refund juga diinformasikan melalui webhook. Ini memperkuat kebutuhan desain event-driven dan idempotent processing. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/issuing/payment-stages))

---

# 13. Transition Table Praktis

| Current State | Event | Next State | Valid? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `INITIATED` | `AUTH_REQUESTED` | `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | Ya | Setelah request ke PSP dibuat. |
| `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | `CUSTOMER_ACTION_REQUIRED` | `REQUIRES_ACTION` | Ya | 3DS/redirect/challenge. |
| `REQUIRES_ACTION` | `CUSTOMER_ACTION_COMPLETED` | `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | Ya | Tunggu final PSP result. |
| `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | `AUTH_SUCCEEDED` | `AUTHORIZED` | Ya | Bisa capture nanti. |
| `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | `PAYMENT_SUCCEEDED_AUTO_CAPTURE` | `CAPTURED` | Ya | Untuk flow auto-capture. |
| `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | `AUTH_FAILED` | `FAILED` | Ya | Hard decline atau rejected. |
| `AUTHORIZED` | `CAPTURE_REQUESTED` | `CAPTURE_PENDING` | Ya | Capture async. |
| `CAPTURE_PENDING` | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED_FULL` | `CAPTURED` | Ya | Full capture. |
| `CAPTURE_PENDING` | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED_PARTIAL` | `PARTIALLY_CAPTURED` | Ya | Partial capture. |
| `PARTIALLY_CAPTURED` | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED_FULL` | `CAPTURED` | Ya | Sisa amount dicapture. |
| `AUTHORIZED` | `AUTH_CANCELLED` | `CANCELLED` | Ya | Void/cancel sebelum capture. |
| `CAPTURED` | `REFUND_REQUESTED` | `REFUND_PENDING` | Ya | Refund async. |
| `REFUND_PENDING` | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED_PARTIAL` | `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED` | Ya | Partial refund. |
| `REFUND_PENDING` | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED_FULL` | `REFUNDED` | Ya | Full refund. |
| `CAPTURED` | `DISPUTE_OPENED` | `DISPUTED` | Ya | Chargeback/dispute lifecycle. |
| `FAILED` | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED` | ? | Tidak langsung | Harus investigasi, jangan overwrite otomatis. |
| `REFUNDED` | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED` | ? | Tidak | Late webhook harus diabaikan atau masuk exception queue. |

---

# 14. Anti-Pattern yang Harus Dihindari

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Satu table `payments` berisi semua hal. | Attempt, event, refund, ledger bercampur. | Pisahkan aggregate, attempt, event, ledger. |
| Status hanya `PAID` / `FAILED`. | Tidak cukup untuk pending, auth-only, partial capture, refund. | Gunakan state machine eksplisit. |
| Timeout dianggap failed. | Bisa menyebabkan order unpaid padahal customer sudah ter-charge. | Gunakan `UNKNOWN` / `PENDING_CONFIRMATION`. |
| Webhook langsung update status tanpa dedup. | Duplicate webhook bisa double update. | Gunakan inbox table + unique event id. |
| Tidak ada ledger. | Finance dan audit sulit. | Append-only ledger. |
| Simpan amount sebagai `double`. | Rounding error. | Gunakan `BIGINT amount_minor` atau `NUMERIC`. |
| Pakai raw provider status di seluruh service. | Vendor lock-in dan sulit multi-provider. | Map ke internal enum. |
| Update status tanpa version check. | Race condition. | Optimistic locking atau row lock. |
| Refund hanya update payment status. | Partial/multiple refund tidak bisa dilacak. | Buat `refunds` sebagai entity sendiri. |
| Tidak menyimpan raw event payload. | Sulit debugging/replay. | Simpan raw payload di `payment_events`/`inbox`. |

---

# 15. Checklist Implementasi Seri 1

Gunakan checklist ini saat mulai membuat payment module.

## Domain

- [ ] Ada `PaymentStatus` internal.
- [ ] Ada transition table yang eksplisit.
- [ ] Ada invariant amount.
- [ ] Ada pemisahan `Payment`, `PaymentAttempt`, `PaymentEvent`, `Refund`, `LedgerEntry`.
- [ ] Ada field `version` untuk optimistic locking.
- [ ] Ada konsep `UNKNOWN` / `PENDING`.

## Database

- [ ] Amount disimpan sebagai `amount_minor BIGINT` atau `NUMERIC`, bukan floating point.
- [ ] Currency disimpan sebagai `CHAR(3)`.
- [ ] Ada check constraint untuk amount.
- [ ] Ada unique constraint untuk provider reference.
- [ ] Ada audit event table.
- [ ] Ada ledger table append-only.

## API

- [ ] `POST /payments`
- [ ] `GET /payments/{paymentId}`
- [ ] `POST /payments/{paymentId}/capture`
- [ ] `POST /payments/{paymentId}/refunds`
- [ ] Semua mutating endpoint menerima `Idempotency-Key`.
- [ ] Error response tidak expose raw PSP error sembarangan.

## Failure Handling

- [ ] Timeout tidak langsung `FAILED`.
- [ ] Late webhook tidak overwrite final state sembarangan.
- [ ] Duplicate webhook tidak membuat double ledger.
- [ ] Concurrent capture/refund dicegah dengan locking/version.
- [ ] Semua state transition tercatat sebagai event.

# 16. Exercise Praktis

Untuk benar-benar menguasai seri ini, buat mini module lokal:

```text
payment-service/
  src/main/java/com/company/payment/
    api/
      PaymentResource.java
    application/
      PaymentApplicationService.java
    domain/
      Payment.java
      PaymentStatus.java
      Money.java
      PaymentEvent.java
      PaymentAttempt.java
      Refund.java
      LedgerEntry.java
    infrastructure/
      persistence/
        PaymentMapper.java
        PaymentMapper.xml
```

Target exercise:

1. Buat `POST /payments`.
2. Simpan payment dengan status `INITIATED`.
3. Simulasikan provider response:
   - success authorization,
   - requires action,
   - failed,
   - timeout.
4. Update status melalui method domain, bukan langsung assign enum.
5. Tambahkan test untuk invalid transition:
   - refund sebelum capture,
   - capture lebih besar dari authorized amount,
   - currency mismatch,
   - duplicate provider reference.

---

# 17. Ringkasan

Di seri ini, fondasi yang harus diingat:

```text
Payment bukan CRUD.
Payment adalah state machine + financial invariant + event timeline.
```

Desain yang sehat:

```text
Payment = niat bisnis internal
PaymentAttempt = percobaan teknis ke provider
PaymentEvent = timeline/audit
LedgerEntry = efek finansial
Refund = lifecycle tersendiri
ProviderReference = mapping ke PSP
```

Kalau fondasi ini benar, seri berikutnya seperti idempotency, webhook, gateway adapter, Camunda saga, dan reconciliation akan jauh lebih mudah.
