## Response:
7/14/2026, 10:16:01 PM

Saya lanjutkan ke **Seri 2/18**. Fokusnya kali ini bukan teori domain lagi, tetapi rancangan schema PostgreSQL yang bisa langsung dipakai sebagai baseline implementasi payment service.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 2/18 - Database Schema Design: `payments`, `attempts`, `events`, `refunds`, `ledger`, `idempotency`, `inbox`, dan `outbox`

Seri ini fokus pada **rancangan database PostgreSQL** untuk payment service berbasis **Java Jersey/JAX-RS + MyBatis**.

Targetnya bukan sekadar “punya tabel payment”, tetapi punya schema yang tahan terhadap:

- retry request,
- duplicate payment,
- timeout ke PSP,
- webhook duplicate,
- webhook out-of-order,
- partial capture,
- partial refund,
- dispute,
- audit,
- reconciliation,
- event publishing yang reliable.

PostgreSQL mendukung constraint seperti `PRIMARY KEY`, `FOREIGN KEY`, `UNIQUE`, dan `CHECK`, yang sangat penting untuk menjaga invariant payment di level database, bukan hanya di Java code. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/ddl-constraints.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) MyBatis juga cocok untuk model seperti ini karena memang menyediakan mapping SQL eksplisit melalui XML/annotation untuk `select`, `insert`, `update`, dan `delete`, sehingga kita bisa mengontrol query finansial secara detail. ([mybatis.org](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Introduction

Untuk payment system, database bukan hanya tempat menyimpan status akhir.

Database harus menjadi:

```text
1. Source of truth untuk payment state.
2. Audit trail untuk semua perubahan.
3. Deduplication layer untuk retry dan webhook.
4. Financial record untuk ledger.
5. Recovery point saat integration failure.
6. Bridge untuk event-driven architecture melalui outbox.
```

Desain yang buruk biasanya seperti ini:

```text
payments
- id
- order_id
- amount
- status
```

Itu terlalu dangkal.

Desain yang lebih aman:

```text
payments
payment_attempts
payment_events
refunds
ledger_entries
idempotency_keys
payment_inbox_events
payment_outbox_events
reconciliation_runs
reconciliation_items
```

---

# 2. Diagram Database Model

```mermaid
erDiagram
    payments ||--o{ payment_attempts : has
    payments ||--o{ payment_events : records
    payments ||--o{ refunds : has
    payments ||--o{ ledger_entries : posts
    payments ||--o{ payment_outbox_events : emits

    payment_attempts ||--o{ payment_events : produces
    refunds ||--o{ ledger_entries : posts
    refunds ||--o{ payment_events : records

    payment_inbox_events ||--o| payment_events : may_create
    reconciliation_runs ||--o{ reconciliation_items : contains
    payments ||--o{ reconciliation_items : reconciled_against

    payments {
        uuid payment_id PK
        varchar order_id
        bigint amount_minor
        char currency
        varchar status
        bigint version
        timestamp created_at
        timestamp updated_at
    }

    payment_attempts {
        uuid attempt_id PK
        uuid payment_id FK
        varchar provider
        varchar provider_payment_id
        varchar status
        timestamp created_at
    }

    payment_events {
        uuid event_id PK
        uuid payment_id FK
        varchar event_type
        varchar source
        jsonb payload
        timestamp created_at
    }

    refunds {
        uuid refund_id PK
        uuid payment_id FK
        bigint amount_minor
        varchar status
        timestamp created_at
    }

    ledger_entries {
        uuid ledger_entry_id PK
        uuid payment_id FK
        varchar entry_type
        varchar direction
        bigint amount_minor
        char currency
    }

    idempotency_keys {
        varchar idempotency_key PK
        varchar operation_type
        varchar request_hash
        varchar status
    }

    payment_inbox_events {
        uuid inbox_event_id PK
        varchar provider
        varchar provider_event_id
        varchar processing_status
    }

    payment_outbox_events {
        uuid outbox_event_id PK
        varchar aggregate_type
        uuid aggregate_id
        varchar event_type
        varchar publish_status
    }
```

---

# 3. Practical Table Decomposition

| No | Table | Fungsi Praktis | Kenapa Dipisah |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | `payments` | Aggregate utama payment internal. | Menyimpan niat bisnis dan state utama. |
| 2 | `payment_attempts` | Satu atau lebih percobaan ke PSP. | Payment bisa retry atau pindah provider. |
| 3 | `payment_events` | Timeline perubahan state. | Untuk audit, debugging, replay analysis. |
| 4 | `refunds` | Lifecycle refund. | Refund bisa partial, multiple, async. |
| 5 | `ledger_entries` | Catatan finansial append-only. | Untuk audit dan reconciliation. |
| 6 | `idempotency_keys` | Dedup request dari client/internal service. | Mencegah double charge akibat retry. |
| 7 | `payment_inbox_events` | Dedup webhook/event masuk. | Mencegah duplicate webhook diproses dua kali. |
| 8 | `payment_outbox_events` | Reliable event publishing. | Mencegah DB update sukses tapi event hilang. |
| 9 | `reconciliation_runs` | Batch rekonsiliasi dengan PSP/bank. | Tracking proses reconcile. |
| 10 | `reconciliation_items` | Detail item reconcile. | Menyimpan match/mismatch per transaksi. |

---

# 4. Naming dan Convention

Gunakan convention yang konsisten.

| Area | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Primary key | `payment_id`, `attempt_id`, `refund_id`, bukan generic `id`. |
| Amount | `amount_minor BIGINT`, bukan `double`. |
| Currency | `currency CHAR(3)` uppercase. |
| Status | `VARCHAR(40)` atau PostgreSQL enum. Untuk enterprise, `VARCHAR` lebih fleksibel untuk migration. |
| Timestamp | `TIMESTAMPTZ`, bukan `TIMESTAMP` biasa. |
| Payload vendor | `JSONB`, bukan raw `TEXT`, kecuali untuk raw signature verification. |
| Audit | Jangan update event/ledger; gunakan append-only. |
| Versioning | `version BIGINT` untuk optimistic locking. |

PostgreSQL menyediakan `jsonb` untuk menyimpan data JSON yang bisa di-index. Dokumentasi PostgreSQL juga menjelaskan bahwa GIN index dapat digunakan untuk mencari key/value dalam banyak dokumen `jsonb`. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/datatype-json.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Schema Detail

## 5.1 Table `payments`

Table ini adalah aggregate utama.

```sql
CREATE TABLE payments (
    payment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    order_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    customer_id VARCHAR(100),
    merchant_id VARCHAR(100),

    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    authorized_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    captured_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    refunded_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    provider VARCHAR(50),
    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),

    capture_mode VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'AUTO',
    payment_method_type VARCHAR(50),

    failure_code VARCHAR(100),
    failure_message TEXT,

    metadata JSONB,

    version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payments_amount_non_negative
        CHECK (amount_minor >= 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payments_authorized_non_negative
        CHECK (authorized_amount_minor >= 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payments_captured_valid
        CHECK (
            captured_amount_minor >= 0
            AND captured_amount_minor <= authorized_amount_minor
        ),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payments_refunded_valid
        CHECK (
            refunded_amount_minor >= 0
            AND refunded_amount_minor <= captured_amount_minor
        ),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payments_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency)),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payments_capture_mode
        CHECK (capture_mode IN ('AUTO', 'MANUAL'))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_payments_order_id
ON payments(order_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payments_customer_id
ON payments(customer_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payments_merchant_id
ON payments merchant_id;

CREATE INDEX ix_payments_status
ON payments(status);

CREATE INDEX ix_payments_created_at
ON payments(created_at);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payments_provider_payment_id
ON payments(provider, provider_payment_id)
WHERE provider IS NOT NULL
  AND provider_payment_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX ix_payments_metadata_gin
ON payments USING GIN(metadata);
```

Catatan: ada typo kecil yang harus diperbaiki di index `merchant_id`.

Versi benar:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_payments_merchant_id
ON payments(merchant_id);
```

### Practical Usage

`payments` menjawab pertanyaan seperti:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM payments
WHERE payment_id = '...';
```

```sql
SELECT *
FROM payments
WHERE order_id = 'ORD-123';
```

```sql
SELECT status, count(*)
FROM payments
WHERE created_at >= now() - interval '1 day'
GROUP BY status;
```

---

## 5.2 Table `payment_attempts`

Satu payment bisa punya beberapa attempt.

Contoh kasus:

```text
Attempt 1: call PSP timeout.
Attempt 2: query status PSP.
Attempt 3: retry authorization dengan idempotency key sama.
Attempt 4: fallback provider, jika business rule mengizinkan.
```

Schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_attempts (
    attempt_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    attempt_type VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_charge_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_request_id VARCHAR(200),

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    request_hash VARCHAR(128),
    request_payload JSONB,
    response_payload JSONB,

    failure_code VARCHAR(100),
    failure_message TEXT,

    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_attempts_type
        CHECK (attempt_type IN (
            'AUTHORIZE',
            'CAPTURE',
            'SALE',
            'VOID',
            'REFUND',
            'QUERY_STATUS'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_attempts_amount_positive
        CHECK (amount_minor > 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_attempts_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_payment_attempts_payment_id
ON payment_attempts(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_attempts_provider_payment_id
ON payment_attempts(provider, provider_payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_attempts_status
ON payment_attempts(status);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_attempts_started_at
ON payment_attempts(started_at);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_attempts_request_payload_gin
ON payment_attempts USING GIN(request_payload);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_attempts_response_payload_gin
ON payment_attempts USING GIN(response_payload);
```

### Practical Usage

Cari semua attempt untuk satu payment:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM payment_attempts
WHERE payment_id = :payment_id
ORDER BY started_at ASC;
```

Cari attempt yang stuck:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM payment_attempts
WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'UNKNOWN')
  AND started_at < now() - interval '15 minutes';
```

---

## 5.3 Table `payment_events`

Ini adalah timeline audit.

Event tidak boleh dipakai hanya untuk “nice to have”. Dalam payment, event adalah alat utama untuk investigasi.

Schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_events (
    event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    attempt_id UUID REFERENCES payment_attempts(attempt_id),
    refund_id UUID,

    event_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    source VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    provider VARCHAR(50),
    provider_event_id VARCHAR(200),

    old_status VARCHAR(40),
    new_status VARCHAR(40),

    payload JSONB,
    raw_payload TEXT,

    created_by VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_events_source
        CHECK (source IN (
            'API',
            'PROVIDER_RESPONSE',
            'WEBHOOK',
            'RECONCILIATION',
            'SYSTEM_JOB',
            'ADMIN'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_payment_events_payment_id
ON payment_events(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_events_attempt_id
ON payment_events(attempt_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_events_refund_id
ON payment_events(refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_events_event_type
ON payment_events(event_type);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_events_created_at
ON payment_events(created_at);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_events_provider_event
ON payment_events(provider, provider_event_id)
WHERE provider IS NOT NULL
  AND provider_event_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_events_payload_gin
ON payment_events USING GIN(payload);
```

### Practical Usage

Payment timeline:

```sql
SELECT event_type,
       source,
       old_status,
       new_status,
       created_at
FROM payment_events
WHERE payment_id = :payment_id
ORDER BY created_at ASC;
```

Cari webhook duplicate:

```sql
SELECT provider, provider_event_id, count(*)
FROM payment_events
WHERE provider_event_id IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY provider, provider_event_id
HAVING count(*) > 1;
```

---

## 5.4 Table `refunds`

Refund harus punya lifecycle sendiri.

Jangan hanya menambah `refund_status` di `payments`.

Alasannya:

```text
1 payment bisa punya banyak refund.
1 refund bisa pending.
1 refund bisa gagal.
1 refund bisa sukses sebagian tergantung provider.
Refund bisa dikonfirmasi lewat webhook.
Refund bisa muncul di settlement report.
```

Schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE refunds (
    refund_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    provider VARCHAR(50),
    provider_refund_id VARCHAR(200),

    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    reason VARCHAR(200),

    requested_by VARCHAR(100),
    approved_by VARCHAR(100),

    request_payload JSONB,
    response_payload JSONB,

    failure_code VARCHAR(100),
    failure_message TEXT,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_refunds_amount_positive
        CHECK (amount_minor > 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_refunds_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_refunds_payment_id
ON refunds(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_refunds_status
ON refunds(status);

CREATE INDEX ix_refunds_created_at
ON refunds(created_at);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_refunds_provider_refund_id
ON refunds(provider, provider_refund_id)
WHERE provider IS NOT NULL
  AND provider_refund_id IS NOT NULL;
```

### Practical Usage

Hitung total refund sukses:

```sql
SELECT COALESCE(sum(amount_minor), 0) AS refunded_amount_minor
FROM refunds
WHERE payment_id = :payment_id
  AND status IN ('SUCCEEDED', 'PARTIALLY_SUCCEEDED');
```

Cek refund yang masih pending:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM refunds
WHERE status IN ('REQUESTED', 'PENDING')
  AND created_at < now() - interval '30 minutes';
```

---

## 5.5 Table `ledger_entries`

Ledger adalah catatan finansial append-only.

Pola praktis:

```text
CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED  -> CREDIT platform/merchant receivable
REFUND_SUCCEEDED   -> DEBIT platform/merchant receivable
CHARGEBACK_LOST    -> DEBIT platform/merchant receivable
FEE_CHARGED        -> DEBIT fee account
SETTLEMENT_PAID    -> CREDIT bank settlement
```

Schema sederhana:

```sql
CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),
    refund_id UUID REFERENCES refunds(refund_id),

    entry_type VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
    direction VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,

    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    account_code VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,

    reference_type VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,
    reference_id VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,

    description TEXT,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_entries_amount_positive
        CHECK (amount_minor > 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_entries_direction
        CHECK (direction IN ('DEBIT', 'CREDIT')),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_entries_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_payment_id
ON ledger_entries(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_refund_id
ON ledger_entries(refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_entry_type
ON ledger_entries(entry_type);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_created_at
ON ledger_entries(created_at);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_ledger_entries_reference
ON ledger_entries(reference_type, reference_id, entry_type);
```

### Practical Usage

Ledger timeline untuk satu payment:

```sql
SELECT entry_type,
       direction,
       amount_minor,
       currency,
       account_code,
       reference_type,
       reference_id,
       created_at
FROM ledger_entries
WHERE payment_id = :payment_id
ORDER BY created_at ASC;
```

Cek balance kasar per payment:

```sql
SELECT payment_id,
       currency,
       sum(
           CASE
               WHEN direction = 'CREDIT' THEN amount_minor
               WHEN direction = 'DEBIT' THEN -amount_minor
           END
       ) AS balance_minor
FROM ledger_entries
WHERE payment_id = :payment_id
GROUP BY payment_id, currency;
```

---

## 5.6 Table `idempotency_keys`

Idempotency mencegah operasi yang sama dijalankan dua kali.

Contoh:

```text
Client call POST /payments.
Network timeout.
Client retry dengan Idempotency-Key sama.
Service harus return response yang sama atau status operation yang sama.
Bukan create payment baru.
```

Schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE idempotency_keys (
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(200) PRIMARY KEY,

    operation_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    operation_key VARCHAR(200),

    request_hash VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    response_status_code INTEGER,
    response_body JSONB,

    locked_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_idempotency_keys_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'PROCESSING',
            'SUCCEEDED',
            'FAILED',
            'EXPIRED'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_keys_operation
ON idempotency_keys(operation_type, operation_key);

CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_keys_status
ON idempotency_keys(status);

CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_keys_locked_until
ON idempotency_keys(locked_until);

CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_keys_created_at
ON idempotency_keys(created_at);
```

### Practical Usage

Insert idempotency key:

```sql
INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (
    idempotency_key,
    operation_type,
    operation_key,
    request_hash,
    status,
    locked_until
)
VALUES (
    :idempotency_key,
    :operation_type,
    :operation_key,
    :request_hash,
    'PROCESSING',
    now() + interval '5 minutes'
)
ON CONFLICT (idempotency_key) DO NOTHING;
```

Kalau insert berhasil, request pertama boleh lanjut.

Kalau insert gagal, load record existing:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM idempotency_keys
WHERE idempotency_key = :idempotency_key;
```

Decision:

| Existing Status | Action |
|---|---|
| `PROCESSING` | Return `409 Conflict` atau tunggu sebentar, tergantung API design. |
| `SUCCEEDED` | Return cached response. |
| `FAILED` | Return cached failure, kecuali policy mengizinkan retry. |
| `EXPIRED` | Bisa reject atau create key baru tergantung retention policy. |

---

## 5.7 Table `payment_inbox_events`

Inbox dipakai untuk dedup webhook/event masuk.

Webhook provider sering bisa dikirim lebih dari sekali. Maka dedup harus terjadi sebelum state transition.

Schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_inbox_events (
    inbox_event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_event_id VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    event_type VARCHAR(100),
    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_refund_id VARCHAR(200),

    raw_payload TEXT NOT NULL,
    parsed_payload JSONB,

    signature_header TEXT,
    verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,

    processing_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'RECEIVED',
    processing_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    last_error TEXT,

    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_inbox_processing_status
        CHECK (processing_status IN (
            'RECEIVED',
            'PROCESSING',
            'PROCESSED',
            'DUPLICATE',
            'FAILED',
            'IGNORED'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_inbox_provider_event
ON payment_inbox_events(provider, provider_event_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_inbox_provider_payment
ON payment_inbox_events(provider, provider_payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_inbox_processing_status
ON payment_inbox_events(processing_status);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_inbox_received_at
ON payment_inbox_events(received_at);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_inbox_payload_gin
ON payment_inbox_events USING GIN(parsed_payload);
```

### Practical Usage

Insert webhook:

```sql
INSERT INTO payment_inbox_events (
    inbox_event_id,
    provider,
    provider_event_id,
    event_type,
    provider_payment_id,
    raw_payload,
    parsed_payload,
    signature_header,
    verified,
    processing_status
)
VALUES (
    :inbox_event_id,
    :provider,
    :provider_event_id,
    :event_type,
    :provider_payment_id,
    :raw_payload,
    CAST(:parsed_payload AS jsonb),
    :signature_header,
    :verified,
    'RECEIVED'
)
ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id)
DO UPDATE SET
    processing_status = 'DUPLICATE';
```

Namun hati-hati: `DO UPDATE SET processing_status = 'DUPLICATE'` bisa merusak status original kalau event pertama sudah `PROCESSED`. Alternatif lebih aman:

```sql
INSERT INTO payment_inbox_events (
    inbox_event_id,
    provider,
    provider_event_id,
    raw_payload,
    verified,
    processing_status
)
VALUES (
    :inbox_event_id,
    :provider,
    :provider_event_id,
    :raw_payload,
    :verified,
    'RECEIVED'
)
ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id)
DO NOTHING;
```

Lalu kalau affected row `0`, anggap duplicate dan return HTTP 200 ke provider.

---

## 5.8 Table `payment_outbox_events`

Outbox menyelesaikan masalah dual-write:

```text
1. Update payment status di DB.
2. Publish event ke broker.

Kalau step 1 sukses tapi step 2 gagal, service lain tidak tahu payment sudah berubah.
Kalau step 2 sukses tapi step 1 rollback, service lain menerima event palsu.
```

Solusinya: insert event outbox di transaksi DB yang sama dengan update payment.

Schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_outbox_events (
    outbox_event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    aggregate_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id UUID NOT NULL,

    event_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    event_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

    payload JSONB NOT NULL,

    publish_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    publish_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    next_attempt_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    last_error TEXT,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_outbox_publish_status
        CHECK (publish_status IN (
            'PENDING',
            'PROCESSING',
            'PUBLISHED',
            'FAILED',
            'DEAD_LETTER'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_payment_outbox_pending
ON payment_outbox_events(publish_status, next_attempt_at);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_outbox_aggregate
ON payment_outbox_events(aggregate_type, aggregate_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_outbox_event_type
ON payment_outbox_events(event_type);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_outbox_created_at
ON payment_outbox_events(created_at);
```

### Practical Usage

Ambil event outbox untuk dipublish:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM payment_outbox_events
WHERE publish_status = 'PENDING'
  AND next_attempt_at <= now()
ORDER BY created_at ASC
LIMIT 100
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
```

`FOR UPDATE` berguna saat beberapa worker mengambil row yang sama. PostgreSQL menjelaskan explicit locking seperti row-level lock untuk mengontrol transaksi yang melakukan update/delete/select-for-update pada row yang sama. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/explicit-locking.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5.9 Table `reconciliation_runs`

Reconciliation biasanya jalan batch.

Schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE reconciliation_runs (
    reconciliation_run_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    report_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,

    report_date DATE NOT NULL,
    report_file_name VARCHAR(255),

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    total_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    matched_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    mismatched_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    missing_internal_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    missing_provider_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_reconciliation_runs_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'STARTED',
            'COMPLETED',
            'FAILED',
            'PARTIAL'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_reconciliation_runs_provider_date
ON reconciliation_runs(provider, report_date);

CREATE INDEX ix_reconciliation_runs_status
ON reconciliation_runs(status);
```

---

## 5.10 Table `reconciliation_items`

Schema:

```sql
CREATE TABLE reconciliation_items (
    reconciliation_item_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    reconciliation_run_id UUID NOT NULL
        REFERENCES reconciliation_runs(reconciliation_run_id),

    payment_id UUID REFERENCES payments(payment_id),
    refund_id UUID REFERENCES refunds(refund_id),

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_refund_id VARCHAR(200),

    item_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    match_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    internal_amount_minor BIGINT,
    provider_amount_minor BIGINT,
    currency CHAR(3),

    difference_minor BIGINT,

    details JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_reconciliation_items_match_status
        CHECK (match_status IN (
            'MATCHED',
            'AMOUNT_MISMATCH',
            'STATUS_MISMATCH',
            'MISSING_INTERNAL',
            'MISSING_PROVIDER',
            'DUPLICATE',
            'IGNORED'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_reconciliation_items_run_id
ON reconciliation_items(reconciliation_run_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_reconciliation_items_payment_id
ON reconciliation_items(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_reconciliation_items_refund_id
ON reconciliation_items(refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_reconciliation_items_match_status
ON reconciliation_items(match_status);

CREATE INDEX ix_reconciliation_items_provider_payment
ON reconciliation_items(provider, provider_payment_id);
```

---

# 6. Transaction Boundary yang Disarankan

## 6.1 Create Payment Transaction

```text
BEGIN
  1. Insert idempotency key PROCESSING.
  2. Insert payments INITIATED.
  3. Insert payment_attempts AUTHORIZE/SALE PENDING.
  4. Insert payment_events PAYMENT_INITIATED.
COMMIT

Call PSP

BEGIN
  5. Update payment status based on PSP response.
  6. Update payment_attempt status.
  7. Insert payment_events PROVIDER_RESPONSE_RECEIVED.
  8. Insert ledger_entries if financially captured.
  9. Insert payment_outbox_events PaymentAuthorized/PaymentCaptured.
  10. Update idempotency key SUCCEEDED with response snapshot.
COMMIT
```

Kenapa call PSP tidak di dalam transaksi DB panjang?

Karena external call bisa lambat atau timeout. Kalau transaksi DB dibuka terlalu lama, row/table bisa terkunci lebih lama dan throughput turun. Untuk update kritikal, gunakan transaksi pendek dengan optimistic locking atau row lock.

PostgreSQL sendiri menyediakan beberapa isolation level, tetapi dokumentasi menjelaskan bahwa secara internal hanya tiga level berbeda yang diimplementasikan karena MVCC; ini penting saat kita merancang transaksi payment yang harus sadar race condition. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/transaction-iso.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. Optimistic Locking Pattern

Field:

```sql
version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
```

Update:

```sql
UPDATE payments
SET status = :new_status,
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE payment_id = :payment_id
  AND status = :expected_status
  AND version = :expected_version;
```

Kalau affected row `0`, jangan langsung anggap error teknis. Kemungkinan:

| Kemungkinan | Makna |
|---|---|
| Payment tidak ditemukan | Return 404. |
| Version berubah | Ada concurrent update. Reload state. |
| Status sudah berubah | Webhook/retry/admin sudah memproses. |
| Transition tidak valid | Return domain error. |

---

# 8. Row Locking Pattern untuk Critical Flow

Untuk operasi seperti capture/refund, kadang lebih aman lock row dulu.

```sql
SELECT *
FROM payments
WHERE payment_id = :payment_id
FOR UPDATE;
```

Lalu validasi:

```text
1. status boleh capture?
2. remaining authorized amount cukup?
3. remaining refundable amount cukup?
4. currency sama?
5. belum ada duplicate request?
```

Gunakan transaksi singkat:

```text
BEGIN
  SELECT payment FOR UPDATE
  validate
  insert attempt
  mark CAPTURE_PENDING
  insert event
COMMIT

Call PSP outside transaction

BEGIN
  SELECT payment FOR UPDATE
  apply provider result
  insert event
  insert ledger
  insert outbox
COMMIT
```

---

# 9. MyBatis Mapper Design

## 9.1 Interface

```java
public interface PaymentMapper {

    int insertPayment(PaymentRecord payment);

    PaymentRecord findPaymentById(UUID paymentId);

    PaymentRecord findPaymentByIdForUpdate(UUID paymentId);

    int updatePaymentState(UpdatePaymentStateCommand command);

    int addCapturedAmount(AddCapturedAmountCommand command);

    int addRefundedAmount(AddRefundedAmountCommand command);

    int insertPaymentAttempt(PaymentAttemptRecord attempt);

    int updatePaymentAttempt(PaymentAttemptUpdateCommand command);

    int insertPaymentEvent(PaymentEventRecord event);

    int insertLedgerEntry(LedgerEntryRecord entry);

    int insertOutboxEvent(OutboxEventRecord event);
}
```

## 9.2 `select ... for update`

```xml
<select id="findPaymentByIdForUpdate" resultMap="PaymentResultMap">
    SELECT
        payment_id,
        order_id,
        customer_id,
        merchant_id,
        amount_minor,
        currency,
        authorized_amount_minor,
        captured_amount_minor,
        refunded_amount_minor,
        status,
        provider,
        provider_payment_id,
        capture_mode,
        payment_method_type,
        failure_code,
        failure_message,
        metadata,
        version,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    FROM payments
    WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
    FOR UPDATE
</select>
```

## 9.3 Insert Payment

```xml
<insert id="insertPayment">
    INSERT INTO payments (
        payment_id,
        order_id,
        customer_id,
        merchant_id,
        amount_minor,
        currency,
        authorized_amount_minor,
        captured_amount_minor,
        refunded_amount_minor,
        status,
        capture_mode,
        payment_method_type,
        metadata,
        version,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    ) VALUES (
        #{paymentId},
        #{orderId},
        #{customerId},
        #{merchantId},
        #{amountMinor},
        #{currency},
        #{authorizedAmountMinor},
        #{capturedAmountMinor},
        #{refundedAmountMinor},
        #{status},
        #{captureMode},
        #{paymentMethodType},
        CAST(#{metadataJson} AS jsonb),
        0,
        now(),
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

## 9.4 Update Capture Amount

```xml
<update id="addCapturedAmount">
    UPDATE payments
    SET captured_amount_minor = captured_amount_minor + #{captureAmountMinor},
        status = CASE
            WHEN captured_amount_minor + #{captureAmountMinor} = authorized_amount_minor
                THEN 'CAPTURED'
            ELSE 'PARTIALLY_CAPTURED'
        END,
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
      AND status IN ('AUTHORIZED', 'CAPTURE_PENDING', 'PARTIALLY_CAPTURED', 'UNKNOWN')
      AND captured_amount_minor + #{captureAmountMinor} <= authorized_amount_minor
      AND currency = #{currency}
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

## 9.5 Insert Event

```xml
<insert id="insertPaymentEvent">
    INSERT INTO payment_events (
        event_id,
        payment_id,
        attempt_id,
        refund_id,
        event_type,
        source,
        provider,
        provider_event_id,
        old_status,
        new_status,
        payload,
        raw_payload,
        created_by,
        created_at
    ) VALUES (
        #{eventId},
        #{paymentId},
        #{attemptId},
        #{refundId},
        #{eventType},
        #{source},
        #{provider},
        #{providerEventId},
        #{oldStatus},
        #{newStatus},
        CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb),
        #{rawPayload},
        #{createdBy},
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

MyBatis XML mapper memang menyediakan elemen mapped statement seperti `insert`, `update`, `delete`, dan `select`, sehingga pola SQL eksplisit seperti di atas natural untuk MyBatis-based application. ([mybatis.org](https://mybatis.org/mybatis-3/sqlmap-xml.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 10. Payment Operation Transaction Patterns

## 10.1 Create Payment

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant API as PaymentResource
    participant SVC as PaymentService
    participant DB as PostgreSQL
    participant PSP as Payment Gateway

    API->>SVC: createPayment(request, idempotencyKey)
    SVC->>DB: insert idempotency PROCESSING
    SVC->>DB: insert payment INITIATED
    SVC->>DB: insert attempt PENDING
    SVC->>DB: insert event PAYMENT_INITIATED
    SVC->>PSP: create payment / authorize
    PSP-->>SVC: response success/pending/failure
    SVC->>DB: update payment state
    SVC->>DB: update attempt
    SVC->>DB: insert event
    SVC->>DB: insert ledger if captured
    SVC->>DB: insert outbox event
    SVC->>DB: update idempotency SUCCEEDED
    SVC-->>API: response
```

## 10.2 Webhook Processing

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant PSP as Payment Gateway
    participant WH as WebhookResource
    participant DB as PostgreSQL
    participant Worker as WebhookProcessor

    PSP->>WH: POST /webhooks/{provider}
    WH->>WH: verify signature
    WH->>DB: insert inbox event ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
    WH-->>PSP: 200 OK

    Worker->>DB: claim RECEIVED events FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    Worker->>DB: find payment by provider ref
    Worker->>DB: apply state transition
    Worker->>DB: insert payment event
    Worker->>DB: insert ledger if needed
    Worker->>DB: insert outbox event
    Worker->>DB: mark inbox PROCESSED
```

## 10.3 Outbox Publishing

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Worker as OutboxPublisher
    participant DB as PostgreSQL
    participant Broker as Message Broker

    Worker->>DB: select pending outbox FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    Worker->>Broker: publish PaymentCaptured
    Broker-->>Worker: ack
    Worker->>DB: mark PUBLISHED
```

---

# 11. Constraint vs Application Validation

Gunakan keduanya.

| Rule | Java Validation | DB Constraint |
|---|---|---|
| Amount tidak negatif | Ya | Ya |
| Currency uppercase | Ya | Ya |
| Captured <= authorized | Ya | Ya |
| Refunded <= captured | Ya | Ya |
| Provider ref unique | Ya | Ya |
| Webhook event unique | Ya | Ya |
| Valid transition | Ya | Sebagian, via SQL WHERE condition |
| Idempotency duplicate | Ya | Ya, primary key |
| Ledger append-only | Ya | Bisa diperkuat via privilege/trigger |

Prinsipnya:

```text
Application menjaga behavior.
Database menjaga invariant.
```

---

# 12. View untuk Operasional

Agar support/ops tidak query banyak table manual, buat view.

## 12.1 Payment Summary View

```sql
CREATE VIEW v_payment_summary AS
SELECT
    p.payment_id,
    p.order_id,
    p.customer_id,
    p.merchant_id,
    p.amount_minor,
    p.currency,
    p.authorized_amount_minor,
    p.captured_amount_minor,
    p.refunded_amount_minor,
    p.status,
    p.provider,
    p.provider_payment_id,
    p.created_at,
    p.updated_at,

    (
        SELECT count(*)
        FROM payment_attempts pa
        WHERE pa.payment_id = p.payment_id
    ) AS attempt_count,

    (
        SELECT count(*)
        FROM refunds r
        WHERE r.payment_id = p.payment_id
    ) AS refund_count,

    (
        SELECT max(pe.created_at)
        FROM payment_events pe
        WHERE pe.payment_id = p.payment_id
    ) AS last_event_at

FROM payments p;
```

## 12.2 Stuck Payment View

```sql
CREATE VIEW v_stuck_payments AS
SELECT *
FROM payments
WHERE status IN (
    'AUTHORIZATION_PENDING',
    'CAPTURE_PENDING',
    'REFUND_PENDING',
    'UNKNOWN'
)
AND updated_at < now() - interval '15 minutes';
```

## 12.3 Reconciliation Mismatch View

```sql
CREATE VIEW v_reconciliation_mismatches AS
SELECT
    ri.reconciliation_item_id,
    ri.reconciliation_run_id,
    ri.payment_id,
    ri.refund_id,
    ri.provider,
    ri.provider_payment_id,
    ri.provider_refund_id,
    ri.item_type,
    ri.match_status,
    ri.internal_amount_minor,
    ri.provider_amount_minor,
    ri.difference_minor,
    ri.currency,
    ri.created_at
FROM reconciliation_items ri
WHERE ri.match_status <> 'MATCHED';
```

---

# 13. Materialized View untuk Reporting

Untuk dashboard volume besar, gunakan materialized view.

```sql
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_daily_payment_metrics AS
SELECT
    date_trunc('day', created_at)::date AS payment_date,
    merchant_id,
    currency,
    status,
    count(*) AS payment_count,
    sum(amount_minor) AS total_amount_minor,
    sum(captured_amount_minor) AS total_captured_minor,
    sum(refunded_amount_minor) AS total_refunded_minor
FROM payments
GROUP BY
    date_trunc('day', created_at)::date,
    merchant_id,
    currency,
    status;
```

Refresh:

```sql
REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_daily_payment_metrics;
```

Untuk production, biasanya refresh dijalankan oleh scheduler atau job terpisah, bukan setiap request API.

---

# 14. Trigger untuk `updated_at`

Optional, tapi praktis.

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION set_updated_at()
RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at = now();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

Apply ke table:

```sql
CREATE TRIGGER trg_payments_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON payments
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_payment_attempts_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON payment_attempts
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_refunds_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON refunds
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();
```

Practical note:

```text
Trigger bagus untuk konsistensi timestamp.
Tapi jangan sembunyikan business logic payment besar di trigger.
Business transition tetap di Java domain/application service.
```

---

# 15. Partitioning Consideration

Untuk payment skala besar, table ini bisa tumbuh sangat cepat:

```text
payment_events
payment_inbox_events
payment_outbox_events
ledger_entries
```

Strategi awal:

| Table | Candidate Partition |
|---|---|
| `payment_events` | Monthly by `created_at` |
| `payment_inbox_events` | Monthly by `received_at` |
| `payment_outbox_events` | Monthly by `created_at` |
| `ledger_entries` | Monthly/quarterly by `created_at` |
| `reconciliation_items` | By `reconciliation_run_id` atau date |

Namun jangan langsung over-engineer. Mulai dengan index yang benar, monitor growth, lalu partition saat data volume dan query pattern sudah jelas.

---

# 16. Data Retention Praktis

Payment data biasanya punya kebutuhan audit dan compliance. Retention harus ditentukan bersama finance, security, legal, dan compliance.

Baseline teknis:

| Data | Retention Direction |
|---|---|
| `payments` | Long retention. |
| `payment_events` | Long retention, minimal untuk audit lifecycle. |
| `ledger_entries` | Long retention, append-only. |
| `payment_inbox_events.raw_payload` | Bisa lebih pendek jika payload besar/sensitif. |
| `payment_outbox_events` | Bisa archive setelah published dan aman. |
| `idempotency_keys` | Bisa TTL, misalnya beberapa hari/bulan tergantung API contract. |
| `reconciliation_items` | Long enough untuk audit finance. |

---

# 17. Security Notes untuk Schema

Payment schema tidak boleh menyimpan:

```text
- PAN / full card number
- CVV
- raw magnetic stripe data
- sensitive authentication data
- plaintext secret/API key
```

Yang boleh disimpan biasanya:

```text
- provider_customer_id
- provider_payment_method_id
- last4 jika memang diperlukan dan diizinkan policy
- card brand jika diperlukan
- expiry month/year jika diperlukan dan sesuai compliance boundary
- token/reference dari PSP
```

Tambahkan masking di log dan payload.

Contoh kolom aman:

```sql
ALTER TABLE payments
ADD COLUMN payment_method_fingerprint VARCHAR(200);

ALTER TABLE payments
ADD COLUMN payment_method_display VARCHAR(80);
```

Contoh isi:

```text
VISA **** 4242
BANK_TRANSFER BCA
EWALLET GOPAY
```

---

# 18. Migration Strategy dengan Flyway/Liquibase

Karena stack Anda juga menyentuh migration, payment schema harus dibuat secara bertahap.

Contoh urutan migration:

```text
V001__create_payments.sql
V002__create_payment_attempts.sql
V003__create_payment_events.sql
V004__create_refunds.sql
V005__create_ledger_entries.sql
V006__create_idempotency_keys.sql
V007__create_payment_inbox_events.sql
V008__create_payment_outbox_events.sql
V009__create_reconciliation_tables.sql
V010__create_payment_views.sql
V011__create_updated_at_triggers.sql
```

Rule migration praktis:

| Rule | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Jangan drop kolom langsung. | Gunakan expand-contract migration. |
| Tambahkan kolom nullable dulu. | Backfill, lalu set `NOT NULL`. |
| Index besar dibuat hati-hati. | Di PostgreSQL production, pertimbangkan `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY`. |
| Hindari enum DB terlalu awal. | `VARCHAR + CHECK` sering lebih mudah diubah. |
| Simpan migration irreversible dengan hati-hati. | Payment data sulit diperbaiki jika rusak. |

---

# 19. Example End-to-End Insert Flow

## 19.1 Create Payment Initial DB Write

```sql
BEGIN;

INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (
    idempotency_key,
    operation_type,
    operation_key,
    request_hash,
    status,
    locked_until
)
VALUES (
    'idem-123',
    'CREATE_PAYMENT',
    'ORD-123',
    'sha256:abc',
    'PROCESSING',
    now() + interval '5 minutes'
);

INSERT INTO payments (
    payment_id,
    order_id,
    customer_id,
    merchant_id,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    authorized_amount_minor,
    captured_amount_minor,
    refunded_amount_minor,
    status,
    capture_mode,
    payment_method_type
)
VALUES (
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
    'ORD-123',
    'CUST-456',
    'MRC-001',
    100000,
    'IDR',
    0,
    0,
    0,
    'INITIATED',
    'AUTO',
    'CARD'
);

INSERT INTO payment_events (
    event_id,
    payment_id,
    event_type,
    source,
    old_status,
    new_status,
    payload
)
VALUES (
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000101',
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
    'PAYMENT_INITIATED',
    'API',
    NULL,
    'INITIATED',
    '{"orderId":"ORD-123"}'
);

COMMIT;
```

## 19.2 Provider Success Response

```sql
BEGIN;

UPDATE payments
SET status = 'CAPTURED',
    provider = 'STRIPE',
    provider_payment_id = 'pi_123',
    authorized_amount_minor = 100000,
    captured_amount_minor = 100000,
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE payment_id = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001'
  AND status IN ('INITIATED', 'AUTHORIZATION_PENDING', 'UNKNOWN');

INSERT INTO payment_events (
    event_id,
    payment_id,
    event_type,
    source,
    provider,
    provider_event_id,
    old_status,
    new_status,
    payload
)
VALUES (
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000102',
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
    'PAYMENT_CAPTURED',
    'PROVIDER_RESPONSE',
    'STRIPE',
    NULL,
    'INITIATED',
    'CAPTURED',
    '{"providerPaymentId":"pi_123"}'
);

INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    account_code,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    description
)
VALUES (
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000201',
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
    'PAYMENT_CAPTURED',
    'CREDIT',
    100000,
    'IDR',
    'MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE',
    'PROVIDER_PAYMENT',
    'pi_123',
    'Payment captured from provider'
);

INSERT INTO payment_outbox_events (
    outbox_event_id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    event_type,
    event_version,
    payload,
    publish_status
)
VALUES (
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000301',
    'PAYMENT',
    '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001',
    'PaymentCaptured',
    1,
    '{"paymentId":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001","orderId":"ORD-123","amountMinor":100000,"currency":"IDR"}',
    'PENDING'
);

UPDATE idempotency_keys
SET status = 'SUCCEEDED',
    response_status_code = 201,
    response_body = '{"paymentId":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001","status":"CAPTURED"}',
    updated_at = now()
WHERE idempotency_key = 'idem-123';

COMMIT;
```

---

# 20. Common Failure Model dan Schema Support

| Failure | Table yang Membantu | Strategy |
|---|---|---|
| Client retry create payment | `idempotency_keys` | Return cached response. |
| PSP timeout | `payment_attempts`, `payments.UNKNOWN` | Query status / wait webhook. |
| Duplicate webhook | `payment_inbox_events` | `UNIQUE(provider, provider_event_id)`. |
| Webhook out-of-order | `payment_events`, state transition guard | Ignore or exception queue. |
| Event publish gagal | `payment_outbox_events` | Retry publisher. |
| Refund double | `refunds`, idempotency | Unique operation key + amount guard. |
| Ledger duplicate | `ledger_entries` | Unique reference. |
| Reconciliation mismatch | `reconciliation_items` | Investigate and repair. |
| Concurrent capture | `payments.version`, `FOR UPDATE` | One transition wins. |
| Provider migration | `provider`, `provider_payment_id` | Adapter-neutral schema. |

---

# 21. Practical Checklist

## Core Tables

- [ ] `payments`
- [ ] `payment_attempts`
- [ ] `payment_events`
- [ ] `refunds`
- [ ] `ledger_entries`
- [ ] `idempotency_keys`
- [ ] `payment_inbox_events`
- [ ] `payment_outbox_events`
- [ ] `reconciliation_runs`
- [ ] `reconciliation_items`

## Constraints

- [ ] `captured_amount_minor <= authorized_amount_minor`
- [ ] `refunded_amount_minor <= captured_amount_minor`
- [ ] `amount_minor >= 0`
- [ ] `currency = upper(currency)`
- [ ] unique provider payment reference
- [ ] unique provider refund reference
- [ ] unique provider webhook event
- [ ] unique ledger reference
- [ ] idempotency key primary key

## Indexes

- [ ] `payment_id`
- [ ] `order_id`
- [ ] `provider_payment_id`
- [ ] `status`
- [ ] `created_at`
- [ ] `publish_status, next_attempt_at`
- [ ] `processing_status`
- [ ] GIN index untuk `JSONB` bila query payload memang dibutuhkan

## Operational Views

- [ ] `v_payment_summary`
- [ ] `v_stuck_payments`
- [ ] `v_reconciliation_mismatches`
- [ ] optional `mv_daily_payment_metrics`

# Ringkasan

Schema payment yang aman minimal harus punya:

```text
payments
payment_attempts
payment_events
refunds
ledger_entries
idempotency_keys
payment_inbox_events
payment_outbox_events
reconciliation_runs
reconciliation_items
```

Prinsip desainnya:

```text
payments           = state utama
payment_attempts   = percobaan teknis ke provider
payment_events     = audit timeline
refunds            = lifecycle refund
ledger_entries     = catatan finansial append-only
idempotency_keys   = dedup client/internal retry
inbox_events       = dedup webhook masuk
outbox_events      = reliable publish event keluar
reconciliation_*   = kontrol finance dan settlement
```

Kalau schema ini benar, payment service jauh lebih siap menghadapi retry, webhook duplicate, audit, refund, dispute, dan reconciliation.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 3/18 - Jersey/JAX-RS Payment API Design

Seri ini membahas **desain API payment service** untuk aplikasi **Java Jersey/JAX-RS**, dengan fokus praktis pada endpoint, DTO, validation, response contract, error model, idempotency, dan operational usability.

Core idea:

```text
Payment API bukan sekadar endpoint CRUD.
Payment API adalah command boundary untuk operasi finansial yang harus idempotent, auditable, traceable, dan state-aware.
```

Jakarta REST/JAX-RS menyediakan annotation seperti `@Path`, `@GET`, `@POST`, `@Consumes`, dan `@Produces` untuk membuat RESTful resource berbasis Java. Jakarta REST juga mendukung request method designator untuk HTTP method umum seperti GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, dan HEAD. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. API Design Principle

Untuk payment service, desain API harus mengikuti beberapa prinsip:

| Prinsip | Penjelasan Praktis |
|---|---|
| **Command-oriented for money movement** | `capture`, `refund`, `void` lebih aman sebagai command endpoint, bukan update field status biasa. |
| **Idempotent mutating API** | Semua endpoint yang bisa membuat efek finansial harus memakai `Idempotency-Key`. |
| **State-aware** | API harus menolak operasi yang tidak valid untuk state saat ini. |
| **Provider-neutral** | Consumer internal tidak boleh bergantung pada detail Stripe/Adyen/PayPal/etc. |
| **Audit-friendly** | Response harus punya `paymentId`, `status`, `amount`, `currency`, `providerReference`, dan `traceId`. |
| **Error contract stable** | Jangan expose raw provider error sebagai contract utama. |
| **Async-ready** | Payment bisa `PENDING`, `REQUIRES_ACTION`, `UNKNOWN`, bukan hanya `SUCCESS` / `FAILED`. |

---

# 2. High-Level API Diagram

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Order / Billing Service] --> B[PaymentResource - JAX-RS]
    B --> C[Request Validation]
    C --> D[Idempotency Guard]
    D --> E[PaymentApplicationService]

    E --> F[Payment State Machine]
    E --> G[Payment Repository - MyBatis]
    E --> H[Gateway Adapter]

    H --> I[External PSP]

    E --> J[(PostgreSQL)]
    J --> J1[payments]
    J --> J2[payment_attempts]
    J --> J3[payment_events]
    J --> J4[refunds]
    J --> J5[ledger_entries]
    J --> J6[idempotency_keys]

    B --> K[ProblemDetailsExceptionMapper]
    K --> L[Stable Error Response]

    M[WebhookResource] --> N[Signature Verification]
    N --> O[Inbox Dedup]
    O --> E
```

---

# 3. API Resource Boundary

Pisahkan API berdasarkan responsibility.

```text
/payment API
  - dipakai oleh internal services / frontend backend
  - create, authorize, capture, refund, void, get status

/webhook API
  - dipanggil oleh external PSP
  - signature verification
  - raw payload preservation
  - inbox dedup

/admin/support API
  - dipakai internal ops/support
  - timeline, retry webhook, reconciliation investigation

/reporting API
  - dipakai finance/BI/internal dashboard
  - summary, mismatch, settlement status
```

Dalam seri ini kita fokus ke **Payment API utama**. Webhook akan dibahas detail di Seri 8.

---

# 4. Recommended Endpoint Map

| No | Endpoint | Method | Idempotency Required | Fungsi |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `/payments` | `POST` | Ya | Create payment / sale / authorization. |
| 2 | `/payments/{paymentId}` | `GET` | Tidak | Ambil status payment. |
| 3 | `/payments/{paymentId}/authorize` | `POST` | Ya | Authorization manual jika create belum langsung authorize. |
| 4 | `/payments/{paymentId}/capture` | `POST` | Ya | Capture full/partial payment. |
| 5 | `/payments/{paymentId}/void` | `POST` | Ya | Void/cancel authorization sebelum capture. |
| 6 | `/payments/{paymentId}/refunds` | `POST` | Ya | Create refund full/partial. |
| 7 | `/payments/{paymentId}/refunds` | `GET` | Tidak | List refund untuk payment. |
| 8 | `/refunds/{refundId}` | `GET` | Tidak | Ambil status refund. |
| 9 | `/payments/{paymentId}/timeline` | `GET` | Tidak | Timeline event payment untuk support/debug. |
| 10 | `/payments/search` | `GET` | Tidak | Search payment by order/customer/provider/status. |
| 11 | `/webhooks/{provider}` | `POST` | Tidak | Webhook PSP; dedup pakai provider event id, bukan client idempotency key. |
| 12 | `/payments/{paymentId}/actions/retry-status-check` | `POST` | Ya/Admin only | Manual/admin trigger untuk query status ke PSP. |

Catatan penting: `capture`, `refund`, dan `void` saya desain sebagai `POST` command endpoint karena operasi tersebut menciptakan side effect finansial. Safe retry-nya bukan karena HTTP method-nya idempotent, tetapi karena **application-level idempotency key**.

Stripe mendokumentasikan idempotency key untuk mengenali retry request dan mengembalikan hasil request pertama untuk key yang sama; Adyen juga mendukung idempotency supaya retry tidak menduplikasi operasi; PayPal memakai `PayPal-Request-Id` untuk REST POST idempotency. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Endpoint Detail

## 5.1 `POST /payments`

Digunakan untuk membuat payment baru.

### Request Headers

| Header | Required | Contoh | Fungsi |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `Idempotency-Key` | Ya | `9f5b8b1d-...` | Mencegah duplicate payment saat retry. |
| `X-Correlation-Id` | Disarankan | `corr-123` | Trace end-to-end antar service. |
| `X-Request-Id` | Disarankan | `req-123` | Trace request spesifik. |
| `Content-Type` | Ya | `application/json` | Payload JSON. |
| `Accept` | Disarankan | `application/json` | Response JSON. |

### Request Body

```json
{
  "orderId": "ORD-2026-0001",
  "customerId": "CUST-1001",
  "merchantId": "MRC-001",
  "amountMinor": 150000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "captureMode": "AUTO",
  "paymentMethod": {
    "type": "CARD_TOKEN",
    "token": "pm_tok_abc123"
  },
  "metadata": {
    "channel": "WEB",
    "cartId": "CART-7788"
  }
}
```

### Response `201 Created`

```json
{
  "paymentId": "2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e",
  "orderId": "ORD-2026-0001",
  "amountMinor": 150000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "status": "CAPTURED",
  "captureMode": "AUTO",
  "provider": "STRIPE",
  "providerPaymentId": "pi_xxx",
  "requiresAction": false,
  "nextAction": null,
  "createdAt": "2026-07-14T14:20:00Z",
  "updatedAt": "2026-07-14T14:20:03Z",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

---

## 5.2 `GET /payments/{paymentId}`

Mengambil status payment internal.

Response:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e",
  "orderId": "ORD-2026-0001",
  "amountMinor": 150000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "authorizedAmountMinor": 150000,
  "capturedAmountMinor": 150000,
  "refundedAmountMinor": 0,
  "status": "CAPTURED",
  "provider": "STRIPE",
  "providerPaymentId": "pi_xxx",
  "createdAt": "2026-07-14T14:20:00Z",
  "updatedAt": "2026-07-14T14:20:03Z"
}
```

---

## 5.3 `POST /payments/{paymentId}/capture`

Untuk manual capture atau partial capture.

Request:

```json
{
  "amountMinor": 100000,
  "reason": "ORDER_READY_TO_FULFILL"
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e",
  "status": "PARTIALLY_CAPTURED",
  "authorizedAmountMinor": 150000,
  "capturedAmountMinor": 100000,
  "remainingCapturableAmountMinor": 50000,
  "currency": "IDR"
}
```

Validasi domain:

```text
payment.status harus AUTHORIZED / PARTIALLY_CAPTURED / CAPTURE_PENDING
capture amount > 0
captured_amount + capture_amount <= authorized_amount
currency payment tidak berubah
Idempotency-Key wajib
```

---

## 5.4 `POST /payments/{paymentId}/refunds`

Untuk full atau partial refund.

Request:

```json
{
  "amountMinor": 50000,
  "reason": "CUSTOMER_REQUEST",
  "requestedBy": "support-agent-001"
}
```

Response `202 Accepted` jika refund async:

```json
{
  "refundId": "61f1d2ae-8a8a-4059-8d8f-7de43b777af6",
  "paymentId": "2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e",
  "amountMinor": 50000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "status": "PENDING",
  "reason": "CUSTOMER_REQUEST",
  "createdAt": "2026-07-14T14:25:00Z"
}
```

Validasi domain:

```text
payment.status harus CAPTURED / PARTIALLY_REFUNDED
refund amount > 0
refunded_amount + refund_amount <= captured_amount
Idempotency-Key wajib
```

---

## 5.5 `POST /payments/{paymentId}/void`

Untuk membatalkan authorization sebelum capture.

Request:

```json
{
  "reason": "ORDER_CANCELLED"
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e",
  "status": "CANCELLED",
  "voidedAt": "2026-07-14T14:30:00Z"
}
```

Validasi domain:

```text
payment.status harus AUTHORIZED / AUTHORIZATION_PENDING
captured_amount harus 0
Idempotency-Key wajib
```

---

# 6. DTO Design dengan Jakarta Validation

Jakarta Bean Validation memungkinkan constraint dideklarasikan memakai annotation pada field, method, class, parameter, atau constructor; built-in constraints tersedia di package `jakarta.validation.constraints`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/beanvalidation/bean-validation/bean-validation.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 6.1 Create Payment Request

```java
package com.company.payment.api.dto;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Positive;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import java.util.Map;

public record CreatePaymentRequest(

        @NotBlank
        String orderId,

        @NotBlank
        String customerId,

        @NotBlank
        String merchantId,

        @Positive
        long amountMinor,

        @NotBlank
        @Pattern(regexp = "^[A-Z]{3}$")
        String currency,

        @NotNull
        CaptureMode captureMode,

        @NotNull
        @Valid
        PaymentMethodRequest paymentMethod,

        Map<String, Object> metadata
) {}
```

```java
package com.company.payment.api.dto;

public enum CaptureMode {
    AUTO,
    MANUAL
}
```

```java
package com.company.payment.api.dto;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;

public record PaymentMethodRequest(

        @NotNull
        PaymentMethodType type,

        @NotBlank
        String token
) {}
```

```java
package com.company.payment.api.dto;

public enum PaymentMethodType {
    CARD_TOKEN,
    BANK_TRANSFER,
    EWALLET,
    VIRTUAL_ACCOUNT,
    QRIS,
    DIRECT_DEBIT
}
```

Practical note:

```text
DTO API boleh berbeda dari domain object.
DTO menerima input.
Domain object menjaga invariant.
Mapper mengubah DTO -> command -> domain.
```

---

```java
package com.company.payment.api.dto;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Positive;

public record CapturePaymentRequest(

        @Positive
        long amountMinor,

        @NotBlank
        String reason
) {}
```

# 7. Response DTO Design

## 7.1 Payment Response

```java
package com.company.payment.api.dto;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public record PaymentResponse(
        UUID paymentId,
        String orderId,
        String customerId,
        String merchantId,

        long amountMinor,
        String currency,

        long authorizedAmountMinor,
        long capturedAmountMinor,
        long refundedAmountMinor,

        String status,
        String captureMode,

        String provider,
        String providerPaymentId,

        boolean requiresAction,
        NextActionResponse nextAction,

        Map<String, Object> metadata,

        Instant createdAt,
        Instant updatedAt,

        String traceId
) {}
```

## 7.2 Next Action Response

Dipakai untuk 3DS, redirect, QR payment, bank transfer instruction, dan sejenisnya.

```java
package com.company.payment.api.dto;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;

public record NextActionResponse(
        String type,
        String redirectUrl,
        String qrContent,
        String instructionText,
        Instant expiresAt,
        Map<String, Object> providerData
) {}
```

Contoh response payment yang butuh customer action:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e",
  "status": "REQUIRES_ACTION",
  "requiresAction": true,
  "nextAction": {
    "type": "REDIRECT",
    "redirectUrl": "https://issuer.example/challenge",
    "expiresAt": "2026-07-14T14:40:00Z"
  }
}
```

# 8. Jersey Resource Implementation

```java
package com.company.payment.api;

import com.company.payment.api.dto.*;
import com.company.payment.application.PaymentApplicationService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

import java.net.URI;
import java.util.UUID;

@Path("/payments")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class PaymentResource {

    private final PaymentApplicationService paymentService;

    public PaymentResource(PaymentApplicationService paymentService) {
        this.paymentService = paymentService;
    }

    @POST
    public Response createPayment(
            @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey,
            @HeaderParam("X-Correlation-Id") String correlationId,
            @Valid CreatePaymentRequest request
    ) {
        PaymentResponse response = paymentService.createPayment(
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId,
                request
        );

        return Response
                .created(URI.create("/payments/" + response.paymentId()))
                .entity(response)
                .build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{paymentId}")
    public Response getPayment(
            @PathParam("paymentId") UUID paymentId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Correlation-Id") String correlationId
    ) {
        PaymentResponse response = paymentService.getPayment(paymentId, correlationId);
        return Response.ok(response).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{paymentId}/capture")
    public Response capturePayment(
            @PathParam("paymentId") UUID paymentId,
            @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey,
            @HeaderParam("X-Correlation-Id") String correlationId,
            @Valid CapturePaymentRequest request
    ) {
        PaymentResponse response = paymentService.capturePayment(
                paymentId,
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId,
                request
        );

        return Response.ok(response).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{paymentId}/void")
    public Response voidPayment(
            @PathParam("paymentId") UUID paymentId,
            @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey,
            @HeaderParam("X-Correlation-Id") String correlationId,
            @Valid VoidPaymentRequest request
    ) {
        PaymentResponse response = paymentService.voidPayment(
                paymentId,
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId,
                request
        );

        return Response.ok(response).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{paymentId}/refunds")
    public Response refundPayment(
            @PathParam("paymentId") UUID paymentId,
            @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey,
            @HeaderParam("X-Correlation-Id") String correlationId,
            @Valid RefundPaymentRequest request
    ) {
        RefundResponse response = paymentService.refundPayment(
                paymentId,
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId,
                request
        );

        return Response
                .accepted(response)
                .build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{paymentId}/timeline")
    public Response getPaymentTimeline(
            @PathParam("paymentId") UUID paymentId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Correlation-Id") String correlationId
    ) {
        PaymentTimelineResponse response = paymentService.getTimeline(paymentId, correlationId);
        return Response.ok(response).build();
    }
}
```

---

# 9. Application Command Layer

Jangan langsung lempar DTO ke domain atau repository. Buat command object agar boundary jelas.

```java
package com.company.payment.application.command;

import java.util.Map;

public record CreatePaymentCommand(
        String idempotencyKey,
        String correlationId,
        String orderId,
        String customerId,
        String merchantId,
        long amountMinor,
        String currency,
        String captureMode,
        String paymentMethodType,
        String paymentMethodToken,
        Map<String, Object> metadata
) {}
```

Mapper dari DTO ke command:

```java
package com.company.payment.api.mapper;

import com.company.payment.api.dto.CreatePaymentRequest;
import com.company.payment.application.command.CreatePaymentCommand;

public final class PaymentApiMapper {

    private PaymentApiMapper() {}

    public static CreatePaymentCommand toCommand(
            String idempotencyKey,
            String correlationId,
            CreatePaymentRequest request
    ) {
        return new CreatePaymentCommand(
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId,
                request.orderId(),
                request.customerId(),
                request.merchantId(),
                request.amountMinor(),
                request.currency(),
                request.captureMode().name(),
                request.paymentMethod().type().name(),
                request.paymentMethod().token(),
                request.metadata()
        );
    }
}
```

Manfaat:

```text
API DTO bisa berubah tanpa langsung merusak domain.
Application command bisa dipakai juga oleh worker/Camunda/internal job.
```

---

# 10. Required Headers Policy

Untuk payment, buat policy eksplisit.

| Endpoint Type | `Idempotency-Key` | `X-Correlation-Id` | Reason |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `POST /payments` | Required | Recommended/Generated | Bisa create charge. |
| `POST /capture` | Required | Recommended/Generated | Bisa capture uang. |
| `POST /refunds` | Required | Recommended/Generated | Bisa mengembalikan uang. |
| `POST /void` | Required | Recommended/Generated | Mengubah state authorization. |
| `GET` endpoints | Not required | Recommended/Generated | Read-only. |
| `POST /webhooks/{provider}` | Not required | Generated | Dedup dari provider event id. |

Header validation bisa dilakukan di filter atau di service.

---

# 11. Idempotency API Behavior

## 11.1 Rule

Untuk mutating payment API:

```text
Same Idempotency-Key + same request hash
  -> return same logical result.

Same Idempotency-Key + different request hash
  -> reject with IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED_WITH_DIFFERENT_REQUEST.

No Idempotency-Key
  -> reject with 400 or 422 depending API convention.
```

## 11.2 Response Cases

| Case | HTTP | Response |
|---|---:|---|
| First request accepted | `201` / `200` / `202` | Normal response. |
| Same key, same payload, already succeeded | Same as first result | Cached response. |
| Same key, same payload, still processing | `409 Conflict` or `202 Accepted` | Operation in progress. |
| Same key, different payload | `409 Conflict` | Key reused incorrectly. |
| Missing key for mutating operation | `400 Bad Request` or `422 Unprocessable Content` | Contract violation. |

RFC 9110 defines the status code space and status code classes; it also includes `422 Unprocessable Content` for content that is syntactically valid but semantically unprocessable. ([IETF Datatracker](https://datatracker.ietf.org/doc/html/rfc9110?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 12. Error Model dengan RFC 9457 Problem Details

Gunakan stable error response. RFC 9457 mendefinisikan “problem detail” untuk membawa detail error yang machine-readable dalam HTTP API dan menggantikan RFC 7807. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc9457/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 12.1 Standard Problem Response

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/payment-invalid-state",
  "title": "Invalid payment state",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "Payment cannot be captured because current status is FAILED.",
  "instance": "/payments/2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e/capture",
  "errorCode": "PAYMENT_INVALID_STATE",
  "traceId": "corr-123",
  "paymentId": "2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e"
}
```

## 12.2 Internal Error Codes

| Error Code | HTTP | Kapan Dipakai |
|---|---:|---|
| `PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND` | `404` | Payment ID tidak ditemukan. |
| `PAYMENT_INVALID_STATE` | `409` | Operasi tidak valid untuk state saat ini. |
| `PAYMENT_AMOUNT_EXCEEDS_AUTHORIZED` | `422` | Capture melebihi authorized amount. |
| `PAYMENT_REFUND_EXCEEDS_CAPTURED` | `422` | Refund melebihi captured amount. |
| `PAYMENT_CURRENCY_MISMATCH` | `422` | Currency tidak cocok. |
| `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED` | `400` | Mutating endpoint tanpa idempotency key. |
| `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED_WITH_DIFFERENT_REQUEST` | `409` | Key sama, payload beda. |
| `PAYMENT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | `504` atau `202/UNKNOWN` | Provider timeout; tergantung apakah operasi diterima internal. |
| `PAYMENT_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` | `503` | PSP unavailable. |
| `PAYMENT_DECLINED` | `402` atau `422` | Payment declined. `402` historically exists, tapi banyak API tetap memilih `422`. |
| `VALIDATION_FAILED` | `400` / `422` | Request tidak valid. |
| `UNAUTHORIZED` | `401` | Missing/invalid auth. |
| `FORBIDDEN` | `403` | Tidak punya permission. |

Practical recommendation:

```text
Gunakan HTTP status untuk kategori umum.
Gunakan errorCode untuk decision logic client.
Jangan biarkan client parsing provider raw message.
```

---

# 13. Exception Mapper

```java
package com.company.payment.api.error;

import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Context;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.UriInfo;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class PaymentExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<PaymentException> {

    @Context
    private UriInfo uriInfo;

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(PaymentException exception) {
        ProblemDetails body = new ProblemDetails(
                exception.type(),
                exception.title(),
                exception.httpStatus(),
                exception.getMessage(),
                uriInfo.getPath(),
                exception.errorCode(),
                exception.traceId(),
                exception.context()
        );

        return Response
                .status(exception.httpStatus())
                .type("application/problem+json")
                .entity(body)
                .build();
    }
}
```

```java
package com.company.payment.api.error;

import java.util.Map;

public record ProblemDetails(
        String type,
        String title,
        int status,
        String detail,
        String instance,
        String errorCode,
        String traceId,
        Map<String, Object> context
) {}
```

Domain exception:

```java
package com.company.payment.api.error;

import java.util.Map;

public class PaymentException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String type;
    private final String title;
    private final int httpStatus;
    private final String errorCode;
    private final String traceId;
    private final Map<String, Object> context;

    public PaymentException(
            String type,
            String title,
            int httpStatus,
            String errorCode,
            String message,
            String traceId,
            Map<String, Object> context
    ) {
        super(message);
        this.type = type;
        this.title = title;
        this.httpStatus = httpStatus;
        this.errorCode = errorCode;
        this.traceId = traceId;
        this.context = context;
    }

    public String type() {
        return type;
    }

    public String title() {
        return title;
    }

    public int httpStatus() {
        return httpStatus;
    }

    public String errorCode() {
        return errorCode;
    }

    public String traceId() {
        return traceId;
    }

    public Map<String, Object> context() {
        return context;
    }
}
```

# 14. Validation Error Mapper

Untuk Bean Validation error:

```java
package com.company.payment.api.error;

import jakarta.validation.ConstraintViolation;
import jakarta.validation.ConstraintViolationException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@Provider
public class ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<ConstraintViolationException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(ConstraintViolationException exception) {
        List<Map<String, Object>> violations = exception.getConstraintViolations()
                .stream()
                .map(this::toViolation)
                .toList();

        ProblemDetails body = new ProblemDetails(
                "https://api.company.internal/problems/validation-failed",
                "Validation failed",
                422,
                "Request body contains invalid fields.",
                null,
                "VALIDATION_FAILED",
                null,
                Map.of("violations", violations)
        );

        return Response
                .status(422)
                .type("application/problem+json")
                .entity(body)
                .build();
    }

    private Map<String, Object> toViolation(ConstraintViolation<?> violation) {
        return Map.of(
                "field", violation.getPropertyPath().toString(),
                "message", violation.getMessage(),
                "invalidValue", String.valueOf(violation.getInvalidValue())
        );
    }
}
```

Contoh response:

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/validation-failed",
  "title": "Validation failed",
  "status": 422,
  "detail": "Request body contains invalid fields.",
  "errorCode": "VALIDATION_FAILED",
  "context": {
    "violations": [
      {
        "field": "amountMinor",
        "message": "must be greater than 0",
        "invalidValue": "0"
      },
      {
        "field": "currency",
        "message": "must match ^[A-Z]{3}$",
        "invalidValue": "idr"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```
