# 15. Correlation ID Filter

Payment debugging sangat bergantung pada traceability.

```java
package com.company.payment.api.filter;

import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.UUID;

@Provider
public class CorrelationIdFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter, ContainerResponseFilter {

    public static final String CORRELATION_ID_HEADER = "X-Correlation-Id";
    public static final String REQUEST_PROPERTY = "correlationId";

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) throws IOException {
        String correlationId = requestContext.getHeaderString(CORRELATION_ID_HEADER);

        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        requestContext.setProperty(REQUEST_PROPERTY, correlationId);
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) throws IOException {
        Object correlationId = requestContext.getProperty(REQUEST_PROPERTY);

        if (correlationId != null) {
            responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle(CORRELATION_ID_HEADER, correlationId.toString());
        }
    }
}
```

Practical usage:

```text
Log semua operasi payment dengan:
- correlationId
- requestId
- paymentId
- orderId
- provider
- providerPaymentId
- idempotencyKey hash, bukan full raw key jika dianggap sensitif
```

---

# 16. Idempotency Guard Filter

Bisa dibuat filter untuk memastikan header ada di mutating endpoint.

```java
package com.company.payment.api.filter;

import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Priorities;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Set;

@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHORIZATION)
public class IdempotencyRequiredFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private static final String IDEMPOTENCY_KEY = "Idempotency-Key";

    private static final Set<String> MUTATING_METHODS = Set.of("POST", "PUT", "PATCH", "DELETE");

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext context) throws IOException {
        String path = context.getUriInfo().getPath();
        String method = context.getMethod();

        if (!requiresIdempotency(method, path)) {
            return;
        }

        String key = context.getHeaderString(IDEMPOTENCY_KEY);

        if (key == null || key.isBlank()) {
            context.abortWith(Response
                    .status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                    .type("application/problem+json")
                    .entity(new ProblemDetails(
                            "https://api.company.internal/problems/idempotency-key-required",
                            "Idempotency key required",
                            400,
                            "Idempotency-Key header is required for mutating payment operations.",
                            "/" + path,
                            "IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED",
                            null,
                            null
                    ))
                    .build());
        }
    }

    private boolean requiresIdempotency(String method, String path) {
        if (!MUTATING_METHODS.contains(method)) {
            return false;
        }

        if (path.startsWith("webhooks/")) {
            return false;
        }

        return path.equals("payments")
                || path.matches("payments/[^/]+/capture")
                || path.matches("payments/[^/]+/refunds")
                || path.matches("payments/[^/]+/void")
                || path.matches("payments/[^/]+/authorize");
    }
}
```

Catatan praktis:

```text
Filter hanya enforce keberadaan header.
Dedup logic tetap di IdempotencyService karena perlu akses DB dan request hash.
```

---

# 17. API Status Code Convention

| Scenario | Recommended HTTP | Body Status |
|---|---:|---|
| Payment created and final captured | `201 Created` | `CAPTURED` |
| Payment created but waiting action | `201 Created` | `REQUIRES_ACTION` |
| Payment accepted but async pending | `202 Accepted` | `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` / `CAPTURE_PENDING` |
| Read payment success | `200 OK` | Current status |
| Capture success | `200 OK` | `CAPTURED` / `PARTIALLY_CAPTURED` |
| Refund request accepted async | `202 Accepted` | `PENDING` |
| Void success | `200 OK` | `CANCELLED` |
| Validation error | `422 Unprocessable Content` | Problem details |
| Missing idempotency key | `400 Bad Request` | Problem details |
| Invalid state transition | `409 Conflict` | Problem details |
| Provider unavailable | `503 Service Unavailable` | Problem details |
| Provider timeout but internal operation accepted | `202 Accepted` | `UNKNOWN` / `PENDING_CONFIRMATION` |
| Provider timeout before operation known | `504 Gateway Timeout` | Problem details or pending response depending design |

Important:

```text
Jangan gunakan HTTP 200 + body status FAILED untuk semua kasus.
HTTP status harus memberi sinyal transport/API-level.
Payment status memberi sinyal domain-level.
```

---

# 18. Search API Design

Untuk support dan internal service lookup.

```http
GET /payments/search?orderId=ORD-2026-0001
GET /payments/search?customerId=CUST-1001&status=CAPTURED
GET /payments/search?provider=STRIPE&providerPaymentId=pi_xxx
GET /payments/search?createdFrom=2026-07-01T00:00:00Z&createdTo=2026-07-14T23:59:59Z
```

Response:

```json
{
  "items": [
    {
      "paymentId": "2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e",
      "orderId": "ORD-2026-0001",
      "amountMinor": 150000,
      "currency": "IDR",
      "status": "CAPTURED",
      "createdAt": "2026-07-14T14:20:00Z"
    }
  ],
  "page": {
    "limit": 50,
    "nextCursor": "eyJjcmVhdGVkQXQiOiIyMDI2LTA3LTE0VDE0OjIwOjAwWiJ9"
  }
}
```

Practical recommendation:

```text
Gunakan cursor pagination untuk payment/event table besar.
Hindari offset pagination untuk data finansial dengan volume besar karena makin mahal saat offset besar.
```

# 19. Timeline API

Endpoint:

```http
GET /payments/{paymentId}/timeline
```

Response:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e",
  "events": [
    {
      "eventType": "PAYMENT_INITIATED",
      "source": "API",
      "oldStatus": null,
      "newStatus": "INITIATED",
      "createdAt": "2026-07-14T14:20:00Z"
    },
    {
      "eventType": "PROVIDER_RESPONSE_RECEIVED",
      "source": "PROVIDER_RESPONSE",
      "oldStatus": "INITIATED",
      "newStatus": "CAPTURED",
      "createdAt": "2026-07-14T14:20:03Z"
    }
  ]
}
```

Manfaat praktis:

```text
Support bisa menjawab:
- payment dibuat kapan?
- call provider sukses/gagal?
- webhook masuk kapan?
- status berubah dari apa ke apa?
- siapa melakukan refund?
- reconciliation memperbaiki status kapan?
```

# 20. Security Boundary API

Payment API harus menghindari menerima atau mengembalikan data sensitif yang tidak perlu.

## Jangan desain request seperti ini

```json
{
  "cardNumber": "4111111111111111",
  "cvv": "123",
  "expiryMonth": "12",
  "expiryYear": "2030"
}
```

## Lebih aman

```json
{
  "paymentMethod": {
    "type": "CARD_TOKEN",
    "token": "pm_tok_abc123"
  }
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
Payment service sebaiknya menerima token/reference dari payment page/PSP/tokenization service.
Jangan jadikan payment API sebagai collector raw card data kecuali memang scope PCI, kontrol, dan compliance sudah disiapkan.
```

---

# 21. API Versioning

Minimal:

```text
/api/v1/payments
```

Atau via routing internal/API gateway.

Practical versioning rule:

| Change | Butuh Version Baru? |
|---|---:|
| Tambah optional response field | Tidak |
| Tambah optional request field | Tidak |
| Rename field | Ya |
| Hapus field | Ya |
| Ubah enum meaning | Ya |
| Ubah status transition semantics | Ya |
| Ubah error code contract | Biasanya ya |
| Ubah required field | Ya |

---

# 22. API Consumer Contract

Payment API consumer harus paham bahwa response awal belum tentu final.

| Status | Consumer Action |
|---|---|
| `CAPTURED` | Order boleh lanjut fulfilment. |
| `AUTHORIZED` | Order boleh reserve stock, capture nanti. |
| `REQUIRES_ACTION` | Arahkan customer ke `nextAction`. |
| `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | Tunggu webhook/poll status. |
| `CAPTURE_PENDING` | Jangan fulfil sampai captured final, kecuali business rule mengizinkan. |
| `FAILED` | Tawarkan retry/payment method lain. |
| `UNKNOWN` | Jangan langsung cancel; tunggu recovery/reconciliation. |
| `REFUNDED` | Update order/finance. |

---

# 23. Anti-Pattern API

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Buruk | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| `PUT /payments/{id}` untuk ubah status bebas | Consumer bisa memaksa state invalid. | Gunakan command endpoint: capture/refund/void. |
| Tidak wajibkan `Idempotency-Key` | Retry bisa double charge/refund. | Enforce di filter/service. |
| Response hanya `{ "success": true }` | Tidak cukup untuk async state. | Return full payment status. |
| Expose raw PSP status ke consumer | Vendor lock-in dan semantics bocor. | Map ke internal status. |
| Semua error `500` | Client tidak bisa membedakan validation/state/provider. | Gunakan Problem Details + errorCode. |
| Timeout langsung response `FAILED` | Bisa salah jika provider sebenarnya berhasil. | Return `UNKNOWN` / pending confirmation. |
| Webhook endpoint share contract dengan internal API | Security dan semantics beda. | Pisahkan `/webhooks/{provider}`. |
| Refund sebagai `PATCH /payments/{id}` | Refund punya lifecycle sendiri. | `POST /payments/{id}/refunds`. |
| Tidak ada timeline endpoint | Support harus query DB manual. | Sediakan `/timeline`. |

---

# 25. Practical Test Cases

## API Contract Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| `POST /payments` tanpa `Idempotency-Key` | `400 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED` |
| `POST /payments` dengan `amountMinor = 0` | `422 VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| `POST /payments` dengan `currency = "idr"` | `422 VALIDATION_FAILED` |
| Retry `POST /payments` dengan key sama dan body sama | Return response yang sama. |
| Retry key sama tapi body beda | `409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED_WITH_DIFFERENT_REQUEST` |
| Capture payment yang sudah `FAILED` | `409 PAYMENT_INVALID_STATE` |
| Refund lebih besar dari captured amount | `422 PAYMENT_REFUND_EXCEEDS_CAPTURED` |
| Get unknown payment ID | `404 PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND` |
| Provider timeout setelah request diterima | Return `UNKNOWN` atau `PENDING_CONFIRMATION`, bukan asal `FAILED`. |
| Timeline payment | Event terurut ascending. |

# 26. Example cURL

## Create Payment

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8080/payments \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Accept: application/json" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: 9f5b8b1d-1234-4567-8901-abcdefabcdef" \
  -H "X-Correlation-Id: corr-local-001" \
  -d '{
    "orderId": "ORD-2026-0001",
    "customerId": "CUST-1001",
    "merchantId": "MRC-001",
    "amountMinor": 150000,
    "currency": "IDR",
    "captureMode": "AUTO",
    "paymentMethod": {
      "type": "CARD_TOKEN",
      "token": "pm_tok_abc123"
    },
    "metadata": {
      "channel": "WEB"
    }
  }'
```

## Capture Payment

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8080/payments/2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e/capture \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: capture-2ef2e8b4-001" \
  -H "X-Correlation-Id: corr-local-002" \
  -d '{
    "amountMinor": 150000,
    "reason": "ORDER_READY_TO_FULFILL"
  }'
```

## Refund Payment

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8080/payments/2ef2e8b4-3a10-40a7-a0d1-6a0c5e596b6e/refunds \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: refund-2ef2e8b4-001" \
  -H "X-Correlation-Id: corr-local-003" \
  -d '{
    "amountMinor": 50000,
    "reason": "CUSTOMER_REQUEST",
    "requestedBy": "support-agent-001"
  }'
```

# 27. Implementation Checklist

## Endpoint

- [ ] `POST /payments`
- [ ] `GET /payments/{paymentId}`
- [ ] `POST /payments/{paymentId}/capture`
- [ ] `POST /payments/{paymentId}/void`
- [ ] `POST /payments/{paymentId}/refunds`
- [ ] `GET /payments/{paymentId}/refunds`
- [ ] `GET /refunds/{refundId}`
- [ ] `GET /payments/{paymentId}/timeline`
- [ ] `GET /payments/search`

## Headers

- [ ] `Idempotency-Key` wajib untuk mutating endpoint.
- [ ] `X-Correlation-Id` diterima atau dibuat otomatis.
- [ ] Response mengembalikan `X-Correlation-Id`.
- [ ] Jangan log full sensitive token/API secret.

## DTO & Validation

- [ ] Amount `@Positive`.
- [ ] Currency `^[A-Z]{3}$`.
- [ ] Required fields `@NotBlank` / `@NotNull`.
- [ ] Nested DTO pakai `@Valid`.
- [ ] Validation error distandardisasi.

## Error Model

- [ ] Gunakan `application/problem+json`.
- [ ] Ada stable `errorCode`.
- [ ] Ada `traceId`.
- [ ] Provider raw error tidak menjadi public contract utama.
- [ ] Invalid state memakai `409`.
- [ ] Semantic validation memakai `422` atau convention lain yang konsisten.

## Domain Safety

- [ ] API tidak bisa update status sembarangan.
- [ ] Capture/refund/void lewat command method.
- [ ] Timeout tidak otomatis `FAILED`.
- [ ] Response mendukung `PENDING`, `REQUIRES_ACTION`, dan `UNKNOWN`.

# Ringkasan

Payment API yang baik punya karakter berikut:

```text
1. Command endpoint untuk operasi finansial.
2. Idempotency wajib untuk mutating request.
3. DTO tervalidasi dengan jelas.
4. Response membawa domain status, bukan sekadar success boolean.
5. Error memakai stable problem details.
6. Correlation ID selalu ada.
7. API tidak expose detail vendor secara berlebihan.
8. Async state seperti PENDING, REQUIRES_ACTION, UNKNOWN dianggap first-class.
```

Desain API yang benar membuat seri berikutnya lebih mudah, terutama idempotency, gateway adapter, webhook, outbox, dan Camunda saga.

## Response:
7/14/2026, 10:22:14 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 4/18**. Fokusnya sekarang sangat praktis: bagaimana membuat mutating Payment API aman saat client retry, network timeout, double-click, worker retry, atau service upstream mengirim request yang sama berkali-kali.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 4/18 - Idempotency Key, Duplicate Prevention, dan Safe Retry

Seri ini membahas salah satu bagian paling kritikal dalam payment system: **bagaimana memastikan retry tidak menyebabkan double charge, double capture, double refund, atau duplicate state transition**.

Core problem-nya:

```text
Client / upstream service mengirim request payment.
Network timeout terjadi.
Client tidak tahu apakah payment sudah diproses atau belum.
Client retry.
Tanpa idempotency, retry bisa membuat charge kedua.
```

Idempotency bukan hanya “pakai UUID di header”. Idempotency harus mencakup:

```text
1. Request identity
2. Request hash
3. Operation scope
4. Locking/concurrency control
5. Response caching
6. Expiry/retention policy
7. Provider idempotency propagation
8. Recovery saat PROCESSING stuck
```

Stripe mendokumentasikan bahwa idempotency bekerja dengan menyimpan status code dan body dari request pertama untuk idempotency key tertentu, lalu subsequent request dengan key sama mengembalikan hasil yang sama. Stripe juga menyarankan key unik seperti UUID v4 dan menghindari data sensitif sebagai idempotency key. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen mendukung idempotency untuk retry API request agar operasi yang sama hanya dilakukan sekali; Adyen memakai header `idempotency-key`, menyarankan UUID, dan menyimpan key minimal 7 hari di company account scope. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/development-resources/api-idempotency)) PayPal memakai `PayPal-Request-Id` untuk REST API `POST` idempotency dan memperingatkan bahwa tanpa header tersebut request dapat terduplikasi. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/api/rest/reference/idempotency/))

---

# 1. Introduction

Dalam payment service, idempotency wajib untuk endpoint yang memiliki **financial side effect**:

| Endpoint | Kenapa Wajib Idempotent |
|---|---|
| `POST /payments` | Bisa membuat payment/charge baru. |
| `POST /payments/{id}/authorize` | Bisa reserve dana customer. |
| `POST /payments/{id}/capture` | Bisa memindahkan dana. |
| `POST /payments/{id}/refunds` | Bisa mengembalikan dana. |
| `POST /payments/{id}/void` | Bisa membatalkan authorization. |
| Admin retry/recovery endpoint | Bisa memicu provider call atau state transition. |

Idempotency bukan untuk menggantikan domain invariant. Idempotency hanya memastikan **logical operation yang sama tidak dieksekusi berkali-kali**. Domain invariant tetap wajib menjaga hal seperti:

```text
captured_amount <= authorized_amount
refunded_amount <= captured_amount
currency tidak berubah
final state tidak mundur sembarangan
```

---

# 2. Diagram Besar

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Client / Order Service] --> B[POST /payments with Idempotency-Key]
    B --> C[Idempotency Filter]
    C --> D[Hash Request Payload]
    D --> E[Idempotency Service]

    E --> F{Key Exists?}

    F -->|No| G[Insert PROCESSING Record]
    G --> H[Execute Payment Command]
    H --> I[Call Gateway Adapter]
    I --> J[Update Payment State]
    J --> K[Insert Event / Ledger / Outbox]
    K --> L[Save Response Snapshot]
    L --> M[Return Response]

    F -->|Yes| N{Request Hash Same?}
    N -->|No| O[409 Key Reused with Different Request]
    N -->|Yes| P{Existing Status}

    P -->|PROCESSING| Q[409/202 Operation In Progress]
    P -->|SUCCEEDED| R[Return Cached Response]
    P -->|FAILED| S[Return Cached Failure or Retry Policy]
    P -->|EXPIRED| T[Reject or Start New Operation by Policy]

    I --> U[Provider Idempotency Key]
    U --> V[External PSP]
```

---

# 3. Scope Idempotency yang Benar

Kesalahan umum: memakai `idempotency_key` global tanpa operation scope.

Lebih aman:

```text
idempotency_key + operation_type + operation_key
```

Contoh:

| Operation | `operation_type` | `operation_key` | Contoh |
|---|---|---|---|
| Create payment | `CREATE_PAYMENT` | `orderId` atau generated logical key | `ORD-123` |
| Capture payment | `CAPTURE_PAYMENT` | `paymentId` | `pay_123` |
| Refund payment | `CREATE_REFUND` | `paymentId` atau `refundRequestId` | `pay_123` |
| Void payment | `VOID_PAYMENT` | `paymentId` | `pay_123` |
| Query provider status | `QUERY_PROVIDER_STATUS` | `paymentId` | `pay_123` |

Kenapa perlu scope?

```text
Idempotency-Key "abc" untuk create payment
tidak boleh bentrok dengan
Idempotency-Key "abc" untuk refund payment.
```

Ada dua pilihan desain:

## Pilihan A - Global Key

```sql
PRIMARY KEY (idempotency_key)
```

Sederhana, tetapi client harus menjamin key unik untuk semua operasi.

## Pilihan B - Scoped Key

```sql
UNIQUE (operation_type, idempotency_key)
```

Lebih fleksibel dan biasanya lebih cocok untuk internal microservice.

Rekomendasi praktis untuk enterprise internal payment service:

```text
Gunakan scoped key:
(operation_type, idempotency_key)
```

---

# 4. Schema `idempotency_keys`

Versi yang lebih production-ready:

```sql
CREATE TABLE idempotency_keys (
    idempotency_record_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    operation_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    operation_key VARCHAR(200),
    idempotency_key VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    request_method VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    request_path VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
    request_hash VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    response_status_code INTEGER,
    response_body JSONB,
    response_headers JSONB,

    payment_id UUID,
    refund_id UUID,

    locked_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_idempotency_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'PROCESSING',
            'SUCCEEDED',
            'FAILED',
            'EXPIRED'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_idempotency_key_not_blank
        CHECK (length(trim(idempotency_key)) > 0)
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_idempotency_operation_key
ON idempotency_keys(operation_type, idempotency_key);

CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_operation_lookup
ON idempotency_keys(operation_type, operation_key);

CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_status
ON idempotency_keys(status);

CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_locked_until
ON idempotency_keys(locked_until);

CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_expires_at
ON idempotency_keys(expires_at);

CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_payment_id
ON idempotency_keys(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_refund_id
ON idempotency_keys(refund_id);
```

PostgreSQL `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT` dapat dipakai untuk menangani unique conflict; dokumentasinya menjelaskan bahwa `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` menghindari insert saat konflik, sedangkan `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` melakukan update terhadap row existing. PostgreSQL juga menyatakan `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` menjamin outcome atomic insert-or-update dalam kondisi high concurrency. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-insert.html))

---

# 5. Status Lifecycle Idempotency

```mermaid
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> PROCESSING
    PROCESSING --> SUCCEEDED
    PROCESSING --> FAILED
    PROCESSING --> EXPIRED

    SUCCEEDED --> [*]
    FAILED --> [*]
    EXPIRED --> [*]
```

Makna tiap status:

| Status | Arti | Behavior saat Retry |
|---|---|---|
| `PROCESSING` | Request pertama sedang diproses atau stuck. | Return `409 Conflict`, `202 Accepted`, atau recover jika lock expired. |
| `SUCCEEDED` | Request pertama selesai sukses. | Return cached response. |
| `FAILED` | Request pertama selesai gagal. | Return cached failure, kecuali policy mengizinkan reprocess. |
| `EXPIRED` | Key sudah di luar retention. | Reject atau perlakukan sebagai request baru sesuai policy. |

Rekomendasi awal:

```text
PROCESSING + locked_until belum lewat
  -> 409 OPERATION_ALREADY_PROCESSING

PROCESSING + locked_until lewat
  -> recovery path: query payment/refund by operation_key, jangan asal execute ulang

SUCCEEDED
  -> return cached response

FAILED
  -> return cached failure untuk deterministic failure
  -> untuk transient failure, sebaiknya tidak mark FAILED permanen terlalu cepat
```

---

# 6. Request Hash

Idempotency key yang sama hanya boleh dipakai untuk request yang sama secara semantik.

Contoh berbahaya:

```text
Request 1:
Idempotency-Key: abc
amountMinor: 100000

Request 2:
Idempotency-Key: abc
amountMinor: 900000
```

Harus ditolak.

## 6.1 Canonical Request Hash

Jangan hash raw JSON string langsung tanpa canonicalization, karena ini bisa berbeda hanya karena urutan field.

```json
{"amountMinor":100000,"currency":"IDR"}
```

dan

```json
{"currency":"IDR","amountMinor":100000}
```

secara semantik sama.

Praktisnya, buat canonical form:

```text
method + path template + normalized body + selected headers
```

Contoh:

```text
POST
/payments
{"amountMinor":100000,"captureMode":"AUTO","currency":"IDR","merchantId":"MRC-001","orderId":"ORD-123","paymentMethod":{"type":"CARD_TOKEN","token":"<redacted-or-hashed>"}}
```

Lalu hash pakai SHA-256.

---

# 7. Java Request Hash Utility

Contoh memakai Jackson:

```java
package com.company.payment.application.idempotency;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.SerializationFeature;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.util.HexFormat;

public final class RequestHashCalculator {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public RequestHashCalculator(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper.copy()
                .configure(SerializationFeature.ORDER_MAP_ENTRIES_BY_KEYS, true);
    }

    public String calculate(
            String method,
            String pathTemplate,
            Object requestBody
    ) {
        try {
            JsonNode tree = objectMapper.valueToTree(requestBody);
            String canonicalBody = objectMapper.writeValueAsString(tree);

            String canonicalRequest = method.toUpperCase()
                    + "\n"
                    + pathTemplate
                    + "\n"
                    + canonicalBody;

            MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
            byte[] hash = digest.digest(canonicalRequest.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

            return "sha256:" + HexFormat.of().formatHex(hash);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to calculate request hash", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan praktis:

```text
Jangan masukkan raw card/token/sensitive data ke string log.
Untuk hash, boleh memasukkan token jika tidak dilog.
Untuk audit/log, simpan hash payload, bukan full sensitive payload.
```

---

# 8. Idempotency Decision Matrix

| Existing Record | Request Hash | Lock | Action |
|---|---|---|---|
| Tidak ada | - | - | Insert `PROCESSING`, jalankan command. |
| Ada `PROCESSING` | Sama | Belum expired | Return `409 OPERATION_ALREADY_PROCESSING` atau `202`. |
| Ada `PROCESSING` | Sama | Expired | Masuk recovery path. |
| Ada `PROCESSING` | Beda | Apa pun | Return `409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED_WITH_DIFFERENT_REQUEST`. |
| Ada `SUCCEEDED` | Sama | - | Return cached response. |
| Ada `SUCCEEDED` | Beda | - | Return `409`. |
| Ada `FAILED` | Sama | - | Return cached failure atau retry by policy. |
| Ada `FAILED` | Beda | - | Return `409`. |
| Ada `EXPIRED` | Sama/Beda | - | Reject atau require new key. |

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text
Jangan otomatis re-execute provider call hanya karena status PROCESSING expired.
Selalu cek payment/refund/attempt state lebih dulu.
```

---

# 9. MyBatis Mapper untuk Idempotency

## 9.1 Record

```java
package com.company.payment.infrastructure.persistence.idempotency;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

public record IdempotencyRecord(
        UUID idempotencyRecordId,
        String operationType,
        String operationKey,
        String idempotencyKey,
        String requestMethod,
        String requestPath,
        String requestHash,
        String status,
        Integer responseStatusCode,
        String responseBodyJson,
        String responseHeadersJson,
        UUID paymentId,
        UUID refundId,
        Instant lockedUntil,
        Instant expiresAt,
        Instant createdAt,
        Instant updatedAt
) {}
```

## 9.2 Mapper Interface

```java
package com.company.payment.infrastructure.persistence.idempotency;

import org.apache.ibatis.annotations.Param;

import java.util.Optional;

public interface IdempotencyMapper {

    int insertProcessing(IdempotencyInsertCommand command);

    Optional<IdempotencyRecord> findByOperationAndKey(
            @Param("operationType") String operationType,
            @Param("idempotencyKey") String idempotencyKey
    );

    Optional<IdempotencyRecord> findByOperationAndKeyForUpdate(
            @Param("operationType") String operationType,
            @Param("idempotencyKey") String idempotencyKey
    );

    int markSucceeded(IdempotencyCompleteCommand command);

    int markFailed(IdempotencyCompleteCommand command);

    int markExpired(
            @Param("operationType") String operationType,
            @Param("idempotencyKey") String idempotencyKey
    );
}
```

## 9.3 Insert Processing

```xml
<insert id="insertProcessing">
    INSERT INTO idempotency_keys (
        idempotency_record_id,
        operation_type,
        operation_key,
        idempotency_key,
        request_method,
        request_path,
        request_hash,
        status,
        locked_until,
        expires_at,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    ) VALUES (
        #{idempotencyRecordId},
        #{operationType},
        #{operationKey},
        #{idempotencyKey},
        #{requestMethod},
        #{requestPath},
        #{requestHash},
        'PROCESSING',
        #{lockedUntil},
        #{expiresAt},
        now(),
        now()
    )
    ON CONFLICT (operation_type, idempotency_key)
    DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

## 9.4 Find for Update

```xml
<select id="findByOperationAndKeyForUpdate" resultMap="IdempotencyResultMap">
    SELECT
        idempotency_record_id,
        operation_type,
        operation_key,
        idempotency_key,
        request_method,
        request_path,
        request_hash,
        status,
        response_status_code,
        response_body,
        response_headers,
        payment_id,
        refund_id,
        locked_until,
        expires_at,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    FROM idempotency_keys
    WHERE operation_type = #{operationType}
      AND idempotency_key = #{idempotencyKey}
    FOR UPDATE
</select>
```

## 9.5 Mark Succeeded

```xml
<update id="markSucceeded">
    UPDATE idempotency_keys
    SET status = 'SUCCEEDED',
        response_status_code = #{responseStatusCode},
        response_body = CAST(#{responseBodyJson} AS jsonb),
        response_headers = CAST(#{responseHeadersJson} AS jsonb),
        payment_id = #{paymentId},
        refund_id = #{refundId},
        locked_until = NULL,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE operation_type = #{operationType}
      AND idempotency_key = #{idempotencyKey}
      AND request_hash = #{requestHash}
      AND status = 'PROCESSING'
</update>
```

## 9.6 Mark Failed

```xml
<update id="markFailed">
    UPDATE idempotency_keys
    SET status = 'FAILED',
        response_status_code = #{responseStatusCode},
        response_body = CAST(#{responseBodyJson} AS jsonb),
        response_headers = CAST(#{responseHeadersJson} AS jsonb),
        payment_id = #{paymentId},
        refund_id = #{refundId},
        locked_until = NULL,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE operation_type = #{operationType}
      AND idempotency_key = #{idempotencyKey}
      AND request_hash = #{requestHash}
      AND status = 'PROCESSING'
</update>
```

---

# 10. Idempotency Service Design

## 10.1 Result Type

```java
package com.company.payment.application.idempotency;

public sealed interface IdempotencyDecision
        permits IdempotencyDecision.Execute,
                IdempotencyDecision.ReturnCached,
                IdempotencyDecision.Processing,
                IdempotencyDecision.Conflict,
                IdempotencyDecision.Recover {

    record Execute() implements IdempotencyDecision {}

    record ReturnCached(
            int statusCode,
            String responseBodyJson,
            String responseHeadersJson
    ) implements IdempotencyDecision {}

    record Processing() implements IdempotencyDecision {}

    record Conflict(String message) implements IdempotencyDecision {}

    record Recover() implements IdempotencyDecision {}
}
```

## 10.2 Service

```java
package com.company.payment.application.idempotency;

import com.company.payment.infrastructure.persistence.idempotency.IdempotencyInsertCommand;
import com.company.payment.infrastructure.persistence.idempotency.IdempotencyMapper;
import com.company.payment.infrastructure.persistence.idempotency.IdempotencyRecord;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

public class IdempotencyService {

    private final IdempotencyMapper mapper;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final Duration processingLockTtl;
    private final Duration keyRetentionTtl;

    public IdempotencyService(
            IdempotencyMapper mapper,
            Clock clock,
            Duration processingLockTtl,
            Duration keyRetentionTtl
    ) {
        this.mapper = mapper;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.processingLockTtl = processingLockTtl;
        this.keyRetentionTtl = keyRetentionTtl;
    }

    public IdempotencyDecision begin(
            String operationType,
            String operationKey,
            String idempotencyKey,
            String method,
            String path,
            String requestHash
    ) {
        validateKey(idempotencyKey);

        Instant now = Instant.now(clock);

        IdempotencyInsertCommand insert = new IdempotencyInsertCommand(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                operationType,
                operationKey,
                idempotencyKey,
                method,
                path,
                requestHash,
                now.plus(processingLockTtl),
                now.plus(keyRetentionTtl)
        );

        int inserted = mapper.insertProcessing(insert);

        if (inserted == 1) {
            return new IdempotencyDecision.Execute();
        }

        IdempotencyRecord existing = mapper
                .findByOperationAndKey(operationType, idempotencyKey)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("Idempotency record disappeared"));

        if (!existing.requestHash().equals(requestHash)) {
            return new IdempotencyDecision.Conflict(
                    "Idempotency key was reused with a different request"
            );
        }

        return switch (existing.status()) {
            case "SUCCEEDED" -> new IdempotencyDecision.ReturnCached(
                    existing.responseStatusCode(),
                    existing.responseBodyJson(),
                    existing.responseHeadersJson()
            );
            case "FAILED" -> new IdempotencyDecision.ReturnCached(
                    existing.responseStatusCode(),
                    existing.responseBodyJson(),
                    existing.responseHeadersJson()
            );
            case "PROCESSING" -> {
                if (existing.lockedUntil() != null && existing.lockedUntil().isBefore(now)) {
                    yield new IdempotencyDecision.Recover();
                }
                yield new IdempotencyDecision.Processing();
            }
            case "EXPIRED" -> new IdempotencyDecision.Conflict(
                    "Idempotency key has expired; use a new key"
            );
            default -> throw new IllegalStateException("Unknown idempotency status: " + existing.status());
        };
    }

    private void validateKey(String idempotencyKey) {
        if (idempotencyKey == null || idempotencyKey.isBlank()) {
            throw new IdempotencyException("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED");
        }

        if (idempotencyKey.length() > 200) {
            throw new IdempotencyException("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_TOO_LONG");
        }

        if (idempotencyKey.contains("\n") || idempotencyKey.contains("\r")) {
            throw new IdempotencyException("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_INVALID");
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 11. Integrasi di `PaymentApplicationService`

## 11.1 Create Payment Flow

```java
package com.company.payment.application;

import com.company.payment.api.dto.CreatePaymentRequest;
import com.company.payment.api.dto.PaymentResponse;
import com.company.payment.application.idempotency.*;

public class PaymentApplicationService {

    private final IdempotencyService idempotencyService;
    private final RequestHashCalculator requestHashCalculator;
    private final PaymentCommandExecutor commandExecutor;
    private final CachedResponseMapper cachedResponseMapper;

    public PaymentResponse createPayment(
            String idempotencyKey,
            String correlationId,
            CreatePaymentRequest request
    ) {
        String requestHash = requestHashCalculator.calculate(
                "POST",
                "/payments",
                request
        );

        IdempotencyDecision decision = idempotencyService.begin(
                "CREATE_PAYMENT",
                request.orderId(),
                idempotencyKey,
                "POST",
                "/payments",
                requestHash
        );

        if (decision instanceof IdempotencyDecision.ReturnCached cached) {
            return cachedResponseMapper.toPaymentResponse(cached.responseBodyJson());
        }

        if (decision instanceof IdempotencyDecision.Processing) {
            throw PaymentErrors.operationAlreadyProcessing(correlationId);
        }

        if (decision instanceof IdempotencyDecision.Conflict conflict) {
            throw PaymentErrors.idempotencyConflict(conflict.message(), correlationId);
        }

        if (decision instanceof IdempotencyDecision.Recover) {
            return recoverCreatePayment(request.orderId(), idempotencyKey, correlationId);
        }

        return commandExecutor.executeCreatePayment(
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId,
                request,
                requestHash
        );
    }

    private PaymentResponse recoverCreatePayment(
            String orderId,
            String idempotencyKey,
            String correlationId
    ) {
        /*
         * Recovery rule:
         * 1. Look up payment by orderId / operationKey.
         * 2. If payment exists and is terminal or pending, return current state and complete idempotency record.
         * 3. If no payment exists, decide whether to allow re-execute.
         * 4. Never blindly call provider again without checking internal state.
         */
        throw PaymentErrors.operationRecoveryRequired(correlationId);
    }
}
```

---

# 12. Critical Transaction Pattern

Ada dua pilihan:

## Option A - Idempotency Record dan Payment Creation dalam Satu Transaksi

```text
BEGIN
  insert idempotency PROCESSING
  insert payment INITIATED
  insert event PAYMENT_INITIATED
COMMIT

call provider

BEGIN
  update payment
  insert attempt/event/ledger/outbox
  update idempotency SUCCEEDED
COMMIT
```

Ini praktis dan aman.

## Option B - Idempotency Insert Dulu, Lalu Command Transaction

```text
BEGIN
  insert idempotency PROCESSING
COMMIT

BEGIN
  insert payment INITIATED
  insert event
COMMIT

call provider

BEGIN
  update payment
  update idempotency
COMMIT
```

Lebih sederhana memisahkan ownership, tetapi ada window di mana idempotency sudah `PROCESSING` sementara payment belum dibuat. Recovery harus lebih cermat.

Rekomendasi:

```text
Untuk create payment, gunakan satu transaksi pendek untuk:
- insert idempotency PROCESSING
- insert payment INITIATED
- insert event PAYMENT_INITIATED

Lalu external PSP call di luar transaksi DB panjang.
```

---

# 13. Provider Idempotency Propagation

Internal idempotency key dan provider idempotency key perlu dihubungkan.

Contoh:

```text
Internal:
operation_type = CREATE_PAYMENT
idempotency_key = idem-abc

Provider:
Stripe Idempotency-Key = idem-abc atau derived key
Adyen idempotency-key = idem-abc atau derived key
PayPal PayPal-Request-Id = idem-abc atau derived key
```

Namun sebaiknya jangan selalu kirim raw internal key apa adanya ke provider. Buat derived key yang stabil dan tidak mengandung data sensitif:

```text
provider_idempotency_key =
  hash(provider + ":" + environment + ":" + operation_type + ":" + internal_idempotency_key)
```

Contoh utility:

```java
package com.company.payment.application.idempotency;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.util.HexFormat;

public final class ProviderIdempotencyKeyFactory {

    private ProviderIdempotencyKeyFactory() {}

    public static String create(
            String provider,
            String environment,
            String operationType,
            String internalIdempotencyKey
    ) {
        try {
            String raw = provider + ":" + environment + ":" + operationType + ":" + internalIdempotencyKey;
            byte[] digest = MessageDigest
                    .getInstance("SHA-256")
                    .digest(raw.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

            return HexFormat.of().formatHex(digest).substring(0, 48);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to create provider idempotency key", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Kenapa perlu derived key?

| Alasan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Avoid leaking internal keys | Internal key bisa punya format atau metadata internal. |
| Provider length limit | Adyen menyebut idempotency key maksimum 64 karakter. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/development-resources/api-idempotency)) |
| Scope jelas | Provider/environment/operation bisa masuk hash. |
| Aman untuk multi-provider | Key yang sama tidak bentrok lintas provider. |

---

# 14. Gateway Client Example

```java
package com.company.payment.infrastructure.gateway;

public interface PaymentGatewayClient {

    GatewayPaymentResult createPayment(GatewayCreatePaymentRequest request);

    GatewayCaptureResult capturePayment(GatewayCaptureRequest request);

    GatewayRefundResult refundPayment(GatewayRefundRequest request);
}
```

Request ke provider:

```java
package com.company.payment.infrastructure.gateway;

public record GatewayCreatePaymentRequest(
        String providerIdempotencyKey,
        String merchantId,
        long amountMinor,
        String currency,
        String paymentMethodToken,
        boolean captureImmediately
) {}
```

HTTP call pseudo-code:

```java
package com.company.payment.infrastructure.gateway.stripe;

import com.company.payment.infrastructure.gateway.GatewayCreatePaymentRequest;
import com.company.payment.infrastructure.gateway.GatewayPaymentResult;

public class StripeGatewayClient implements PaymentGatewayClient {

    private final HttpClientWrapper http;

    @Override
    public GatewayPaymentResult createPayment(GatewayCreatePaymentRequest request) {
        return http.post("/v1/payment_intents")
                .header("Idempotency-Key", request.providerIdempotencyKey())
                .jsonBody(mapToStripePayload(request))
                .execute(GatewayPaymentResult.class);
    }
}
```

Provider-specific header harus mengikuti provider masing-masing: Stripe memakai `Idempotency-Key`, Adyen memakai `idempotency-key`, sedangkan PayPal memakai `PayPal-Request-Id` untuk REST POST idempotency. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. Safe Retry Pattern

Retry hanya aman jika memenuhi syarat:

```text
1. Same operation
2. Same idempotency key
3. Same request hash
4. Same provider idempotency key
5. Domain state masih mengizinkan
6. Retry error tergolong transient
```

## 15.1 Retryable vs Non-Retryable

| Error | Retry? | Catatan |
|---|---:|---|
| Network timeout | Ya, dengan idempotency. | Jangan ubah payment ke `FAILED` langsung. |
| HTTP 502/503/504 dari PSP | Ya, terbatas. | Backoff + idempotency. |
| Connection refused | Ya, terbatas. | Circuit breaker bisa aktif. |
| Rate limit | Ya, setelah delay. | Perhatikan provider policy. |
| Card declined | Tidak | Business failure, bukan transient. |
| Insufficient funds | Tidak langsung | Customer perlu action/metode lain. |
| Invalid API key | Tidak | Config/security issue. |
| Invalid request payload | Tidak | Bug internal/contract. |
| Currency unsupported | Tidak | Config/routing issue. |
| Duplicate provider request | Jangan blindly retry | Query status by provider reference. |

---

# 16. Timeout Handling

Timeout adalah kasus paling berbahaya.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant C as Client
    participant API as Payment API
    participant DB as DB
    participant PSP as PSP

    C->>API: POST /payments idem-123
    API->>DB: insert payment INITIATED + idem PROCESSING
    API->>PSP: create payment with provider idem key
    PSP--xAPI: response lost / timeout
    API->>DB: mark payment UNKNOWN or AUTHORIZATION_PENDING
    API->>DB: save idempotency response pending/unknown
    API-->>C: 202 UNKNOWN/PENDING_CONFIRMATION

    C->>API: retry same idem-123
    API->>DB: find same idempotency record
    API-->>C: return cached UNKNOWN/PENDING response
```

Jangan lakukan ini:

```text
Timeout -> mark FAILED
```

Lebih aman:

```text
Timeout -> UNKNOWN / PENDING_CONFIRMATION
```

Lalu recovery:

```text
1. Query provider by provider reference jika ada.
2. Tunggu webhook.
3. Reconcile lewat settlement/report.
4. Jangan membuat charge baru sampai status lama jelas.
```

---

# 17. Response Caching Policy

Untuk `SUCCEEDED`, simpan response snapshot.

```json
{
  "paymentId": "b0d1e3cc-1df0-4d4d-bab4-991ea6f1a9f0",
  "orderId": "ORD-123",
  "status": "CAPTURED",
  "amountMinor": 100000,
  "currency": "IDR"
}
```

Untuk `FAILED`, simpan response jika failure deterministic.

Contoh deterministic:

```text
VALIDATION_FAILED
PAYMENT_INVALID_STATE
PAYMENT_AMOUNT_EXCEEDS_AUTHORIZED
IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED_WITH_DIFFERENT_REQUEST
```

Contoh transient:

```text
PROVIDER_TIMEOUT
PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE
DATABASE_DEADLOCK
```

Rekomendasi:

| Failure Type | Simpan sebagai `FAILED` Idempotency? |
|---|---:|
| Validation error sebelum side effect | Boleh, tapi banyak sistem tidak menyimpan. |
| Invalid state | Boleh. |
| Provider hard decline | Boleh, jika sudah pasti. |
| Provider timeout | Jangan final failed; simpan pending/unknown response atau recovery-needed. |
| Internal crash saat processing | Tetap `PROCESSING` sampai recovery. |

---

# 18. API Behavior Examples

## 18.1 First Create Payment

Request:

```http
POST /payments
Idempotency-Key: idem-001
Content-Type: application/json
```

Response:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "pay-001",
  "status": "CAPTURED",
  "amountMinor": 100000,
  "currency": "IDR"
}
```

DB:

```text
idempotency_keys:
operation_type = CREATE_PAYMENT
idempotency_key = idem-001
request_hash = sha256:abc
status = SUCCEEDED
response_status_code = 201
```

## 18.2 Retry Same Key Same Payload

Response harus sama secara logical:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "pay-001",
  "status": "CAPTURED",
  "amountMinor": 100000,
  "currency": "IDR"
}
```

Tidak boleh create `pay-002`.

## 18.3 Same Key Different Payload

Request kedua:

```json
{
  "orderId": "ORD-123",
  "amountMinor": 999000,
  "currency": "IDR"
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/idempotency-key-conflict",
  "title": "Idempotency key conflict",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "Idempotency key was reused with a different request.",
  "errorCode": "IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED_WITH_DIFFERENT_REQUEST",
  "traceId": "corr-001"
}
```

---

# 19. Handling Concurrent Same-Key Requests

Dua request bisa masuk bersamaan:

```text
T1: POST /payments Idempotency-Key: abc
T2: POST /payments Idempotency-Key: abc
```

Dengan unique index:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_idempotency_operation_key
ON idempotency_keys(operation_type, idempotency_key);
```

Hanya satu yang berhasil insert `PROCESSING`.

Request kedua akan:

```text
insert affected rows = 0
load existing record
if PROCESSING -> return 409/202
```

Jangan biarkan dua thread sama-sama call PSP.

---

# 20. Should We Return `409` or `202` for Processing?

Ada dua style.

## Option A - `409 Conflict`

```json
{
  "errorCode": "OPERATION_ALREADY_PROCESSING",
  "detail": "A request with the same idempotency key is still processing."
}
```

Cocok jika client sebaiknya retry nanti.

## Option B - `202 Accepted`

```json
{
  "operationStatus": "PROCESSING",
  "paymentId": "pay-001",
  "retryAfterSeconds": 3
}
```

Cocok jika sistem punya operation tracking.

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text
Untuk internal microservice:
- 409 untuk duplicate concurrent request yang belum selesai.
- Sertakan Retry-After jika cocok.

Untuk async payment operation:
- 202 jika operation sudah diterima dan punya paymentId/status yang bisa dipoll.
```

---

# 21. `operation_key` untuk Create Payment

Idempotency key mencegah duplicate request, tetapi tidak selalu cukup mencegah duplicate payment untuk order yang sama.

Contoh:

```text
Client salah generate dua idempotency key berbeda:
Request A: orderId ORD-123, Idempotency-Key abc
Request B: orderId ORD-123, Idempotency-Key xyz
```

Kalau business rule menyatakan satu order hanya boleh punya satu active payment, tambahkan unique constraint atau business guard.

Contoh:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payments_order_active
ON payments(order_id)
WHERE status IN (
    'INITIATED',
    'REQUIRES_ACTION',
    'AUTHORIZATION_PENDING',
    'AUTHORIZED',
    'CAPTURE_PENDING',
    'CAPTURED',
    'PARTIALLY_CAPTURED',
    'UNKNOWN'
);
```

Catatan:

```text
Unique order payment rule tergantung domain.
Subscription, split payment, partial payment, atau retry dengan payment method baru bisa butuh model berbeda.
```

Untuk domain yang mendukung multiple attempts di satu order, lebih baik:

```text
orders punya payment_session_id
payment_session punya banyak payment_attempts
hanya satu successful captured payment per payable item
```

---

# 22. Refund Idempotency

Refund lebih sensitif karena user support/admin sering klik ulang.

Endpoint:

```http
POST /payments/{paymentId}/refunds
Idempotency-Key: refund-abc
```

Operation scope:

```text
operation_type = CREATE_REFUND
operation_key = paymentId
idempotency_key = refund-abc
request_hash = hash(amount + reason + paymentId)
```

Tambahkan juga optional client-generated refund request id:

```json
{
  "refundRequestId": "rrq-2026-00001",
  "amountMinor": 50000,
  "reason": "CUSTOMER_REQUEST"
}
```

Constraint:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_refunds_payment_refund_request
ON refunds(payment_id, refund_request_id)
WHERE refund_request_id IS NOT NULL;
```

Refund invariant:

```sql
UPDATE payments
SET refunded_amount_minor = refunded_amount_minor + #{refundAmountMinor},
    status = CASE
        WHEN refunded_amount_minor + #{refundAmountMinor} = captured_amount_minor
            THEN 'REFUNDED'
        ELSE 'PARTIALLY_REFUNDED'
    END,
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
  AND status IN ('CAPTURED', 'PARTIALLY_REFUNDED')
  AND refunded_amount_minor + #{refundAmountMinor} <= captured_amount_minor
  AND version = #{version};
```

Idempotency mencegah request duplicate. SQL guard mencegah refund melebihi captured amount. Keduanya diperlukan.

---

# 23. Capture Idempotency

Partial capture perlu idempotency ekstra hati-hati.

Contoh:

```text
Authorized amount = 100000
Capture request 1 = 40000, key cap-1
Capture request 2 = 40000, key cap-1 retry
```

Harus menghasilkan satu capture 40000.

Tapi:

```text
Capture request 1 = 40000, key cap-1
Capture request 2 = 40000, key cap-2
```

Bisa valid jika memang business ingin capture dua kali 40000.

Maka idempotency key harus merepresentasikan **logical capture command**, bukan hanya amount.

Praktis:

```json
{
  "captureRequestId": "capreq-ORD-123-shipment-1",
  "amountMinor": 40000,
  "reason": "SHIPMENT_1_READY"
}
```

Constraint:

```sql
ALTER TABLE payment_attempts
ADD COLUMN client_operation_id VARCHAR(200);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_attempts_client_operation
ON payment_attempts(payment_id, attempt_type, client_operation_id)
WHERE client_operation_id IS NOT NULL;
```

---

# 24. Stuck `PROCESSING` Recovery

Skenario:

```text
1. Insert idempotency PROCESSING.
2. Insert payment INITIATED.
3. Service crash sebelum mark SUCCEEDED/FAILED.
```

Retry akan melihat `PROCESSING` tapi `locked_until` sudah lewat.

Recovery algorithm:

```text
Given operation_type + operation_key + idempotency_key:

1. Load idempotency record FOR UPDATE.
2. Verify request_hash same.
3. Check related payment/refund by operation_key.
4. If payment exists:
   a. If terminal or pending known -> build response from current state.
   b. Mark idempotency SUCCEEDED with reconstructed response.
   c. Return response.
5. If payment does not exist:
   a. If no provider call could have happened -> allow re-execute.
   b. If uncertain -> create manual recovery task / return 409 recovery required.
6. Never blindly issue a second provider charge if previous call may have reached PSP.
```

Pseudo-code:

```java
public PaymentResponse recoverCreatePayment(
        String operationKey,
        String idempotencyKey,
        String requestHash,
        String correlationId
) {
    IdempotencyRecord record = idempotencyMapper
            .findByOperationAndKeyForUpdate("CREATE_PAYMENT", idempotencyKey)
            .orElseThrow();

    if (!record.requestHash().equals(requestHash)) {
        throw PaymentErrors.idempotencyConflict(
                "Idempotency key was reused with a different request",
                correlationId
        );
    }

    return paymentRepository.findByOrderId(operationKey)
            .map(payment -> {
                PaymentResponse response = paymentResponseMapper.fromPayment(payment, correlationId);
                idempotencyMapper.markSucceeded(toCompleteCommand(record, response));
                return response;
            })
            .orElseThrow(() -> PaymentErrors.operationRecoveryRequired(correlationId));
}
```

---

# 25. Idempotency + Outbox

Saat operation sukses, lakukan dalam satu transaksi:

```text
BEGIN
  update payment state
  insert payment_event
  insert ledger_entry
  insert outbox_event
  update idempotency_keys -> SUCCEEDED with response snapshot
COMMIT
```

Kenapa?

Kalau idempotency sudah `SUCCEEDED`, tapi outbox belum dibuat, retry akan return cached success sementara service lain tidak pernah menerima event.

Rule:

```text
Idempotency success harus mencerminkan bahwa semua side effect internal yang wajib sudah durable.
```

---

# 26. Idempotency + Webhook

Webhook biasanya **tidak memakai client Idempotency-Key**.

Webhook dedup memakai:

```text
provider + provider_event_id
```

Tetapi webhook processing tetap harus idempotent.

Contoh duplicate webhook:

```text
provider = STRIPE
provider_event_id = evt_123
```

Constraint:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_inbox_provider_event
ON payment_inbox_events(provider, provider_event_id);
```

Webhook processing harus menjaga:

```text
1. Insert inbox event ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING.
2. If duplicate, return 200 to provider.
3. State transition guarded.
4. Ledger reference unique.
5. Outbox event unique/dedup if needed.
```

Jangan campur `idempotency_keys` untuk internal API dengan `payment_inbox_events` untuk webhook. Mereka mirip, tapi scope dan source-nya berbeda.

---

# 27. Retry Backoff Policy

Contoh policy untuk provider call:

| Attempt | Delay |
|---:|---:|
| 1 | Immediate |
| 2 | 200 ms |
| 3 | 1 s |
| 4 | 5 s |
| 5 | Stop / async recovery |

Jangan terlalu agresif karena PSP bisa rate-limit.

Pseudo-code:

```java
public GatewayPaymentResult callWithRetry(GatewayCreatePaymentRequest request) {
    int maxAttempts = 3;

    for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
        try {
            return gatewayClient.createPayment(request);
        } catch (GatewayTimeoutException | GatewayUnavailableException e) {
            if (attempt == maxAttempts) {
                throw e;
            }
            sleep(backoff(attempt));
        } catch (GatewayBusinessDeclineException e) {
            throw e;
        }
    }

    throw new IllegalStateException("unreachable");
}
```

Important:

```text
Retry provider call harus selalu memakai provider idempotency key yang sama.
```

---

# 28. Idempotency Key Generation Guidance

Client/upstream service sebaiknya generate key per logical operation.

Contoh:

```text
UUID v4 random:
7b6e6ef1-5864-4d68-9128-c64045f1f8b3
```

Atau deterministic logical key:

```text
order:ORD-123:create-payment:v1
payment:pay-123:capture:shipment-1:v1
payment:pay-123:refund:case-456:v1
```

Trade-off:

| Key Type | Pros | Cons |
|---|---|---|
| Random UUID | Collision rendah, aman. | Client harus menyimpan untuk retry. |
| Deterministic | Mudah recreate saat retry. | Harus hati-hati agar tidak bocor data sensitif dan tidak reuse untuk operasi berbeda. |
| Business ID | Mudah trace. | Bisa berisi PII/order sensitive; risiko key reuse. |

Rekomendasi:

```text
Internal service:
- boleh deterministic tapi jangan mengandung PII.
- sertakan operation type/version.

External/public client:
- gunakan UUID v4/random high entropy.
```

---

# 29. Security Considerations

Jangan gunakan data sensitif sebagai idempotency key.

Hindari:

```text
customer-email@example.com
card-last4-4242
phone-number
national-id
```

Lebih aman:

```text
random UUID
hash dari logical operation
opaque request id
```

Stripe secara eksplisit menyarankan menghindari data sensitif seperti email address atau personal identifier sebagai idempotency key. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Logging:

```text
Log:
- idempotency_key_hash
- operation_type
- operation_key
- request_hash
- status

Jangan log:
- raw payment token
- raw card data
- raw sensitive personal identifier
```

---

# 30. API Error Contract

## 30.1 Missing Key

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/idempotency-key-required",
  "title": "Idempotency key required",
  "status": 400,
  "detail": "Idempotency-Key header is required for this payment operation.",
  "errorCode": "IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

## 30.2 Reused Key with Different Request

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/idempotency-key-conflict",
  "title": "Idempotency key conflict",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "The same Idempotency-Key was used with a different request payload.",
  "errorCode": "IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED_WITH_DIFFERENT_REQUEST",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

## 30.3 Operation Still Processing

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/operation-processing",
  "title": "Operation still processing",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "A request with the same Idempotency-Key is still being processed.",
  "errorCode": "OPERATION_ALREADY_PROCESSING",
  "traceId": "corr-123",
  "retryAfterSeconds": 3
}
```

## 30.4 Recovery Required

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/operation-recovery-required",
  "title": "Operation recovery required",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "The previous operation did not complete cleanly and requires recovery before it can be retried.",
  "errorCode": "OPERATION_RECOVERY_REQUIRED",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

---

# 31. Practical Implementation Flow per Endpoint

## 31.1 `POST /payments`

```text
1. Validate Idempotency-Key exists.
2. Calculate request hash.
3. IdempotencyService.begin(CREATE_PAYMENT, orderId, key, hash).
4. If cached -> return cached.
5. If processing -> 409/202.
6. If conflict -> 409.
7. If execute:
   a. insert payment INITIATED
   b. insert attempt
   c. call PSP with provider idempotency key
   d. update state
   e. insert event/ledger/outbox
   f. mark idempotency SUCCEEDED with response
8. If timeout:
   a. mark payment UNKNOWN/PENDING_CONFIRMATION
   b. save response snapshot as pending
   c. do not create second payment
```

## 31.2 `POST /payments/{id}/capture`

```text
1. operation_type = CAPTURE_PAYMENT
2. operation_key = paymentId
3. hash includes amountMinor + captureRequestId + reason
4. lock payment row
5. validate remaining capturable amount
6. create capture attempt
7. call PSP with provider idempotency key
8. update captured amount with SQL guard
9. insert ledger/outbox
10. cache response
```

## 31.3 `POST /payments/{id}/refunds`

```text
1. operation_type = CREATE_REFUND
2. operation_key = paymentId
3. hash includes amountMinor + refundRequestId + reason
4. lock payment row
5. validate remaining refundable amount
6. insert refund PENDING
7. call PSP with provider idempotency key
8. update refund/payment state if immediate
9. insert event/ledger/outbox
10. cache response
```

---

# 32. Testing Strategy

## 32.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Missing key | Throw `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED`. |
| Same key same hash succeeded | Return cached response. |
| Same key different hash | Conflict. |
| Processing not expired | Operation already processing. |
| Processing expired | Recovery path. |
| Failed deterministic | Return cached failure. |
| Expired key | Reject or new operation by policy. |

## 32.2 Integration Tests with PostgreSQL

Gunakan Testcontainers.

```text
1. Two concurrent insertProcessing same key -> only one succeeds.
2. Retry same key same payload -> cached response.
3. Retry same key different payload -> 409.
4. Crash simulation after idempotency PROCESSING before payment insert -> recovery required.
5. Crash simulation after payment insert before idempotency success -> recovery reconstructs response.
6. Provider timeout -> payment UNKNOWN, retry returns same UNKNOWN.
7. Duplicate refund request -> only one refund row.
8. Duplicate capture request -> only one capture attempt for same client_operation_id.
```

## 32.3 Concurrency Test Pseudo-code

```java
@Test
void concurrentSameIdempotencyKeyOnlyExecutesOnce() throws Exception {
    String key = "idem-" + UUID.randomUUID();

    int threadCount = 20;
    ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(threadCount);
    CountDownLatch ready = new CountDownLatch(threadCount);
    CountDownLatch start = new CountDownLatch(1);

    List<Future<PaymentResponse>> futures = new ArrayList<>();

    for (int i = 0; i < threadCount; i++) {
        futures.add(executor.submit(() -> {
            ready.countDown();
            start.await();

            return paymentClient.createPayment(
                    key,
                    new CreatePaymentRequest(
                            "ORD-123",
                            "CUST-1",
                            "MRC-1",
                            100000,
                            "IDR",
                            CaptureMode.AUTO,
                            new PaymentMethodRequest(PaymentMethodType.CARD_TOKEN, "pm_tok"),
                            Map.of()
                    )
            );
        }));
    }

    ready.await();
    start.countDown();

    List<PaymentResponse> responses = new ArrayList<>();
    for (Future<PaymentResponse> future : futures) {
        try {
            responses.add(future.get());
        } catch (ExecutionException ignored) {
            // Some requests may receive OPERATION_ALREADY_PROCESSING depending API policy.
        }
    }

    assertThat(paymentRepository.countByOrderId("ORD-123")).isEqualTo(1);
    assertThat(gatewayMock.countCreatePaymentCalls()).isEqualTo(1);
}
```

---

# 33. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Bahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Idempotency key hanya dicek di memory | Hilang saat restart; tidak aman multi-pod. | Simpan di PostgreSQL/Redis durable sesuai kebutuhan. |
| Tidak menyimpan request hash | Key sama bisa dipakai untuk amount berbeda. | Simpan canonical request hash. |
| Tidak cache response | Retry menghasilkan response berbeda/membingungkan. | Simpan response snapshot. |
| Lock terlalu lama sambil call PSP | DB contention tinggi. | Transaksi pendek; call PSP di luar transaksi panjang. |
| Timeout dianggap failed | Bisa salah jika PSP berhasil. | Gunakan `UNKNOWN`/pending + recovery. |
| Provider retry tanpa idempotency key sama | Bisa double charge di PSP. | Propagate provider idempotency key. |
| Idempotency key global tanpa scope | Bentrok antar operasi. | Gunakan `operation_type + key`. |
| Webhook pakai same idempotency table | Source dan semantics beda. | Gunakan inbox dedup. |
| Tidak punya recovery untuk `PROCESSING` stuck | Retry selamanya gagal. | Gunakan `locked_until` + recovery algorithm. |
| Menghapus key terlalu cepat | Client retry telat bisa duplicate. | Retention sesuai API contract dan provider behavior. |

---

# 34. Observability untuk Idempotency

Metrics yang wajib:

| Metric | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `idempotency.begin.total` | Jumlah request mutating dengan idempotency. |
| `idempotency.cache_hit.total` | Retry yang mendapat cached response. |
| `idempotency.conflict.total` | Key reused dengan payload beda. |
| `idempotency.processing.total` | Request duplicate saat masih processing. |
| `idempotency.recovery.total` | Stuck processing yang masuk recovery. |
| `idempotency.expired.total` | Key expired. |
| `provider.retry.total` | Retry ke PSP. |
| `provider.timeout.total` | Timeout provider. |
| `payment.unknown.total` | Payment masuk unknown/pending confirmation. |

Structured log minimal:

```json
{
  "event": "idempotency_decision",
  "operationType": "CREATE_PAYMENT",
  "operationKey": "ORD-123",
  "idempotencyKeyHash": "sha256:...",
  "requestHash": "sha256:...",
  "decision": "RETURN_CACHED",
  "paymentId": "pay-123",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

---

# 35. Retention Policy

Provider punya retention berbeda. Stripe API v1 idempotency key dapat dipangkas otomatis setelah setidaknya 24 jam, sedangkan Stripe API v2 mendefinisikan replay dalam 30 hari untuk request yang memenuhi scope tertentu; Adyen menyimpan idempotency key minimal 7 hari; PayPal menyatakan server menyimpan `PayPal-Request-Id` untuk suatu periode waktu dan durasinya bergantung API terkait. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk internal payment service, rekomendasi awal:

| Operation | Retention |
|---|---:|
| Create payment | 30-90 hari |
| Capture | 30-90 hari |
| Refund | 90-180 hari |
| Void | 30-90 hari |
| Admin recovery | 90-180 hari |

Kenapa lebih lama dari provider?

```text
Internal retry, support investigation, audit, dan reconciliation sering butuh horizon lebih panjang dari provider idempotency cache.
```

Expired job:

```sql
UPDATE idempotency_keys
SET status = 'EXPIRED',
    updated_at = now()
WHERE status IN ('SUCCEEDED', 'FAILED')
  AND expires_at < now();
```

Atau archive:

```text
Move old idempotency records to archive table before deletion.
```

---

# 36. Minimal End-to-End Example

## First Request

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8080/payments \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: idem-create-ord-123-v1" \
  -H "X-Correlation-Id: corr-001" \
  -d '{
    "orderId": "ORD-123",
    "customerId": "CUST-1",
    "merchantId": "MRC-1",
    "amountMinor": 100000,
    "currency": "IDR",
    "captureMode": "AUTO",
    "paymentMethod": {
      "type": "CARD_TOKEN",
      "token": "pm_tok_abc"
    }
  }'
```

Response:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "f6ca2d13-1d94-4d9d-a736-e6c520cfbdbc",
  "orderId": "ORD-123",
  "status": "CAPTURED",
  "amountMinor": 100000,
  "currency": "IDR"
}
```

## Retry Same Request

```bash
curl -X POST http://localhost:8080/payments \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -H "Idempotency-Key: idem-create-ord-123-v1" \
  -H "X-Correlation-Id: corr-002" \
  -d '{
    "orderId": "ORD-123",
    "customerId": "CUST-1",
    "merchantId": "MRC-1",
    "amountMinor": 100000,
    "currency": "IDR",
    "captureMode": "AUTO",
    "paymentMethod": {
      "type": "CARD_TOKEN",
      "token": "pm_tok_abc"
    }
  }'
```

Expected:

```text
No new payment.
No new provider charge.
Return cached payment response.
```

---

# 37. Implementation Checklist

## API

- [ ] Mutating endpoint wajib `Idempotency-Key`.
- [ ] Header length divalidasi.
- [ ] Header tidak boleh mengandung newline/control character.
- [ ] Same key + different request hash → `409`.
- [ ] Same key + same request + success → cached response.
- [ ] Same key + same request + processing → `409`/`202`.
- [ ] Error response memakai stable `errorCode`.

## Database

- [ ] Ada table `idempotency_keys`.
- [ ] Unique index `(operation_type, idempotency_key)`.
- [ ] Ada `request_hash`.
- [ ] Ada `response_status_code`.
- [ ] Ada `response_body`.
- [ ] Ada `locked_until`.
- [ ] Ada `expires_at`.
- [ ] Ada relation optional ke `payment_id` / `refund_id`.

## Provider

- [ ] Provider call menerima derived idempotency key.
- [ ] Retry provider memakai key yang sama.
- [ ] Timeout tidak membuat duplicate provider call tanpa status check.
- [ ] Provider idempotency behavior didokumentasikan per adapter.

## Recovery

- [ ] `PROCESSING` stuck punya recovery path.
- [ ] Recovery cek internal payment/refund dulu.
- [ ] Recovery tidak blindly call PSP ulang.
- [ ] Ada metric dan alert untuk stuck processing.

## Testing

- [ ] Concurrent same key.
- [ ] Same key different payload.
- [ ] Provider timeout.
- [ ] Crash after idempotency insert.
- [ ] Crash after payment insert.
- [ ] Duplicate refund.
- [ ] Duplicate capture.
- [ ] Cached response correctness.

---

# 38. Ringkasan

Idempotency dalam payment service harus dilihat sebagai **financial safety mechanism**, bukan sekadar header.

Desain yang benar:

```text
Idempotency-Key
+ operation_type
+ operation_key
+ request_hash
+ durable PROCESSING lock
+ provider idempotency propagation
+ response snapshot
+ recovery path
```

Aturan paling penting:

```text
Retry boleh terjadi.
Side effect finansial tidak boleh dobel.
Timeout tidak boleh langsung dianggap gagal.
Same key dengan payload beda harus ditolak.
Webhook dedup berbeda dari API idempotency.
```

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 5/18**: Payment Gateway Adapter Pattern. Fokusnya membuat integrasi PSP tetap vendor-neutral, mudah diganti, aman untuk retry/idempotency, dan tidak mencemari domain payment dengan payload provider.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 5/18 - Payment Gateway Adapter Pattern

Seri ini fokus ke **Payment Gateway Adapter Pattern**: bagaimana membuat integrasi PSP/payment gateway seperti Stripe, Adyen, PayPal, bank transfer, virtual account, QRIS, atau provider lokal tetap **terisolasi dari domain payment internal**.

Core idea:

```text id="tgxpqm"
Payment domain tidak boleh tahu detail payload Stripe, Adyen, PayPal, atau provider lain.
Domain hanya tahu konsep bisnis:
authorize, capture, void, refund, query status.
```

Provider punya istilah, status, header, error code, idempotency behavior, webhook payload, dan retry behavior yang berbeda-beda. Misalnya Stripe `PaymentIntent` punya status seperti `requires_payment_method`, `requires_confirmation`, `requires_action`, `processing`, `requires_capture`, `canceled`, dan `succeeded`; Stripe juga memakai idempotency key untuk retry aman. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/payment_intents/object?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen memakai `resultCode` untuk menunjukkan current status payment, tetapi dokumentasinya mengingatkan bahwa status payment bisa berubah setelah result code awal diterima, sehingga order management sebaiknya tidak hanya bergantung pada response awal. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/build-your-integration/payment-result-codes?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PayPal Payments API mendukung operasi authorize, capture authorized payment, refund captured payment, dan show payment information; untuk idempotency REST POST, PayPal memakai header `PayPal-Request-Id`. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/payments/v2/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Introduction

Tanpa adapter pattern, payment service biasanya berubah menjadi seperti ini:

```text id="0dnwk8"
PaymentService
  -> if provider == STRIPE, build Stripe payload
  -> if provider == ADYEN, build Adyen payload
  -> if provider == PAYPAL, build PayPal payload
  -> map raw status everywhere
  -> map raw error everywhere
  -> leak provider-specific field to domain/API
```

Itu cepat rusak ketika:

- provider bertambah,
- provider diganti,
- payload API provider berubah,
- ada routing multi-provider,
- ada fallback provider,
- butuh sandbox/mock PSP,
- butuh test tanpa memanggil provider asli,
- butuh normalize error dan status.

Desain yang lebih sehat:

```text id="qg1iki"
PaymentApplicationService
  -> PaymentGatewayRouter
  -> PaymentGatewayClient interface
  -> StripePaymentGatewayClient
  -> AdyenPaymentGatewayClient
  -> PayPalPaymentGatewayClient
  -> MockPaymentGatewayClient
```

Jersey/JAX-RS tetap menjadi boundary API internal service. Untuk call keluar ke provider, Jakarta REST juga menyediakan client API untuk mengakses REST resource dari aplikasi Java. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest-client/rest-client.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Diagram Architecture

```mermaid id="vg06co"
flowchart TD
    A[PaymentResource - Jersey/JAX-RS] --> B[PaymentApplicationService]

    B --> C[Payment Domain Service]
    B --> D[PaymentGatewayRouter]

    D --> E{Provider Selection}

    E --> F[StripeGatewayClient]
    E --> G[AdyenGatewayClient]
    E --> H[PayPalGatewayClient]
    E --> I[BankTransferGatewayClient]
    E --> J[MockGatewayClient]

    F --> K[Stripe API]
    G --> L[Adyen API]
    H --> M[PayPal API]
    I --> N[Bank / Local Rails]
    J --> O[Local Mock PSP]

    F --> P[Provider Request Mapper]
    G --> P
    H --> P

    F --> Q[Provider Response Mapper]
    G --> Q
    H --> Q

    Q --> R[GatewayResult - Provider Neutral]
    R --> B

    B --> S[(PostgreSQL)]
    S --> S1[payments]
    S --> S2[payment_attempts]
    S --> S3[payment_events]
    S --> S4[ledger_entries]
    S --> S5[outbox_events]
```

---

# 3. Practical Decomposition Table

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | Provider-neutral interface | Abstraksi operasi payment umum. | `PaymentGatewayClient` |
| 2 | Provider request model | Request internal yang tidak bocor vendor. | `GatewayAuthorizeRequest`, `GatewayCaptureRequest`, `GatewayRefundRequest` |
| 3 | Provider result model | Response internal yang stabil. | `GatewayPaymentResult`, `GatewayRefundResult` |
| 4 | Provider adapter | Implementasi per PSP. | `StripeGatewayClient`, `AdyenGatewayClient`, `PayPalGatewayClient` |
| 5 | Request mapper | Mapping internal request → provider payload. | `StripeRequestMapper`, `AdyenRequestMapper` |
| 6 | Response mapper | Mapping provider response → internal result. | `StripeResponseMapper`, `AdyenResponseMapper` |
| 7 | Error mapper | Mapping provider error → internal error code. | `GatewayErrorMapper` |
| 8 | Status mapper | Mapping provider status → internal status. | `GatewayStatusMapper` |
| 9 | Capability model | Mengetahui fitur provider. | `GatewayCapabilities` |
| 10 | Routing | Pilih provider berdasarkan merchant/currency/method. | `PaymentGatewayRouter` |
| 11 | Idempotency propagation | Kirim derived idempotency key ke provider. | `ProviderIdempotencyKeyFactory` |
| 12 | Timeout handling | Bedakan timeout, decline, invalid request, provider down. | `GatewayException` hierarchy |
| 13 | Observability | Log, metric, trace per provider call. | structured log + metrics |
| 14 | Sandbox/mock | Test tanpa provider asli. | `MockPaymentGatewayClient` |
| 15 | Contract testing | Pastikan adapter mapping benar. | fixture-based tests |

---

# 4. Adapter Boundary

Payment application layer hanya boleh melihat interface seperti ini:

```java id="r6a5er"
package com.company.payment.gateway;

public interface PaymentGatewayClient {

    GatewayAuthorizeResult authorize(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request);

    GatewayCaptureResult capture(GatewayCaptureRequest request);

    GatewayVoidResult voidAuthorization(GatewayVoidRequest request);

    GatewayRefundResult refund(GatewayRefundRequest request);

    GatewayPaymentStatusResult getPaymentStatus(GatewayPaymentStatusRequest request);

    GatewayRefundStatusResult getRefundStatus(GatewayRefundStatusRequest request);

    GatewayCapabilities capabilities();
}
```

Catatan desain:

```text id="4rnuvj"
Interface ini tidak menyebut Stripe PaymentIntent,
Adyen pspReference,
atau PayPal authorization_id sebagai first-class domain.
Semua detail vendor masuk ke providerReference / providerMetadata.
```

---

# 7. Status Mapping Strategy

Jangan pakai provider status langsung di `payments.status`.

Gunakan mapping eksplisit.

## 7.1 Stripe Mapping Example

Stripe `PaymentIntent` status mencakup `requires_payment_method`, `requires_confirmation`, `requires_action`, `processing`, `requires_capture`, `canceled`, dan `succeeded`; untuk manual capture, Stripe menyatakan capture dilakukan terhadap PaymentIntent yang statusnya `requires_capture`. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/payment_intents/object?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Stripe Status | Internal Gateway Status | Payment Domain Status |
|---|---|---|
| `requires_payment_method` | `FAILED` atau `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` tergantung context | `FAILED` / `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` |
| `requires_confirmation` | `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` |
| `requires_action` | `REQUIRES_ACTION` | `REQUIRES_ACTION` |
| `processing` | `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` / `CAPTURE_PENDING` | `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` / `CAPTURE_PENDING` |
| `requires_capture` | `AUTHORIZED` | `AUTHORIZED` |
| `succeeded` | `CAPTURED` | `CAPTURED` |
| `canceled` | `CANCELLED` | `CANCELLED` |

## 7.2 Adyen Mapping Example

Adyen `resultCode` menunjukkan current status payment, tetapi status bisa berubah setelah response awal; Adyen merekomendasikan agar result code tidak langsung dipakai untuk update order management sebagai final truth. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/build-your-integration/payment-result-codes?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Adyen Result Code | Internal Gateway Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `Authorised` | `AUTHORIZED` atau `CAPTURED` tergantung capture mode | Perlu lihat capture mode. |
| `Received` | `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | Payment diterima, belum final. |
| `Pending` | `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | Tunggu webhook/status update. |
| `PresentToShopper` | `REQUIRES_ACTION` | Ada instruksi/action ke shopper. |
| `RedirectShopper` | `REQUIRES_ACTION` | Redirect flow. |
| `Refused` | `FAILED` | Decline/failure. |
| `Cancelled` | `CANCELLED` | Cancelled. |
| Unknown/new code | `UNKNOWN` | Jangan drop; masuk exception/monitoring. |

## 7.3 PayPal Mapping Example

PayPal Payments API menyediakan operasi authorize, capture, refund, dan show payment information; PayPal Orders API juga membedakan order dengan `intent=AUTHORIZE` dan `intent=CAPTURE`. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/payments/v2/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| PayPal Concept | Internal Gateway Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Authorization created | `AUTHORIZED` | Capture later. |
| Capture completed | `CAPTURED` | Funds captured. |
| Capture pending | `CAPTURE_PENDING` | Tunggu final status. |
| Authorization voided | `CANCELLED` | Void/cancel auth. |
| Refund completed | `SUCCEEDED` refund status | Refund lifecycle terpisah. |
| Provider timeout | `UNKNOWN` | Query status / webhook / reconciliation. |
