# 8. Gateway Capabilities

Tidak semua provider mendukung kemampuan yang sama.

```java id="v9xbo3"
package com.company.payment.gateway.model;

import java.util.Set;

public record GatewayCapabilities(
        String provider,
        Set<String> supportedCurrencies,
        Set<String> supportedPaymentMethodTypes,
        boolean supportsAuthorizeOnly,
        boolean supportsManualCapture,
        boolean supportsPartialCapture,
        boolean supportsMultiplePartialCapture,
        boolean supportsVoid,
        boolean supportsRefund,
        boolean supportsPartialRefund,
        boolean supportsWebhook,
        boolean supportsIdempotency,
        boolean supportsStatusQuery
) {}
```

Contoh:

```java id="feges5"
public final class StripeGatewayCapabilities {

    public static GatewayCapabilities capabilities() {
        return new GatewayCapabilities(
                "STRIPE",
                Set.of("USD", "EUR", "IDR"),
                Set.of("CARD_TOKEN", "EWALLET"),
                true,
                true,
                true,
                false,
                true,
                true,
                true,
                true,
                true,
                true
        );
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="95qoyt"
Jangan hardcode capabilities tanpa verifikasi provider dan merchant account config.
Capabilities sering tergantung country, currency, payment method, merchant setup, dan contract.
```

---

# 9. Payment Gateway Router

Router bertugas memilih provider.

```java id="0slz0a"
package com.company.payment.gateway;

import com.company.payment.gateway.model.GatewayAuthorizeRequest;

public interface PaymentGatewayRouter {

    PaymentGatewayClient routeForAuthorize(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request);

    PaymentGatewayClient routeForCapture(String provider);

    PaymentGatewayClient routeForRefund(String provider);

    PaymentGatewayClient routeForStatusQuery(String provider);
}
```

Implementasi sederhana:

```java id="06jf3l"
package com.company.payment.gateway.routing;

import com.company.payment.gateway.PaymentGatewayClient;
import com.company.payment.gateway.PaymentGatewayRouter;
import com.company.payment.gateway.model.GatewayAuthorizeRequest;

import java.util.Map;

public class ConfigBasedPaymentGatewayRouter implements PaymentGatewayRouter {

    private final Map<String, PaymentGatewayClient> clients;
    private final MerchantPaymentConfigRepository configRepository;

    public ConfigBasedPaymentGatewayRouter(
            Map<String, PaymentGatewayClient> clients,
            MerchantPaymentConfigRepository configRepository
    ) {
        this.clients = clients;
        this.configRepository = configRepository;
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentGatewayClient routeForAuthorize(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        MerchantPaymentConfig config = configRepository.findByMerchantId(request.merchantId());

        String provider = config.selectProvider(
                request.currency(),
                request.paymentMethodType(),
                request.captureImmediately()
        );

        PaymentGatewayClient client = clients.get(provider);

        if (client == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("No payment gateway client registered for provider: " + provider);
        }

        validateCapability(client, request);

        return client;
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentGatewayClient routeForCapture(String provider) {
        return getRequired(provider);
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentGatewayClient routeForRefund(String provider) {
        return getRequired(provider);
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentGatewayClient routeForStatusQuery(String provider) {
        return getRequired(provider);
    }

    private PaymentGatewayClient getRequired(String provider) {
        PaymentGatewayClient client = clients.get(provider);
        if (client == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Unknown payment provider: " + provider);
        }
        return client;
    }

    private void validateCapability(PaymentGatewayClient client, GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        var capabilities = client.capabilities();

        if (!capabilities.supportedCurrencies().contains(request.currency())) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Provider does not support currency: " + request.currency());
        }

        if (!capabilities.supportedPaymentMethodTypes().contains(request.paymentMethodType())) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Provider does not support payment method: " + request.paymentMethodType());
        }

        if (!request.captureImmediately() && !capabilities.supportsManualCapture()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Provider does not support manual capture");
        }
    }
}
```

# 10. Provider Client Skeleton

## 10.1 Common HTTP Wrapper

Jangan menyebar HTTP code di semua adapter. Buat wrapper.

```java id="5okn5u"
package com.company.payment.gateway.http;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

public interface GatewayHttpClient {

    GatewayHttpResponse post(
            String provider,
            String url,
            Map<String, String> headers,
            String body,
            Duration timeout
    );

    GatewayHttpResponse get(
            String provider,
            String url,
            Map<String, String> headers,
            Duration timeout
    );
}
```

```java id="xrpwqa"
package com.company.payment.gateway.http;

import java.util.Map;

public record GatewayHttpResponse(
        int statusCode,
        Map<String, String> headers,
        String body
) {
    public boolean is2xx() {
        return statusCode >= 200 && statusCode < 300;
    }

    public boolean is5xx() {
        return statusCode >= 500 && statusCode < 600;
    }

    public boolean is4xx() {
        return statusCode >= 400 && statusCode < 500;
    }
}
```

## 10.2 Stripe Gateway Client Skeleton

```java id="t6qc28"
package com.company.payment.gateway.stripe;

import com.company.payment.gateway.PaymentGatewayClient;
import com.company.payment.gateway.http.GatewayHttpClient;
import com.company.payment.gateway.model.*;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

public class StripeGatewayClient implements PaymentGatewayClient {

    private static final String PROVIDER = "STRIPE";

    private final GatewayHttpClient httpClient;
    private final StripeRequestMapper requestMapper;
    private final StripeResponseMapper responseMapper;
    private final StripeErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final StripeConfig config;

    public StripeGatewayClient(
            GatewayHttpClient httpClient,
            StripeRequestMapper requestMapper,
            StripeResponseMapper responseMapper,
            StripeErrorMapper errorMapper,
            StripeConfig config
    ) {
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.requestMapper = requestMapper;
        this.responseMapper = responseMapper;
        this.errorMapper = errorMapper;
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayAuthorizeResult authorize(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        String body = requestMapper.toCreatePaymentIntentPayload(request);

        var response = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.baseUrl() + "/v1/payment_intents",
                Map.of(
                        "Authorization", "Bearer " + config.apiKey(),
                        "Idempotency-Key", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
                ),
                body,
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toAuthorizeResult(response.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayCaptureResult capture(GatewayCaptureRequest request) {
        String body = requestMapper.toCapturePaymentIntentPayload(request);

        var response = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.baseUrl() + "/v1/payment_intents/" + request.providerPaymentId() + "/capture",
                Map.of(
                        "Authorization", "Bearer " + config.apiKey(),
                        "Idempotency-Key", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
                ),
                body,
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toCaptureResult(response.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayVoidResult voidAuthorization(GatewayVoidRequest request) {
        String body = requestMapper.toCancelPaymentIntentPayload(request);

        var response = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.baseUrl() + "/v1/payment_intents/" + request.providerPaymentId() + "/cancel",
                Map.of(
                        "Authorization", "Bearer " + config.apiKey(),
                        "Idempotency-Key", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
                ),
                body,
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toVoidResult(response.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayRefundResult refund(GatewayRefundRequest request) {
        String body = requestMapper.toRefundPayload(request);

        var response = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.baseUrl() + "/v1/refunds",
                Map.of(
                        "Authorization", "Bearer " + config.apiKey(),
                        "Idempotency-Key", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
                ),
                body,
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toRefundResult(response.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayPaymentStatusResult getPaymentStatus(GatewayPaymentStatusRequest request) {
        var response = httpClient.get(
                PROVIDER,
                config.baseUrl() + "/v1/payment_intents/" + request.providerPaymentId(),
                Map.of("Authorization", "Bearer " + config.apiKey()),
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toPaymentStatusResult(response.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayRefundStatusResult getRefundStatus(GatewayRefundStatusRequest request) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Not implemented yet");
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayCapabilities capabilities() {
        return StripeGatewayCapabilities.capabilities();
    }
}
```

Stripe manual capture flow uses `capture_method=manual` style behavior, where successful payment can move to `requires_capture` and capture is done later on the uncaptured PaymentIntent. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/place-a-hold-on-a-payment-method?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 10.3 Adyen Gateway Client Skeleton

Adyen uses an idempotency header named `idempotency-key`, and Adyen documents that keys are checked for uniqueness at company account scope and are valid for at least 7 days after first submission. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/development-resources/api-idempotency?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="a20avm"
package com.company.payment.gateway.adyen;

import com.company.payment.gateway.PaymentGatewayClient;
import com.company.payment.gateway.http.GatewayHttpClient;
import com.company.payment.gateway.model.*;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

public class AdyenGatewayClient implements PaymentGatewayClient {

    private static final String PROVIDER = "ADYEN";

    private final GatewayHttpClient httpClient;
    private final AdyenRequestMapper requestMapper;
    private final AdyenResponseMapper responseMapper;
    private final AdyenErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final AdyenConfig config;

    public AdyenGatewayClient(
            GatewayHttpClient httpClient,
            AdyenRequestMapper requestMapper,
            AdyenResponseMapper responseMapper,
            AdyenErrorMapper errorMapper,
            AdyenConfig config
    ) {
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.requestMapper = requestMapper;
        this.responseMapper = responseMapper;
        this.errorMapper = errorMapper;
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayAuthorizeResult authorize(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        String body = requestMapper.toPaymentsPayload(request);

        var response = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.checkoutBaseUrl() + "/v71/payments",
                Map.of(
                        "x-api-key", config.apiKey(),
                        "idempotency-key", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/json"
                ),
                body,
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toAuthorizeResult(response.body(), request.captureImmediately());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayCaptureResult capture(GatewayCaptureRequest request) {
        String body = requestMapper.toCapturePayload(request);

        var response = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.paymentsBaseUrl() + "/v71/payments/" + request.providerPaymentId() + "/captures",
                Map.of(
                        "x-api-key", config.apiKey(),
                        "idempotency-key", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/json"
                ),
                body,
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toCaptureResult(response.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayRefundResult refund(GatewayRefundRequest request) {
        String body = requestMapper.toRefundPayload(request);

        var response = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.paymentsBaseUrl() + "/v71/payments/" + request.providerPaymentId() + "/refunds",
                Map.of(
                        "x-api-key", config.apiKey(),
                        "idempotency-key", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/json"
                ),
                body,
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toRefundResult(response.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayVoidResult voidAuthorization(GatewayVoidRequest request) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Void not implemented yet for Adyen adapter");
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayPaymentStatusResult getPaymentStatus(GatewayPaymentStatusRequest request) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Status query not implemented yet for Adyen adapter");
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayRefundStatusResult getRefundStatus(GatewayRefundStatusRequest request) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Refund status query not implemented yet for Adyen adapter");
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayCapabilities capabilities() {
        return AdyenGatewayCapabilities.capabilities();
    }
}
```

Catatan penting:

```text id="1s4unq"
Jangan menganggap semua endpoint/path Adyen sama untuk semua integration type.
Adyen punya Checkout API, Payments API, Management API, Terminal API, dan konfigurasi merchant yang memengaruhi behavior.
Adapter harus dipisah per integration style bila perlu.
```

## 10.4 PayPal Gateway Client Skeleton

PayPal REST API uses OAuth 2.0 access tokens and JSON responses; for idempotent REST POST calls, PayPal uses the `PayPal-Request-Id` request header. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/api/rest/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```java id="mxs7fj"
package com.company.payment.gateway.paypal;

import com.company.payment.gateway.PaymentGatewayClient;
import com.company.payment.gateway.http.GatewayHttpClient;
import com.company.payment.gateway.model.*;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

public class PayPalGatewayClient implements PaymentGatewayClient {

    private static final String PROVIDER = "PAYPAL";

    private final GatewayHttpClient httpClient;
    private final PayPalAccessTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final PayPalRequestMapper requestMapper;
    private final PayPalResponseMapper responseMapper;
    private final PayPalErrorMapper errorMapper;
    private final PayPalConfig config;

    public PayPalGatewayClient(
            GatewayHttpClient httpClient,
            PayPalAccessTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            PayPalRequestMapper requestMapper,
            PayPalResponseMapper responseMapper,
            PayPalErrorMapper errorMapper,
            PayPalConfig config
    ) {
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.requestMapper = requestMapper;
        this.responseMapper = responseMapper;
        this.errorMapper = errorMapper;
        this.config = config;
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayAuthorizeResult authorize(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        String accessToken = tokenProvider.getAccessToken();
        String body = requestMapper.toCreateOrderPayload(request, "AUTHORIZE");

        var createOrderResponse = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.baseUrl() + "/v2/checkout/orders",
                Map.of(
                        "Authorization", "Bearer " + accessToken,
                        "PayPal-Request-Id", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/json"
                ),
                body,
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!createOrderResponse.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(createOrderResponse);
        }

        return responseMapper.toAuthorizeResult(createOrderResponse.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayCaptureResult capture(GatewayCaptureRequest request) {
        String accessToken = tokenProvider.getAccessToken();

        var response = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.baseUrl() + "/v2/payments/authorizations/"
                        + request.providerAuthorizationId()
                        + "/capture",
                Map.of(
                        "Authorization", "Bearer " + accessToken,
                        "PayPal-Request-Id", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/json"
                ),
                "{}",
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toCaptureResult(response.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayRefundResult refund(GatewayRefundRequest request) {
        String accessToken = tokenProvider.getAccessToken();
        String body = requestMapper.toRefundPayload(request);

        var response = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.baseUrl() + "/v2/payments/captures/"
                        + request.providerCaptureId()
                        + "/refund",
                Map.of(
                        "Authorization", "Bearer " + accessToken,
                        "PayPal-Request-Id", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/json"
                ),
                body,
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toRefundResult(response.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayVoidResult voidAuthorization(GatewayVoidRequest request) {
        String accessToken = tokenProvider.getAccessToken();

        var response = httpClient.post(
                PROVIDER,
                config.baseUrl() + "/v2/payments/authorizations/"
                        + request.providerAuthorizationId()
                        + "/void",
                Map.of(
                        "Authorization", "Bearer " + accessToken,
                        "PayPal-Request-Id", request.providerIdempotencyKey(),
                        "Content-Type", "application/json"
                ),
                "{}",
                Duration.ofSeconds(10)
        );

        if (!response.is2xx()) {
            throw errorMapper.toException(response);
        }

        return responseMapper.toVoidResult(response.body());
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayPaymentStatusResult getPaymentStatus(GatewayPaymentStatusRequest request) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Not implemented yet");
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayRefundStatusResult getRefundStatus(GatewayRefundStatusRequest request) {
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Not implemented yet");
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayCapabilities capabilities() {
        return PayPalGatewayCapabilities.capabilities();
    }
}
```

# 11. Request Mapper Example

## 11.1 Stripe Request Mapper

```java id="hmqbzi"
package com.company.payment.gateway.stripe;

import com.company.payment.gateway.model.GatewayAuthorizeRequest;
import com.company.payment.gateway.model.GatewayCaptureRequest;
import com.company.payment.gateway.model.GatewayRefundRequest;

import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

public class StripeRequestMapper {

    public String toCreatePaymentIntentPayload(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        Map<String, String> form = new LinkedHashMap<>();

        form.put("amount", String.valueOf(request.amountMinor()));
        form.put("currency", request.currency().toLowerCase());
        form.put("payment_method", request.paymentMethodToken());
        form.put("confirm", "true");
        form.put("capture_method", request.captureImmediately() ? "automatic" : "manual");
        form.put("metadata[payment_id]", request.paymentId().toString());
        form.put("metadata[order_id]", request.orderId());
        form.put("metadata[merchant_id]", request.merchantId());

        return toFormUrlEncoded(form);
    }

    public String toCapturePaymentIntentPayload(GatewayCaptureRequest request) {
        Map<String, String> form = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        form.put("amount_to_capture", String.valueOf(request.amountMinor()));
        return toFormUrlEncoded(form);
    }

    public String toRefundPayload(GatewayRefundRequest request) {
        Map<String, String> form = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        form.put("payment_intent", request.providerPaymentId());
        form.put("amount", String.valueOf(request.amountMinor()));
        form.put("metadata[payment_id]", request.paymentId().toString());
        form.put("metadata[refund_id]", request.refundId().toString());
        return toFormUrlEncoded(form);
    }

    public String toCancelPaymentIntentPayload(com.company.payment.gateway.model.GatewayVoidRequest request) {
        Map<String, String> form = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        form.put("cancellation_reason", "requested_by_customer");
        return toFormUrlEncoded(form);
    }

    private String toFormUrlEncoded(Map<String, String> form) {
        return form.entrySet()
                .stream()
                .map(e -> encode(e.getKey()) + "=" + encode(e.getValue()))
                .collect(Collectors.joining("&"));
    }

    private String encode(String value) {
        return URLEncoder.encode(value, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }
}
```

## 11.2 Adyen Request Mapper

```java id="xqxchc"
package com.company.payment.gateway.adyen;

import com.company.payment.gateway.model.GatewayAuthorizeRequest;
import com.company.payment.gateway.model.GatewayCaptureRequest;
import com.company.payment.gateway.model.GatewayRefundRequest;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.util.Map;

public class AdyenRequestMapper {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final AdyenConfig config;

    public AdyenRequestMapper(ObjectMapper objectMapper, AdyenConfig config) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.config = config;
    }

    public String toPaymentsPayload(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        return writeJson(Map.of(
                "merchantAccount", config.merchantAccount(),
                "reference", request.paymentId().toString(),
                "amount", Map.of(
                        "currency", request.currency(),
                        "value", request.amountMinor()
                ),
                "paymentMethod", Map.of(
                        "type", request.paymentMethodType(),
                        "storedPaymentMethodId", request.paymentMethodToken()
                ),
                "shopperReference", request.orderId(),
                "captureDelayHours", request.captureImmediately() ? 0 : 999
        ));
    }

    public String toCapturePayload(GatewayCaptureRequest request) {
        return writeJson(Map.of(
                "merchantAccount", config.merchantAccount(),
                "amount", Map.of(
                        "currency", request.currency(),
                        "value", request.amountMinor()
                ),
                "reference", request.paymentId().toString()
        ));
    }

    public String toRefundPayload(GatewayRefundRequest request) {
        return writeJson(Map.of(
                "merchantAccount", config.merchantAccount(),
                "amount", Map.of(
                        "currency", request.currency(),
                        "value", request.amountMinor()
                ),
                "reference", request.refundId().toString()
        ));
    }

    private String writeJson(Object payload) {
        try {
            return objectMapper.writeValueAsString(payload);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to serialize Adyen payload", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="oo2rlr"
Payload Adyen di atas adalah skeleton konseptual.
Dalam real implementation, field paymentMethod, shopperInteraction, returnUrl, browserInfo, recurringProcessingModel, dan storePaymentMethod sangat tergantung integration type.
```

# 12. Response Mapper Example

## 12.1 Stripe Response Mapper

```java id="ut1vp4"
package com.company.payment.gateway.stripe;

import com.company.payment.gateway.model.*;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;

public class StripeResponseMapper {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public StripeResponseMapper(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }

    public GatewayAuthorizeResult toAuthorizeResult(String body) {
        JsonNode root = read(body);

        String status = root.path("status").asText();
        GatewayPaymentStatus mappedStatus = mapPaymentIntentStatus(status);

        return new GatewayAuthorizeResult(
                "STRIPE",
                root.path("id").asText(null),
                null,
                root.path("latest_charge").asText(null),
                mappedStatus,
                root.path("amount").asLong(0),
                mappedStatus == GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED ? root.path("amount_received").asLong(0) : 0,
                root.path("currency").asText("").toUpperCase(),
                mappedStatus == GatewayPaymentStatus.REQUIRES_ACTION,
                mapNextAction(root.path("next_action")),
                status,
                root.path("request_id").asText(null),
                Map.of("rawObjectType", root.path("object").asText(null)),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    public GatewayCaptureResult toCaptureResult(String body) {
        JsonNode root = read(body);

        String status = root.path("status").asText();
        GatewayPaymentStatus mappedStatus = mapPaymentIntentStatus(status);

        return new GatewayCaptureResult(
                "STRIPE",
                root.path("id").asText(null),
                root.path("latest_charge").asText(null),
                mappedStatus,
                root.path("amount_received").asLong(0),
                root.path("currency").asText("").toUpperCase(),
                status,
                root.path("request_id").asText(null),
                Map.of(),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    public GatewayRefundResult toRefundResult(String body) {
        JsonNode root = read(body);
        String status = root.path("status").asText();

        return new GatewayRefundResult(
                "STRIPE",
                root.path("payment_intent").asText(null),
                root.path("id").asText(null),
                mapRefundStatus(status),
                root.path("amount").asLong(0),
                root.path("currency").asText("").toUpperCase(),
                status,
                root.path("request_id").asText(null),
                Map.of(),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    public GatewayVoidResult toVoidResult(String body) {
        JsonNode root = read(body);

        return new GatewayVoidResult(
                "STRIPE",
                root.path("id").asText(null),
                GatewayPaymentStatus.CANCELLED,
                root.path("status").asText(),
                Map.of(),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    public GatewayPaymentStatusResult toPaymentStatusResult(String body) {
        JsonNode root = read(body);
        String status = root.path("status").asText();

        return new GatewayPaymentStatusResult(
                "STRIPE",
                root.path("id").asText(null),
                mapPaymentIntentStatus(status),
                status,
                root.path("amount").asLong(0),
                root.path("amount_received").asLong(0),
                root.path("currency").asText("").toUpperCase(),
                Map.of()
        );
    }

    private GatewayPaymentStatus mapPaymentIntentStatus(String status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case "requires_action" -> GatewayPaymentStatus.REQUIRES_ACTION;
            case "requires_capture" -> GatewayPaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED;
            case "succeeded" -> GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED;
            case "canceled" -> GatewayPaymentStatus.CANCELLED;
            case "processing", "requires_confirmation" -> GatewayPaymentStatus.AUTHORIZATION_PENDING;
            case "requires_payment_method" -> GatewayPaymentStatus.FAILED;
            default -> GatewayPaymentStatus.UNKNOWN;
        };
    }

    private GatewayRefundStatus mapRefundStatus(String status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case "succeeded" -> GatewayRefundStatus.SUCCEEDED;
            case "failed" -> GatewayRefundStatus.FAILED;
            case "canceled" -> GatewayRefundStatus.CANCELLED;
            case "pending" -> GatewayRefundStatus.PENDING;
            default -> GatewayRefundStatus.UNKNOWN;
        };
    }

    private GatewayNextAction mapNextAction(JsonNode nextAction) {
        if (nextAction == null || nextAction.isMissingNode() || nextAction.isNull()) {
            return null;
        }

        return new GatewayNextAction(
                nextAction.path("type").asText(null),
                nextAction.path("redirect_to_url").path("url").asText(null),
                null,
                null,
                null,
                Map.of()
        );
    }

    private JsonNode read(String body) {
        try {
            return objectMapper.readTree(body);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to parse Stripe response", e);
        }
    }
}
```

# 13. Error Handling Model

Provider error jangan langsung dilempar sebagai raw message.

Buat hierarchy:

```java id="o9rb8q"
package com.company.payment.gateway.error;

public abstract class GatewayException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String provider;
    private final String providerErrorCode;
    private final String providerRequestId;
    private final boolean retryable;

    protected GatewayException(
            String message,
            String provider,
            String providerErrorCode,
            String providerRequestId,
            boolean retryable
    ) {
        super(message);
        this.provider = provider;
        this.providerErrorCode = providerErrorCode;
        this.providerRequestId = providerRequestId;
        this.retryable = retryable;
    }

    public String provider() {
        return provider;
    }

    public String providerErrorCode() {
        return providerErrorCode;
    }

    public String providerRequestId() {
        return providerRequestId;
    }

    public boolean retryable() {
        return retryable;
    }
}
```

```java id="0mfm2t"
}
```

---

# 14. Error Mapping Table

| Provider Error Type | Internal Gateway Exception | Retry? | Payment State |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Network timeout | `GatewayTimeoutException` | Ya, with same idempotency key | `UNKNOWN` / `PENDING_CONFIRMATION` |
| HTTP 502/503/504 | `GatewayUnavailableException` | Ya, limited | `UNKNOWN` / `PENDING_CONFIRMATION` |
| Rate limit | `GatewayUnavailableException` or `GatewayRateLimitException` | Ya, delayed | Keep pending |
| Card declined | `GatewayDeclinedException` | Tidak | `FAILED` |
| Insufficient funds | `GatewayDeclinedException` | Tidak langsung | `FAILED` / customer action |
| Invalid API key | `GatewayInvalidRequestException` or config exception | Tidak | Internal incident |
| Invalid payload | `GatewayInvalidRequestException` | Tidak | Internal bug/config |
| Unknown provider status | `GatewayUnknownException` | Tidak langsung | `UNKNOWN` |
| Duplicate provider request | Depends | Query status | Keep current state |

PayPal explicitly says calls that fail with network timeouts or HTTP 500 can be retried while the server stores the `PayPal-Request-Id`; this is the kind of provider behavior that adapter retry policy should encode, not scatter in domain code. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/api/rest/requests/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. Adapter Result Handling in Application Service

```java id="qvvcgh"
public PaymentResponse authorizePayment(CreatePaymentCommand command) {
    Payment payment = paymentRepository.createInitiated(command);

    String providerIdempotencyKey = providerIdempotencyKeyFactory.create(
            payment.providerCandidate(),
            environment,
            "AUTHORIZE",
            command.idempotencyKey()
    );

    GatewayAuthorizeRequest gatewayRequest = new GatewayAuthorizeRequest(
            payment.paymentId(),
            payment.orderId(),
            payment.merchantId(),
            payment.amountMinor(),
            payment.currency(),
            command.paymentMethodType(),
            command.paymentMethodToken(),
            command.captureImmediately(),
            providerIdempotencyKey,
            command.correlationId(),
            command.metadata()
    );

    PaymentGatewayClient gatewayClient = gatewayRouter.routeForAuthorize(gatewayRequest);

    try {
        GatewayAuthorizeResult result = gatewayClient.authorize(gatewayRequest);

        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            paymentRepository.applyGatewayAuthorizeResult(payment.paymentId(), result);
            paymentEventRepository.insertProviderResponseEvent(payment.paymentId(), result);

            if (result.status() == GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED) {
                ledgerRepository.insertPaymentCaptured(payment.paymentId(), result);
                outboxRepository.insertPaymentCapturedEvent(payment.paymentId(), result);
            }

            return paymentResponseMapper.fromGatewayResult(payment.paymentId(), result);
        });

    } catch (GatewayTimeoutException e) {
        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            paymentRepository.markUnknown(payment.paymentId(), e.provider(), e.providerRequestId());
            paymentEventRepository.insertGatewayTimeoutEvent(payment.paymentId(), e);
            outboxRepository.insertPaymentPendingConfirmationEvent(payment.paymentId());

            return paymentResponseMapper.pendingConfirmation(payment.paymentId(), command.correlationId());
        });

    } catch (GatewayDeclinedException e) {
        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            paymentRepository.markFailed(payment.paymentId(), e.providerErrorCode(), e.getMessage());
            paymentEventRepository.insertGatewayDeclinedEvent(payment.paymentId(), e);
            outboxRepository.insertPaymentFailedEvent(payment.paymentId());

            return paymentResponseMapper.failed(payment.paymentId(), command.correlationId());
        });
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

```text id="73gfas"
Adapter hanya mengembalikan result/error provider-neutral.
Application service yang memutuskan update DB, event, ledger, dan outbox.
```

---

# 16. Timeout and Unknown State

Timeout dari adapter harus menghasilkan exception yang jelas:

```java id="94pu72"
try {
    return gatewayClient.authorize(request);
} catch (GatewayTimeoutException e) {
    // Do not mark FAILED directly.
    // Mark UNKNOWN/PENDING_CONFIRMATION and schedule status query.
}
```

Jangan lakukan:

```java id="xgqbrs"
catch (Exception e) {
    payment.status = FAILED;
}
```

Karena timeout bisa berarti provider sudah menerima request dan payment berhasil, tetapi response hilang.

# 17. Mock Gateway Client

Mock adapter penting untuk local development, test, dan chaos simulation.

```java id="dyxid0"
package com.company.payment.gateway.mock;

import com.company.payment.gateway.PaymentGatewayClient;
import com.company.payment.gateway.model.*;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

public class MockPaymentGatewayClient implements PaymentGatewayClient {

    @Override
    public GatewayAuthorizeResult authorize(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        String scenario = String.valueOf(request.metadata().getOrDefault("mockScenario", "CAPTURED"));

        return switch (scenario) {
            case "REQUIRES_ACTION" -> requiresAction(request);
            case "AUTHORIZED" -> authorized(request);
            case "FAILED" -> failed(request);
            case "TIMEOUT" -> throw new com.company.payment.gateway.error.GatewayTimeoutException("MOCK", null);
            default -> captured(request);
        };
    }

    private GatewayAuthorizeResult captured(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        return new GatewayAuthorizeResult(
                "MOCK",
                "mock_pi_" + request.paymentId(),
                "mock_auth_" + request.paymentId(),
                "mock_charge_" + request.paymentId(),
                GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED,
                request.amountMinor(),
                request.amountMinor(),
                request.currency(),
                false,
                null,
                "mock_captured",
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                Map.of(),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    private GatewayAuthorizeResult authorized(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        return new GatewayAuthorizeResult(
                "MOCK",
                "mock_pi_" + request.paymentId(),
                "mock_auth_" + request.paymentId(),
                null,
                GatewayPaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED,
                request.amountMinor(),
                0,
                request.currency(),
                false,
                null,
                "mock_authorized",
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                Map.of(),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    private GatewayAuthorizeResult requiresAction(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        return new GatewayAuthorizeResult(
                "MOCK",
                "mock_pi_" + request.paymentId(),
                null,
                null,
                GatewayPaymentStatus.REQUIRES_ACTION,
                request.amountMinor(),
                0,
                request.currency(),
                true,
                new GatewayNextAction(
                        "REDIRECT",
                        "http://localhost:8080/mock-psp/challenge/" + request.paymentId(),
                        null,
                        null,
                        Instant.now().plusSeconds(900),
                        Map.of()
                ),
                "mock_requires_action",
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                Map.of(),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    private GatewayAuthorizeResult failed(GatewayAuthorizeRequest request) {
        return new GatewayAuthorizeResult(
                "MOCK",
                "mock_pi_" + request.paymentId(),
                null,
                null,
                GatewayPaymentStatus.FAILED,
                0,
                0,
                request.currency(),
                false,
                null,
                "mock_failed",
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                Map.of("failureCode", "MOCK_DECLINED"),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayCaptureResult capture(GatewayCaptureRequest request) {
        return new GatewayCaptureResult(
                "MOCK",
                request.providerPaymentId(),
                "mock_capture_" + request.paymentId(),
                GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED,
                request.amountMinor(),
                request.currency(),
                "mock_capture_succeeded",
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                Map.of(),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayRefundResult refund(GatewayRefundRequest request) {
        return new GatewayRefundResult(
                "MOCK",
                request.providerPaymentId(),
                "mock_refund_" + request.refundId(),
                GatewayRefundStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                request.amountMinor(),
                request.currency(),
                "mock_refund_succeeded",
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                Map.of(),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayVoidResult voidAuthorization(GatewayVoidRequest request) {
        return new GatewayVoidResult(
                "MOCK",
                request.providerPaymentId(),
                GatewayPaymentStatus.CANCELLED,
                "mock_voided",
                Map.of(),
                Instant.now()
        );
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayPaymentStatusResult getPaymentStatus(GatewayPaymentStatusRequest request) {
        return new GatewayPaymentStatusResult(
                "MOCK",
                request.providerPaymentId(),
                GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED,
                "mock_captured",
                1000,
                1000,
                "IDR",
                Map.of()
        );
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayRefundStatusResult getRefundStatus(GatewayRefundStatusRequest request) {
        return new GatewayRefundStatusResult(
                "MOCK",
                request.providerRefundId(),
                GatewayRefundStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                "mock_refund_succeeded",
                Map.of()
        );
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayCapabilities capabilities() {
        return new GatewayCapabilities(
                "MOCK",
                Set.of("IDR", "USD", "EUR"),
                Set.of("CARD_TOKEN", "BANK_TRANSFER", "EWALLET", "QRIS"),
                true,
                true,
                true,
                true,
                true,
                true,
                true,
                true,
                true,
                true
        );
    }
}
```

# 18. Provider Configuration

Jangan hardcode secret/config di code.

```java id="c3axks"
package com.company.payment.gateway.config;

public record ProviderConfig(
        String provider,
        String environment,
        String baseUrl,
        String apiKeySecretRef,
        String merchantAccount,
        int connectTimeoutMillis,
        int readTimeoutMillis,
        int maxRetryAttempts,
        boolean enabled
) {}
```

```yaml id="ko5ece"
payment:

      enabled: true

      connect-timeout-millis: 2000
```

# 20. Adapter Testing Strategy

## 20.1 Unit Test: Status Mapper

```java id="lrqt3h"
@Test
void stripeRequiresCaptureMapsToAuthorized() {
    StripeResponseMapper mapper = new StripeResponseMapper(new ObjectMapper());

    GatewayPaymentStatus status = mapper.mapPaymentIntentStatusForTest("requires_capture");

    assertThat(status).isEqualTo(GatewayPaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED);
}
```

## 20.2 Fixture-based Response Test

```text id="w53up6"
src/test/resources/provider-fixtures/
  stripe/
    payment_intent_succeeded.json

    payment_intent_requires_capture.json
    payment_intent_canceled.json
```

Test:

```java id="knd5g0"
@Test
void stripeSucceededFixtureMapsToCaptured() throws Exception {
    String json = Files.readString(Path.of(
            "src/test/resources/provider-fixtures/stripe/payment_intent_succeeded.json"
    ));

    GatewayAuthorizeResult result = mapper.toAuthorizeResult(json);

    assertThat(result.status()).isEqualTo(GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED);
    assertThat(result.providerPaymentId()).isNotBlank();
}
```

## 20.3 HTTP Contract Test

Gunakan mock server seperti WireMock atau mock HTTP server:

```text id="qvyra2"
1. Assert adapter sends correct idempotency header.
2. Assert adapter sends correct authorization header.
3. Assert timeout maps to GatewayTimeoutException.
4. Assert 5xx maps to GatewayUnavailableException.
5. Assert provider decline maps to GatewayDeclinedException.
6. Assert unknown status maps to UNKNOWN, not FAILED.
```

## 20.4 Integration Sandbox Test

```text id="sp1lxu"
1. Create sandbox payment.
2. Manual capture flow.
3. Refund flow.
4. Provider timeout simulation, if available.
5. Webhook replay.
6. Invalid API key.
7. Unsupported currency.
```

---

# 21. Practical Failure Scenarios

| Scenario | Expected Adapter Behavior | Expected Application Behavior |
|---|---|---|
| Provider returns success captured | Map to `GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED` | Update payment `CAPTURED`, insert ledger/outbox. |
| Provider requires action | Map to `REQUIRES_ACTION` + `nextAction` | Return action to consumer. |
| Provider manual auth success | Map to `AUTHORIZED` | Wait capture command. |
| Provider 3DS pending | Map to `REQUIRES_ACTION` / `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | Wait callback/webhook. |
| Provider timeout | Throw `GatewayTimeoutException` | Mark `UNKNOWN` / pending confirmation. |
| Provider 503 | Throw retryable exception | Retry limited or mark pending. |
| Provider decline | Throw `GatewayDeclinedException` or result `FAILED` | Mark payment `FAILED`. |
| Provider unknown status | Map `UNKNOWN` | Alert + status query. |
| Provider duplicate request response | Map by provider semantics | Query status if needed. |
| Provider response missing required fields | Throw mapping exception | Mark attempt failed/internal error, not payment failed blindly. |

---

# 22. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Domain service build raw Stripe/Adyen payload | Domain jadi vendor-coupled. | Provider-specific mapper. |
| Provider status disimpan langsung sebagai internal status | Multi-provider sulit. | Explicit status mapping. |
| Semua provider error dianggap `FAILED` | Timeout/5xx bisa sebenarnya sukses. | Error hierarchy + unknown state. |
| Idempotency key tidak dikirim ke provider | Retry internal bisa tetap duplicate di PSP. | Derived provider idempotency key. |
| Provider router hardcode di `if/else` besar | Sulit maintain saat provider bertambah. | Registry/map-based router. |
| Tidak punya mock PSP | Testing lambat dan rapuh. | `MockPaymentGatewayClient`. |
| Tidak menyimpan raw provider status | Debugging sulit. | Simpan `rawProviderStatus` di attempt/event. |
| Tidak menyimpan provider request id | Sulit support ke PSP. | Simpan provider request/reference ID. |
| Adapter update database langsung | Responsibility bocor. | Adapter hanya return result; app service update DB. |
| Unknown provider status dianggap failed | Bisa salah secara finansial. | Map ke `UNKNOWN` dan alert. |

---

# 23. Implementation Checklist

## Interface

- [ ] Ada `PaymentGatewayClient`.
- [ ] Ada method `authorize`, `capture`, `voidAuthorization`, `refund`, `getPaymentStatus`.
- [ ] Ada `GatewayCapabilities`.
- [ ] Tidak ada class provider-specific di domain layer.

## Request / Response

- [ ] Request model provider-neutral.
- [ ] Result model provider-neutral.
- [ ] Semua result menyimpan `rawProviderStatus`.
- [ ] Semua result menyimpan provider reference.
- [ ] Response mapper punya fallback `UNKNOWN`.

## Error

- [ ] Timeout tidak jadi `FAILED`.
- [ ] 5xx provider dianggap retryable.
- [ ] Decline dianggap non-retryable.
- [ ] Invalid config/API key jadi incident.
- [ ] Unknown error tidak silently swallowed.

## Idempotency

- [ ] Provider idempotency key dikirim untuk mutating call.
- [ ] Provider key derived dari internal key.
- [ ] Retry provider memakai key yang sama.
- [ ] Header provider sesuai provider: Stripe `Idempotency-Key`, Adyen `idempotency-key`, PayPal `PayPal-Request-Id`.

## Routing

- [ ] Routing berdasarkan merchant/currency/payment method.
- [ ] Capability dicek sebelum provider call.
- [ ] Provider disabled tidak boleh dipilih.
- [ ] Capture/refund memakai provider yang sama dengan original payment.

## Testing

- [ ] Fixture test per provider status.
- [ ] HTTP contract test header/payload.
- [ ] Timeout test.
- [ ] Decline test.
- [ ] Unknown status test.
- [ ] Mock provider untuk local dev.

```text id="aa31ge"
  -> GatewayAuthorizeResult
  -> update payments/payment_attempts/payment_events
```

Test minimal:

```text id="gkvzk6"
1. mockScenario=CAPTURED -> payment CAPTURED.
2. mockScenario=AUTHORIZED -> payment AUTHORIZED.
3. mockScenario=REQUIRES_ACTION -> payment REQUIRES_ACTION + nextAction.
4. mockScenario=FAILED -> payment FAILED.
5. mockScenario=TIMEOUT -> payment UNKNOWN.
6. duplicate retry uses same provider idempotency key.
7. unknown raw provider status maps to UNKNOWN.
```

# 25. Ringkasan

Payment Gateway Adapter Pattern membuat payment service tetap sehat karena:

```text id="h6b6gd"
1. Domain tidak tercemar vendor payload.
2. Provider bisa diganti/ditambah tanpa rewrite payment logic.
3. Status mapping eksplisit.
4. Error handling konsisten.
5. Retry dan idempotency bisa distandarkan.
6. Mock/sandbox testing lebih mudah.
7. Multi-provider routing lebih aman.
```

Prinsip paling penting:

```text id="uep7wi"
Adapter tidak boleh menentukan business truth sendirian.
Adapter hanya menerjemahkan request/response provider.
PaymentApplicationService tetap menjadi pemilik state transition, event, ledger, dan outbox.
```

## Response:
7/14/2026, 10:30:47 PM

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 6/18 - Multi-Provider Routing dan Provider Abstraction

Seri ini fokus pada **cara memilih payment provider secara deterministic** dalam payment service berbasis **Java Jersey/JAX-RS + PostgreSQL/MyBatis**.

Masalah yang ingin diselesaikan:

```text
Untuk payment request tertentu, provider mana yang harus dipakai?

Stripe?
Adyen?
PayPal?
Bank transfer provider lokal?
Virtual account provider?
QRIS provider?
Mock provider?
Fallback provider?
```

Routing payment tidak boleh asal `if currency == IDR then provider A`. Dalam production, pemilihan provider harus mempertimbangkan:

- merchant,
- country,
- currency,
- payment method,
- capture mode,
- provider capability,
- provider health,
- contract/commercial rule,
- risk/fraud rule,
- settlement preference,
- failover policy,
- deterministic repeatability.

Provider juga punya cakupan dan constraint berbeda. Stripe mendokumentasikan kategori payment method seperti cards, bank debits, bank redirects, bank transfers, BNPL, real-time payments, vouchers, dan wallets; Stripe juga menyediakan daftar supported currencies dan aturan formatting/limit per currency. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/payment-methods/overview?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen menyediakan halaman payment methods untuk online integration seperti Drop-in, Components, dan API-only, sedangkan PayPal REST API memakai OAuth 2.0 access token dan JSON response. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/payment-methods?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Core Principle

```text
Payment routing harus deterministic untuk satu logical payment operation.
```

Artinya:

```text
Create payment pakai provider A.
Capture harus tetap ke provider A.
Refund harus tetap ke provider A.
Webhook provider A harus resolve ke payment yang dibuat lewat provider A.
Reconciliation juga harus memakai reference provider A.
```

Routing hanya boleh terjadi pada fase awal seperti:

```text
CREATE_PAYMENT / AUTHORIZE / SALE
```

Setelah payment punya provider reference, operasi berikutnya harus mengikuti provider original.

---

# 2. Diagram

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Payment API - Jersey/JAX-RS] --> B[PaymentApplicationService]

    B --> C[PaymentRoutingService]
    C --> D[Merchant Payment Config]
    C --> E[Provider Capability Registry]
    C --> F[Provider Health Snapshot]
    C --> G[Routing Rules]
    C --> H[Risk / Business Rules]

    D --> I[(PostgreSQL)]
    E --> I
    F --> I
    G --> I

    C --> J{Selected Provider}

    J --> K[Stripe Adapter]
    J --> L[Adyen Adapter]
    J --> M[PayPal Adapter]
    J --> N[Local Bank / VA / QRIS Adapter]
    J --> O[Mock Adapter]

    K --> P[External PSP]
    L --> P
    M --> P
    N --> P

    B --> Q[Persist Routing Decision]
    Q --> R[(payments.provider)]
    Q --> S[(payment_attempts.provider)]
    Q --> T[(provider_references)]
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Provider Registry** | Daftar provider yang tersedia dan enabled. | `PaymentProviderRegistry` |
| 2 | **Provider Capability** | Fitur provider: currency, method, auth/capture/refund. | `GatewayCapabilities` |
| 3 | **Merchant Config** | Provider mana yang boleh dipakai merchant tertentu. | `merchant_payment_configs` |
| 4 | **Routing Rule** | Rule pemilihan provider berdasarkan condition. | `payment_routing_rules` |
| 5 | **Deterministic Routing** | Satu payment operation harus memilih provider secara repeatable. | `PaymentRoutingDecision` |
| 6 | **Provider Health** | Hindari provider yang down/degraded. | `provider_health_status` |
| 7 | **Fallback Policy** | Kapan boleh fallback ke provider lain. | `fallback_allowed`, `fallback_strategy` |
| 8 | **Provider Stickiness** | Capture/refund/status query harus pakai provider original. | `payments.provider` |
| 9 | **Routing Audit** | Kenapa provider X dipilih. | `payment_routing_decisions` |
| 10 | **Config Versioning** | Routing rule berubah tanpa merusak payment lama. | `config_version` |
| 11 | **Risk-Aware Routing** | Routing berdasarkan risk, country, BIN, fraud score. | `RoutingContext` |
| 12 | **Commercial Routing** | Routing berdasarkan fee, settlement, FX, contract. | priority/scoring rule |
| 13 | **Testing & Simulation** | Simulasi rule tanpa real PSP call. | routing dry-run endpoint/test |
| 14 | **Operational Override** | Disable provider, force provider, maintenance mode. | admin config/feature flag |
| 15 | **Observability** | Metric success/decline/latency per provider. | dashboard + alert |

---

# 4. Routing Input Model

Routing tidak boleh hanya menerima `currency`. Minimal perlu context.

```java
package com.company.payment.routing;

import java.util.Map;

public record PaymentRoutingContext(
        String merchantId,
        String customerId,
        String orderId,

        long amountMinor,
        String currency,

        String paymentMethodType,
        String customerCountry,
        String billingCountry,
        String issuerCountry,

        boolean captureImmediately,
        boolean recurring,
        boolean offSession,

        String channel,
        String environment,

        Integer riskScore,
        Map<String, Object> metadata
) {}
```

Contoh context:

```json
{
  "merchantId": "MRC-001",
  "orderId": "ORD-2026-1001",
  "amountMinor": 250000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "paymentMethodType": "CARD_TOKEN",
  "customerCountry": "ID",
  "billingCountry": "ID",
  "captureImmediately": true,
  "channel": "WEB",
  "environment": "PROD",
  "riskScore": 42
}
```

# 5. Routing Decision Model

Routing decision harus disimpan, bukan hanya dihitung lalu dilupakan.

```java
package com.company.payment.routing;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public record PaymentRoutingDecision(
        UUID decisionId,
        UUID paymentId,

        String selectedProvider,
        String selectedProviderAccount,

        String routingRuleId,
        String routingRuleVersion,

        String decisionReason,
        List<String> evaluatedProviders,
        List<String> rejectedProviders,

        boolean fallbackUsed,
        String fallbackFromProvider,

        Map<String, Object> decisionAttributes,

        Instant decidedAt
) {}
```

Kenapa perlu disimpan?

```text
1. Debugging: kenapa payment ini masuk provider A?
2. Audit: rule apa yang aktif saat payment dibuat?
3. Reconciliation: provider original harus diketahui.
4. Incident: apakah payment gagal karena routing buruk?
5. Rollback: perubahan rule baru tidak boleh mengubah payment lama.
```

---

# 6. Database Schema

## 6.1 `payment_providers`

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_providers (
    provider_code VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY,

    display_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    provider_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
    environment VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,

    supports_authorize BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    supports_capture BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    supports_partial_capture BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    supports_void BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    supports_refund BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    supports_partial_refund BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    supports_webhook BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    supports_status_query BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    supports_idempotency BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_providers_type
        CHECK (provider_type IN (
            'CARD_PROCESSOR',
            'WALLET',
            'BANK_TRANSFER',
            'VIRTUAL_ACCOUNT',
            'QR_PAYMENT',
            'PAYPAL',
            'MOCK'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_providers_environment
        CHECK (environment IN ('LOCAL', 'SANDBOX', 'STAGING', 'PROD'))
);
```

---

## 6.2 `provider_supported_currencies`

```sql
CREATE TABLE provider_supported_currencies (
    provider_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL
        REFERENCES payment_providers(provider_code),

    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    min_amount_minor BIGINT,
    max_amount_minor BIGINT,

    enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (provider_code, currency),

    CONSTRAINT chk_provider_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency))
);
```

---

## 6.3 `provider_supported_payment_methods`

```sql
CREATE TABLE provider_supported_payment_methods (
    provider_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL
        REFERENCES payment_providers(provider_code),

    payment_method_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    country_code CHAR(2),
    currency CHAR(3),

    enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (
        provider_code,
        payment_method_type,
        country_code,
        currency
    )
);
```

Catatan praktis:

```text
Provider capability bisa berbeda berdasarkan country/currency/method.
Jangan hanya simpan satu boolean global: supports_card = true.
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE merchant_payment_configs (
    merchant_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    provider_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL
        REFERENCES payment_providers(provider_code),

    provider_account_id VARCHAR(200),

    priority INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
    enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,

    settlement_currency CHAR(3),
    merchant_country CHAR(2),

    config_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (merchant_id, provider_code, config_version)
);
```

## 6.5 `payment_routing_rules`

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_routing_rules (
    routing_rule_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    rule_name VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    rule_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

    merchant_id VARCHAR(100),
    provider_code VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL
        REFERENCES payment_providers(provider_code),

    payment_method_type VARCHAR(50),
    currency CHAR(3),
    customer_country CHAR(2),
    min_amount_minor BIGINT,
    max_amount_minor BIGINT,

    capture_mode VARCHAR(30),
    channel VARCHAR(50),

    priority INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
    enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,

    fallback_allowed BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,

    valid_from TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    valid_until TIMESTAMPTZ,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_routing_rule_capture_mode
        CHECK (
            capture_mode IS NULL
            OR capture_mode IN ('AUTO', 'MANUAL')
        )
);
```

```sql
ON payment_routing_rules (

    merchant_id,

ON payment_routing_rules(valid_from, valid_until);
```

```sql
ON payment_routing_decisions(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_routing_decisions_provider
ON payment_routing_decisions(selected_provider);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_routing_decisions_decided_at
ON payment_routing_decisions(decided_at);
```

## 6.7 `provider_health_status`

```sql
CREATE TABLE provider_health_status (
    provider_code VARCHAR(50) PRIMARY KEY
        REFERENCES payment_providers(provider_code),

    health_status VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,

    last_success_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    last_failure_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    recent_success_rate NUMERIC(6, 3),
    recent_timeout_rate NUMERIC(6, 3),
    recent_p95_latency_ms INTEGER,

    manually_disabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    disabled_reason TEXT,

    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_provider_health_status
        CHECK (health_status IN (
            'HEALTHY',
            'DEGRADED',
            'UNHEALTHY',
            'MAINTENANCE',
            'UNKNOWN'
        ))
);
```

# 7. Routing Algorithm

## 7.1 High-Level Flow

```text
1. Build PaymentRoutingContext.
2. Load merchant provider config.
3. Load enabled provider capabilities.
4. Filter provider by:
   - enabled
   - environment
   - merchant allowed provider
   - currency
   - payment method
   - country
   - amount range
   - capture capability
   - refund/capture support if needed
5. Check provider health.
6. Apply routing rules by priority.
7. Apply tie-breaker deterministically.
8. Persist routing decision.
9. Use selected provider adapter.
```

---

## 7.2 Routing Decision Tree

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[PaymentRoutingContext] --> B[Find Merchant Config]
    B --> C{Merchant Has Enabled Providers?}

    C -->|No| Z[Routing Failed]

    C -->|Yes| D[Filter by Provider Enabled]
    D --> E[Filter by Currency]
    E --> F[Filter by Payment Method]
    F --> G[Filter by Country]
    G --> H[Filter by Amount Limit]
    H --> I[Filter by Capture Mode Capability]
    I --> J[Filter by Health / Maintenance]

    J --> K{Candidates Left?}
    K -->|No| L[Try Fallback Policy]
    L --> M{Fallback Allowed?}
    M -->|No| Z
    M -->|Yes| N[Select Fallback Provider]

    K -->|Yes| O[Apply Routing Rules]
    O --> P[Sort by Priority]
    P --> Q[Stable Tie-Breaker]
    Q --> R[Persist Decision]
    N --> R
    R --> S[Return Selected Provider]
```

---

# 8. Java Model: Capability

```java
package com.company.payment.routing;

import java.util.Set;

public record ProviderCapability(
        String providerCode,

        Set<String> supportedCurrencies,
        Set<String> supportedPaymentMethodTypes,
        Set<String> supportedCountries,

        boolean supportsAuthorize,
        boolean supportsCapture,
        boolean supportsPartialCapture,
        boolean supportsVoid,
        boolean supportsRefund,
        boolean supportsPartialRefund,
        boolean supportsWebhook,
        boolean supportsStatusQuery,
        boolean supportsIdempotency,

        boolean enabled
) {
    public boolean supportsCurrency(String currency) {
        return supportedCurrencies.contains(currency);
    }

    public boolean supportsPaymentMethod(String paymentMethodType) {
        return supportedPaymentMethodTypes.contains(paymentMethodType);
    }

    public boolean supportsCountry(String country) {
        return country == null || supportedCountries.isEmpty() || supportedCountries.contains(country);
    }
}
```

---

# 9. Java Model: Provider Health

```java
package com.company.payment.routing;

public record ProviderHealth(
        String providerCode,
        ProviderHealthStatus status,
        boolean manuallyDisabled,
        double recentSuccessRate,
        double recentTimeoutRate,
        Integer recentP95LatencyMs
) {
    public boolean canAcceptNewPayment() {
        return !manuallyDisabled
                && (status == ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
                || status == ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED);
    }
}
```

```java
package com.company.payment.routing;

public enum ProviderHealthStatus {
    HEALTHY,
    DEGRADED,
    UNHEALTHY,
    MAINTENANCE,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

Practical rule:

```text
DEGRADED boleh menerima traffic terbatas.
UNHEALTHY dan MAINTENANCE tidak boleh menerima payment baru.
Capture/refund existing payment tetap perlu policy khusus karena harus ke provider original.
```
