# 10. Routing Service Interface

```java
package com.company.payment.routing;

import java.util.UUID;

public interface PaymentRoutingService {

    PaymentRoutingDecision selectProviderForCreatePayment(
            UUID paymentId,
            PaymentRoutingContext context
    );

    String providerForExistingPayment(UUID paymentId);

    PaymentRoutingDecision dryRun(PaymentRoutingContext context);
}
```

`dryRun` berguna untuk:

```text
1. Testing rule baru.
2. Admin console.
3. Validasi config sebelum deploy.
4. Incident investigation.
```

---

# 11. Routing Service Implementation

```java
package com.company.payment.routing;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

public class DefaultPaymentRoutingService implements PaymentRoutingService {

    private final MerchantPaymentConfigRepository merchantConfigRepository;
    private final ProviderCapabilityRepository capabilityRepository;
    private final ProviderHealthRepository healthRepository;
    private final PaymentRoutingRuleRepository ruleRepository;
    private final PaymentRoutingDecisionRepository decisionRepository;

    public DefaultPaymentRoutingService(
            MerchantPaymentConfigRepository merchantConfigRepository,
            ProviderCapabilityRepository capabilityRepository,
            ProviderHealthRepository healthRepository,
            PaymentRoutingRuleRepository ruleRepository,
            PaymentRoutingDecisionRepository decisionRepository
    ) {
        this.merchantConfigRepository = merchantConfigRepository;
        this.capabilityRepository = capabilityRepository;
        this.healthRepository = healthRepository;
        this.ruleRepository = ruleRepository;
        this.decisionRepository = decisionRepository;
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentRoutingDecision selectProviderForCreatePayment(
            UUID paymentId,
            PaymentRoutingContext context
    ) {
        List<MerchantProviderConfig> merchantProviders =
                merchantConfigRepository.findEnabledByMerchantId(context.merchantId());

        if (merchantProviders.isEmpty()) {
            throw new PaymentRoutingException(
                    "NO_MERCHANT_PROVIDER_CONFIG",
                    "No enabled payment provider configured for merchant"
            );
        }

        List<ProviderCandidate> candidates = merchantProviders.stream()
                .map(config -> buildCandidate(config, context))
                .filter(candidate -> candidate.isProviderEnabled())
                .filter(candidate -> candidate.supportsCurrency(context.currency()))
                .filter(candidate -> candidate.supportsPaymentMethod(context.paymentMethodType()))
                .filter(candidate -> candidate.supportsCountry(context.customerCountry()))
                .filter(candidate -> candidate.supportsCaptureMode(context.captureImmediately()))
                .filter(candidate -> candidate.isAmountWithinLimit(context.amountMinor()))
                .filter(candidate -> candidate.canAcceptNewPayment())
                .toList();

        if (candidates.isEmpty()) {
            throw new PaymentRoutingException(
                    "NO_ELIGIBLE_PROVIDER",
                    "No eligible payment provider found for this payment context"
            );
        }

        List<PaymentRoutingRule> matchingRules =
                ruleRepository.findMatchingRules(context);

        ProviderCandidate selected = selectByRuleAndPriority(candidates, matchingRules, context);

        PaymentRoutingDecision decision = new PaymentRoutingDecision(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                paymentId,
                selected.providerCode(),
                selected.providerAccountId(),
                selected.matchedRuleId(),
                selected.matchedRuleVersion(),
                selected.decisionReason(),
                candidates.stream().map(ProviderCandidate::providerCode).toList(),
                List.of(),
                false,
                null,
                selected.decisionAttributes(),
                Instant.now()
        );

        decisionRepository.insert(decision);

        return decision;
    }

    @Override
    public String providerForExistingPayment(UUID paymentId) {
        return decisionRepository.findByPaymentId(paymentId)
                .map(PaymentRoutingDecision::selectedProvider)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new PaymentRoutingException(
                        "ROUTING_DECISION_NOT_FOUND",
                        "Routing decision not found for payment"
                ));
    }

    @Override
    public PaymentRoutingDecision dryRun(PaymentRoutingContext context) {
        UUID fakePaymentId = UUID.randomUUID();

        /*
         * In real implementation, do not insert decision.
         * Return computed decision only.
         */
        throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Implement dry-run without persistence");
    }

    private ProviderCandidate buildCandidate(
            MerchantProviderConfig config,
            PaymentRoutingContext context
    ) {
        ProviderCapability capability =
                capabilityRepository.findByProviderCode(config.providerCode());

        ProviderHealth health =
                healthRepository.findByProviderCode(config.providerCode());

        return ProviderCandidate.from(config, capability, health, context);
    }

    private ProviderCandidate selectByRuleAndPriority(
            List<ProviderCandidate> candidates,
            List<PaymentRoutingRule> rules,
            PaymentRoutingContext context
    ) {
        for (PaymentRoutingRule rule : rules.stream()
                .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(PaymentRoutingRule::priority))
                .toList()) {

            for (ProviderCandidate candidate : candidates) {
                if (candidate.providerCode().equals(rule.providerCode())) {
                    return candidate.withMatchedRule(rule);
                }
            }
        }

        return candidates.stream()
                .sorted(
                        Comparator
                                .comparingInt(ProviderCandidate::merchantPriority)
                                .thenComparing(ProviderCandidate::providerCode)
                )
                .findFirst()
                .orElseThrow();
    }
}
```

---

# 12. Provider Candidate

```java
package com.company.payment.routing;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public record ProviderCandidate(
        String providerCode,
        String providerAccountId,

        int merchantPriority,

        ProviderCapability capability,
        ProviderHealth health,

        String matchedRuleId,
        Integer matchedRuleVersion,
        String decisionReason,

        Long minAmountMinor,
        Long maxAmountMinor,

        Map<String, Object> decisionAttributes
) {
    public static ProviderCandidate from(
            MerchantProviderConfig config,
            ProviderCapability capability,
            ProviderHealth health,
            PaymentRoutingContext context
    ) {
        return new ProviderCandidate(
                config.providerCode(),
                config.providerAccountId(),
                config.priority(),
                capability,
                health,
                null,
                null,
                "Selected by merchant priority and capability match",
                config.minAmountMinor(),
                config.maxAmountMinor(),
                Map.of(
                        "merchantId", context.merchantId(),
                        "currency", context.currency(),
                        "paymentMethodType", context.paymentMethodType()
                )
        );
    }

    public boolean isProviderEnabled() {
        return capability.enabled();
    }

    public boolean supportsCurrency(String currency) {
        return capability.supportsCurrency(currency);
    }

    public boolean supportsPaymentMethod(String paymentMethodType) {
        return capability.supportsPaymentMethod(paymentMethodType);
    }

    public boolean supportsCountry(String country) {
        return capability.supportsCountry(country);
    }

    public boolean supportsCaptureMode(boolean captureImmediately) {
        if (captureImmediately) {
            return capability.supportsCapture();
        }
        return capability.supportsAuthorize() && capability.supportsCapture();
    }

    public boolean isAmountWithinLimit(long amountMinor) {
        if (minAmountMinor != null && amountMinor < minAmountMinor) {
            return false;
        }
        if (maxAmountMinor != null && amountMinor > maxAmountMinor) {
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    public boolean canAcceptNewPayment() {
        return health.canAcceptNewPayment();
    }

    public ProviderCandidate withMatchedRule(PaymentRoutingRule rule) {
        return new ProviderCandidate(
                providerCode,
                providerAccountId,
                merchantPriority,
                capability,
                health,
                rule.routingRuleId().toString(),
                rule.ruleVersion(),
                "Selected by routing rule: " + rule.ruleName(),
                minAmountMinor,
                maxAmountMinor,
                decisionAttributes
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 13. Routing Rule Matching

```java
package com.company.payment.routing;

import java.util.UUID;

public record PaymentRoutingRule(
        UUID routingRuleId,
        String ruleName,
        int ruleVersion,

        String merchantId,
        String providerCode,
        String paymentMethodType,
        String currency,
        String customerCountry,

        Long minAmountMinor,
        Long maxAmountMinor,

        String captureMode,
        String channel,

        int priority,
        boolean fallbackAllowed
) {
    public boolean matches(PaymentRoutingContext context) {
        return matchesNullable(merchantId, context.merchantId())
                && matchesNullable(paymentMethodType, context.paymentMethodType())
                && matchesNullable(currency, context.currency())
                && matchesNullable(customerCountry, context.customerCountry())
                && matchesAmount(context.amountMinor())
                && matchesCaptureMode(context.captureImmediately())
                && matchesNullable(channel, context.channel());
    }

    private boolean matchesNullable(String ruleValue, String actualValue) {
        return ruleValue == null || ruleValue.equals(actualValue);
    }

    private boolean matchesAmount(long amountMinor) {
        if (minAmountMinor != null && amountMinor < minAmountMinor) {
            return false;
        }
        if (maxAmountMinor != null && amountMinor > maxAmountMinor) {
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    private boolean matchesCaptureMode(boolean captureImmediately) {
        if (captureMode == null) {
            return true;
        }

        String actual = captureImmediately ? "AUTO" : "MANUAL";
        return captureMode.equals(actual);
    }
}
```

---

# 14. MyBatis Mapper

## 14.1 Routing Rule Query

```xml
<select id="findMatchingRules" resultMap="PaymentRoutingRuleResultMap">
    SELECT
        routing_rule_id,
        rule_name,
        rule_version,
        merchant_id,
        provider_code,
        payment_method_type,
        currency,
        customer_country,
        min_amount_minor,
        max_amount_minor,
        capture_mode,
        channel,
        priority,
        fallback_allowed
    FROM payment_routing_rules
    WHERE enabled = true
      AND valid_from <= now()
      AND (valid_until IS NULL OR valid_until > now())

      AND (merchant_id IS NULL OR merchant_id = #{merchantId})
      AND (payment_method_type IS NULL OR payment_method_type = #{paymentMethodType})
      AND (currency IS NULL OR currency = #{currency})
      AND (customer_country IS NULL OR customer_country = #{customerCountry})
      AND (channel IS NULL OR channel = #{channel})

      AND (min_amount_minor IS NULL OR min_amount_minor <= #{amountMinor})
      AND (max_amount_minor IS NULL OR max_amount_minor >= #{amountMinor})

      AND (
          capture_mode IS NULL
          OR capture_mode = CASE
              WHEN #{captureImmediately} = true THEN 'AUTO'
              ELSE 'MANUAL'
          END
      )
    ORDER BY priority ASC, rule_name ASC
</select>
```

---

## 14.2 Insert Routing Decision

```xml
<insert id="insertRoutingDecision">
    INSERT INTO payment_routing_decisions (
        decision_id,
        payment_id,
        selected_provider,
        selected_provider_account,
        routing_rule_id,
        routing_rule_version,
        decision_reason,
        evaluated_providers,
        rejected_providers,
        decision_attributes,
        fallback_used,
        fallback_from_provider,
        decided_at
    ) VALUES (
        #{decisionId},
        #{paymentId},
        #{selectedProvider},
        #{selectedProviderAccount},
        #{routingRuleId},
        #{routingRuleVersion},
        #{decisionReason},
        CAST(#{evaluatedProvidersJson} AS jsonb),
        CAST(#{rejectedProvidersJson} AS jsonb),
        CAST(#{decisionAttributesJson} AS jsonb),
        #{fallbackUsed},
        #{fallbackFromProvider},
        #{decidedAt}
    )
</insert>
```

---

## 14.3 Existing Payment Provider Lookup

```xml
<select id="findProviderByPaymentId" resultType="string">
    SELECT selected_provider
    FROM payment_routing_decisions
    WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
</select>
```

Capture/refund/status query harus memakai lookup ini, bukan routing ulang.

# 15. Payment Application Service Integration

## 15.1 Create Payment

```java
public PaymentResponse createPayment(CreatePaymentCommand command) {
    UUID paymentId = UUID.randomUUID();

    PaymentRoutingContext routingContext = new PaymentRoutingContext(
            command.merchantId(),
            command.customerId(),
            command.orderId(),
            command.amountMinor(),
            command.currency(),
            command.paymentMethodType(),
            command.customerCountry(),
            command.billingCountry(),
            command.issuerCountry(),
            command.captureImmediately(),
            command.recurring(),
            command.offSession(),
            command.channel(),
            command.environment(),
            command.riskScore(),
            command.metadata()
    );

    PaymentRoutingDecision routingDecision =
            routingService.selectProviderForCreatePayment(paymentId, routingContext);

    PaymentGatewayClient gatewayClient =
            gatewayRegistry.getRequired(routingDecision.selectedProvider());

    paymentRepository.insertInitiatedPayment(
            paymentId,
            command,
            routingDecision.selectedProvider(),
            routingDecision.selectedProviderAccount()
    );

    GatewayAuthorizeRequest gatewayRequest = gatewayRequestFactory.createAuthorizeRequest(
            paymentId,
            command,
            routingDecision
    );

    GatewayAuthorizeResult result = gatewayClient.authorize(gatewayRequest);

    return paymentResultApplier.applyAuthorizeResult(paymentId, result);
}
```

---

## 15.2 Capture Existing Payment

```java
public PaymentResponse capturePayment(CapturePaymentCommand command) {
    Payment payment = paymentRepository.findByIdForUpdate(command.paymentId());

    String provider = routingService.providerForExistingPayment(command.paymentId());

    PaymentGatewayClient gatewayClient = gatewayRegistry.getRequired(provider);

    GatewayCaptureRequest gatewayRequest = gatewayRequestFactory.createCaptureRequest(
            payment,
            command
    );

    GatewayCaptureResult result = gatewayClient.capture(gatewayRequest);

    return paymentResultApplier.applyCaptureResult(payment.paymentId(), result);
}
```

Important:

```text
Capture tidak boleh routing ulang.
Refund tidak boleh routing ulang.
Void tidak boleh routing ulang.
Status query tidak boleh routing ulang.
```

---

# 16. Failover vs Fallback

Ini bagian yang sering salah.

## 16.1 Fallback Sebelum Provider Call

Aman jika provider belum menerima request.

```text
Provider A down sebelum call dibuat.
Routing memilih Provider B.
Aman.
```

## 16.2 Fallback Setelah Timeout

Berbahaya.

```text
Call ke Provider A timeout.
Mungkin Provider A berhasil charge.
Lalu sistem fallback ke Provider B.
Customer bisa ter-charge dua kali.
```

Rule:

```text
Kalau request sudah dikirim ke provider dan status tidak jelas,
jangan fallback ke provider lain untuk logical payment yang sama.
Masuk UNKNOWN / PENDING_CONFIRMATION dulu.
```

## 16.3 Fallback Matrix

| Kondisi | Boleh Fallback? | Penjelasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Provider disabled sebelum call | Ya | Belum ada side effect. |
| Provider health `UNHEALTHY` sebelum call | Ya | Belum ada side effect. |
| Capability tidak cocok | Ya | Provider tidak eligible. |
| Provider reject karena invalid config | Tidak otomatis | Ini incident/config issue. |
| Provider hard decline kartu | Tidak | Decline adalah business result. |
| Network timeout setelah request dikirim | Tidak langsung | Status unknown. |
| HTTP 500 setelah request dikirim | Hati-hati | Query status dulu jika possible. |
| Rate limit sebelum request accepted | Mungkin | Tergantung provider semantics. |
| Webhook terlambat | Tidak | Tunggu/reconcile. |
| Capture gagal di provider original | Tidak ke provider lain | Capture harus ke provider original. |
| Refund gagal di provider original | Tidak ke provider lain | Refund harus ke provider original. |

PayPal secara eksplisit mendokumentasikan retry untuk network timeout atau HTTP 500 dengan `PayPal-Request-Id` selama server masih menyimpan ID tersebut; pola ini memperkuat prinsip bahwa retry harus memakai idempotency terhadap provider yang sama, bukan langsung pindah provider. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/api/rest/requests/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 17. Deterministic Tie-Breaker

Jika dua provider sama-sama eligible, jangan pilih random tanpa audit.

Contoh buruk:

```java
return candidates.get(new Random().nextInt(candidates.size()));
```

Masalah:

```text
Retry request bisa pilih provider berbeda.
Debugging sulit.
A/B routing tidak terkontrol.
```

Lebih baik:

```java
private ProviderCandidate stableTieBreaker(
        List<ProviderCandidate> candidates,
        PaymentRoutingContext context
) {
    return candidates.stream()
            .sorted(
                    Comparator
                            .comparingInt(ProviderCandidate::merchantPriority)
                            .thenComparing(candidate -> stableHashScore(
                                    context.orderId(),
                                    candidate.providerCode()
                            ))
                            .thenComparing(ProviderCandidate::providerCode)
            )
            .findFirst()
            .orElseThrow();
}

private int stableHashScore(String key, String providerCode) {
    return Math.abs((key + ":" + providerCode).hashCode());
}
```

Untuk weighted routing:

```text
Gunakan deterministic weighted bucket berdasarkan orderId/paymentId hash.
Jangan random per request.
```

---

# 18. Weighted Routing

Weighted routing berguna untuk:

```text
1. Gradual migration provider.
2. A/B test provider.
3. Cost optimization.
4. Success-rate optimization.
5. Load sharing.
```

Contoh rule:

| Provider | Weight |
|---|---:|
| STRIPE | 70 |
| ADYEN | 30 |

Deterministic bucket:

```java
public String selectWeightedProvider(
        String stableKey,
        List<WeightedProvider> providers
) {
    int totalWeight = providers.stream()
            .mapToInt(WeightedProvider::weight)
            .sum();

    int bucket = Math.floorMod(stableKey.hashCode(), totalWeight);

    int cumulative = 0;
    for (WeightedProvider provider : providers) {
        cumulative += provider.weight();
        if (bucket < cumulative) {
            return provider.providerCode();
        }
    }

    throw new IllegalStateException("No provider selected");
}
```

Important:

```text
Stable key sebaiknya paymentId/orderId.
Jangan pakai current timestamp.
Jangan pakai requestId karena retry bisa berubah.
```

---

# 19. Provider Health Strategy

Provider health tidak boleh hanya berdasarkan ping endpoint.

Gunakan signal:

| Signal | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Success rate | Payment sukses per provider dalam window tertentu. |
| Timeout rate | Timeout meningkat → provider degraded. |
| 5xx rate | Provider/server issue. |
| p95/p99 latency | Latency naik → customer impact. |
| Webhook lag | Provider lambat kirim event. |
| Reconciliation mismatch | Provider data tidak cocok dengan internal. |
| Manual override | Ops disable provider. |

Health update bisa dari:

```text
1. Metrics pipeline.
2. Scheduled job.
3. Admin console.
4. Incident automation.
5. Circuit breaker state.
```

---

# 20. Circuit Breaker Interaction

Circuit breaker berguna, tetapi payment perlu hati-hati.

```text
Circuit breaker open sebelum request dikirim
  -> provider dianggap unavailable, boleh routing ke provider lain.

Circuit breaker timeout setelah request dikirim
  -> payment status unknown, jangan fallback langsung.
```

Practical rule:

```text
Routing service hanya boleh menggunakan circuit breaker state untuk pre-call decision.
Application service tetap harus handle post-call timeout sebagai UNKNOWN.
```

# 23. Routing Error Model

Gunakan error code yang stabil.

| Error Code | HTTP | Kapan |
|---|---:|---|
| `NO_MERCHANT_PROVIDER_CONFIG` | `422` / `409` | Merchant belum punya provider config. |
| `NO_ELIGIBLE_PROVIDER` | `422` / `503` | Tidak ada provider cocok. |
| `PROVIDER_DISABLED` | `503` | Provider dimatikan manual. |
| `PROVIDER_UNHEALTHY` | `503` | Semua provider eligible tidak sehat. |
| `PROVIDER_CAPABILITY_MISMATCH` | `422` | Provider tidak support currency/method/capture mode. |
| `ROUTING_DECISION_NOT_FOUND` | `500` / `409` | Existing payment tidak punya routing decision. |
| `ROUTING_CONFIG_CONFLICT` | `500` / admin error | Rule ambigu atau invalid. |
| `FALLBACK_NOT_ALLOWED` | `409` | Provider gagal tapi fallback tidak aman. |

Contoh response:

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/no-eligible-payment-provider",
  "title": "No eligible payment provider",
  "status": 422,
  "detail": "No enabled provider supports CARD_TOKEN with currency IDR for merchant MRC-001.",
  "errorCode": "NO_ELIGIBLE_PROVIDER",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

# 26. Testing Strategy

## 26.1 Unit Test Routing Filter

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Merchant without config | `NO_MERCHANT_PROVIDER_CONFIG` |
| Provider disabled | Provider rejected |
| Currency unsupported | Provider rejected |
| Payment method unsupported | Provider rejected |
| Manual capture but provider does not support capture | Provider rejected |
| Provider unhealthy | Provider rejected for new payment |
| Multiple eligible providers | Lowest priority wins |
| Same order/payment context | Same provider selected |
| Existing payment capture | Uses original provider |
| Timeout after provider call | No fallback, status unknown |

## 26.2 Example Unit Test

```java
@Test
void shouldSelectProviderByPriorityWhenMultipleEligible() {
    PaymentRoutingContext context = new PaymentRoutingContext(
            "MRC-001",
            "CUST-001",
            "ORD-001",
            100000,
            "IDR",
            "CARD_TOKEN",
            "ID",
            "ID",
            "ID",
            true,
            false,
            false,
            "WEB",
            "PROD",
            20,
            Map.of()
    );

    PaymentRoutingDecision decision =
            routingService.selectProviderForCreatePayment(UUID.randomUUID(), context);

    assertThat(decision.selectedProvider()).isEqualTo("ADYEN");
}
```

## 26.3 Integration Test with PostgreSQL

```text
1. Insert provider configs.
2. Insert capabilities.
3. Insert merchant configs.
4. Insert routing rules.
5. Call routing service.
6. Verify decision persisted.
7. Verify capture/refund lookup uses persisted provider.
```

---

# 27. Common Anti-Pattern

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Buruk | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Routing ulang saat capture/refund | Bisa call provider berbeda dari original payment. | Persist routing decision. |
| Random provider per request | Retry bisa pilih provider berbeda. | Stable deterministic routing. |
| Fallback setelah timeout | Bisa double charge. | Mark `UNKNOWN`, query provider. |
| Provider capability hardcoded di service | Sulit update. | Capability registry/config. |
| Tidak ada routing audit | Sulit debug kenapa provider dipilih. | `payment_routing_decisions`. |
| Satu config global untuk semua merchant | Merchant/account/country berbeda. | Merchant-specific config. |
| Health check hanya ping | Ping sehat tidak berarti payment sukses. | Pakai success/timeout/latency/payment metrics. |
| Semua decline dianggap provider buruk | Decline bisa karena kartu/customer. | Pisahkan decline vs provider error. |
| Rule ambigu tanpa priority | Provider selection tidak predictable. | Priority + deterministic tie-breaker. |
| Config berubah tanpa version | Payment lama sulit diaudit. | Rule/config versioning. |

## Safety

- [ ] Provider disabled tidak menerima payment baru.
- [ ] Provider unhealthy tidak menerima payment baru.
- [ ] Timeout setelah provider call tidak fallback langsung.

- [ ] Fallback hanya sebelum provider side effect.

Buat module berikut:

```text
  routing/

    PaymentRoutingDecision.java

    PaymentRoutingMapper.xml
```

Target implementasi:

```text
1. Seed provider: STRIPE, ADYEN, PAYPAL, MOCK.
2. Seed capability currency/method per provider.
3. Seed merchant config MRC-001.
4. Seed routing rule:
   - IDR CARD_TOKEN -> ADYEN
   - USD CARD_TOKEN -> STRIPE
   - PAYPAL method -> PAYPAL
   - LOCAL test -> MOCK
5. Implement dry-run routing.
6. Persist routing decision saat create payment.
7. Capture/refund mengambil provider dari routing decision.
```

Test wajib:

```text
1. IDR + CARD_TOKEN memilih ADYEN.
2. USD + CARD_TOKEN memilih STRIPE.
3. PAYPAL method memilih PAYPAL.
4. Provider disabled membuat provider tidak dipilih.
5. Manual capture hanya memilih provider yang support auth/capture.
6. Retry create payment menghasilkan provider yang sama.
7. Capture existing payment tidak routing ulang.
8. Timeout provider tidak fallback ke provider lain.
```

# 30. Ringkasan

Multi-provider routing adalah **control plane** untuk payment service.

Prinsip utamanya:

```text
1. Routing hanya untuk payment baru.
2. Existing payment harus stick ke provider original.
3. Provider selection harus deterministic.
4. Capability dan merchant config harus eksplisit.
5. Health boleh mempengaruhi pre-call routing.
6. Timeout post-call tidak boleh langsung fallback.
7. Semua routing decision harus diaudit.
```

Kalau routing salah, efeknya bukan sekadar request gagal. Efeknya bisa berupa:

```text
- double charge,
- refund ke provider salah,
- reconciliation mismatch,
- webhook tidak bisa dimap,
- audit trail rusak,
- customer dispute,
- financial loss.
```

## Response:
7/14/2026, 10:35:04 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 7/18**: authorization, capture, void, dan settlement flow. Ini bagian lifecycle uang: kapan dana hanya di-hold, kapan benar-benar dicapture, kapan auth harus dibatalkan, dan bagaimana status settlement tidak disamakan dengan payment captured.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 7/18 - Authorization, Capture, Void, dan Settlement Flow

Seri ini fokus pada **lifecycle uang** dalam payment service: kapan dana hanya di-*hold*, kapan benar-benar di-*capture*, kapan authorization harus di-*void*, dan kenapa **captured ≠ settled ≠ paid out to bank**.

Core idea:

```text
Authorization = dana di-reserve / di-hold
Capture       = dana benar-benar ditagihkan / dipindahkan
Void          = membatalkan authorization sebelum capture
Settlement    = dana masuk ke balance/settlement provider
Payout        = dana ditransfer dari provider ke rekening merchant
```

Dalam Stripe, separate authorization dan capture dilakukan dengan `capture_method=manual`, yang menginstruksikan Stripe untuk mengotorisasi amount tetapi tidak langsung melakukan capture. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/place-a-hold-on-a-payment-method?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen juga mendokumentasikan manual capture sebagai cara untuk settle funds saat fulfillment/order shipped, dan mendukung flow seperti authorization adjustment serta partial authorization. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/capture?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PayPal Checkout mendukung model two-step authorize-and-capture; authorization menempatkan hold pada dana buyer dan dokumentasi PayPal menyebut authorization valid selama 29 hari. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/checkout/standard/customize/authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Payment lifecycle jangan diperlakukan sebagai satu status sederhana `PAID`.

Lebih tepat:

```text
Customer agrees to pay
  -> Authorization requested
  -> Authorization approved
  -> Business validates fulfillment
  -> Capture requested
  -> Capture confirmed
  -> Funds settle in provider balance
  -> Payout sent to merchant bank
  -> Reconciliation confirms actual money movement
```

Dalam sistem internal:

```text
AUTHORIZED  belum tentu uang sudah diterima.
CAPTURED    belum tentu uang sudah masuk rekening bank.
SETTLED     belum tentu sudah paid out.
PAID_OUT    baru berarti payout masuk rekening merchant, jika data payout sudah cocok.
```

Stripe menjelaskan settlement time sebagai waktu antara payment/funding transaction dibuat sampai funds tersedia di Stripe balance, dan settlement timing dapat bervariasi berdasarkan negara serta payment method. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/balances?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Diagram Lifecycle

```mermaid
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> INITIATED

    INITIATED --> AUTHORIZATION_PENDING: authorize requested
    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING --> REQUIRES_ACTION: 3DS / redirect / challenge
    REQUIRES_ACTION --> AUTHORIZATION_PENDING: customer completed action

    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING --> AUTHORIZED: auth approved
    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING --> FAILED: auth declined
    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING --> UNKNOWN: timeout / unclear result

    AUTHORIZED --> CAPTURE_PENDING: capture requested
    AUTHORIZED --> VOID_PENDING: void requested
    AUTHORIZED --> AUTH_EXPIRED: auth expired

    CAPTURE_PENDING --> CAPTURED: full capture succeeded
    CAPTURE_PENDING --> PARTIALLY_CAPTURED: partial capture succeeded
    CAPTURE_PENDING --> UNKNOWN: timeout / unclear result
    CAPTURE_PENDING --> CAPTURE_FAILED: capture failed

    PARTIALLY_CAPTURED --> CAPTURE_PENDING: next capture requested
    PARTIALLY_CAPTURED --> CAPTURED: remaining captured
    PARTIALLY_CAPTURED --> VOID_PENDING: cancel remaining authorization

    VOID_PENDING --> VOIDED: void succeeded
    VOID_PENDING --> VOID_FAILED: void failed
    VOID_PENDING --> UNKNOWN: timeout / unclear result

    CAPTURED --> SETTLEMENT_PENDING: waiting provider settlement
    SETTLEMENT_PENDING --> SETTLED: available in provider balance
    SETTLED --> PAYOUT_PENDING: payout scheduled
    PAYOUT_PENDING --> PAID_OUT: payout completed

    CAPTURED --> REFUND_PENDING
    SETTLED --> REFUND_PENDING
    PAID_OUT --> REFUND_PENDING

    FAILED --> [*]
    VOIDED --> [*]
    AUTH_EXPIRED --> [*]
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Authorization Flow** | Reserve dana tanpa langsung capture. | `authorizePayment()` |
| 2 | **Auto-Capture Flow** | Authorize + capture langsung. | `captureMode=AUTO` |
| 3 | **Manual Capture Flow** | Capture setelah order siap fulfilled. | `captureMode=MANUAL` |
| 4 | **Partial Capture** | Capture sebagian amount. | `captured_amount_minor` incremental |
| 5 | **Multiple Capture** | Beberapa capture untuk satu authorization jika provider mendukung. | `payment_captures` / `payment_attempts` |
| 6 | **Void Authorization** | Cancel authorization sebelum capture. | `voidAuthorization()` |
| 7 | **Authorization Expiry** | Auth hold bisa expire. | scheduled expiry job |
| 8 | **Settlement Tracking** | Track funds available di provider balance. | `settlements` / `settlement_items` |
| 9 | **Payout Tracking** | Track transfer provider ke bank. | `payouts` / `payout_items` |
| 10 | **Ledger Posting** | Catat efek finansial per lifecycle. | `ledger_entries` |
| 11 | **Invariant Enforcement** | Cegah capture/refund invalid. | DB constraint + domain validation |
| 12 | **Provider Mapping** | Map Stripe/Adyen/PayPal status ke internal. | `GatewayStatusMapper` |
| 13 | **Timeout Handling** | Jangan mark failed sembarangan. | `UNKNOWN` / status query |
| 14 | **Webhook Confirmation** | Capture/settlement/refund bisa async. | inbox dedup + event processor |
| 15 | **Reconciliation** | Cocokkan captured/settled/paid out. | reconciliation job |

---

# 4. Authorization vs Capture vs Settlement

| Concept | Makna | Apakah Dana Sudah Diterima Merchant? | Contoh Internal Status |
|---|---|---:|---|
| Authorization | Issuer/provider menyetujui dan menahan dana. | Belum | `AUTHORIZED` |
| Capture | Merchant meminta dana ditagihkan/dipindahkan. | Belum tentu masuk rekening | `CAPTURED` |
| Settlement | Dana tersedia di provider/acquirer balance. | Hampir, tapi belum tentu payout bank | `SETTLED` |
| Payout | Provider transfer dana ke rekening merchant. | Ya, setelah bank statement cocok | `PAID_OUT` |
| Void | Auth dibatalkan sebelum capture. | Tidak | `VOIDED` |
| Refund | Dana dikembalikan setelah capture. | Mengurangi receivable/balance | `REFUNDED` / `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED` |

Adyen menjelaskan capture sebagai tahap ketika authorized amount dikurangkan dari balance account, dan partial capture dapat membuat sisa amount yang belum dicapture masih bisa expire atau dibatalkan. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/issuing/payment-stages?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 5. Recommended Internal Status

Untuk seri ini, status payment bisa diperluas seperti berikut:

```java
public enum PaymentStatus {
    INITIATED,

    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING,
    REQUIRES_ACTION,
    AUTHORIZED,
    AUTH_EXPIRED,

    CAPTURE_PENDING,
    PARTIALLY_CAPTURED,
    CAPTURED,
    CAPTURE_FAILED,

    VOID_PENDING,
    VOIDED,
    VOID_FAILED,

    SETTLEMENT_PENDING,
    SETTLED,

    PAYOUT_PENDING,
    PAID_OUT,

    REFUND_PENDING,
    PARTIALLY_REFUNDED,
    REFUNDED,

    FAILED,
    CANCELLED,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

Catatan praktis:

```text
Untuk banyak aplikasi, status utama payment cukup sampai CAPTURED.
Settlement dan payout bisa dikelola di aggregate/reporting terpisah.

Tapi untuk payment-heavy / finance-sensitive platform,
SETTLEMENT dan PAYOUT sebaiknya dimodelkan eksplisit.
```

---

# 6. Recommended Tables Tambahan

Di seri sebelumnya kita sudah punya:

```text
payments
payment_attempts
payment_events
refunds
ledger_entries
idempotency_keys
payment_inbox_events
payment_outbox_events
reconciliation_runs
reconciliation_items
```

Untuk lifecycle authorization/capture/settlement yang lebih rapi, tambahkan:

```text
payment_authorizations
payment_captures
payment_voids
settlements
settlement_items
payouts
payout_items
```

---

## 6.1 `payment_authorizations`

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_authorizations (
    authorization_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_authorization_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),

    authorized_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    authorized_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    voided_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    request_payload JSONB,
    response_payload JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_authorizations_amount_positive
        CHECK (authorized_amount_minor > 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_authorizations_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency)),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_authorizations_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'PENDING',
            'AUTHORIZED',
            'REQUIRES_ACTION',
            'FAILED',
            'VOIDED',
            'EXPIRED',
            'UNKNOWN'
        ))
);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_authorizations_payment_id
ON payment_authorizations(payment_id);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_authorizations_provider_auth
ON payment_authorizations(provider, provider_authorization_id)
WHERE provider_authorization_id IS NOT NULL;
```

---

## 6.2 `payment_captures`

Capture sebaiknya entity sendiri karena bisa partial/multiple.

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_captures (
    capture_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),
    authorization_id UUID REFERENCES payment_authorizations(authorization_id),

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_capture_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),

    capture_request_id VARCHAR(200),

    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    captured_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    settled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    request_payload JSONB,
    response_payload JSONB,

    failure_code VARCHAR(100),
    failure_message TEXT,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_captures_amount_positive
        CHECK (amount_minor > 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_captures_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency)),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_captures_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'REQUESTED',
            'PENDING',
            'SUCCEEDED',
            'FAILED',
            'CANCELLED',
            'UNKNOWN'
        ))
);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_captures_payment_id
ON payment_captures(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_captures_authorization_id
ON payment_captures(authorization_id);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_captures_provider_capture
ON payment_captures(provider, provider_capture_id)
WHERE provider_capture_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_captures_request_id
ON payment_captures(payment_id, capture_request_id)
WHERE capture_request_id IS NOT NULL;
```

## 6.3 `payment_voids`

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_voids (
    void_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),
    authorization_id UUID REFERENCES payment_authorizations(authorization_id),

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_void_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_authorization_id VARCHAR(200),

    void_request_id VARCHAR(200),

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    reason VARCHAR(200),

    voided_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    request_payload JSONB,
    response_payload JSONB,

    failure_code VARCHAR(100),
    failure_message TEXT,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_voids_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'REQUESTED',
            'PENDING',
            'SUCCEEDED',
            'FAILED',
            'UNKNOWN'
        ))
);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_voids_payment_id
ON payment_voids(payment_id);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_voids_request_id
ON payment_voids(payment_id, void_request_id)
WHERE void_request_id IS NOT NULL;
```

PayPal’s void endpoint cancels an authorized payment by authorization ID, and PayPal notes that an authorized payment that has been fully captured cannot be voided. ([Paypal Docs](https://docs.paypal.ai/reference/api/rest/authorizations/void-authorized-payment?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 6.4 `settlements`

Settlement adalah batch/record dari provider yang menjelaskan dana sudah tersedia/settled.

```sql
CREATE TABLE settlements (
    settlement_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_settlement_id VARCHAR(200),

    settlement_date DATE NOT NULL,
    settlement_currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    gross_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    fee_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    report_file_name VARCHAR(255),
    raw_report_metadata JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_settlements_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (settlement_currency = upper(settlement_currency)),

    CONSTRAINT chk_settlements_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'RECEIVED',
            'PROCESSING',
            'RECONCILED',
            'FAILED',
            'PARTIAL'
        ))
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_settlements_provider_settlement
ON settlements(provider, provider_settlement_id)
WHERE provider_settlement_id IS NOT NULL;
```

## 6.5 `settlement_items`

```sql
CREATE TABLE settlement_items (
    settlement_item_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    settlement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES settlements(settlement_id),

    payment_id UUID REFERENCES payments(payment_id),
    capture_id UUID REFERENCES payment_captures(capture_id),
    refund_id UUID REFERENCES refunds(refund_id),

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_capture_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_refund_id VARCHAR(200),

    item_type VARCHAR(60) NOT NULL,

    gross_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    fee_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    settlement_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    raw_item JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_settlement_items_type
        CHECK (item_type IN (
            'CAPTURE',
            'REFUND',
            'CHARGEBACK',
            'FEE',
            'ADJUSTMENT',
            'PAYOUT'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_settlement_items_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency))
);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlement_items_payment_id
ON settlement_items(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlement_items_capture_id
ON settlement_items(capture_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlement_items_refund_id
ON settlement_items(refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlement_items_provider_refs
ON settlement_items(provider, provider_payment_id, provider_capture_id);
```

Stripe’s payout reconciliation report groups transactions included in settlement batches by reporting category, which is the type of information this kind of table is meant to normalize internally. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/reports/payout-reconciliation?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. Flow 1 - Auto-Capture

Auto-capture cocok jika bisnis langsung fulfill setelah payment sukses.

Contoh:

```text
Customer checkout
  -> authorize + capture immediately
  -> payment CAPTURED
  -> order can proceed
```

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Order as Order Service
    participant API as Payment API
    participant DB as PostgreSQL
    participant PSP as Payment Provider

    Order->>API: POST /payments captureMode=AUTO
    API->>DB: insert payment INITIATED
    API->>DB: insert attempt SALE/AUTHORIZE
    API->>PSP: authorize + capture
    PSP-->>API: captured / pending / failed
    API->>DB: update payment CAPTURED
    API->>DB: insert capture record
    API->>DB: insert ledger PAYMENT_CAPTURED
    API->>DB: insert outbox PaymentCaptured
    API-->>Order: payment status CAPTURED
```

## Implementation Rule

```text
AUTO capture:
- Jika provider response final captured -> CAPTURED
- Jika provider response pending -> CAPTURE_PENDING / UNKNOWN
- Jika requires action -> REQUIRES_ACTION
- Jika decline -> FAILED
```

---

# 8. Flow 2 - Manual Capture

Manual capture cocok untuk:

```text
- stock harus dicek dulu
- booking/travel/hotel/rental
- order butuh fraud review
- shipment partial
- CPQ/order management dengan fulfilment bertahap
- high-value order
```

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Order as Order Service
    participant Pay as Payment Service
    participant PSP as Provider
    participant DB as PostgreSQL
    participant Fulfill as Fulfillment Service

    Order->>Pay: POST /payments captureMode=MANUAL
    Pay->>PSP: authorize only
    PSP-->>Pay: authorization approved
    Pay->>DB: payment AUTHORIZED
    Pay-->>Order: AUTHORIZED

    Order->>Fulfill: reserve stock / prepare order
    Fulfill-->>Order: ready to ship

    Order->>Pay: POST /payments/{id}/capture
    Pay->>DB: validate capturable amount
    Pay->>PSP: capture authorization
    PSP-->>Pay: capture succeeded
    Pay->>DB: payment CAPTURED
    Pay->>DB: ledger PAYMENT_CAPTURED
    Pay-->>Order: CAPTURED
```

Stripe supports placing a hold on a payment method and capturing later with `capture_method=manual`. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/place-a-hold-on-a-payment-method?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PayPal describes authorize-and-capture as useful when a task must be completed before finalizing the payment, such as verifying stock. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/checkout/standard/customize/authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 9. Flow 3 - Partial Capture

Partial capture berarti hanya sebagian authorized amount yang dicapture.

Contoh:

```text
Authorized amount = 1,000,000 IDR
Shipment 1 capture = 400,000 IDR
Shipment 2 capture = 300,000 IDR
Remaining 300,000 IDR void/release
```

Adyen documents partial capture by specifying a value less than the authorized amount; the remainder is released back to the shopper in the POS capture flow, and its issuing/payment-stage docs describe partial captures with remaining authorized amount possibly expiring or being canceled. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/point-of-sale/capturing-payments?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Stripe also documents multicapture for PaymentIntents when manual capture is used and when payment method support is available. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/multicapture?platform=web&ui=elements&utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## Partial Capture State

```text
AUTHORIZED
  -> PARTIALLY_CAPTURED
  -> PARTIALLY_CAPTURED
  -> CAPTURED

or

AUTHORIZED
  -> PARTIALLY_CAPTURED
  -> VOIDED remaining amount
```

## SQL Guard

```sql
UPDATE payments
SET captured_amount_minor = captured_amount_minor + #{captureAmountMinor},
    status = CASE
        WHEN captured_amount_minor + #{captureAmountMinor} = authorized_amount_minor
            THEN 'CAPTURED'
        ELSE 'PARTIALLY_CAPTURED'
    END,
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
  AND status IN ('AUTHORIZED', 'PARTIALLY_CAPTURED', 'CAPTURE_PENDING', 'UNKNOWN')
  AND captured_amount_minor + #{captureAmountMinor} <= authorized_amount_minor
  AND currency = #{currency}
  AND version = #{version};
```

Invariant:

```text
sum(successful captures) <= authorized amount
```

---

# 10. Flow 4 - Void Authorization

Void dilakukan jika authorization sudah berhasil tetapi order tidak jadi dicapture.

Contoh:

```text
Order canceled
Stock unavailable
Fraud rejected
Customer failed verification
Manual review failed
```

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant Order as Order Service
    participant Pay as Payment Service
    participant PSP as Provider
    participant DB as PostgreSQL

    Order->>Pay: POST /payments/{id}/void
    Pay->>DB: load payment FOR UPDATE
    Pay->>DB: validate status AUTHORIZED/PARTIALLY_CAPTURED
    Pay->>PSP: void authorization
    PSP-->>Pay: void succeeded
    Pay->>DB: update payment VOIDED or PARTIALLY_CAPTURED
    Pay->>DB: insert event AUTHORIZATION_VOIDED
    Pay->>DB: insert outbox PaymentVoided
    Pay-->>Order: VOIDED
```

Void rule:

| Current State | Void Allowed? | Notes |
|---|---:|---|
| `AUTHORIZED` | Ya | Cancel full authorization. |
| `PARTIALLY_CAPTURED` | Mungkin | Void remaining authorization jika provider mendukung. |
| `CAPTURED` | Tidak | Gunakan refund, bukan void. |
| `FAILED` | Tidak | Tidak ada auth valid. |
| `AUTH_EXPIRED` | Tidak perlu | Auth sudah expired. |
| `VOIDED` | Idempotent success | Return existing result. |

---

# 11. Domain Service Design

## 11.1 Payment Aggregate Method

```java
public class Payment {

    private UUID paymentId;
    private long authorizedAmountMinor;
    private long capturedAmountMinor;
    private long refundedAmountMinor;
    private String currency;
    private PaymentStatus status;
    private long version;

    public void markAuthorized(long amountMinor, String currency) {
        requireStatus(
                PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZATION_PENDING,
                PaymentStatus.REQUIRES_ACTION,
                PaymentStatus.UNKNOWN
        );

        requireSameCurrency(currency);
        requirePositive(amountMinor);

        this.authorizedAmountMinor = amountMinor;
        this.status = PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED;
    }

    public void markCapturePending(long amountMinor, String currency) {
        requireStatus(
                PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED,
                PaymentStatus.PARTIALLY_CAPTURED,
                PaymentStatus.UNKNOWN
        );

        requireSameCurrency(currency);
        requirePositive(amountMinor);

        if (capturedAmountMinor + amountMinor > authorizedAmountMinor) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("capture amount exceeds authorized amount");
        }

        this.status = PaymentStatus.CAPTURE_PENDING;
    }

    public void applyCaptureSucceeded(long amountMinor, String currency) {
        requireStatus(
                PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED,
                PaymentStatus.CAPTURE_PENDING,
                PaymentStatus.PARTIALLY_CAPTURED,
                PaymentStatus.UNKNOWN
        );

        requireSameCurrency(currency);
        requirePositive(amountMinor);

        long newCapturedAmount = capturedAmountMinor + amountMinor;

        if (newCapturedAmount > authorizedAmountMinor) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("captured amount exceeds authorized amount");
        }

        this.capturedAmountMinor = newCapturedAmount;
        this.status = newCapturedAmount == authorizedAmountMinor
                ? PaymentStatus.CAPTURED
                : PaymentStatus.PARTIALLY_CAPTURED;
    }

    public void markVoided() {
        requireStatus(PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED, PaymentStatus.VOID_PENDING);

        if (capturedAmountMinor > 0) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("fully voiding a partially captured payment is not allowed");
        }

        this.status = PaymentStatus.VOIDED;
    }

    public void markUnknown() {
        if (isFinal()) {
            return;
        }
        this.status = PaymentStatus.UNKNOWN;
    }

    private boolean isFinal() {
        return status == PaymentStatus.CAPTURED
                || status == PaymentStatus.VOIDED
                || status == PaymentStatus.FAILED
                || status == PaymentStatus.REFUNDED;
    }

    private void requireSameCurrency(String currency) {
        if (!this.currency.equals(currency)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("currency mismatch");
        }
    }

    private void requirePositive(long amountMinor) {
        if (amountMinor <= 0) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("amount must be positive");
        }
    }

    private void requireStatus(PaymentStatus... allowedStatuses) {
        for (PaymentStatus allowed : allowedStatuses) {
            if (this.status == allowed) {
                return;
            }
        }
        throw new IllegalStateException("invalid state transition from " + status);
    }
}
```

---

# 12. Application Service: Authorize

```java
public PaymentResponse authorizePayment(CreatePaymentCommand command) {
    UUID paymentId = UUID.randomUUID();

    return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
        paymentRepository.insertPaymentInitiated(paymentId, command);
        paymentEventRepository.insertPaymentInitiated(paymentId, command);
        return paymentId;
    }).let(createdPaymentId -> {
        GatewayAuthorizeRequest gatewayRequest =
                gatewayRequestFactory.createAuthorizeRequest(createdPaymentId, command);

        try {
            GatewayAuthorizeResult result =
                    gatewayRouter.routeForAuthorize(gatewayRequest)
                            .authorize(gatewayRequest);

            return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                paymentRepository.applyAuthorizeResult(createdPaymentId, result);
                authorizationRepository.insertAuthorization(createdPaymentId, result);
                paymentEventRepository.insertAuthorizeResultEvent(createdPaymentId, result);

                if (result.status() == GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED) {
                    captureRepository.insertCaptureFromAuthorizeResult(createdPaymentId, result);
                    ledgerRepository.insertPaymentCaptured(createdPaymentId, result);
                    outboxRepository.insertPaymentCaptured(createdPaymentId, result);
                }

                return paymentResponseMapper.fromPaymentId(createdPaymentId);
            });

        } catch (GatewayTimeoutException timeout) {
            return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                paymentRepository.markUnknown(createdPaymentId);
                paymentEventRepository.insertGatewayTimeoutEvent(createdPaymentId, timeout);
                outboxRepository.insertPaymentPendingConfirmation(createdPaymentId);
                return paymentResponseMapper.fromPaymentId(createdPaymentId);
            });
        }
    });
}
```

Catatan: contoh di atas pseudo-code Java-style; di implementasi nyata, hindari method non-Java seperti `.let()` kecuali Anda membuat helper sendiri.

---

# 13. Application Service: Capture

```java
public PaymentResponse capturePayment(CapturePaymentCommand command) {
    Payment payment = transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
        Payment locked = paymentRepository.findByIdForUpdate(command.paymentId());

        paymentDomainService.validateCaptureAllowed(
                locked,
                command.amountMinor(),
                command.currency()
        );

        captureRepository.insertCaptureRequested(
                command.captureId(),
                command.paymentId(),
                command.captureRequestId(),
                command.amountMinor(),
                command.currency()
        );

        paymentRepository.markCapturePending(
                command.paymentId(),
                locked.version()
        );

        paymentEventRepository.insertCaptureRequested(command);

        return locked;
    });

    GatewayCaptureRequest gatewayRequest =
            gatewayRequestFactory.createCaptureRequest(payment, command);

    try {
        GatewayCaptureResult result =
                gatewayRegistry.getRequired(payment.provider())
                        .capture(gatewayRequest);

        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            Payment locked = paymentRepository.findByIdForUpdate(command.paymentId());

            int updated = paymentRepository.addCapturedAmount(
                    command.paymentId(),
                    result.capturedAmountMinor(),
                    result.currency(),
                    locked.version()
            );

            if (updated != 1) {
                throw new ConcurrentPaymentUpdateException(command.paymentId());
            }

            captureRepository.markCaptureSucceeded(
                    command.captureId(),
                    result.providerCaptureId(),
                    result
            );

            paymentEventRepository.insertCaptureSucceeded(command.paymentId(), result);

            ledgerRepository.insertPaymentCaptured(
                    command.paymentId(),
                    command.captureId(),
                    result.capturedAmountMinor(),
                    result.currency(),
                    result.providerCaptureId()
            );

            outboxRepository.insertPaymentCaptured(command.paymentId(), result);

            return paymentResponseMapper.fromPaymentId(command.paymentId());
        });

    } catch (GatewayTimeoutException timeout) {
        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            captureRepository.markCaptureUnknown(command.captureId(), timeout);
            paymentRepository.markUnknown(command.paymentId());
            paymentEventRepository.insertCaptureTimeout(command.paymentId(), timeout);
            outboxRepository.insertPaymentPendingConfirmation(command.paymentId());
            return paymentResponseMapper.fromPaymentId(command.paymentId());
        });
    }
}
```

Important:

```text
Capture request dibuat dulu sebagai record internal.
Provider timeout tidak boleh menghapus capture request.
Recovery akan query provider dengan provider reference/idempotency key.
```

---

# 14. Application Service: Void

```java
public PaymentResponse voidAuthorization(VoidPaymentCommand command) {
    Payment payment = transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
        Payment locked = paymentRepository.findByIdForUpdate(command.paymentId());

        paymentDomainService.validateVoidAllowed(locked);

        voidRepository.insertVoidRequested(
                command.voidId(),
                command.paymentId(),
                command.voidRequestId(),
                command.reason()
        );

        paymentRepository.markVoidPending(command.paymentId(), locked.version());
        paymentEventRepository.insertVoidRequested(command);

        return locked;
    });

    GatewayVoidRequest gatewayRequest =
            gatewayRequestFactory.createVoidRequest(payment, command);

    try {
        GatewayVoidResult result =
                gatewayRegistry.getRequired(payment.provider())
                        .voidAuthorization(gatewayRequest);

        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            paymentRepository.markVoided(command.paymentId());
            voidRepository.markVoidSucceeded(command.voidId(), result);
            paymentEventRepository.insertVoidSucceeded(command.paymentId(), result);
            outboxRepository.insertPaymentVoided(command.paymentId(), result);

            return paymentResponseMapper.fromPaymentId(command.paymentId());
        });

    } catch (GatewayTimeoutException timeout) {
        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            voidRepository.markVoidUnknown(command.voidId(), timeout);
            paymentRepository.markUnknown(command.paymentId());
            paymentEventRepository.insertVoidTimeout(command.paymentId(), timeout);

            return paymentResponseMapper.fromPaymentId(command.paymentId());
        });
    }
}
```

---

# 15. API Design

## 15.1 Authorize / Create Payment

```http
POST /payments
Idempotency-Key: create-ord-123-v1
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json
{
  "orderId": "ORD-123",
  "customerId": "CUST-001",
  "merchantId": "MRC-001",
  "amountMinor": 1000000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "captureMode": "MANUAL",
  "paymentMethod": {
    "type": "CARD_TOKEN",
    "token": "pm_tok_abc"
  }
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "0d675590-f9f3-4f97-9f77-34983fca2faa",
  "orderId": "ORD-123",
  "status": "AUTHORIZED",
  "authorizedAmountMinor": 1000000,
  "capturedAmountMinor": 0,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "captureMode": "MANUAL"
}
```

---

## 15.2 Capture

```http
POST /payments/{paymentId}/capture
Idempotency-Key: capture-shipment-1
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json
{
  "captureRequestId": "shipment-1",
  "amountMinor": 400000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "reason": "SHIPMENT_1_READY"
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "0d675590-f9f3-4f97-9f77-34983fca2faa",
  "status": "PARTIALLY_CAPTURED",
  "authorizedAmountMinor": 1000000,
  "capturedAmountMinor": 400000,
  "remainingCapturableAmountMinor": 600000,
  "currency": "IDR"
}
```

---

## 15.3 Void

```http
POST /payments/{paymentId}/void
Idempotency-Key: void-ord-123-v1
Content-Type: application/json
```

```json
{
  "voidRequestId": "void-ord-123",
  "reason": "ORDER_CANCELLED"
}
```

Response:

```json
{
  "paymentId": "0d675590-f9f3-4f97-9f77-34983fca2faa",
  "status": "VOIDED",
  "voidedAt": "2026-07-14T15:30:00Z"
}
```

# 16. Ledger Posting

Payment ledger harus membedakan authorization dan capture.

Authorization biasanya bukan revenue/receivable final. Capture adalah event finansial yang lebih kuat.

| Event | Ledger Entry? | Direction | Notes |
|---|---:|---|---|
| Authorization approved | Optional memo ledger | None / reserved | Bisa dicatat sebagai reserved, bukan revenue. |
| Capture succeeded | Ya | CREDIT | Merchant receivable/payment captured. |
| Partial capture | Ya | CREDIT | Per capture amount. |
| Void succeeded | Optional | Release reserve | Tidak ada revenue. |
| Settlement received | Ya | CREDIT/DEBIT adjustment | Sesuaikan gross/fee/net. |
| Fee charged | Ya | DEBIT | Provider/acquirer fee. |
| Payout completed | Ya | CREDIT bank / debit provider balance | Tergantung chart of accounts. |
| Refund succeeded | Ya | DEBIT | Mengurangi receivable/revenue. |

Contoh ledger capture:

```sql
INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    capture_id,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    account_code,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    description
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    #{paymentId},
    #{captureId},
    'PAYMENT_CAPTURED',
    'CREDIT',
    #{amountMinor},
    #{currency},
    'MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE',
    'PROVIDER_CAPTURE',
    #{providerCaptureId},
    'Payment capture succeeded'
);
```

---

# 17. Settlement Flow

Payment captured belum tentu langsung settled.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant PSP as Provider
    participant Job as Settlement Import Job
    participant DB as PostgreSQL
    participant Recon as Reconciliation Service
    participant Finance as Finance/Ops

    PSP-->>Job: settlement / balance / payout report
    Job->>DB: insert settlements
    Job->>DB: insert settlement_items
    Recon->>DB: match provider refs with captures/refunds
    Recon->>DB: mark captures SETTLED
    Recon->>DB: insert fee ledger entries
    Recon->>DB: create mismatch items
    Finance->>DB: review mismatches
```

Stripe’s Balance summary report is described as similar to a bank statement and provides itemized CSV export of transaction history, which is the type of artifact commonly used for reconciliation. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/reports/balance?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Stripe also documents payout reconciliation for matching payouts received in a bank account with transactions included in the payout. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/payouts/reconciliation?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 18. Settlement Status Model

```java
public enum SettlementStatus {
    NOT_APPLICABLE,
    SETTLEMENT_PENDING,
    SETTLED,
    PAYOUT_PENDING,
    PAID_OUT,
    SETTLEMENT_FAILED,
    RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH
}
```

Untuk payment aggregate, jangan terlalu cepat mencampur status operasional dengan settlement status.

Lebih baik:

```text
payments.status = CAPTURED
payments.settlement_status = SETTLEMENT_PENDING
```

atau table terpisah:

```text
payment_captures.status = SUCCEEDED
payment_captures.settlement_status = SETTLED
```

---

# 19. Authorization Expiry Job

Authorization punya masa berlaku. PayPal menyebut authorization hold valid selama 29 hari; provider/card network lain bisa punya window berbeda tergantung payment method, region, merchant category, dan provider policy. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/checkout/standard/customize/authorization/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Job internal:

```text
Every 15 minutes / hourly:
  1. Find AUTHORIZED payments with expires_at < now().
  2. Query provider if needed.
  3. If still uncaptured and expired, mark AUTH_EXPIRED.
  4. Insert payment_event AUTHORIZATION_EXPIRED.
  5. Insert outbox PaymentAuthorizationExpired.
  6. Notify order service.
```

SQL:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM payments p
JOIN payment_authorizations a
  ON a.payment_id = p.payment_id
WHERE p.status = 'AUTHORIZED'
  AND a.expires_at IS NOT NULL
  AND a.expires_at < now()
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
```

Application action:

```text
If order still needs payment:
  -> ask customer to pay again
  -> create new payment authorization
  -> do not try capture expired auth blindly
```

---

# 20. Invariants

| Invariant | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| `captured_amount_minor <= authorized_amount_minor` | Tidak boleh capture lebih dari auth. |
| `refunded_amount_minor <= captured_amount_minor` | Tidak boleh refund lebih dari captured. |
| `sum(successful captures) = payments.captured_amount_minor` | Aggregate harus cocok dengan detail capture. |
| `capture.currency = payment.currency` | Currency tidak boleh berubah. |
| `void` hanya sebelum full capture | Fully captured payment harus refund, bukan void. |
| `capture` harus ke provider original | Jangan routing ulang. |
| `refund` harus ke provider original | Refund harus refer provider capture/payment original. |
| `settled_amount` tidak boleh dianggap sama dengan `captured_amount` sebelum report provider masuk. | Captured dan settled berbeda. |
| `paid_out` harus berdasar payout/bank reconciliation. | Jangan set paid out dari capture response. |
| Timeout tidak final. | Masuk `UNKNOWN` / recovery. |

---

# 21. Provider Mapping Examples

## 21.1 Stripe

| Stripe Concept | Internal Meaning |
|---|---|
| `capture_method=manual` | Authorize now, capture later. |
| `requires_capture` | `AUTHORIZED` |
| `succeeded` | `CAPTURED` |
| `canceled` | `VOIDED` / `CANCELLED` |
| `processing` | `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` / `CAPTURE_PENDING` |
| Balance/payout report | Settlement/payout reconciliation source |

Stripe documents `capture_method=manual` for separate authorization and capture, and a PaymentIntent that requires capture is part of the manual capture flow. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/place-a-hold-on-a-payment-method?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 21.2 Adyen

| Adyen Concept | Internal Meaning |
|---|---|
| Authorisation | `AUTHORIZED` |
| Manual capture | `CAPTURE_PENDING` → `CAPTURED` |
| Partial capture | `PARTIALLY_CAPTURED` |
| Cancel/void remaining | `VOIDED` / remaining auth released |
| Settlement report | `SETTLED` / reconciliation |

Adyen’s capture docs describe manual capture and partial capture support, with `/capture` requiring merchant account, capture amount, and original authorization reference in classic recurring example. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/capture?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 21.3 PayPal

| PayPal Concept | Internal Meaning |
|---|---|
| Authorize order/payment | `AUTHORIZED` |
| Capture authorization | `CAPTURED` |
| Void authorization | `VOIDED` |
| Refund capture | Refund lifecycle |
| Authorization valid window | `expires_at` handling |

PayPal Payments v2 exposes capture authorized payment and refund captured payment operations, and PayPal’s authorize/capture guide describes the 2-step model. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/payments/v2/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 22. Failure Handling Matrix

| Scenario | Internal State | Action |
|---|---|---|
| Authorization approved | `AUTHORIZED` | Wait capture or auto-capture. |
| Authorization requires 3DS | `REQUIRES_ACTION` | Return `nextAction`. |
| Authorization declined | `FAILED` | Notify order/payment method retry. |
| Authorization timeout | `UNKNOWN` | Query provider / wait webhook. |
| Capture requested | `CAPTURE_PENDING` | Await provider response/webhook. |
| Capture success full | `CAPTURED` | Ledger + outbox. |
| Capture success partial | `PARTIALLY_CAPTURED` | Ledger per capture. |
| Capture timeout | `UNKNOWN` | Query provider; do not duplicate capture blindly. |
| Void success | `VOIDED` | Notify order. |
| Void timeout | `UNKNOWN` | Query provider; avoid capture until clear. |
| Auth expired | `AUTH_EXPIRED` | Require new payment. |
| Settlement report missing capture | `RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH` | Finance investigation. |
| Capture exists but payout missing | `PAYOUT_PENDING` / mismatch | Reconcile payout report. |

---

# 23. MyBatis Mapper Examples

## 23.1 Insert Capture Requested

```xml
<insert id="insertCaptureRequested">
    INSERT INTO payment_captures (
        capture_id,
        payment_id,
        authorization_id,
        provider,
        provider_payment_id,
        capture_request_id,
        amount_minor,
        currency,
        status,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    ) VALUES (
        #{captureId},
        #{paymentId},
        #{authorizationId},
        #{provider},
        #{providerPaymentId},
        #{captureRequestId},
        #{amountMinor},
        #{currency},
        'REQUESTED',
        now(),
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

## 23.2 Mark Capture Succeeded

```xml
<update id="markCaptureSucceeded">
    UPDATE payment_captures
    SET status = 'SUCCEEDED',
        provider_capture_id = #{providerCaptureId},
        response_payload = CAST(#{responsePayloadJson} AS jsonb),
        captured_at = now(),
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE capture_id = #{captureId}
      AND status IN ('REQUESTED', 'PENDING', 'UNKNOWN')
</update>
```

## 23.3 Mark Payment Captured Amount

```xml
<update id="addCapturedAmount">
    UPDATE payments
    SET captured_amount_minor = captured_amount_minor + #{amountMinor},
        status = CASE
            WHEN captured_amount_minor + #{amountMinor} = authorized_amount_minor
                THEN 'CAPTURED'
            ELSE 'PARTIALLY_CAPTURED'
        END,
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
      AND status IN ('AUTHORIZED', 'CAPTURE_PENDING', 'PARTIALLY_CAPTURED', 'UNKNOWN')
      AND currency = #{currency}
      AND captured_amount_minor + #{amountMinor} <= authorized_amount_minor
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

## 23.4 Mark Void Succeeded

```xml
<update id="markPaymentVoided">
    UPDATE payments
    SET status = 'VOIDED',
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
      AND status IN ('AUTHORIZED', 'VOID_PENDING', 'UNKNOWN')
      AND captured_amount_minor = 0
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

# 24. API Error Codes

| Error Code | HTTP | Kapan |
|---|---:|---|
| `PAYMENT_NOT_AUTHORIZED` | `409` | Capture sebelum auth. |
| `PAYMENT_ALREADY_CAPTURED` | `409` | Capture duplicate full amount. |
| `CAPTURE_AMOUNT_EXCEEDS_AUTHORIZED` | `422` | Capture > remaining authorized. |
| `CAPTURE_NOT_SUPPORTED` | `422` | Provider/payment method tidak support manual capture. |
| `PARTIAL_CAPTURE_NOT_SUPPORTED` | `422` | Provider tidak support partial capture. |
| `AUTHORIZATION_EXPIRED` | `409` | Auth sudah expired. |
| `PAYMENT_CANNOT_BE_VOIDED` | `409` | Payment sudah captured/failed/refunded. |
| `VOID_AFTER_CAPTURE_NOT_ALLOWED` | `409` | Void fully captured payment. |
| `SETTLEMENT_NOT_FOUND` | `404` | Settlement report/item tidak ditemukan. |
| `PAYMENT_STATUS_UNKNOWN` | `202` / `409` | Status butuh recovery. |

Contoh:

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/capture-amount-exceeds-authorized",
  "title": "Capture amount exceeds authorized amount",
  "status": 422,
  "detail": "Requested capture amount exceeds remaining authorized amount.",
  "errorCode": "CAPTURE_AMOUNT_EXCEEDS_AUTHORIZED",
  "paymentId": "0d675590-f9f3-4f97-9f77-34983fca2faa",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

# 25. Camunda Integration Point

Manual capture sering cocok diorkestrasi dengan Camunda.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Order Created] --> B[Authorize Payment]
    B --> C{Authorized?}

    C -->|No| D[Payment Failed]
    C -->|Requires Action| E[Wait Customer Action]
    E --> B

    C -->|Yes| F[Reserve Inventory]
    F --> G{Inventory Available?}

    G -->|No| H[Void Authorization]
    H --> I[Order Cancelled]

    G -->|Yes| J[Fraud Review]
    J --> K{Approved?}

    K -->|No| H
    K -->|Yes| L[Capture Payment]

    L --> M{Captured?}
    M -->|Yes| N[Fulfill Order]
    M -->|Unknown| O[Wait Provider Status/Webhook]
    M -->|Failed| P[Manual Review]
```

Rule praktis:

```text
Camunda orchestrates business workflow.
Payment service owns payment state transition and financial invariant.
```

# 27. Testing Strategy

## 27.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Capture before authorization | Reject. |
| Capture full authorized amount | `CAPTURED`. |
| Capture partial amount | `PARTIALLY_CAPTURED`. |
| Capture amount > authorized | Reject. |
| Second capture exceeds remaining amount | Reject. |
| Void authorized payment | `VOIDED`. |
| Void captured payment | Reject. |
| Timeout during capture | `UNKNOWN`, not `FAILED`. |
| Auth expiry job | `AUTH_EXPIRED`. |
| Settlement item matches capture | Mark settled. |
| Settlement amount mismatch | Create reconciliation mismatch. |

## 27.2 Integration Tests

```text
1. Create manual capture payment.
2. Mock provider returns AUTHORIZED.
3. Capture partial amount.
4. Verify payment_captures row.
5. Verify payments.captured_amount_minor updated.
6. Verify ledger entry inserted.
7. Verify outbox event inserted.
8. Capture remaining amount.
9. Verify status CAPTURED.
10. Import settlement report.
11. Verify capture marked SETTLED.
```

## 27.3 Concurrency Tests

```text
Scenario:
Authorized amount = 100000
Two concurrent capture requests = 70000 each

Expected:
Only one succeeds.
Second fails due to remaining authorized amount guard.
captured_amount_minor <= authorized_amount_minor always true.
```

---

# 28. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Menganggap `AUTHORIZED` sebagai `PAID` | Dana belum diterima. | Fulfillment besar tunggu capture. |
| Menganggap `CAPTURED` sebagai settled bank money | Settlement/payout bisa delay/fail. | Track settlement/payout. |
| Void setelah capture | Salah operation; harus refund. | Enforce state guard. |
| Capture retry tanpa idempotency | Bisa double capture. | Capture request ID + idempotency. |
| Partial capture tanpa detail table | Sulit audit. | `payment_captures`. |
| Auth expiry tidak dimonitor | Capture bisa gagal telat. | expiry job. |
| Timeout capture langsung failed | Provider mungkin sudah capture. | `UNKNOWN` + query status. |
| Capture/refund routing ulang | Provider salah. | Provider stickiness. |
| Settlement dicampur payment event biasa | Finance sulit reconcile. | settlement tables. |
| Tidak bedakan gross/fee/net | Accounting tidak cocok. | settlement item + ledger fee. |

# 29. Implementation Checklist

## Authorization

- [ ] Support `captureMode=AUTO`.
- [ ] Support `captureMode=MANUAL`.
- [ ] Simpan `payment_authorizations`.
- [ ] Simpan `expires_at` jika provider memberi/aturan internal tahu.
- [ ] Handle `REQUIRES_ACTION`.
- [ ] Timeout masuk `UNKNOWN`.

## Capture

- [ ] Endpoint `POST /payments/{paymentId}/capture`.
- [ ] Wajib `Idempotency-Key`.
- [ ] Optional `captureRequestId`.
- [ ] Simpan `payment_captures`.
- [ ] Enforce `captured <= authorized`.
- [ ] Support partial capture hanya jika provider mendukung.
- [ ] Insert ledger + outbox saat success.

## Void

- [ ] Endpoint `POST /payments/{paymentId}/void`.
- [ ] Void hanya sebelum full capture.
- [ ] Simpan `payment_voids`.
- [ ] Timeout void masuk `UNKNOWN`.
- [ ] Duplicate void idempotent.

## Settlement

- [ ] Ada `settlements`.
- [ ] Ada `settlement_items`.
- [ ] Track gross/fee/net.
- [ ] Match provider references dengan captures/refunds.
- [ ] Create mismatch items.
- [ ] Jangan set paid out tanpa payout/bank evidence.

## Operations

- [ ] Expiry job untuk authorization.
- [ ] Status query recovery job.
- [ ] Settlement import job.
- [ ] Payout reconciliation job.
- [ ] Alerts untuk capture unknown, auth expiring soon, settlement mismatch.

Target skenario:

```text
1. Create payment captureMode=MANUAL.
2. Mock provider returns AUTHORIZED.
3. Capture 40% amount.
4. Verify status PARTIALLY_CAPTURED.
5. Capture remaining 60%.
6. Verify status CAPTURED.
7. Try void after full capture -> rejected.
8. Create another manual payment.
9. Void before capture -> VOIDED.
10. Simulate capture timeout -> UNKNOWN.
11. Query mock provider -> CAPTURED.
12. Import settlement item -> SETTLED.
```

# Ringkasan

Authorization, capture, void, dan settlement harus dimodelkan sebagai lifecycle yang berbeda:

```text
Authorization = hold/reserve
Capture       = financial charge/money movement begins
Void          = release hold before capture
Settlement    = provider/acquirer confirms funds availability
Payout        = funds reach merchant bank
```

Prinsip penting:

```text
1. Jangan anggap AUTHORIZED sebagai PAID.
2. Jangan anggap CAPTURED sebagai PAID_OUT.
3. Capture harus idempotent.
4. Partial capture perlu detail table.
5. Void hanya sebelum full capture.
6. Timeout masuk UNKNOWN, bukan FAILED.
7. Settlement dan payout butuh reconciliation.
```
