## Response:
7/14/2026, 10:37:38 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 8/18**: webhook receiver, signature verification, dan inbox dedup. Fokusnya membuat endpoint webhook aman, cepat mengembalikan response ke provider, tahan duplicate/out-of-order event, dan tidak langsung merusak state payment.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 8/18 - Webhook Receiver, Signature Verification, dan Inbox Dedup

Seri ini membahas salah satu bagian paling rawan dalam payment integration: **webhook**.

Webhook adalah jalur utama bagi provider untuk memberi tahu perubahan status payment secara asynchronous. Dalam payment system, response awal dari provider sering belum final. Status final bisa datang lewat webhook beberapa detik, menit, bahkan jam kemudian.

Core idea:

```text id="vjuqul"
Webhook endpoint jangan langsung dianggap sebagai business logic endpoint.
Webhook endpoint adalah ingestion layer:
  1. receive raw payload
  2. verify authenticity
  3. deduplicate
  4. persist
  5. return fast
  6. process asynchronously
```

Stripe mewajibkan penggunaan **raw request body** untuk signature verification; perubahan apa pun terhadap body sebelum verifikasi bisa membuat signature verification gagal. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/webhooks?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen mendukung HMAC signature untuk webhook dan HMAC key harus disimpan aman di sistem penerima. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/development-resources/webhooks/secure-webhooks/verify-hmac-signatures?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PayPal mendokumentasikan verifikasi webhook melalui signature/certificate headers atau dengan mengirim data webhook ke verify signature endpoint PayPal. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/api/rest/webhooks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Introduction

Payment webhook harus didesain untuk menghadapi:

```text id="s2rcrj"
1. Duplicate webhook
2. Out-of-order webhook
3. Late webhook
4. Fake/spoofed webhook
5. Invalid signature
6. Webhook retry dari provider
7. Provider timeout saat menunggu response
8. Payload schema berubah
9. Event tidak bisa dimap ke payment internal
10. Event sukses tapi state internal sudah final
```

Kesalahan umum:

```text id="s2lgws"
Provider webhook masuk
  -> langsung update payments.status
  -> langsung insert ledger
  -> response lama
  -> kalau gagal, provider retry
  -> duplicate event bikin double ledger
```

Desain yang lebih aman:

```text id="rx9io2"
WebhookResource
  -> baca raw body
  -> verify signature
  -> parse minimal metadata
  -> insert inbox event with unique(provider, provider_event_id)
  -> return 200/202 fast
  -> async worker process inbox event
  -> state transition guarded
  -> ledger/outbox idempotent
```

---

# 2. Diagram

```mermaid id="kdi1gv"
flowchart TD
    A[Payment Provider Webhook] --> B[WebhookResource - Jersey/JAX-RS]

    B --> C[Read Raw Body]
    C --> D[Signature Verification]
    D --> E{Valid Signature?}

    E -->|No| F[Reject 400/401]
    E -->|Yes| G[Extract Provider Event ID]

    G --> H[Insert payment_inbox_events]
    H --> I{Duplicate?}

    I -->|Yes| J[Return 200 OK]
    I -->|No| K[Return 200/202 Quickly]

    K --> L[Webhook Processor Worker]
    J --> L

    L --> M[Claim Inbox Event FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED]
    M --> N[Parse Provider Payload]
    N --> O[Resolve Payment by Provider Reference]
    O --> P{Payment Found?}

    P -->|No| Q[Mark NEEDS_INVESTIGATION]
    P -->|Yes| R[Map Provider Event to Internal Event]

    R --> S[Validate State Transition]
    S --> T{Valid Transition?}

    T -->|No| U[Ignore / Exception Queue]
    T -->|Yes| V[Update Payment State]

    V --> W[Insert Payment Event]
    W --> X[Insert Ledger if Financial]
    X --> Y[Insert Outbox Event]
    Y --> Z[Mark Inbox PROCESSED]
```

---

# 3. Practical Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Webhook Endpoint** | Endpoint provider-specific. | `POST /webhooks/{provider}` |
| 2 | **Raw Body Handling** | Baca payload sebelum JSON mapping. | `InputStream` / `byte[] rawBody` |
| 3 | **Signature Verification** | Validasi authenticity/integrity. | `WebhookSignatureVerifier` |
| 4 | **Provider Header Extraction** | Ambil signature, event id, timestamp, cert URL. | `WebhookHeaders` |
| 5 | **Inbox Dedup** | Cegah event sama diproses dua kali. | `payment_inbox_events` |
| 6 | **Fast ACK** | Return cepat ke provider. | `200 OK` setelah persist aman |
| 7 | **Async Processor** | Proses event di worker. | `WebhookProcessor` |
| 8 | **Event Mapping** | Map provider event → internal event. | `ProviderWebhookMapper` |
| 9 | **Payment Resolution** | Cari payment/refund/capture internal dari provider reference. | `PaymentReferenceResolver` |
| 10 | **State Guard** | Cegah late/out-of-order event merusak status. | `PaymentStateTransitionService` |
| 11 | **Ledger Idempotency** | Cegah double ledger dari duplicate event. | unique ledger reference |
| 12 | **Outbox Publishing** | Publish internal event reliable. | `payment_outbox_events` |
| 13 | **Retry / DLQ** | Event gagal diproses bisa retry. | status `FAILED`, `DEAD_LETTER` |
| 14 | **Observability** | Monitor lag/failure/invalid signature. | metrics/logs |
| 15 | **Replay Tooling** | Reprocess event untuk recovery. | admin replay endpoint |

---

# 4. Webhook Endpoint Design

Gunakan endpoint provider-specific:

```text id="1onxb1"
POST /webhooks/stripe
POST /webhooks/adyen
POST /webhooks/paypal
POST /webhooks/mock
```

Atau generic:

```text id="xnap13"
POST /webhooks/{provider}
```

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text id="ufyhhf"
Gunakan /webhooks/{provider}, tapi internal handler tetap provider-specific.
```

Kenapa?

```text id="934qpy"
1. Signature verification berbeda per provider.
2. Header berbeda per provider.
3. Event id path berbeda.
4. Payload schema berbeda.
5. Response expectation bisa berbeda.
```

---

# 5. Important: Raw Body First

Jangan biarkan framework otomatis parse JSON sebelum signature verification, terutama untuk provider seperti Stripe.

Stripe mensyaratkan raw body request untuk signature verification dan memperingatkan bahwa manipulasi body oleh framework bisa membuat verification gagal. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/webhooks?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jakarta REST/JAX-RS memakai `MessageBodyReader` untuk memetakan HTTP request entity body ke method parameter, sehingga untuk webhook yang butuh raw body, lebih aman menerima `InputStream`/`byte[]` atau `String` raw body dan mengontrol parsing sendiri setelah signature verification. ([jakarta.ee](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh buruk:

```java id="um3ihn"
@POST
@Path("/webhooks/stripe")
public Response handleStripeWebhook(StripeWebhookEvent event) {
    // Body sudah diparse, raw body hilang/berubah.
}
```

Contoh lebih aman:

```java id="qv16gi"
@POST
@Path("/webhooks/{provider}")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public Response receiveWebhook(
        @PathParam("provider") String provider,
        @Context HttpHeaders headers,
        InputStream inputStream
) {
    byte[] rawBody = inputStream.readAllBytes();

    // verify signature using rawBody
    // parse JSON only after signature verification
}
```

---

# 6. Webhook Resource Skeleton

```java id="wir6ww"
package com.company.payment.api.webhook;

import com.company.payment.application.webhook.WebhookIngestionService;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.io.InputStream;

@Path("/webhooks")
public class WebhookResource {

    private final WebhookIngestionService ingestionService;

    public WebhookResource(WebhookIngestionService ingestionService) {
        this.ingestionService = ingestionService;
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{provider}")
    @Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
    @Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
    public Response receiveWebhook(
            @PathParam("provider") String provider,
            @Context HttpHeaders headers,
            @Context UriInfo uriInfo,
            InputStream requestBody
    ) {
        try {
            byte[] rawBody = requestBody.readAllBytes();

            WebhookIngestionResult result = ingestionService.ingest(
                    provider,
                    toHeaderMap(headers),
                    rawBody,
                    uriInfo.getRequestUri().toString()
            );

            /*
             * Return 200 even for duplicate valid event,
             * so provider does not keep retrying.
             */
            return Response.ok(new WebhookAckResponse(
                    result.inboxEventId(),
                    result.status()
            )).build();

        } catch (InvalidWebhookSignatureException e) {
            return Response.status(Response.Status.UNAUTHORIZED)
                    .entity(new WebhookAckResponse(null, "INVALID_SIGNATURE"))
                    .build();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            /*
             * Be careful:
             * If DB persist failed, return 500 so provider retries.
             * If processing failed after persist, return 200 and let worker retry.
             */
            return Response.serverError()
                    .entity(new WebhookAckResponse(null, "INGESTION_FAILED"))
                    .build();
        }
    }

    private java.util.Map<String, java.util.List<String>> toHeaderMap(HttpHeaders headers) {
        return headers.getRequestHeaders();
    }
}
```

```java id="hbit5f"
package com.company.payment.api.webhook;

import java.util.UUID;

public record WebhookAckResponse(
        UUID inboxEventId,
        String status
) {}
```

---

# 7. Ingestion vs Processing

Pisahkan dua fase:

```text id="1efzjk"
Ingestion:
  - raw body
  - verify signature
  - parse minimal event metadata
  - insert inbox
  - return response

Processing:
  - parse full payload
  - map provider event
  - resolve payment/refund/capture
  - apply state transition
  - insert ledger/outbox
  - mark processed
```

Alasan:

| Alasan | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Fast provider ACK | Provider tidak menunggu business logic panjang. |
| Retry internal controlled | Kalau business processing gagal, worker retry sendiri. |
| Dedup reliable | Event sudah durable sebelum diproses. |
| Debugging mudah | Raw payload tersimpan. |
| Replay bisa dilakukan | Admin bisa reprocess event. |

---

# 8. `payment_inbox_events` Schema

Gunakan schema yang cukup lengkap.

```sql id="ax9wfl"
CREATE TABLE payment_inbox_events (
    inbox_event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_event_id VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    event_type VARCHAR(120),
    event_created_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_authorization_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_capture_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_refund_id VARCHAR(200),

    raw_payload TEXT NOT NULL,
    parsed_payload JSONB,

    signature_headers JSONB,
    signature_valid BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,

    processing_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'RECEIVED',
    processing_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    locked_until TIMESTAMPTZ,
    next_attempt_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    last_error_code VARCHAR(100),
    last_error_message TEXT,

    received_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_inbox_processing_status
        CHECK (processing_status IN (
            'RECEIVED',
            'PROCESSING',
            'PROCESSED',
            'DUPLICATE',
            'IGNORED',
            'FAILED',
            'DEAD_LETTER',
            'NEEDS_INVESTIGATION'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql id="7n7xvg"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_inbox_provider_event
ON payment_inbox_events(provider, provider_event_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_inbox_status_next_attempt
ON payment_inbox_events(processing_status, next_attempt_at);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_inbox_provider_payment
ON payment_inbox_events(provider, provider_payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_inbox_provider_capture
ON payment_inbox_events(provider, provider_capture_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_inbox_provider_refund
ON payment_inbox_events(provider, provider_refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_inbox_received_at
ON payment_inbox_events(received_at);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_inbox_payload_gin
ON payment_inbox_events USING GIN(parsed_payload);
```

---

# 9. Insert Inbox Dedup Pattern

Gunakan unique constraint:

```sql id="roa85p"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_inbox_provider_event
ON payment_inbox_events(provider, provider_event_id);
```

Insert:

```sql id="t8vlyv"
INSERT INTO payment_inbox_events (
    inbox_event_id,
    provider,
    provider_event_id,
    event_type,
    event_created_at,
    provider_payment_id,
    provider_capture_id,
    provider_refund_id,
    raw_payload,
    parsed_payload,
    signature_headers,
    signature_valid,
    processing_status,
    received_at
) VALUES (
    #{inboxEventId},
    #{provider},
    #{providerEventId},
    #{eventType},
    #{eventCreatedAt},
    #{providerPaymentId},
    #{providerCaptureId},
    #{providerRefundId},
    #{rawPayload},
    CAST(#{parsedPayloadJson} AS jsonb),
    CAST(#{signatureHeadersJson} AS jsonb),
    true,
    'RECEIVED',
    now()
)
ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id)
DO NOTHING;
```

Decision:

| Affected Row | Meaning | Response ke Provider |
|---:|---|---|
| `1` | Event baru disimpan. | `200 OK` |
| `0` | Duplicate event. | `200 OK` |

Kenapa duplicate juga `200 OK`?

```text id="jklkus"
Karena kalau return error, provider akan retry lagi.
Duplicate valid event bukan error ingestion.
```

# 10. Signature Verification Interface

```java id="3gldek"
package com.company.payment.webhook.security;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public interface WebhookSignatureVerifier {

    boolean supports(String provider);

    WebhookVerificationResult verify(
            Map<String, List<String>> headers,
            byte[] rawBody,
            String endpointUri
    );
}
```

```java id="gejg2k"
package com.company.payment.webhook.security;

import java.util.Map;

public record WebhookVerificationResult(
        boolean valid,
        String provider,
        String failureReason,
        Map<String, Object> verificationMetadata
) {}
```

Registry:

```java id="vdann0"
package com.company.payment.webhook.security;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class WebhookSignatureVerifierRegistry {

    private final List<WebhookSignatureVerifier> verifiers;

    public WebhookSignatureVerifierRegistry(List<WebhookSignatureVerifier> verifiers) {
        this.verifiers = verifiers;
    }

    public WebhookVerificationResult verify(
            String provider,
            Map<String, List<String>> headers,
            byte[] rawBody,
            String endpointUri
    ) {
        return verifiers.stream()
                .filter(v -> v.supports(provider))
                .findFirst()
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported webhook provider: " + provider))
                .verify(headers, rawBody, endpointUri);
    }
}
```

---

# 11. Stripe Signature Verification

Stripe webhook verification membutuhkan:

```text id="d78eas"
1. raw request body
2. Stripe-Signature header
3. endpoint signing secret
```

Stripe secara eksplisit mengingatkan bahwa raw body harus tidak dimanipulasi framework sebelum signature verification. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/webhooks?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh skeleton:

```java id="1pgdts"
package com.company.payment.webhook.security.stripe;

import com.company.payment.webhook.security.WebhookSignatureVerifier;
import com.company.payment.webhook.security.WebhookVerificationResult;
import com.stripe.exception.SignatureVerificationException;
import com.stripe.net.Webhook;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class StripeWebhookSignatureVerifier implements WebhookSignatureVerifier {

    private final StripeWebhookSecretResolver secretResolver;

    public StripeWebhookSignatureVerifier(StripeWebhookSecretResolver secretResolver) {
        this.secretResolver = secretResolver;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean supports(String provider) {
        return "stripe".equalsIgnoreCase(provider) || "STRIPE".equalsIgnoreCase(provider);
    }

    @Override
    public WebhookVerificationResult verify(
            Map<String, List<String>> headers,
            byte[] rawBody,
            String endpointUri
    ) {
        String signature = firstHeader(headers, "Stripe-Signature");
        String endpointSecret = secretResolver.resolve(endpointUri);

        if (signature == null || signature.isBlank()) {
            return invalid("Missing Stripe-Signature header");
        }

        try {
            String payload = new String(rawBody, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

            Webhook.constructEvent(
                    payload,
                    signature,
                    endpointSecret
            );

            return new WebhookVerificationResult(
                    true,
                    "STRIPE",
                    null,
                    Map.of("signatureHeaderPresent", true)
            );

        } catch (SignatureVerificationException e) {
            return invalid("Stripe signature verification failed");
        }
    }

    private WebhookVerificationResult invalid(String reason) {
        return new WebhookVerificationResult(
                false,
                "STRIPE",
                reason,
                Map.of()
        );
    }

    private String firstHeader(Map<String, List<String>> headers, String name) {
        for (Map.Entry<String, List<String>> entry : headers.entrySet()) {
            if (entry.getKey().equalsIgnoreCase(name)) {
                return entry.getValue().isEmpty() ? null : entry.getValue().get(0);
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}
```

Practical note:

```text id="ynuxqx"
Stripe endpoint secret berbeda antara:
- Dashboard webhook endpoint
- Stripe CLI local forwarding
- Environment sandbox/prod
```

Stripe signature troubleshooting docs juga menekankan penggunaan endpoint secret yang benar dan raw request body yang tidak berubah. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/webhooks/signature?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 12. Adyen HMAC Verification

Adyen menggunakan HMAC signature untuk webhook. HMAC key dibuat/diatur di Customer Area pada konfigurasi webhook, lalu webhook event ditandatangani dengan key tersebut. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/development-resources/webhooks/secure-webhooks/verify-hmac-signatures?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Adyen punya format signature tergantung webhook type. Untuk standard notifications lama, HMAC biasanya dihitung dari string field notification item; untuk webhooks yang lebih baru, struktur bisa berbeda. Karena itu, buat verifier provider-specific dan jangan generalisasi terlalu cepat.

Skeleton konseptual:

```java id="d0w146"
package com.company.payment.webhook.security.adyen;

import com.company.payment.webhook.security.WebhookSignatureVerifier;
import com.company.payment.webhook.security.WebhookVerificationResult;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import javax.crypto.Mac;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class AdyenWebhookSignatureVerifier implements WebhookSignatureVerifier {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final AdyenHmacKeyResolver keyResolver;

    public AdyenWebhookSignatureVerifier(
            ObjectMapper objectMapper,
            AdyenHmacKeyResolver keyResolver
    ) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.keyResolver = keyResolver;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean supports(String provider) {
        return "adyen".equalsIgnoreCase(provider) || "ADYEN".equalsIgnoreCase(provider);
    }

    @Override
    public WebhookVerificationResult verify(
            Map<String, List<String>> headers,
            byte[] rawBody,
            String endpointUri
    ) {
        try {
            JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(rawBody);

            /*
             * This is provider-schema dependent.
             * For Adyen standard notifications, signature is often inside:
             * notificationItems[0].NotificationRequestItem.additionalData.hmacSignature
             */
            String receivedSignature = extractHmacSignature(root);

            if (receivedSignature == null || receivedSignature.isBlank()) {
                return invalid("Missing Adyen HMAC signature");
            }

            String dataToSign = buildAdyenDataToSign(root);
            String hmacKey = keyResolver.resolve(endpointUri, root);

            String computedSignature = hmacSha256Base64(dataToSign, hmacKey);

            boolean valid = MessageDigest.isEqual(
                    computedSignature.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8),
                    receivedSignature.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
            );

            if (!valid) {
                return invalid("Adyen HMAC signature verification failed");
            }

            return new WebhookVerificationResult(
                    true,
                    "ADYEN",
                    null,
                    Map.of("hmacSignaturePresent", true)
            );

        } catch (Exception e) {
            return invalid("Adyen HMAC verification error");
        }
    }

    private String extractHmacSignature(JsonNode root) {
        JsonNode notificationItem = root
                .path("notificationItems")
                .path(0)
                .path("NotificationRequestItem");

        return notificationItem
                .path("additionalData")
                .path("hmacSignature")
                .asText(null);
    }

    private String buildAdyenDataToSign(JsonNode root) {
        /*
         * Implement exactly according to Adyen webhook type documentation.
         * Do not invent ordering. Different Adyen webhook families may differ.
         */
        JsonNode item = root
                .path("notificationItems")
                .path(0)
                .path("NotificationRequestItem");

        return escape(item.path("pspReference").asText(""))
                + ":"
                + escape(item.path("originalReference").asText(""))
                + ":"
                + escape(item.path("merchantAccountCode").asText(""))
                + ":"
                + escape(item.path("merchantReference").asText(""))
                + ":"
                + escape(item.path("amount").path("value").asText(""))
                + ":"
                + escape(item.path("amount").path("currency").asText(""))
                + ":"
                + escape(item.path("eventCode").asText(""))
                + ":"
                + escape(item.path("success").asText(""));
    }

    private String escape(String value) {
        return value
                .replace("\\", "\\\\")
                .replace(":", "\\:");
    }

    private String hmacSha256Base64(String data, String key) throws Exception {
        Mac mac = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA256");
        mac.init(new SecretKeySpec(hexToBytes(key), "HmacSHA256"));
        byte[] rawHmac = mac.doFinal(data.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        return Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(rawHmac);
    }

    private byte[] hexToBytes(String hex) {
        int len = hex.length();
        byte[] result = new byte[len / 2];

        for (int i = 0; i < len; i += 2) {
            result[i / 2] = (byte) Integer.parseInt(hex.substring(i, i + 2), 16);
        }

        return result;
    }

    private WebhookVerificationResult invalid(String reason) {
        return new WebhookVerificationResult(
                false,
                "ADYEN",
                reason,
                Map.of()
        );
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

```text id="2qq882"
Untuk Adyen, implementasi HMAC harus mengikuti webhook type yang Anda aktifkan.
Jangan copy format standard notification jika integration Anda memakai webhook family lain.
```

---

# 13. PayPal Webhook Verification

PayPal webhook verification bisa dilakukan dengan dua pendekatan:

```text id="xdsqug"
1. Verifikasi signature sendiri memakai certificate/header.
2. Kirim webhook ID, body, dan header ke PayPal verify signature endpoint.
```

PayPal mendokumentasikan bahwa verification dapat dilakukan dengan menghitung CRC32 dan memverifikasi signature, atau mem-post message, webhook ID, dan header information ke endpoint PayPal untuk diverifikasi. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/api/rest/webhooks/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Dokumentasi PayPal webhook API juga memuat header seperti `PAYPAL-CERT-URL`, `PAYPAL-TRANSMISSION-ID`, dan `PAYPAL-TRANSMISSION-SIG` untuk signature verification. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/webhooks/v1/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Skeleton via PayPal verify endpoint:

```java id="0ip6ad"
package com.company.payment.webhook.security.paypal;

import com.company.payment.webhook.security.WebhookSignatureVerifier;
import com.company.payment.webhook.security.WebhookVerificationResult;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

public class PayPalWebhookSignatureVerifier implements WebhookSignatureVerifier {

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final PayPalVerifySignatureClient verifyClient;
    private final PayPalWebhookIdResolver webhookIdResolver;

    public PayPalWebhookSignatureVerifier(
            ObjectMapper objectMapper,
            PayPalVerifySignatureClient verifyClient,
            PayPalWebhookIdResolver webhookIdResolver
    ) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.verifyClient = verifyClient;
        this.webhookIdResolver = webhookIdResolver;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean supports(String provider) {
        return "paypal".equalsIgnoreCase(provider) || "PAYPAL".equalsIgnoreCase(provider);
    }

    @Override
    public WebhookVerificationResult verify(
            Map<String, List<String>> headers,
            byte[] rawBody,
            String endpointUri
    ) {
        try {
            String webhookId = webhookIdResolver.resolve(endpointUri);

            PayPalVerifyWebhookSignatureRequest request =
                    new PayPalVerifyWebhookSignatureRequest(
                            firstHeader(headers, "PAYPAL-AUTH-ALGO"),
                            firstHeader(headers, "PAYPAL-CERT-URL"),
                            firstHeader(headers, "PAYPAL-TRANSMISSION-ID"),
                            firstHeader(headers, "PAYPAL-TRANSMISSION-SIG"),
                            firstHeader(headers, "PAYPAL-TRANSMISSION-TIME"),
                            webhookId,
                            objectMapper.readTree(rawBody)
                    );

            boolean verified = verifyClient.verify(request);

            if (!verified) {
                return invalid("PayPal webhook signature verification failed");
            }

            return new WebhookVerificationResult(
                    true,
                    "PAYPAL",
                    null,
                    Map.of("verificationMode", "PAYPAL_VERIFY_ENDPOINT")
            );

        } catch (Exception e) {
            return invalid("PayPal webhook verification error");
        }
    }

    private String firstHeader(Map<String, List<String>> headers, String name) {
        for (Map.Entry<String, List<String>> entry : headers.entrySet()) {
            if (entry.getKey().equalsIgnoreCase(name)) {
                return entry.getValue().isEmpty() ? null : entry.getValue().get(0);
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    private WebhookVerificationResult invalid(String reason) {
        return new WebhookVerificationResult(
                false,
                "PAYPAL",
                reason,
                Map.of()
        );
    }
}
```

Trade-off:

| Mode | Kelebihan | Kekurangan |
|---|---|---|
| Verify sendiri | Tidak bergantung call balik ke PayPal. | Implementasi crypto/cert lebih kompleks. |
| Verify endpoint PayPal | Lebih sederhana. | Ada latency dan dependency external saat webhook masuk. |

Rekomendasi praktis awal:

```text id="cp640c"
Untuk fase awal, gunakan verify endpoint PayPal agar correctness lebih mudah.
Untuk high-throughput/latency-sensitive, evaluasi local verification + cert caching.
```

---

# 14. Webhook Metadata Extraction

Setelah signature valid, parse minimal metadata.

```java id="mj4jxl"
package com.company.payment.webhook;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;

public record WebhookEnvelope(
        String provider,
        String providerEventId,
        String eventType,
        Instant eventCreatedAt,

        String providerPaymentId,
        String providerAuthorizationId,
        String providerCaptureId,
        String providerRefundId,

        byte[] rawBody,
        Map<String, Object> parsedPayload,
        Map<String, Object> signatureMetadata
) {}
```

Interface:

```java id="22akxd"
package com.company.payment.webhook;

public interface WebhookEnvelopeParser {

    boolean supports(String provider);

    WebhookEnvelope parse(String provider, byte[] rawBody, WebhookSignatureContext signatureContext);
}
```

Provider-specific parser:

```text id="nl6x8m"
Stripe:
  event.id
  event.type
  event.created
  event.data.object.id

Adyen:
  pspReference
  originalReference
  eventCode
  merchantReference
  success

PayPal:
  event.id
  event.event_type
  event.create_time
  event.resource.id
```

---

# 15. Ingestion Service

```java id="x8bbvr"
package com.company.payment.application.webhook;

import com.company.payment.webhook.*;
import com.company.payment.webhook.security.*;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public class WebhookIngestionService {

    private final WebhookSignatureVerifierRegistry verifierRegistry;
    private final WebhookEnvelopeParserRegistry parserRegistry;
    private final PaymentInboxEventRepository inboxRepository;
    private final JsonSerializer jsonSerializer;

    public WebhookIngestionService(
            WebhookSignatureVerifierRegistry verifierRegistry,
            WebhookEnvelopeParserRegistry parserRegistry,
            PaymentInboxEventRepository inboxRepository,
            JsonSerializer jsonSerializer
    ) {
        this.verifierRegistry = verifierRegistry;
        this.parserRegistry = parserRegistry;
        this.inboxRepository = inboxRepository;
        this.jsonSerializer = jsonSerializer;
    }

    public WebhookIngestionResult ingest(
            String provider,
            Map<String, List<String>> headers,
            byte[] rawBody,
            String endpointUri
    ) {
        WebhookVerificationResult verification =
                verifierRegistry.verify(provider, headers, rawBody, endpointUri);

        if (!verification.valid()) {
            throw new InvalidWebhookSignatureException(
                    provider,
                    verification.failureReason()
            );
        }

        WebhookEnvelope envelope =
                parserRegistry.parse(provider, rawBody, new WebhookSignatureContext(
                        verification.verificationMetadata()
                ));

        UUID inboxEventId = UUID.randomUUID();

        PaymentInboxEventRecord record = new PaymentInboxEventRecord(
                inboxEventId,
                envelope.provider(),
                envelope.providerEventId(),
                envelope.eventType(),
                envelope.eventCreatedAt(),
                envelope.providerPaymentId(),
                envelope.providerAuthorizationId(),
                envelope.providerCaptureId(),
                envelope.providerRefundId(),
                new String(rawBody, StandardCharsets.UTF_8),
                jsonSerializer.toJson(envelope.parsedPayload()),
                jsonSerializer.toJson(headers),
                true
        );

        int inserted = inboxRepository.insertIfAbsent(record);

        if (inserted == 0) {
            return new WebhookIngestionResult(null, "DUPLICATE");
        }

        return new WebhookIngestionResult(inboxEventId, "RECEIVED");
    }
}
```

```java id="zlcj7c"
package com.company.payment.application.webhook;

import java.util.UUID;

public record WebhookIngestionResult(
        UUID inboxEventId,
        String status
) {}
```

---

# 16. Worker Claim Pattern

Webhook processor harus aman untuk multi-worker/multi-pod.

Gunakan:

```sql id="km0nsd"
SELECT *
FROM payment_inbox_events
WHERE processing_status IN ('RECEIVED', 'FAILED')
  AND next_attempt_at <= now()
ORDER BY received_at ASC
LIMIT #{limit}
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
```

Lalu mark processing:

```sql id="u6ng13"
UPDATE payment_inbox_events
SET processing_status = 'PROCESSING',
    processing_attempts = processing_attempts + 1,
    locked_until = now() + interval '5 minutes',
    last_error_code = NULL,
    last_error_message = NULL
WHERE inbox_event_id = #{inboxEventId}
  AND processing_status IN ('RECEIVED', 'FAILED');
```

PostgreSQL `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` sering dipakai untuk worker queue karena worker lain akan melewati row yang sedang terkunci. Ini cocok untuk inbox/outbox worker agar event tidak diklaim dua worker sekaligus.

---

# 17. Webhook Processor Skeleton

```java id="9kvkgq"
package com.company.payment.application.webhook;

import java.util.List;

public class WebhookProcessorWorker {

    private final PaymentInboxEventRepository inboxRepository;
    private final WebhookEventProcessor processor;
    private final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;

    public void processBatch(int batchSize) {
        List<PaymentInboxEventRecord> events =
                transactionTemplate.execute(() -> inboxRepository.claimNextBatch(batchSize));

        for (PaymentInboxEventRecord event : events) {
            processSingle(event);
        }
    }

    private void processSingle(PaymentInboxEventRecord event) {
        try {
            transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                processor.process(event);
                inboxRepository.markProcessed(event.inboxEventId());
                return null;
            });

        } catch (RecoverableWebhookProcessingException e) {
            transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                inboxRepository.markFailedForRetry(
                        event.inboxEventId(),
                        e.errorCode(),
                        e.getMessage(),
                        e.nextAttemptAt()
                );
                return null;
            });

        } catch (NonRecoverableWebhookProcessingException e) {
            transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                inboxRepository.markDeadLetter(
                        event.inboxEventId(),
                        e.errorCode(),
                        e.getMessage()
                );
                return null;
            });
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 18. Event Processor

```java id="uqzu1x"
package com.company.payment.application.webhook;

public class WebhookEventProcessor {

    private final ProviderWebhookEventMapperRegistry mapperRegistry;
    private final PaymentReferenceResolver referenceResolver;
    private final PaymentStateTransitionService transitionService;
    private final PaymentEventRepository paymentEventRepository;
    private final LedgerRepository ledgerRepository;
    private final OutboxRepository outboxRepository;

    public void process(PaymentInboxEventRecord inboxEvent) {
        ProviderWebhookEvent mappedEvent =
                mapperRegistry.map(inboxEvent.provider(), inboxEvent.parsedPayloadJson());

        PaymentReference reference =
                referenceResolver.resolve(mappedEvent);

        if (reference.notFound()) {
            throw new RecoverableWebhookProcessingException(
                    "PAYMENT_REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND",
                    "Payment reference not found for webhook event",
                    mappedEvent.retryLater()
            );
        }

        TransitionResult result =
                transitionService.applyProviderEvent(reference.paymentId(), mappedEvent);

        if (result.ignored()) {
            paymentEventRepository.insertIgnoredWebhookEvent(
                    reference.paymentId(),
                    inboxEvent,
                    mappedEvent,
                    result.reason()
            );
            return;
        }

        paymentEventRepository.insertPaymentEvent(
                reference.paymentId(),
                inboxEvent,
                mappedEvent,
                result.oldStatus(),
                result.newStatus()
        );

        if (result.requiresLedgerEntry()) {
            ledgerRepository.insertIfAbsent(result.ledgerEntry());
        }

        if (result.requiresOutboxEvent()) {
            outboxRepository.insert(result.outboxEvent());
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 19. Provider Event Mapping

Internal event model:

```java id="qfx6b3"
package com.company.payment.webhook.mapping;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;

public record ProviderWebhookEvent(
        String provider,
        String providerEventId,
        String eventType,

        ProviderWebhookEventCategory category,

        String providerPaymentId,
        String providerAuthorizationId,
        String providerCaptureId,
        String providerRefundId,

        long amountMinor,
        String currency,

        boolean success,
        String rawStatus,
        Instant eventCreatedAt,

        Map<String, Object> rawData
) {
    public boolean retryLater() {
        return category == ProviderWebhookEventCategory.PAYMENT_PENDING
                || category == ProviderWebhookEventCategory.UNKNOWN;
    }
}
```

```java id="q5hmw7"
package com.company.payment.webhook.mapping;

public enum ProviderWebhookEventCategory {
    PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED,
    PAYMENT_CAPTURED,
    PAYMENT_FAILED,
    PAYMENT_CANCELLED,

    CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED,
    CAPTURE_FAILED,

    REFUND_SUCCEEDED,
    REFUND_FAILED,

    DISPUTE_OPENED,
    DISPUTE_WON,
    DISPUTE_LOST,

    SETTLEMENT_RECEIVED,
    PAYOUT_PAID,

    PAYMENT_PENDING,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

Mapping table contoh:

| Provider Event | Internal Category | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Stripe `payment_intent.succeeded` | `PAYMENT_CAPTURED` | Auto-capture success. |
| Stripe `payment_intent.payment_failed` | `PAYMENT_FAILED` | Payment failed. |
| Stripe `payment_intent.canceled` | `PAYMENT_CANCELLED` | Cancelled/voided. |
| Stripe `charge.refunded` | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED` | Refund event. |
| Adyen `AUTHORISATION` success true | `PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED` | Depends capture mode. |
| Adyen `CAPTURE` success true | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED` | Capture confirmed. |
| Adyen `REFUND` success true | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED` | Refund confirmed. |
| PayPal capture completed event | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED` | Capture lifecycle. |
| PayPal refund completed event | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED` | Refund lifecycle. |

Catatan penting:

```text id="zl5h0r"
Event mapping harus provider-specific dan version-aware.
Jangan pakai string matching longgar tanpa fixture tests.
```

---

# 20. Payment Reference Resolution

Webhook harus resolve ke internal entity.

Urutan resolusi:

```text id="1i0rud"
1. provider + provider_payment_id -> payments
2. provider + provider_authorization_id -> payment_authorizations
3. provider + provider_capture_id -> payment_captures
4. provider + provider_refund_id -> refunds
5. merchantReference / metadata[payment_id] -> payments
6. if not found -> NEEDS_INVESTIGATION or retry later
```

Repository:

```java id="0xhyuy"
package com.company.payment.application.webhook;

public interface PaymentReferenceResolver {

    PaymentReference resolve(ProviderWebhookEvent event);
}
```

```java id="kuaf38"
package com.company.payment.application.webhook;

import java.util.UUID;

public record PaymentReference(
        UUID paymentId,
        UUID authorizationId,
        UUID captureId,
        UUID refundId,
        boolean found
) {
    public static PaymentReference notFound() {
        return new PaymentReference(null, null, null, null, false);
    }

    public boolean notFound() {
        return !found;
    }
}
```

SQL examples:

```sql id="p9oy7u"
SELECT payment_id
FROM payments
WHERE provider = #{provider}
  AND provider_payment_id = #{providerPaymentId};
```

```sql id="2d198i"
SELECT payment_id, capture_id
FROM payment_captures
WHERE provider = #{provider}
  AND provider_capture_id = #{providerCaptureId};
```

```sql id="1cu1h5"
SELECT payment_id, refund_id
FROM refunds
WHERE provider = #{provider}
  AND provider_refund_id = #{providerRefundId};
```

---

# 21. Out-of-Order Event Handling

Webhook bisa datang tidak sesuai urutan.

Contoh:

```text id="wwn5a7"
1. CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED webhook datang dulu
2. AUTHORIZED webhook datang belakangan
```

Kalau late `AUTHORIZED` overwrite `CAPTURED`, status rusak.

Gunakan transition guard.

```java id="nejrkm"
public TransitionResult applyProviderEvent(UUID paymentId, ProviderWebhookEvent event) {
    Payment payment = paymentRepository.findByIdForUpdate(paymentId);

    return switch (event.category()) {
        case PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED -> applyAuthorized(payment, event);
        case PAYMENT_CAPTURED, CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED -> applyCaptured(payment, event);
        case PAYMENT_FAILED -> applyFailed(payment, event);
        case REFUND_SUCCEEDED -> applyRefundSucceeded(payment, event);
        default -> TransitionResult.ignored("Unsupported event category");
    };
}

private TransitionResult applyAuthorized(Payment payment, ProviderWebhookEvent event) {
    if (payment.status() == PaymentStatus.CAPTURED
            || payment.status() == PaymentStatus.PARTIALLY_CAPTURED
            || payment.status() == PaymentStatus.REFUNDED) {
        return TransitionResult.ignored(
                "Late authorization event ignored because payment is already financially advanced"
        );
    }

    if (payment.status() == PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED) {
        return TransitionResult.ignored("Duplicate authorization event");
    }

    paymentRepository.markAuthorized(payment.paymentId(), event.amountMinor(), event.currency());
    return TransitionResult.updated(payment.status(), PaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED);
}
```

---

# 22. State Transition Guard Matrix

| Current State | Incoming Event | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `AUTHORIZATION_PENDING` | `PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED` | Mark `AUTHORIZED`. |
| `REQUIRES_ACTION` | `PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED` | Mark `AUTHORIZED`. |
| `AUTHORIZED` | `PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED` | Ignore duplicate. |
| `CAPTURED` | `PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED` | Ignore late event. |
| `AUTHORIZED` | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED` | Mark `CAPTURED`/`PARTIALLY_CAPTURED`. |
| `CAPTURE_PENDING` | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED` | Mark captured. |
| `UNKNOWN` | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED` | Mark captured if amount valid. |
| `FAILED` | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED` | Needs investigation, do not blindly overwrite. |
| `CAPTURED` | `PAYMENT_FAILED` | Ignore or investigate; do not downgrade. |
| `REFUNDED` | `CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED` | Ignore/investigate. |
| `CAPTURED` | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED` | Apply refund. |
| `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED` | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED` | Apply incremental refund. |
| `FAILED` | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED` | Needs investigation. |

Practical rule:

```text id="272i5k"
Late event boleh disimpan sebagai audit event,
tapi tidak boleh otomatis downgrade state final.
```

---

# 23. Ledger Idempotency

Duplicate webhook tidak boleh membuat duplicate ledger entry.

Gunakan unique reference:

```sql id="i5c31b"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_ledger_entries_reference
ON ledger_entries(reference_type, reference_id, entry_type);
```

Contoh:

```text id="pylhat"
reference_type = PROVIDER_CAPTURE
reference_id   = stripe_charge_123 / adyen_psp_capture_123
entry_type     = PAYMENT_CAPTURED
```

Insert:

```sql id="s5mawv"
INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    account_code,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    description
)
VALUES (
    #{ledgerEntryId},
    #{paymentId},
    #{entryType},
    #{direction},
    #{amountMinor},
    #{currency},
    #{accountCode},
    #{referenceType},
    #{referenceId},
    #{description}
)
ON CONFLICT (reference_type, reference_id, entry_type)
DO NOTHING;
```

---

# 24. Webhook Response Codes

| Scenario | Response | Reason |
|---|---:|---|
| Signature valid, new event persisted | `200 OK` | Provider tidak perlu retry. |
| Signature valid, duplicate event | `200 OK` | Duplicate bukan ingestion error. |
| Signature invalid | `400` / `401` | Jangan persist sebagai valid event. |
| Unsupported provider | `404` / `400` | Misconfiguration. |
| Cannot read body | `400` | Bad request. |
| DB unavailable before persist | `500` | Provider harus retry. |
| Business processing later fails | `200` dari ingestion | Worker retry internal. |
| Event type unknown but persisted | `200` | Mark ignored/unknown internally. |

Catatan:

```text id="94d40h"
Jangan return 500 hanya karena business transition gagal setelah event berhasil dipersist.
Kalau event sudah durable, recovery menjadi tanggung jawab internal worker.
```

---

# 25. Handling Unknown Event Types

Provider bisa menambah event baru.

Jangan crash seluruh webhook system.

```text id="b7if5s"
Unknown event:
  -> signature valid
  -> insert inbox
  -> processor marks IGNORED or NEEDS_INVESTIGATION
  -> alert if event type important/unrecognized
```

Mapping:

```java id="27ey5v"
default -> new ProviderWebhookEvent(
        provider,
        providerEventId,
        eventType,
        ProviderWebhookEventCategory.UNKNOWN,
        providerPaymentId,
        null,
        null,
        null,
        0,
        null,
        false,
        rawStatus,
        eventCreatedAt,
        rawData
);
```

---

# 26. Retry and Dead Letter

Processor retry policy:

| Attempt | Delay |
|---:|---:|
| 1 | immediate |
| 2 | 1 minute |
| 3 | 5 minutes |
| 4 | 15 minutes |
| 5 | 1 hour |
| >5 | dead letter / investigation |

SQL update failed:

```sql id="5zd2gy"
UPDATE payment_inbox_events
SET processing_status = 'FAILED',
    last_error_code = #{errorCode},
    last_error_message = #{errorMessage},
    next_attempt_at = #{nextAttemptAt},
    locked_until = NULL,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE inbox_event_id = #{inboxEventId};
```

Dead letter:

```sql id="j75z4v"
UPDATE payment_inbox_events
SET processing_status = 'DEAD_LETTER',
    last_error_code = #{errorCode},
    last_error_message = #{errorMessage},
    locked_until = NULL,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE inbox_event_id = #{inboxEventId};
```

Kapan `NEEDS_INVESTIGATION`?

```text id="m2i089"
- provider reference tidak ditemukan setelah beberapa retry
- event contradicts final state
- amount mismatch
- currency mismatch
- provider event says captured but internal payment failed
```

---

# 27. Replay Webhook

Ops/support perlu replay.

Endpoint admin:

```http id="z75x1k"
POST /admin/webhooks/inbox/{inboxEventId}/replay
```

Rule:

```text id="q6vu3h"
Replay tidak boleh bypass dedup/transition guard.
Replay hanya mengubah status inbox agar diproses ulang.
Ledger/outbox tetap idempotent.
```

SQL:

```sql id="m2zx84"
UPDATE payment_inbox_events
SET processing_status = 'RECEIVED',
    next_attempt_at = now(),
    locked_until = NULL,
    last_error_code = NULL,
    last_error_message = NULL,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE inbox_event_id = #{inboxEventId}
  AND processing_status IN ('FAILED', 'DEAD_LETTER', 'NEEDS_INVESTIGATION', 'IGNORED');
```

Audit:

```text id="58fwry"
Simpan siapa yang replay, kapan, alasan, dan correlationId.
```

---

# 28. Security Checklist

| Area | Checklist |
|---|---|
| Raw body | Jangan parse sebelum signature verification. |
| Signature | Verify per provider. |
| Secret | Webhook secret/HMAC key disimpan di secret manager. |
| Rotation | Support multiple active secrets saat rotation. |
| Timestamp | Jika provider punya timestamp tolerance, enforce replay protection. |
| Headers | Simpan signature headers untuk audit, tapi jangan log secret. |
| TLS | Webhook endpoint wajib HTTPS di production. |
| IP allowlist | Optional defense-in-depth; jangan gantikan signature verification. |
| Payload | Jangan percaya field amount/status tanpa signature valid. |
| Logging | Jangan log payload penuh jika mengandung PII/sensitive data. |
| Replay attack | Signature timestamp + dedup provider event id. |
| Unknown event | Persist + mark ignored/investigation, bukan crash. |

---

# 29. Secret Rotation Pattern

Webhook secret bisa diganti.

Support:

```text id="tt4ib3"
active secrets:
  - current
  - previous during grace period
```

Verifier mencoba semua active secret:

```java id="qo5544"
for (String secret : secretResolver.resolveActiveSecrets(provider, endpointUri)) {
    if (verifyWithSecret(secret, headers, rawBody)) {
        return valid(secret.fingerprint());
    }
}
return invalid();
```

Simpan fingerprint, bukan secret:

```json id="8p25dl"
{
  "secretFingerprint": "sha256:8ab1...",
  "verifiedAt": "2026-07-14T15:00:00Z"
}
```

---

# 30. Observability

Metrics wajib:

| Metric | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `webhook.received.total` | Jumlah webhook diterima per provider. |
| `webhook.signature.invalid.total` | Signature invalid. |
| `webhook.duplicate.total` | Duplicate event. |
| `webhook.ingestion.failed.total` | Gagal persist. |
| `webhook.processing.succeeded.total` | Processing sukses. |
| `webhook.processing.failed.total` | Processing gagal retry. |
| `webhook.dead_letter.total` | Dead letter. |
| `webhook.needs_investigation.total` | Manual investigation. |
| `webhook.processing.lag.seconds` | Selisih received_at sampai processed_at. |
| `webhook.provider.event_age.seconds` | Selisih provider event time sampai received_at. |

Structured log:

```json id="f2s9qw"
{
  "event": "webhook_ingested",
  "provider": "STRIPE",
  "providerEventId": "evt_123",
  "eventType": "payment_intent.succeeded",
  "providerPaymentId": "pi_123",
  "inboxEventId": "0f5e3c63-64ee-41a7-a9bc-5017c9465eb3",
  "duplicate": false,
  "signatureValid": true,
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

Alert:

```text id="gglyye"
- invalid signature spike
- dead letter > threshold
- processing lag > SLA
- unknown event type spike
- reference not found spike
- duplicate webhook spike abnormal
```

# 31. Testing Strategy

## 31.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Valid Stripe signature | Accepted. |
| Stripe body changed after signing | Rejected. |
| Missing signature header | Rejected. |
| Valid Adyen HMAC | Accepted. |
| Invalid Adyen HMAC | Rejected. |
| PayPal verify endpoint returns success | Accepted. |
| Duplicate provider event id | Insert affected row `0`, return 200. |
| Unknown event type | Persist, mark ignored/unknown. |
| Late authorized event after captured | Ignored, no downgrade. |
| Duplicate capture webhook | No duplicate ledger. |

## 31.2 Integration Tests

```text id="a165p3"
1. Start PostgreSQL Testcontainers.
2. POST valid webhook raw payload.
3. Verify inbox row inserted.
4. POST same event again.
5. Verify only one inbox row.
6. Run worker.
7. Verify payment status updated.
8. Verify payment_event inserted.
9. Verify ledger inserted once.
10. Run worker/replay again.
11. Verify ledger still once.
```

```text id="9t5c1j"
src/test/resources/webhook-fixtures/

    payment_intent_succeeded.json
```

# 33. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Parse JSON sebelum verify signature | Raw body berubah, signature gagal/invalid trust. | Verify raw body dulu. |
| Webhook langsung update payment tanpa inbox | Duplicate event bisa double update. | Inbox dedup. |
| Return 500 setelah event sudah tersimpan | Provider retry terus, duplicate bertambah. | Return 200 setelah durable persist. |
| Tidak simpan raw payload | Tidak bisa audit/replay. | Simpan raw payload. |
| Tidak simpan provider event id | Tidak bisa dedup. | Unique provider + event id. |
| Late event overwrite final status | State rusak. | Transition guard. |
| Ledger insert tanpa unique reference | Duplicate ledger. | Unique ledger reference. |
| Tidak support secret rotation | Rotation downtime. | Active current + previous secret. |
| Semua unknown event di-drop | Event penting bisa hilang. | Persist + ignored/investigation. |
| Webhook processing sync panjang | Provider timeout dan retry. | Async worker. |
| Menganggap IP allowlist cukup | IP bisa berubah/kurang reliable. | Signature verification wajib. |

---

# 34. Implementation Checklist

## Endpoint

- [ ] `POST /webhooks/{provider}`.
- [ ] Baca raw body sebagai `byte[]`.
- [ ] Jangan bind langsung ke provider DTO sebelum verification.
- [ ] Return cepat setelah inbox insert.
- [ ] Duplicate valid event return `200 OK`.

## Security

- [ ] Stripe raw body + `Stripe-Signature` + endpoint secret.
- [ ] Adyen HMAC verification sesuai webhook type.
- [ ] PayPal signature verification via endpoint atau local cert verification.
- [ ] Secrets di secret manager.
- [ ] Support secret rotation.
- [ ] Invalid signature tidak diproses.

## Inbox

- [ ] Table `payment_inbox_events`.
- [ ] Unique `(provider, provider_event_id)`.
- [ ] Simpan raw payload.
- [ ] Simpan parsed payload.
- [ ] Simpan signature headers metadata.
- [ ] Ada status `RECEIVED`, `PROCESSING`, `PROCESSED`, `FAILED`, `DEAD_LETTER`.

## Worker

- [ ] Claim pakai `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`.
- [ ] Map provider event ke internal event.
- [ ] Resolve payment/refund/capture.
- [ ] Apply state transition guard.
- [ ] Insert payment event.
- [ ] Insert ledger idempotently.
- [ ] Insert outbox event.
- [ ] Mark processed.

## Failure Handling

- [ ] Reference not found → retry/needs investigation.
- [ ] Unknown event type → ignored/investigation, bukan crash.
- [ ] Duplicate event → no-op.
- [ ] Out-of-order event → guarded.
- [ ] Late event → audit only/no downgrade.
- [ ] Processing failure → retry with backoff.
- [ ] Max attempts → dead letter.

## Observability

- [ ] Metrics invalid signature.
- [ ] Metrics duplicate.
- [ ] Metrics processing lag.
- [ ] Metrics dead letter.
- [ ] Logs provider event id.
- [ ] Admin replay endpoint.

```text id="haionv"
  webhook/parser/
    WebhookEnvelopeParser.java
    StripeWebhookEnvelopeParser.java
    AdyenWebhookEnvelopeParser.java
    PayPalWebhookEnvelopeParser.java

  webhook/mapping/
    ProviderWebhookEvent.java
    ProviderWebhookEventMapper.java

  infrastructure/persistence/
    PaymentInboxEventMapper.java
    PaymentInboxEventMapper.xml
```

Target test:

```text id="yrj6zd"
1. Valid webhook inserts inbox event.
2. Invalid signature rejected.
3. Duplicate webhook returns 200 and does not insert second row.
4. Worker processes CAPTURE_SUCCEEDED event.
5. Duplicate process does not create duplicate ledger.
6. Late AUTHORIZED after CAPTURED is ignored.
7. Unknown event type marked IGNORED.
8. Reference not found goes to FAILED retry, then NEEDS_INVESTIGATION.
9. Replay failed event processes successfully.
10. Processing lag metric emitted.
```

---

# Ringkasan

Webhook payment harus didesain sebagai **durable asynchronous ingestion pipeline**, bukan sekadar REST callback biasa.

Prinsip utamanya:

```text id="2gaed7"
1. Verify signature before trust.
2. Use raw body for providers that require it.
3. Persist valid webhook before business processing.
4. Dedup by provider + provider_event_id.
5. Return fast to provider.
6. Process asynchronously.
7. Guard state transitions.
8. Make ledger/outbox idempotent.
9. Support retry, dead letter, and replay.
10. Never let late webhook downgrade financial truth.
```

Dengan desain ini, payment service akan tahan terhadap duplicate webhook, fake webhook, provider retry, out-of-order event, dan partial failure.

---

**Seri 9/18 - Outbox Pattern dan Event Publishing ke Order/Billing/Fulfillment: desain reliable event publishing setelah payment state berubah, event schema, ordering, retry publisher, idempotent consumer, dan failure recovery.**
