# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 9/18 - Outbox Pattern dan Event Publishing ke Order/Billing/Fulfillment

Seri ini fokus pada **reliable event publishing** setelah payment state berubah.

Masalah utamanya disebut **dual-write problem**:

```text id="r6wcnf"
Payment service update database:
  payments.status = CAPTURED

Lalu payment service publish event:
  PaymentCaptured -> Order/Billing/Fulfillment

Apa yang terjadi kalau DB update sukses, tapi publish event gagal?
```

Tanpa outbox, sistem bisa masuk kondisi berbahaya:

```text id="pz1z40"
Payment sudah CAPTURED di database,
tetapi Order Service tidak pernah menerima PaymentCaptured.
Order tetap unpaid.
Fulfillment tidak jalan.
Billing tidak update.
Customer sudah ter-charge.
```

Transactional Outbox menyelesaikan problem ini dengan cara menyimpan **business state change** dan **event yang harus dipublish** dalam transaksi database yang sama. AWS Prescriptive Guidance menjelaskan outbox pattern sebagai solusi untuk dual-write issue ketika satu operasi harus menulis ke database dan mengirim message/event notification ke sistem lain. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/prescriptive-guidance/latest/cloud-design-patterns/transactional-outbox.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Microservices.io juga mendeskripsikan transactional outbox sebagai pattern ketika service perlu update aggregate di database dan publish message/event tanpa memakai distributed transaction. ([microservices.io](https://microservices.io/patterns/data/transactional-outbox.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Introduction

Payment service tidak boleh hanya mengubah status internal. Ia harus memberi tahu service lain:

| Payment Event | Consumer Umum | Dampak |
|---|---|---|
| `PaymentAuthorized` | Order, Fulfillment | Order boleh reserve stock / continue workflow. |
| `PaymentRequiresAction` | Checkout/UI, Order | Customer perlu 3DS/redirect/action. |
| `PaymentCaptured` | Order, Billing, Fulfillment, Notification | Order boleh diproses/fulfilled. |
| `PaymentFailed` | Order, Checkout, Notification | Order unpaid / retry payment. |
| `PaymentVoided` | Order, Inventory | Release order/reservation. |
| `RefundRequested` | Order, Support, Billing | Refund sedang diproses. |
| `RefundSucceeded` | Order, Billing, Notification | Order/refund state update. |
| `DisputeOpened` | Risk, Support, Billing | Case/evidence workflow. |
| `PaymentSettlementMatched` | Finance | Reconciliation matched. |
| `PaymentReconciliationMismatch` | Finance/Ops | Investigation needed. |

Problemnya: publish event ke broker tidak bisa dijadikan satu transaksi ACID dengan PostgreSQL biasa.

Maka pola yang aman:

```text id="ld1w1t"
BEGIN
  update payments
  insert payment_events
  insert ledger_entries
  insert payment_outbox_events
COMMIT

OutboxPublisher later:
  select pending outbox
  publish to broker
  mark published
```

Debezium Outbox Event Router juga menjelaskan outbox pattern sebagai cara aman dan reliable untuk bertukar data antar microservice dengan menghindari inconsistency antara state internal service di database dan event yang dikonsumsi service lain. ([Debezium](https://debezium.io/documentation/reference/stable/transformations/outbox-event-router.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Diagram

```mermaid id="v9gm24"
flowchart TD
    A[Payment API / Webhook Processor] --> B[PaymentApplicationService]

    B --> C[PostgreSQL Transaction]
    C --> D[Update payments]
    C --> E[Insert payment_events]
    C --> F[Insert ledger_entries]
    C --> G[Insert payment_outbox_events]

    G --> H[(payment_outbox_events)]

    H --> I[Outbox Publisher Worker]
    I --> J[Claim events FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED]
    J --> K[Publish to Broker]
    K --> L{Publish Success?}

    L -->|Yes| M[Mark PUBLISHED]
    L -->|No Retryable| N[Mark FAILED with next_attempt_at]
    L -->|No Non-retryable| O[Mark DEAD_LETTER]

    K --> P[Message Broker / Event Bus]

    P --> Q[Order Service]
    P --> R[Billing Service]
    P --> S[Fulfillment Service]
    P --> T[Notification Service]
    P --> U[Finance / Reconciliation Service]

    Q --> V[Consumer Inbox / Idempotent Consumer]
    R --> V
    S --> V
```

---

# 3. Kenapa Payment Butuh Outbox?

Tanpa outbox, biasanya ada dua pilihan buruk.

## 3.1 DB dulu, lalu publish event

```java id="n06srs"
paymentRepository.markCaptured(paymentId);
broker.publish(new PaymentCaptured(paymentId));
```

Failure:

```text id="haue8n"
DB update sukses.
Service crash sebelum broker.publish.
Payment CAPTURED tapi event hilang.
```

## 3.2 Publish event dulu, lalu DB

```java id="jgdv2j"
broker.publish(new PaymentCaptured(paymentId));
paymentRepository.markCaptured(paymentId);
```

Failure:

```text id="nzj2jd"
Event terkirim.
DB rollback/gagal.
Consumer mengira payment captured padahal tidak.
```

Outbox memperbaiki ini:

```text id="fcryz5"
DB state dan outbox event ditulis dalam transaksi yang sama.
Kalau commit sukses, event pasti bisa dipublish nanti.
Kalau rollback, event tidak ada.
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Outbox Table** | Menyimpan event yang harus dipublish. | `payment_outbox_events` |
| 2 | **Transactional Write** | Update payment + insert outbox dalam satu DB transaction. | `PaymentResultApplier` |
| 3 | **Event Schema** | Contract event untuk consumer. | `PaymentCapturedEvent`, etc. |
| 4 | **Publisher Worker** | Publish pending events ke broker. | `OutboxPublisherWorker` |
| 5 | **Claiming Strategy** | Multi-worker safe processing. | `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` |
| 6 | **Retry Policy** | Retry publish failure. | `next_attempt_at`, `publish_attempts` |
| 7 | **Dead Letter** | Event yang gagal terus dipisahkan. | `DEAD_LETTER` status |
| 8 | **Ordering** | Event order per aggregate. | `aggregate_id`, `sequence_no` |
| 9 | **Idempotent Consumer** | Consumer tahan duplicate event. | consumer inbox |
| 10 | **Event Versioning** | Contract bisa evolve. | `event_version`, schema version |
| 11 | **Payload Design** | Snapshot vs reference. | event payload JSON |
| 12 | **Broker Abstraction** | Kafka/RabbitMQ/EventBridge/etc. | `EventPublisher` interface |
| 13 | **CDC Alternative** | Debezium tailing outbox table. | CDC connector |
| 14 | **Observability** | Lag, failure, DLQ, publish count. | metrics/logs |
| 15 | **Replay / Repair** | Re-publish event aman. | admin replay endpoint |

---

# 5. Outbox Table Design

```sql id="w0z1um"
CREATE TABLE payment_outbox_events (
    outbox_event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    aggregate_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id UUID NOT NULL,

    aggregate_version BIGINT,
    aggregate_sequence BIGINT,

    event_type VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    event_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

    event_key VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    destination_topic VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    headers JSONB,

    publish_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    publish_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    next_attempt_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    locked_until TIMESTAMPTZ,

    published_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    last_error_code VARCHAR(100),
    last_error_message TEXT,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_outbox_status
        CHECK (publish_status IN (
            'PENDING',
            'PROCESSING',
            'PUBLISHED',
            'FAILED',
            'DEAD_LETTER'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql id="u5avzx"
CREATE INDEX ix_payment_outbox_pending
ON payment_outbox_events(publish_status, next_attempt_at);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_outbox_aggregate
ON payment_outbox_events(aggregate_type, aggregate_id, aggregate_sequence);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_outbox_event_type
ON payment_outbox_events(event_type);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_outbox_created_at
ON payment_outbox_events(created_at);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_outbox_event_key
ON payment_outbox_events(event_key);
```

## Field penting

| Field | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `outbox_event_id` | ID unik event. |
| `aggregate_type` | Contoh: `PAYMENT`, `REFUND`, `DISPUTE`. |
| `aggregate_id` | Biasanya `payment_id` atau `refund_id`. |
| `aggregate_version` | Version aggregate saat event dibuat. |
| `aggregate_sequence` | Sequence per aggregate untuk ordering. |
| `event_type` | Contoh `PaymentCaptured`. |
| `event_version` | Versi schema event. |
| `event_key` | Key idempotency event; bisa dipakai broker key. |
| `destination_topic` | Target topic/exchange/event bus. |
| `payload` | Isi event. |
| `headers` | Metadata: traceId, correlationId, schema version. |
| `publish_status` | Lifecycle publish. |
| `next_attempt_at` | Retry schedule. |
| `published_at` | Waktu publish sukses. |

---

# 6. Outbox Status Lifecycle

```mermaid id="e65x1p"
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> PENDING

    PENDING --> PROCESSING: claimed by worker
    FAILED --> PROCESSING: retry

    PROCESSING --> PUBLISHED: broker ack
    PROCESSING --> FAILED: retryable failure
    PROCESSING --> DEAD_LETTER: non-retryable/max attempts

    PUBLISHED --> [*]
    DEAD_LETTER --> PENDING: manual replay
```

Rule:

| Status | Arti |
|---|---|
| `PENDING` | Event siap dipublish. |
| `PROCESSING` | Event sedang dipublish worker. |
| `PUBLISHED` | Broker ack sukses. |
| `FAILED` | Publish gagal, akan retry. |
| `DEAD_LETTER` | Gagal terlalu sering / butuh manual intervention. |

---

# 7. Transactional Write Pattern

Saat payment captured:

```text id="lkbm2u"
BEGIN
  1. update payments -> CAPTURED
  2. insert payment_events -> PAYMENT_CAPTURED
  3. insert ledger_entries -> PAYMENT_CAPTURED
  4. insert payment_outbox_events -> PaymentCaptured
COMMIT
```

Contoh SQL konseptual:

```sql id="sewly3"
BEGIN;

UPDATE payments
SET status = 'CAPTURED',
    captured_amount_minor = captured_amount_minor + :amount_minor,
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE payment_id = :payment_id
  AND captured_amount_minor + :amount_minor <= authorized_amount_minor
  AND version = :version;

INSERT INTO payment_events (
    event_id,
    payment_id,
    event_type,
    source,
    old_status,
    new_status,
    payload,
    created_at
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    :payment_id,
    'PAYMENT_CAPTURED',
    'PROVIDER_RESPONSE',
    :old_status,
    'CAPTURED',
    :event_payload::jsonb,
    now()
);

INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    account_code,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    description
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    :payment_id,
    'PAYMENT_CAPTURED',
    'CREDIT',
    :amount_minor,
    :currency,
    'MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE',
    'PROVIDER_CAPTURE',
    :provider_capture_id,
    'Payment captured'
)
ON CONFLICT (reference_type, reference_id, entry_type)
DO NOTHING;

INSERT INTO payment_outbox_events (
    outbox_event_id,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    aggregate_version,
    aggregate_sequence,
    event_type,
    event_version,
    event_key,
    destination_topic,
    payload,
    headers,
    publish_status
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    'PAYMENT',
    :payment_id,
    :new_version,
    :aggregate_sequence,
    'PaymentCaptured',
    1,
    :event_key,
    'payment.events',
    :outbox_payload::jsonb,
    :headers::jsonb,
    'PENDING'
);

COMMIT;
```

Prinsipnya:

```text id="nlc8dl"
Kalau payment status commit, outbox event ikut commit.
Kalau payment rollback, outbox event juga rollback.
```

---

# 8. Event Type yang Direkomendasikan

| Event Type | Kapan Dibuat | Consumer |
|---|---|---|
| `PaymentInitiated` | Payment record dibuat. | Analytics, Order optional |
| `PaymentRequiresAction` | 3DS/redirect/customer action required. | Checkout, Order |
| `PaymentAuthorized` | Auth approved. | Order, Fulfillment |
| `PaymentAuthorizationExpired` | Auth expired. | Order, Notification |
| `PaymentCaptureRequested` | Capture diminta. | Ops/Analytics optional |
| `PaymentCaptured` | Capture sukses. | Order, Billing, Fulfillment |
| `PaymentPartiallyCaptured` | Partial capture sukses. | Order, Billing |
| `PaymentFailed` | Payment failed. | Order, Notification |
| `PaymentVoided` | Auth voided. | Order, Inventory |
| `RefundRequested` | Refund dibuat. | Support, Billing |
| `RefundSucceeded` | Refund sukses. | Order, Billing, Notification |
| `RefundFailed` | Refund gagal. | Support, Billing |
| `DisputeOpened` | Chargeback/dispute masuk. | Risk, Support |
| `PaymentSettlementMatched` | Settlement cocok. | Finance |
| `PaymentReconciliationMismatch` | Reconciliation mismatch. | Finance/Ops |

---

# 9. Event Payload Design

Ada dua gaya:

## 9.1 Thin Event / Reference Event

```json id="c927u2"
{
  "eventId": "2c575283-21ea-4e25-9d03-92c4f142bb43",
  "eventType": "PaymentCaptured",
  "eventVersion": 1,
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-14T16:00:00Z",
  "paymentId": "6e3a9e6f-2505-4d58-a8a4-9f552c061d4f",
  "orderId": "ORD-123"
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="h7xx0a"
Payload kecil.
Consumer fetch detail jika perlu.
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="usol5p"
Consumer harus call balik Payment Service.
Lebih coupling.
Lebih lambat.
Bisa gagal kalau payment service down.
```

## 9.2 Rich Event / Snapshot Event

```json id="noebt4"
{
  "eventId": "2c575283-21ea-4e25-9d03-92c4f142bb43",
  "eventType": "PaymentCaptured",
  "eventVersion": 1,
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-14T16:00:00Z",
  "payment": {
    "paymentId": "6e3a9e6f-2505-4d58-a8a4-9f552c061d4f",
    "orderId": "ORD-123",
    "merchantId": "MRC-001",
    "customerId": "CUST-001",
    "status": "CAPTURED",
    "amountMinor": 100000,
    "currency": "IDR",
    "authorizedAmountMinor": 100000,
    "capturedAmountMinor": 100000,
    "refundedAmountMinor": 0,
    "provider": "ADYEN",
    "providerPaymentId": "psp_123"
  },
  "capture": {
    "captureId": "a4dd2c7c-ea0d-4dd5-a438-5451852d1f07",
    "amountMinor": 100000,
    "currency": "IDR",
    "providerCaptureId": "cap_123",
    "capturedAt": "2026-07-14T16:00:00Z"
  }
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="0i2tpy"
Consumer lebih mandiri.
Mengurangi synchronous call antar-service.
Lebih cocok untuk event-driven system.
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="n1r59a"
Payload lebih besar.
Schema evolution harus disiplin.
Jangan masukkan data sensitif.
```

Rekomendasi untuk payment:

```text id="a90aoq"
Gunakan rich event terbatas:
cukup detail untuk consumer mengambil keputusan,
tanpa raw provider payload dan tanpa sensitive data.
```

---

# 10. Standard Event Envelope

Gunakan envelope konsisten.

```json id="svkogm"
{
  "eventId": "2c575283-21ea-4e25-9d03-92c4f142bb43",
  "eventType": "PaymentCaptured",
  "eventVersion": 1,
  "aggregateType": "PAYMENT",
  "aggregateId": "6e3a9e6f-2505-4d58-a8a4-9f552c061d4f",
  "aggregateVersion": 12,
  "aggregateSequence": 8,
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-14T16:00:00Z",
  "producer": "payment-service",
  "correlationId": "corr-123",
  "causationId": "webhook-evt-123",
  "idempotencyKey": "capture-shipment-1",
  "payload": {}
}
```

Field penting:

| Field | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `eventId` | Dedup consumer. |
| `eventType` | Routing/handling consumer. |
| `eventVersion` | Schema evolution. |
| `aggregateId` | Ordering per payment. |
| `aggregateVersion` | Version saat event dibuat. |
| `aggregateSequence` | Urutan event per aggregate. |
| `correlationId` | Trace across services. |
| `causationId` | Event/request yang menyebabkan event ini. |
| `payload` | Data domain. |

---

# 11. Java Event Model

```java id="kaapau"
package com.company.payment.outbox;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public record DomainEventEnvelope<T>(
        UUID eventId,
        String eventType,
        int eventVersion,

        String aggregateType,
        UUID aggregateId,
        Long aggregateVersion,
        Long aggregateSequence,

        Instant occurredAt,

        String producer,
        String correlationId,
        String causationId,
        String idempotencyKey,

        T payload,

        Map<String, Object> metadata
) {}
```

Payment captured payload:

```java id="wmsu2i"
package com.company.payment.events;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

public record PaymentCapturedPayload(
        UUID paymentId,
        String orderId,
        String merchantId,
        String customerId,

        long amountMinor,
        String currency,

        long authorizedAmountMinor,
        long capturedAmountMinor,
        long refundedAmountMinor,

        String provider,
        String providerPaymentId,

        UUID captureId,
        long captureAmountMinor,
        String providerCaptureId,
        Instant capturedAt
) {}
```

---

# 12. Outbox Event Factory

```java id="7tszbe"
package com.company.payment.outbox;

import com.company.payment.events.PaymentCapturedPayload;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public class PaymentOutboxEventFactory {

    private final Clock clock;

    public PaymentOutboxEventFactory(Clock clock) {
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public DomainEventEnvelope<PaymentCapturedPayload> paymentCaptured(
            UUID eventId,
            UUID paymentId,
            long aggregateVersion,
            long aggregateSequence,
            String correlationId,
            String causationId,
            String idempotencyKey,
            PaymentCapturedPayload payload
    ) {
        return new DomainEventEnvelope<>(
                eventId,
                "PaymentCaptured",
                1,
                "PAYMENT",
                paymentId,
                aggregateVersion,
                aggregateSequence,
                Instant.now(clock),
                "payment-service",
                correlationId,
                causationId,
                idempotencyKey,
                payload,
                Map.of(
                        "schema", "payment.events.PaymentCaptured.v1",
                        "contentType", "application/json"
                )
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 13. Insert Outbox dari Application Service

```java id="d4kxf9"
public PaymentResponse applyCaptureSucceeded(
        UUID paymentId,
        GatewayCaptureResult result,
        String correlationId,
        String causationId,
        String idempotencyKey
) {
    return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
        PaymentRecord payment = paymentMapper.findByIdForUpdate(paymentId);

        paymentMapper.addCapturedAmount(
                paymentId,
                result.capturedAmountMinor(),
                result.currency(),
                payment.version()
        );

        CaptureRecord capture = captureMapper.markSucceededAndReturn(
                paymentId,
                result.providerCaptureId(),
                result.capturedAmountMinor()
        );

        ledgerMapper.insertPaymentCapturedIfAbsent(
                paymentId,
                capture.captureId(),
                result.providerCaptureId(),
                result.capturedAmountMinor(),
                result.currency()
        );

        long nextSequence = outboxMapper.nextAggregateSequence("PAYMENT", paymentId);

        PaymentCapturedPayload payload = new PaymentCapturedPayload(
                paymentId,
                payment.orderId(),
                payment.merchantId(),
                payment.customerId(),
                payment.amountMinor(),
                payment.currency(),
                payment.authorizedAmountMinor(),
                payment.capturedAmountMinor() + result.capturedAmountMinor(),
                payment.refundedAmountMinor(),
                payment.provider(),
                payment.providerPaymentId(),
                capture.captureId(),
                result.capturedAmountMinor(),
                result.providerCaptureId(),
                result.capturedAt()
        );

        DomainEventEnvelope<PaymentCapturedPayload> event =
                outboxEventFactory.paymentCaptured(
                        UUID.randomUUID(),
                        paymentId,
                        payment.version() + 1,
                        nextSequence,
                        correlationId,
                        causationId,
                        idempotencyKey,
                        payload
                );

        outboxMapper.insert(eventToRecord(event, "payment.events"));

        return paymentResponseMapper.fromPaymentId(paymentId);
    });
}
```

---

# 14. Aggregate Sequence

Ordering event payment penting.

Contoh urutan:

```text id="r4rm5u"
1 PaymentInitiated
2 PaymentAuthorized
3 PaymentCaptureRequested
4 PaymentCaptured
5 RefundRequested
6 RefundSucceeded
```

Gunakan sequence per aggregate:

```sql id="o7wx4k"
CREATE TABLE aggregate_event_sequences (
    aggregate_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id UUID NOT NULL,
    next_sequence BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,

    PRIMARY KEY (aggregate_type, aggregate_id)
);
```

Ambil sequence:

```sql id="z3qzo0"
INSERT INTO aggregate_event_sequences (
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id,
    next_sequence
)
VALUES (
    #{aggregateType},
    #{aggregateId},
    2
)
ON CONFLICT (aggregate_type, aggregate_id)
DO UPDATE SET next_sequence = aggregate_event_sequences.next_sequence + 1
RETURNING next_sequence - 1 AS allocated_sequence;
```

Atau gunakan `payments.version` sebagai ordering surrogate jika setiap state transition increment version.

Practical note:

```text id="6ep90t"
Ordering global sulit dan sering tidak perlu.
Ordering per aggregate/payment biasanya cukup.
```

---

# 15. Outbox Publisher Worker

## 15.1 Claim Batch

```sql id="weobvr"
SELECT *
FROM payment_outbox_events
WHERE publish_status IN ('PENDING', 'FAILED')
  AND next_attempt_at <= now()
ORDER BY created_at ASC
LIMIT #{limit}
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
```

Mark processing:

```sql id="bjvyxs"
UPDATE payment_outbox_events
SET publish_status = 'PROCESSING',
    publish_attempts = publish_attempts + 1,
    locked_until = now() + interval '5 minutes',
    updated_at = now()
WHERE outbox_event_id = #{outboxEventId}
  AND publish_status IN ('PENDING', 'FAILED');
```

Microservices.io describes polling publisher as a pattern where a publisher polls the outbox table for unpublished messages and then publishes them; it also notes this works with any SQL database, while ordering can be tricky. ([microservices.io](https://microservices.io/patterns/data/polling-publisher.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 15.2 Worker Skeleton

```java id="lqk7e0"
package com.company.payment.outbox;

import java.util.List;

public class OutboxPublisherWorker {

    private final OutboxRepository outboxRepository;
    private final EventPublisher eventPublisher;
    private final OutboxRetryPolicy retryPolicy;
    private final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;

    public void publishBatch(int batchSize) {
        List<OutboxEventRecord> events =
                transactionTemplate.execute(() -> outboxRepository.claimNextBatch(batchSize));

        for (OutboxEventRecord event : events) {
            publishSingle(event);
        }
    }

    private void publishSingle(OutboxEventRecord event) {
        try {
            eventPublisher.publish(toMessage(event));

            transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                outboxRepository.markPublished(event.outboxEventId());
                return null;
            });

        } catch (RetryablePublishException e) {
            transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                outboxRepository.markFailedForRetry(
                        event.outboxEventId(),
                        e.errorCode(),
                        e.getMessage(),
                        retryPolicy.nextAttemptAt(event.publishAttempts())
                );
                return null;
            });

        } catch (NonRetryablePublishException e) {
            transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                outboxRepository.markDeadLetter(
                        event.outboxEventId(),
                        e.errorCode(),
                        e.getMessage()
                );
                return null;
            });
        }
    }

    private BrokerMessage toMessage(OutboxEventRecord event) {
        return new BrokerMessage(
                event.destinationTopic(),
                event.eventKey(),
                event.payloadJson(),
                event.headersJson()
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 16. Event Publisher Interface

Supaya bisa pakai Kafka/RabbitMQ/EventBridge/internal broker tanpa bocor ke domain.

```java id="8kwmig"
package com.company.payment.outbox;

public interface EventPublisher {

    void publish(BrokerMessage message);
}
```

```java id="j8xr64"
package com.company.payment.outbox;

public record BrokerMessage(
        String topic,
        String key,
        String payloadJson,
        String headersJson
) {}
```

---

# 17. Kafka Publisher Example

```java id="bpf3rc"
package com.company.payment.outbox.kafka;

import com.company.payment.outbox.*;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.KafkaProducer;
import org.apache.kafka.clients.producer.ProducerRecord;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

public class KafkaEventPublisher implements EventPublisher {

    private final KafkaProducer<String, String> producer;

    public KafkaEventPublisher(KafkaProducer<String, String> producer) {
        this.producer = producer;
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(BrokerMessage message) {
        try {
            ProducerRecord<String, String> record = new ProducerRecord<>(
                    message.topic(),
                    message.key(),
                    message.payloadJson()
            );

            record.headers().add(
                    "x-event-key",
                    message.key().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
            );

            if (message.headersJson() != null) {
                record.headers().add(
                        "x-event-headers-json",
                        message.headersJson().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
                );
            }

            producer.send(record).get();

        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new RetryablePublishException(
                    "KAFKA_PUBLISH_FAILED",
                    "Failed to publish event to Kafka",
                    e
            );
        }
    }
}
```

Kafka delivery semantics are nuanced: Confluent documentation notes that by default Kafka provides at-least-once delivery, and exactly-once semantics require producer/consumer configuration such as transactions and idempotence. ([Confluent Documentation](https://docs.confluent.io/kafka/design/delivery-semantics.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Important practical point:

```text id="6v0y3b"
Outbox publisher + normal broker usually gives at-least-once delivery.
Consumer must be idempotent.
Do not assume exactly-once end-to-end just because outbox exists.
```

---

# 18. RabbitMQ Publisher Example

```java id="vdook2"
package com.company.payment.outbox.rabbitmq;

import com.company.payment.outbox.*;
import com.rabbitmq.client.Channel;
import com.rabbitmq.client.AMQP;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Map;

public class RabbitMqEventPublisher implements EventPublisher {

    private final Channel channel;
    private final String exchangeName;

    public RabbitMqEventPublisher(Channel channel, String exchangeName) {
        this.channel = channel;
        this.exchangeName = exchangeName;
    }

    @Override
    public void publish(BrokerMessage message) {
        try {
            AMQP.BasicProperties properties = new AMQP.BasicProperties.Builder()
                    .contentType("application/json")
                    .messageId(message.key())
                    .headers(Map.of(
                            "x-event-key", message.key(),
                            "x-event-headers-json", message.headersJson()
                    ))
                    .deliveryMode(2)
                    .build();

            channel.basicPublish(
                    exchangeName,
                    message.topic(),
                    properties,
                    message.payloadJson().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
            );

        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new RetryablePublishException(
                    "RABBITMQ_PUBLISH_FAILED",
                    "Failed to publish event to RabbitMQ",
                    e
            );
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 19. Retry Policy

```java id="psh7ch"
package com.company.payment.outbox;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.Duration;

public class OutboxRetryPolicy {

    private final Clock clock;

    public OutboxRetryPolicy(Clock clock) {
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public Instant nextAttemptAt(int attempts) {
        Duration delay = switch (attempts) {
            case 0, 1 -> Duration.ofSeconds(10);
            case 2 -> Duration.ofMinutes(1);
            case 3 -> Duration.ofMinutes(5);
            case 4 -> Duration.ofMinutes(15);
            default -> Duration.ofHours(1);
        };

        return Instant.now(clock).plus(delay);
    }

    public boolean shouldDeadLetter(int attempts) {
        return attempts >= 10;
    }
}
```

SQL mark failed:

```sql id="xaodyj"
UPDATE payment_outbox_events
SET publish_status = 'FAILED',
    last_error_code = #{errorCode},
    last_error_message = #{errorMessage},
    next_attempt_at = #{nextAttemptAt},
    locked_until = NULL,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE outbox_event_id = #{outboxEventId};
```

Mark published:

```sql id="n49893"
UPDATE payment_outbox_events
SET publish_status = 'PUBLISHED',
    published_at = now(),
    locked_until = NULL,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE outbox_event_id = #{outboxEventId};
```

Dead letter:

```sql id="ovp88u"
UPDATE payment_outbox_events
SET publish_status = 'DEAD_LETTER',
    last_error_code = #{errorCode},
    last_error_message = #{errorMessage},
    locked_until = NULL,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE outbox_event_id = #{outboxEventId};
```

# 20. Handling Worker Crash

Skenario:

```text id="fkkn6l"
Worker claim event -> status PROCESSING
Worker publish ke broker sukses
Worker crash sebelum mark PUBLISHED
```

Akibat:

```text id="pn1uw9"
Event mungkin sudah terkirim.
DB masih PROCESSING.
Recovery akan publish ulang.
Consumer bisa menerima duplicate.
```

Solusi:

```text id="y9qihs"
1. Outbox publisher boleh publish duplicate.
2. Event punya eventId/eventKey stabil.
3. Consumer wajib idempotent.
4. PROCESSING stuck dikembalikan ke FAILED/PENDING setelah locked_until lewat.
```

Recovery query:

```sql id="y1oryw"
UPDATE payment_outbox_events
SET publish_status = 'FAILED',
    next_attempt_at = now(),
    locked_until = NULL,
    last_error_code = 'PROCESSING_LOCK_EXPIRED',
    last_error_message = 'Outbox event processing lock expired'
WHERE publish_status = 'PROCESSING'
  AND locked_until < now();
```

---

# 21. Idempotent Consumer Pattern

Karena delivery at-least-once, consumer harus dedup.

Consumer table:

```sql id="qpkbi3"
CREATE TABLE consumed_events (
    consumer_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    event_id UUID NOT NULL,

    event_type VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id UUID NOT NULL,

    processed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (consumer_name, event_id)
);
```

Consumer flow:

```text id="7n6jux"
BEGIN
  insert consumed_events(consumer_name, event_id)
  if conflict -> duplicate, skip
  apply business update
COMMIT
```

SQL:

```sql id="mmx0ve"
INSERT INTO consumed_events (
    consumer_name,
    event_id,
    event_type,
    aggregate_type,
    aggregate_id
)
VALUES (
    #{consumerName},
    #{eventId},
    #{eventType},
    #{aggregateType},
    #{aggregateId}
)
ON CONFLICT (consumer_name, event_id)
DO NOTHING;
```

If affected row `0`:

```text id="01xhxk"
Event already processed.
Return success/no-op.
```

---

# 22. Consumer Example: Order Service

When `PaymentCaptured` received:

```text id="x4nm3v"
1. Dedup event_id.
2. Load order by orderId.
3. If order already PAID -> no-op.
4. If amount/currency match payable amount -> mark PAID.
5. Emit OrderPaymentConfirmed if needed.
6. If mismatch -> mark payment mismatch / investigation.
```

Pseudo-code:

```java id="jwfwnm"
public void handlePaymentCaptured(DomainEventEnvelope<PaymentCapturedPayload> event) {
    transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
        int inserted = consumedEventRepository.insertIfAbsent(
                "order-service",
                event.eventId(),
                event.eventType(),
                event.aggregateType(),
                event.aggregateId()
        );

        if (inserted == 0) {
            return null;
        }

        PaymentCapturedPayload payload = event.payload();

        Order order = orderRepository.findByOrderIdForUpdate(payload.orderId());

        if (order.status() == OrderStatus.PAID) {
            return null;
        }

        if (order.payableAmountMinor() != payload.capturedAmountMinor()
                || !order.currency().equals(payload.currency())) {
            orderRepository.markPaymentMismatch(
                    order.orderId(),
                    payload.paymentId(),
                    payload.capturedAmountMinor(),
                    payload.currency()
            );
            return null;
        }

        orderRepository.markPaid(order.orderId(), payload.paymentId());

        return null;
    });
}
```

---

# 23. Ordering Strategy

Outbox tidak otomatis menjamin consumer menerima event dalam urutan benar, terutama jika:

```text id="mx0czr"
- banyak publisher worker
- broker punya banyak partition/queue
- retry membuat event lama dipublish setelah event baru
```

Strategi:

| Strategy | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Key by `aggregateId` | Kafka partition sama untuk satu payment. |
| Use `aggregateSequence` | Consumer bisa detect gap/out-of-order. |
| Single worker per aggregate | Sulit, jarang perlu. |
| Consumer state guard | Jangan downgrade state. |
| Retry delayed events | Consumer bisa buffer/ignore sesuai rule. |

Kafka key:

```text id="8msqwy"
message.key = paymentId
```

Event header:

```json id="tr4pzh"
{
  "aggregateSequence": 8
}
```

Consumer guard:

```text id="w74k3a"
If received sequence <= last_processed_sequence:
  duplicate/late -> ignore

If received sequence > last_processed_sequence + 1:
  gap detected -> retry/delay/investigate
```

Consumer sequence table:

```sql id="8spn7r"
CREATE TABLE consumer_aggregate_positions (
    consumer_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id UUID NOT NULL,
    last_sequence BIGINT NOT NULL,

    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    PRIMARY KEY (consumer_name, aggregate_type, aggregate_id)
);
```

---

# 24. Outbox Publishing Modes

Ada dua pendekatan utama.

## 24.1 Polling Publisher

Worker query outbox table.

```text id="tc6ydr"
Payment Service Worker
  -> SELECT pending outbox
  -> publish broker
  -> mark published
```

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan |
|---|
| Mudah dipahami. |
| Tidak butuh CDC infrastructure. |
| Bisa jalan di on-prem sederhana. |
| Cocok untuk PostgreSQL + Kubernetes worker. |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan |
|---|
| Polling overhead. |
| Ordering tricky. |
| Tuning batch/lock/retry perlu hati-hati. |
| Risiko publish duplicate tetap ada. |

## 24.2 Transaction Log Tailing / CDC

CDC membaca WAL/binlog lalu publish.

```text id="qshco6"
PostgreSQL WAL
  -> Debezium connector
  -> Outbox Event Router
  -> Kafka topic
```

Microservices.io menjelaskan transaction log tailing sebagai mekanisme untuk membaca database transaction log dan publish setiap message/event yang dimasukkan ke outbox ke message broker, dengan PostgreSQL WAL sebagai salah satu sumber log. ([microservices.io](https://microservices.io/patterns/data/transaction-log-tailing.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Debezium sendiri adalah platform CDC yang menangkap perubahan row-level dari database dan stream perubahan tersebut; halaman Debezium menyebut latest stable saat hasil pencarian ini adalah 3.6. ([Debezium](https://debezium.io/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Kelebihan:

| Kelebihan |
|---|
| Near real-time. |
| Tidak perlu polling manual. |
| Cocok untuk Kafka ecosystem. |
| Mengurangi beban worker custom. |

Kekurangan:

| Kekurangan |
|---|
| Butuh Kafka Connect/Debezium infra. |
| Operasional lebih kompleks. |
| Schema/event router harus dikonfigurasi benar. |
| Debugging CDC pipeline butuh skill khusus. |

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text id="4ekc1l"
Awal implementasi:
  gunakan polling publisher.

Jika volume tinggi / Kafka sudah mature:
  evaluasi Debezium Outbox Event Router.
```

---

# 25. Debezium Outbox Event Router Shape

Debezium Outbox Event Router umumnya mengharapkan outbox table dengan field seperti aggregate id/type, event type, payload, dan id. Dokumentasi Debezium menjelaskan connector dapat menangkap perubahan di outbox table, menerapkan outbox event router single message transformation, dan route record ke topic tertentu. ([Debezium](https://debezium.io/documentation/reference/stable/transformations/outbox-event-router.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh schema yang CDC-friendly:

```sql id="ecz0uq"
CREATE TABLE payment_outbox_events (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    aggregatetype VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    aggregateid VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    type VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    payload JSONB NOT NULL,
    timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
```

Namun untuk aplikasi payment enterprise, schema operational yang lebih kaya sering tetap berguna. Bisa dibuat view/table khusus CDC jika perlu.

---

# 26. Event Versioning

Event schema akan berubah.

Rule:

| Change | Compatible? | Cara |
|---|---:|---|
| Tambah optional field | Ya | Same version atau minor version. |
| Hapus field | Tidak | New version. |
| Rename field | Tidak | New version. |
| Ubah makna enum | Tidak | New version. |
| Ubah type amount | Tidak | New version. |
| Tambah event type baru | Ya, jika consumer ignore unknown. | Document contract. |

Event version:

```json id="l9ly8k"
{
  "eventType": "PaymentCaptured",
  "eventVersion": 2
}
```

Consumer harus:

```text id="fzrvrz"
- ignore unknown fields
- reject/park unsupported major version
- never crash entire consumer group for one unknown event
```

---

# 27. Event Payload: Sensitive Data Rules

Payment event tidak boleh membawa:

```text id="9jjyhb"
- raw card number
- CVV
- full payment token jika sensitive
- raw provider payload
- secret/API key
- excessive PII
```

Boleh membawa:

```text id="u8dj1s"
- paymentId
- orderId
- merchantId
- customerId jika internal dan allowed
- amountMinor
- currency
- status
- provider code
- provider reference id jika non-sensitive
- captureId/refundId
- occurredAt
```

Practical rule:

```text id="bl4hwk"
Outbox event adalah integration contract.
Jangan jadikan event sebagai dump database row atau raw PSP response.
```

---

# 28. Topics / Routing Keys

Contoh topic design:

| Topic / Routing Key | Event |
|---|---|
| `payment.events` | Semua payment domain event. |
| `payment.captured` | Hanya captured event. |
| `payment.refund.events` | Refund events. |
| `payment.dispute.events` | Dispute events. |
| `payment.reconciliation.events` | Settlement/reconciliation events. |

Rekomendasi:

```text id="c2gwck"
Untuk awal:
  payment.events dengan eventType di envelope.

Untuk volume/ownership besar:
  pisahkan topic per bounded stream.
```

Kafka key:

```text id="lz57b9"
key = paymentId
```

RabbitMQ routing key:

```text id="xsxvxx"
payment.captured
payment.refund.succeeded
payment.failed
```

# 29. Outbox Mapper MyBatis

## 29.1 Interface

```java id="2a82a5"
package com.company.payment.infrastructure.persistence.outbox;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

public interface PaymentOutboxMapper {

    int insert(OutboxEventRecord record);

    Long nextAggregateSequence(String aggregateType, UUID aggregateId);

    List<OutboxEventRecord> claimNextBatch(int limit);

    int markPublished(UUID outboxEventId);

    int markFailedForRetry(OutboxRetryUpdate update);

    int markDeadLetter(OutboxDeadLetterUpdate update);

    int releaseExpiredProcessingLocks();
}
```

## 29.2 Insert

```xml id="b4hcrc"
<insert id="insert">
    INSERT INTO payment_outbox_events (
        outbox_event_id,
        aggregate_type,
        aggregate_id,
        aggregate_version,
        aggregate_sequence,
        event_type,
        event_version,
        event_key,
        destination_topic,
        payload,
        headers,
        publish_status,
        next_attempt_at,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    ) VALUES (
        #{outboxEventId},
        #{aggregateType},
        #{aggregateId},
        #{aggregateVersion},
        #{aggregateSequence},
        #{eventType},
        #{eventVersion},
        #{eventKey},
        #{destinationTopic},
        CAST(#{payloadJson} AS jsonb),
        CAST(#{headersJson} AS jsonb),
        'PENDING',
        now(),
        now(),
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

## 29.3 Claim Batch

```xml id="r5sasz"
<select id="claimNextBatch" resultMap="OutboxEventResultMap">
    SELECT
        outbox_event_id,
        aggregate_type,
        aggregate_id,
        aggregate_version,
        aggregate_sequence,
        event_type,
        event_version,
        event_key,
        destination_topic,
        payload,
        headers,
        publish_status,
        publish_attempts,
        next_attempt_at,
        locked_until,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    FROM payment_outbox_events
    WHERE publish_status IN ('PENDING', 'FAILED')
      AND next_attempt_at <= now()
    ORDER BY created_at ASC
    LIMIT #{limit}
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
</select>
```

Di service, setelah select dalam transaksi yang sama, mark rows sebagai `PROCESSING`.

```xml id="rksgt0"
<update id="markProcessing">
    UPDATE payment_outbox_events
    SET publish_status = 'PROCESSING',
        publish_attempts = publish_attempts + 1,
        locked_until = now() + interval '5 minutes',
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE outbox_event_id = #{outboxEventId}
      AND publish_status IN ('PENDING', 'FAILED')
</update>
```

# 31. Observability

Metrics wajib:

| Metric | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `outbox.events.created.total` | Event dibuat. |
| `outbox.events.published.total` | Event publish sukses. |
| `outbox.events.failed.total` | Publish failure. |
| `outbox.events.dead_letter.total` | Dead letter count. |
| `outbox.pending.count` | Backlog. |
| `outbox.processing.count` | Sedang diproses. |
| `outbox.lag.seconds` | `now - created_at` untuk pending tertua. |
| `outbox.publish.latency.ms` | Latency publish broker. |
| `outbox.retry.count` | Retry attempts. |
| `consumer.duplicate_event.total` | Duplicate di consumer. |

Structured log:

```json id="m4je1n"
{
  "event": "outbox_published",
  "outboxEventId": "2c575283-21ea-4e25-9d03-92c4f142bb43",
  "eventType": "PaymentCaptured",
  "aggregateType": "PAYMENT",
  "aggregateId": "6e3a9e6f-2505-4d58-a8a4-9f552c061d4f",
  "aggregateSequence": 8,
  "destinationTopic": "payment.events",
  "eventKey": "payment:6e3a9e6f-2505-4d58-a8a4-9f552c061d4f:seq:8",
  "publishAttempts": 1,
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

Alerts:

```text id="h9c96x"
- pending count too high
- oldest pending event age > SLA
- dead letter count > 0
- publish failure rate spike
- processing locks expired repeatedly
- consumer duplicate spike
```

---

# 32. Failure Handling Matrix

| Failure | Outbox Behavior | Consumer Requirement |
|---|---|---|
| DB update rollback | Outbox event rollback too. | No event consumed. |
| DB update commit, publisher down | Event remains pending. | Later publish. |
| Broker publish fails | Mark failed retry. | No impact yet. |
| Broker publish succeeds, worker crash before mark published | Event may republish. | Dedup by eventId. |
| Consumer crashes after processing before ack | Broker redelivery. | Dedup by consumed_events. |
| Event out of order | Consumer guard by sequence/state. | Ignore/retry/park. |
| Payload schema unsupported | Dead letter or schema error. | Consumer must not crash globally. |
| Topic misconfigured | Publisher retry/dead letter. | Ops alert. |
| Outbox backlog large | Scale workers / fix broker. | Monitor lag. |

---

# 33. Testing Strategy

## 33.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Payment captured inserts outbox in same transaction | Outbox row exists after commit. |
| Payment capture rollback | No outbox row. |
| Event key duplicate | Insert rejected/no duplicate. |
| Publisher success | Mark `PUBLISHED`. |
| Publisher retryable failure | Mark `FAILED`, set `next_attempt_at`. |
| Publisher non-retryable failure | Mark `DEAD_LETTER`. |
| Expired processing lock | Reset to retryable status. |
| Replay dead letter | Back to `PENDING`. |

## 33.2 Integration Tests

```text id="q5y2ed"
1. Start PostgreSQL Testcontainers.
2. Capture payment inside transaction.
3. Assert payment status CAPTURED.
4. Assert ledger entry exists.
5. Assert outbox event PENDING.
6. Run outbox publisher with mock broker success.
7. Assert outbox PUBLISHED.
8. Run publisher again.
9. Assert no duplicate publish for PUBLISHED event.
```

## 33.3 Crash Simulation

```text id="j10f75"
Scenario:
1. Claim event PROCESSING.
2. Publish to mock broker.
3. Simulate crash before mark PUBLISHED.
4. Expire processing lock.
5. Publisher retries.
6. Broker receives same event twice.
7. Consumer dedup processes once.
```

Expected:

```text id="0kg6z0"
Duplicate delivery possible.
Duplicate business effect impossible.
```

---

# 34. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Publish broker langsung dalam payment transaction | Broker bukan bagian dari DB transaction. | Insert outbox row. |
| Update DB lalu publish tanpa outbox | Event bisa hilang. | Transactional outbox. |
| Mark outbox published sebelum broker ack | Event bisa hilang. | Mark after ack. |
| Menganggap outbox = exactly once end-to-end | Salah; duplicate masih mungkin. | Idempotent consumer. |
| Tidak ada eventId | Consumer sulit dedup. | Stable eventId. |
| Tidak ada aggregate key | Ordering sulit. | Key by paymentId. |
| Payload berisi raw provider response | Sensitive/coupling. | Contract event. |
| Tidak ada dead letter | Poison event retry selamanya. | DLQ/dead letter state. |
| Tidak monitor backlog | Event delay tidak terlihat. | Lag metrics. |
| Consumer tidak idempotent | Duplicate event bikin double action. | Consumer inbox. |

---

# 35. Implementation Checklist

## Producer Side

- [ ] `payment_outbox_events` table.
- [ ] Insert outbox dalam transaksi yang sama dengan payment update.
- [ ] Event envelope punya `eventId`, `eventType`, `eventVersion`.
- [ ] Event punya `aggregateId`, `aggregateSequence`.
- [ ] Payload tidak mengandung sensitive data.
- [ ] Unique `event_key`.
- [ ] Payment captured/refunded/failed/voided menghasilkan event.

## Publisher

- [ ] Worker claim pakai `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`.
- [ ] Mark `PROCESSING`.
- [ ] Publish ke broker.
- [ ] Mark `PUBLISHED` setelah ack.
- [ ] Retry failure dengan backoff.
- [ ] Dead letter setelah max attempts.
- [ ] Release expired processing locks.
- [ ] Admin replay endpoint.

## Consumer

- [ ] Consumer dedup by `eventId`.
- [ ] Consumer state guard.
- [ ] Consumer handles out-of-order event.
- [ ] Consumer ignores unknown optional fields.
- [ ] Consumer handles unsupported event version safely.
- [ ] Consumer logs correlationId/causationId.

## Observability

- [ ] Pending count.
- [ ] Oldest pending age.
- [ ] Publish success/failure.
- [ ] Dead letter alert.
- [ ] Consumer duplicate metric.
- [ ] End-to-end event lag.

Buat module:

```text id="jsqwou"
payment-service/
  outbox/
    DomainEventEnvelope.java
    PaymentOutboxEventFactory.java
    EventPublisher.java
    BrokerMessage.java
    OutboxPublisherWorker.java
    OutboxRetryPolicy.java

  events/
    PaymentCapturedPayload.java
    PaymentFailedPayload.java
    RefundSucceededPayload.java

  infrastructure/persistence/outbox/
    PaymentOutboxMapper.java
    PaymentOutboxMapper.xml
    OutboxEventRecord.java
```

Target:

```text id="79c3ui"
1. Saat payment CAPTURED, insert outbox PaymentCaptured.
2. Saat payment FAILED, insert outbox PaymentFailed.
3. Saat refund SUCCEEDED, insert outbox RefundSucceeded.
4. Worker publish event ke mock broker.
5. Mark published.
6. Simulasi broker down -> failed retry.
7. Simulasi max retry -> dead letter.
8. Replay dead letter.
9. Consumer mock dedup eventId.
10. Crash simulation publish duplicate tapi consumer hanya apply sekali.
```

---

# 37. Ringkasan

Outbox Pattern adalah fondasi reliable event publishing untuk payment service.

Prinsip utamanya:

```text id="k0agzc"
1. Payment state update dan outbox insert harus satu transaksi.
2. Publisher terpisah membaca outbox dan publish ke broker.
3. Delivery biasanya at-least-once.
4. Consumer wajib idempotent.
5. Event harus punya ID, version, aggregateId, sequence, correlationId.
6. Ordering dijaga per aggregate, bukan global.
7. Retry/dead-letter/replay wajib ada.
8. Jangan publish raw provider payload.
```

Dengan outbox, payment service bisa menghindari kondisi paling berbahaya:

```text id="lmbe8h"
Customer sudah ter-charge,
payment DB sudah CAPTURED,
tapi Order/Billing/Fulfillment tidak pernah tahu.
```

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 10/18 - Refund Management: Full, Partial, Multiple Refund, Async Webhook, Ledger, dan Reconciliation

Seri ini fokus pada **refund lifecycle** dalam payment service berbasis **Java Jersey/JAX-RS + PostgreSQL/MyBatis**.

Refund terlihat sederhana dari sisi user: “kembalikan uang customer”. Tetapi dari sisi sistem, refund adalah workflow finansial yang harus aman terhadap:

```text
1. duplicate refund request
2. support agent klik dua kali
3. partial refund berkali-kali
4. refund request diterima provider tapi hasilnya async
5. refund webhook terlambat atau duplicate
6. refund gagal karena capture belum settled / provider rule
7. refund melebihi captured amount
8. refund setelah chargeback/dispute
9. mismatch settlement/reconciliation
10. audit dan approval requirement
```

Stripe mendukung refund terhadap charge yang sudah dibuat dan dokumentasi create refund menyebut partial refund dapat dilakukan dengan menentukan amount, bahkan dapat dilakukan beberapa kali selama total refund tidak melebihi charge. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/refunds?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen menjelaskan refund request bisa memerlukan beberapa jam untuk diproses dan outcome dapat dikonfirmasi lewat webhook seperti `REFUND_FAILED`, sedangkan untuk beberapa flow outcome refund memang diinformasikan secara asynchronous melalui webhook. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/refund?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PayPal Payments API menyediakan endpoint refund captured payment; full refund dapat memakai empty payload, sedangkan partial refund menyertakan `amount` object. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/payments/v2/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Refund jangan diperlakukan sebagai sekadar update:

```sql
UPDATE payments SET status = 'REFUNDED';
```

Itu terlalu berbahaya.

Model yang lebih benar:

```text
Payment
  -> has many Refunds

Refund
  -> has its own lifecycle
  -> has provider reference
  -> has idempotency key
  -> has event timeline
  -> has ledger impact
  -> has reconciliation status
```

Satu payment bisa punya:

```text
captured amount = 1,000,000 IDR

refund #1 = 200,000 IDR
refund #2 = 300,000 IDR
refund #3 = 500,000 IDR

total refunded = 1,000,000 IDR
payment status = REFUNDED
```

Atau:

```text
captured amount = 1,000,000 IDR

refund #1 = 250,000 IDR

total refunded = 250,000 IDR
payment status = PARTIALLY_REFUNDED
```

---

# 2. Diagram Refund Lifecycle

```mermaid
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> REQUESTED

    REQUESTED --> APPROVAL_PENDING: approval required
    APPROVAL_PENDING --> APPROVED: approved
    APPROVAL_PENDING --> REJECTED: rejected

    REQUESTED --> PROVIDER_PENDING: sent to provider
    APPROVED --> PROVIDER_PENDING: sent to provider

    PROVIDER_PENDING --> SUCCEEDED: provider response/webhook success
    PROVIDER_PENDING --> FAILED: provider response/webhook failed
    PROVIDER_PENDING --> UNKNOWN: timeout/unclear provider result

    UNKNOWN --> SUCCEEDED: status query/webhook confirms success
    UNKNOWN --> FAILED: status query/webhook confirms failure
    UNKNOWN --> NEEDS_INVESTIGATION: cannot determine

    FAILED --> RETRY_PENDING: retry allowed
    RETRY_PENDING --> PROVIDER_PENDING: retry sent

    SUCCEEDED --> SETTLEMENT_PENDING: waiting settlement/report
    SETTLEMENT_PENDING --> RECONCILED: matched in report
    SETTLEMENT_PENDING --> RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH: mismatch

    REJECTED --> [*]
    SUCCEEDED --> [*]
    RECONCILED --> [*]
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Refund Domain Model** | Refund sebagai entity sendiri. | `Refund`, `RefundStatus` |
| 2 | **Full Refund** | Refund seluruh captured amount tersisa. | `refundFullPayment()` |
| 3 | **Partial Refund** | Refund sebagian amount. | `refundPartialPayment()` |
| 4 | **Multiple Refund** | Banyak refund untuk satu payment. | `refunds` table + invariant total |
| 5 | **Refund Idempotency** | Cegah double refund. | `Idempotency-Key`, `refundRequestId` |
| 6 | **Approval Workflow** | Approval untuk high-value/manual refund. | status `APPROVAL_PENDING` |
| 7 | **Provider Adapter** | Call PSP refund API. | `GatewayRefundRequest` |
| 8 | **Async Refund Outcome** | Provider bisa pending/async. | webhook `REFUND_SUCCEEDED/FAILED` |
| 9 | **Refund Webhook Mapping** | Map provider refund event. | `ProviderWebhookEventCategory.REFUND_*` |
| 10 | **Ledger Posting** | Catat financial reversal. | `ledger_entries` debit |
| 11 | **Payment Aggregate Update** | Update `refunded_amount_minor`. | guarded SQL |
| 12 | **Outbox Event** | Notify order/billing/support. | `RefundSucceeded`, `RefundFailed` |
| 13 | **Reconciliation** | Cocokkan refund dengan settlement/provider report. | `reconciliation_items` |
| 14 | **Support Console** | Support bisa request/refund/retry/replay. | admin/support endpoint |
| 15 | **Audit & Compliance** | Reason, actor, approval, trace. | `refund_events`/`payment_events` |

---

# 4. Refund Types

| Type | Makna | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| **Full Refund** | Mengembalikan semua captured amount yang belum direfund. | Customer cancel seluruh order. |
| **Partial Refund** | Mengembalikan sebagian captured amount. | Item tertentu tidak tersedia. |
| **Multiple Partial Refund** | Beberapa refund bertahap. | Shipment/item berbeda. |
| **Goodwill Refund** | Refund karena gesture support. | Kompensasi customer. |
| **Manual Refund** | Refund dibuat support/admin. | Case handling. |
| **Automated Refund** | Refund dari workflow order/cancel/failure. | Auto refund ketika order gagal fulfilled. |
| **Dispute-related Refund** | Refund karena dispute/chargeback prevention. | Avoid chargeback. |
| **Unreferenced Refund** | Refund tidak langsung terkait original payment; provider-specific dan lebih risk-sensitive. | Tidak selalu didukung/diizinkan. |

Rekomendasi awal untuk microservice payment:

```text
Mulai dari referenced refund saja:
refund harus terkait payment/capture internal yang sudah CAPTURED.
```

---

# 5. Refund Status Model

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.refund;

public enum RefundStatus {
    REQUESTED,

    APPROVAL_PENDING,
    APPROVED,
    REJECTED,

    PROVIDER_PENDING,

    SUCCEEDED,
    FAILED,
    UNKNOWN,

    RETRY_PENDING,
    NEEDS_INVESTIGATION,

    SETTLEMENT_PENDING,
    RECONCILED,
    RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH,

    CANCELLED
}
```

Mapping ke payment status:

| Refund State | Payment State Impact |
|---|---|
| `REQUESTED` | Payment tetap `CAPTURED` / `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED`. |
| `PROVIDER_PENDING` | Payment bisa `REFUND_PENDING`. |
| `SUCCEEDED` partial | Payment `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED`. |
| `SUCCEEDED` full remaining | Payment `REFUNDED`. |
| `FAILED` | Payment kembali ke prior captured/refunded state. |
| `UNKNOWN` | Payment `REFUND_PENDING` atau `UNKNOWN`, tergantung design. |
| `RECONCILED` | Payment status tidak harus berubah; reconciliation status berubah. |

---

# 6. Database Schema

## 6.1 `refunds`

```sql
CREATE TABLE refunds (
    refund_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    refund_request_id VARCHAR(200),

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_capture_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_refund_id VARCHAR(200),

    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    reason_code VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    reason_detail TEXT,

    requested_by VARCHAR(100),
    approved_by VARCHAR(100),
    rejected_by VARCHAR(100),

    requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    rejected_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    sent_to_provider_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    succeeded_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    failed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    failure_code VARCHAR(100),
    failure_message TEXT,

    idempotency_key VARCHAR(200),
    provider_idempotency_key VARCHAR(200),

    request_payload JSONB,
    response_payload JSONB,

    reconciliation_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NOT_RECONCILED',

    version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_refunds_amount_positive
        CHECK (amount_minor > 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_refunds_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency)),

    CONSTRAINT chk_refunds_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'REQUESTED',
            'APPROVAL_PENDING',
            'APPROVED',
            'REJECTED',
            'PROVIDER_PENDING',
            'SUCCEEDED',
            'FAILED',
            'UNKNOWN',
            'RETRY_PENDING',
            'NEEDS_INVESTIGATION',
            'SETTLEMENT_PENDING',
            'RECONCILED',
            'RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH',
            'CANCELLED'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_refunds_reconciliation_status
        CHECK (reconciliation_status IN (
            'NOT_RECONCILED',
            'PENDING',
            'MATCHED',
            'MISMATCH',
            'MISSING_PROVIDER',
            'MISSING_INTERNAL'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_refunds_payment_id
ON refunds(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_refunds_status
ON refunds(status);

CREATE INDEX ix_refunds_provider_refund
ON refunds(provider, provider_refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_refunds_provider_payment
ON refunds(provider, provider_payment_id);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_refunds_provider_refund
ON refunds(provider, provider_refund_id)
WHERE provider_refund_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_refunds_payment_request
ON refunds(payment_id, refund_request_id)
WHERE refund_request_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX ix_refunds_requested_at
ON refunds(requested_at);
```

## 6.2 Why `refund_request_id`?

`Idempotency-Key` biasanya dari HTTP/request layer. `refund_request_id` adalah business/client operation id.

Contoh:

```text
refundRequestId = "case-123-item-456-refund"
Idempotency-Key = "7433c529-01bb-46a8-9fb2-78ce88bb3d84"
```

Manfaat:

```text
1. Support/admin retry tetap bisa trace refund business intent.
2. Capture/refund operation dedup tidak hanya bergantung HTTP header.
3. Batch/Camunda job bisa memakai deterministic business operation id.
```

---

# 7. Optional: `refund_events`

Bisa pakai `payment_events`, tetapi jika refund workflow kompleks, table khusus membantu.

```sql
CREATE TABLE refund_events (
    refund_event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    refund_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES refunds(refund_id),
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    event_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    source VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    old_status VARCHAR(40),
    new_status VARCHAR(40),

    provider VARCHAR(50),
    provider_event_id VARCHAR(200),

    payload JSONB,
    raw_payload TEXT,

    created_by VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_refund_events_source
        CHECK (source IN (
            'API',
            'ADMIN',
            'SYSTEM_JOB',
            'PROVIDER_RESPONSE',
            'WEBHOOK',
            'RECONCILIATION',
            'CAMUNDA'
        ))
);

CREATE INDEX ix_refund_events_refund_id
ON refund_events(refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_refund_events_payment_id
ON refund_events(payment_id);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_refund_events_provider_event
ON refund_events(provider, provider_event_id)
WHERE provider IS NOT NULL AND provider_event_id IS NOT NULL;
```

---

# 8. Refund Invariants

Refund invariants wajib dijaga di Java dan SQL.

| Invariant | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| `refund.amount_minor > 0` | Refund tidak boleh nol/negatif. |
| `refund.currency = payment.currency` | Currency harus sama. |
| `sum(successful refunds) <= captured_amount` | Tidak boleh refund lebih dari captured amount. |
| Pending refund juga perlu dihitung dalam hold calculation. | Cegah dua pending refund yang totalnya melebihi captured. |
| Refund hanya untuk payment yang sudah captured/partially captured. | Jangan refund authorization-only. |
| Fully refunded payment tidak bisa direfund lagi. | No over-refund. |
| Duplicate `refundRequestId` tidak boleh membuat refund kedua. | Business dedup. |
| Duplicate provider refund webhook tidak boleh double update/ledger. | Webhook dedup. |
| Refund succeeded harus menghasilkan ledger entry. | Audit finance. |
| Refund failed tidak boleh menambah `refunded_amount_minor`. | State correctness. |

Important nuance:

```text
Jangan hanya cek successful refund.
Cek juga pending/provider_pending refund agar concurrent refund tidak overbook refundable amount.
```

---

# 9. Refundable Amount Calculation

```sql
SELECT
    p.payment_id,
    p.captured_amount_minor,
    p.refunded_amount_minor,

    COALESCE(SUM(
        CASE
            WHEN r.status IN (
                'REQUESTED',
                'APPROVAL_PENDING',
                'APPROVED',
                'PROVIDER_PENDING',
                'UNKNOWN',
                'RETRY_PENDING'
            )
            THEN r.amount_minor
            ELSE 0
        END
    ), 0) AS pending_refund_amount_minor,

    p.captured_amount_minor
      - p.refunded_amount_minor
      - COALESCE(SUM(
            CASE
                WHEN r.status IN (
                    'REQUESTED',
                    'APPROVAL_PENDING',
                    'APPROVED',
                    'PROVIDER_PENDING',
                    'UNKNOWN',
                    'RETRY_PENDING'
                )
                THEN r.amount_minor
                ELSE 0
            END
        ), 0) AS available_refundable_amount_minor

FROM payments p
LEFT JOIN refunds r
  ON r.payment_id = p.payment_id
WHERE p.payment_id = #{paymentId}
GROUP BY
    p.payment_id,
    p.captured_amount_minor,
    p.refunded_amount_minor;
```

Rule:

```text
available_refundable_amount_minor harus >= requested refund amount.
```

---

# 10. API Design

## 10.1 Create Refund

```http
POST /payments/{paymentId}/refunds
Idempotency-Key: refund-case-123-item-456-v1
Content-Type: application/json
```

Request:

```json
{
  "refundRequestId": "case-123-item-456-refund",
  "amountMinor": 250000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "reasonCode": "ITEM_UNAVAILABLE",
  "reasonDetail": "Item SKU-456 is unavailable after order confirmation.",
  "requestedBy": "support-agent-001"
}
```

Response `202 Accepted` jika async:

```json
{
  "refundId": "3ae3fa22-f72b-42a0-aac8-d8ebf2a6550b",
  "paymentId": "de004e58-7e62-4a8c-8fc0-d5a0b5db1b7a",
  "amountMinor": 250000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "status": "PROVIDER_PENDING",
  "reasonCode": "ITEM_UNAVAILABLE",
  "createdAt": "2026-07-14T16:45:00Z"
}
```

Response `200 OK` jika provider langsung sukses:

```json
{
  "refundId": "3ae3fa22-f72b-42a0-aac8-d8ebf2a6550b",
  "paymentId": "de004e58-7e62-4a8c-8fc0-d5a0b5db1b7a",
  "amountMinor": 250000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "status": "SUCCEEDED",
  "provider": "STRIPE",
  "providerRefundId": "re_123",
  "succeededAt": "2026-07-14T16:45:03Z"
}
```

## 10.2 Full Refund Convenience Endpoint

Boleh ada:

```http
POST /payments/{paymentId}/refunds/full
```

Tetapi secara internal tetap dihitung menjadi amount tertentu:

```text
amount = captured_amount - refunded_amount - pending_refund_amount
```

Rekomendasi:

```text
Endpoint umum POST /payments/{paymentId}/refunds lebih fleksibel.
Full refund bisa amount omitted hanya jika API contract jelas.
```

# 11. DTO

```java
package com.company.payment.api.dto;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Pattern;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Positive;

public record CreateRefundRequest(

        @NotBlank
        String refundRequestId,

        @Positive
        long amountMinor,

        @NotBlank
        @Pattern(regexp = "^[A-Z]{3}$")
        String currency,

        @NotBlank
        String reasonCode,

        String reasonDetail,

        @NotBlank
        String requestedBy
) {}
```

```java
package com.company.payment.api.dto;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

public record RefundResponse(
        UUID refundId,
        UUID paymentId,

        long amountMinor,
        String currency,

        String status,
        String reasonCode,

        String provider,
        String providerRefundId,

        Instant requestedAt,
        Instant succeededAt,
        Instant failedAt,

        String failureCode,
        String failureMessage,

        String traceId
) {}
```
