# 12. Jersey Resource

```java
package com.company.payment.api;

import com.company.payment.api.dto.CreateRefundRequest;
import com.company.payment.api.dto.RefundResponse;
import com.company.payment.application.RefundApplicationService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

import java.util.UUID;

@Path("/payments/{paymentId}/refunds")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class RefundResource {

    private final RefundApplicationService refundService;

    public RefundResource(RefundApplicationService refundService) {
        this.refundService = refundService;
    }

    @POST
    public Response createRefund(
            @PathParam("paymentId") UUID paymentId,
            @HeaderParam("Idempotency-Key") String idempotencyKey,
            @HeaderParam("X-Correlation-Id") String correlationId,
            @Valid CreateRefundRequest request
    ) {
        RefundResponse response = refundService.createRefund(
                paymentId,
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId,
                request
        );

        if ("SUCCEEDED".equals(response.status())) {
            return Response.ok(response).build();
        }

        return Response.accepted(response).build();
    }

    @GET
    public Response listRefunds(
            @PathParam("paymentId") UUID paymentId
    ) {
        return Response.ok(refundService.listRefunds(paymentId)).build();
    }
}
```

Separate read endpoint:

```java
@Path("/refunds")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class RefundLookupResource {

    private final RefundApplicationService refundService;

    public RefundLookupResource(RefundApplicationService refundService) {
        this.refundService = refundService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{refundId}")
    public Response getRefund(@PathParam("refundId") UUID refundId) {
        return Response.ok(refundService.getRefund(refundId)).build();
    }
}
```

---

# 13. Refund Application Flow

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant API as RefundResource
    participant SVC as RefundApplicationService
    participant DB as PostgreSQL
    participant PSP as Payment Provider
    participant WH as Webhook Processor
    participant OB as Outbox Publisher

    API->>SVC: POST refund with Idempotency-Key
    SVC->>DB: idempotency begin
    SVC->>DB: lock payment
    SVC->>DB: calculate available refundable amount
    SVC->>DB: insert refund REQUESTED/PROVIDER_PENDING
    SVC->>DB: insert refund event
    SVC->>PSP: create refund with provider idempotency key
    PSP-->>SVC: accepted/succeeded/failed/timeout

    alt provider immediate success
        SVC->>DB: mark refund SUCCEEDED
        SVC->>DB: update payment refunded amount
        SVC->>DB: insert ledger refund
        SVC->>DB: insert outbox RefundSucceeded
    else provider accepted async
        SVC->>DB: mark refund PROVIDER_PENDING
        SVC->>DB: insert outbox RefundRequested
    else timeout
        SVC->>DB: mark refund UNKNOWN
        SVC->>DB: insert event RefundUnknown
    end

    PSP-->>WH: refund webhook
    WH->>DB: inbox dedup
    WH->>DB: mark refund SUCCEEDED/FAILED
    WH->>DB: ledger/outbox if success
    OB->>OB: publish RefundSucceeded/RefundFailed
```

---

# 14. Service Implementation Skeleton

```java
package com.company.payment.application;

import com.company.payment.api.dto.CreateRefundRequest;
import com.company.payment.api.dto.RefundResponse;

import java.util.UUID;

public class RefundApplicationService {

    private final IdempotencyService idempotencyService;
    private final RequestHashCalculator requestHashCalculator;
    private final PaymentRepository paymentRepository;
    private final RefundRepository refundRepository;
    private final PaymentGatewayRegistry gatewayRegistry;
    private final GatewayRequestFactory gatewayRequestFactory;
    private final RefundResultApplier refundResultApplier;
    private final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;

    public RefundResponse createRefund(
            UUID paymentId,
            String idempotencyKey,
            String correlationId,
            CreateRefundRequest request
    ) {
        String requestHash = requestHashCalculator.calculate(
                "POST",
                "/payments/{paymentId}/refunds",
                request
        );

        IdempotencyDecision decision = idempotencyService.begin(
                "CREATE_REFUND",
                paymentId.toString(),
                idempotencyKey,
                "POST",
                "/payments/{paymentId}/refunds",
                requestHash
        );

        if (decision instanceof IdempotencyDecision.ReturnCached cached) {
            return RefundResponseMapper.fromJson(cached.responseBodyJson());
        }

        if (decision instanceof IdempotencyDecision.Processing) {
            throw PaymentErrors.operationAlreadyProcessing(correlationId);
        }

        if (decision instanceof IdempotencyDecision.Conflict conflict) {
            throw PaymentErrors.idempotencyConflict(conflict.message(), correlationId);
        }

        RefundPreparation prepared = transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            PaymentRecord payment = paymentRepository.findByIdForUpdate(paymentId);

            validateRefundAllowed(payment, request);

            long availableRefundableAmount =
                    refundRepository.calculateAvailableRefundableAmount(paymentId);

            if (request.amountMinor() > availableRefundableAmount) {
                throw PaymentErrors.refundExceedsCapturedAmount(paymentId, correlationId);
            }

            UUID refundId = UUID.randomUUID();

            RefundRecord refund = refundRepository.insertProviderPending(
                    refundId,
                    payment,
                    request,
                    idempotencyKey,
                    buildProviderIdempotencyKey(payment.provider(), idempotencyKey)
            );

            refundRepository.insertRefundEvent(
                    refundId,
                    paymentId,
                    "REFUND_REQUESTED",
                    null,
                    "PROVIDER_PENDING",
                    correlationId
            );

            return new RefundPreparation(payment, refund);
        });

        PaymentGatewayClient gateway =
                gatewayRegistry.getRequired(prepared.payment().provider());

        GatewayRefundRequest gatewayRequest =
                gatewayRequestFactory.createRefundRequest(prepared.payment(), prepared.refund());

        try {
            GatewayRefundResult result = gateway.refund(gatewayRequest);

            RefundResponse response = refundResultApplier.applyProviderRefundResult(
                    prepared.payment(),
                    prepared.refund(),
                    result,
                    correlationId,
                    idempotencyKey,
                    requestHash
            );

            return response;

        } catch (GatewayTimeoutException e) {
            return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                refundRepository.markUnknown(prepared.refund().refundId(), e);
                refundRepository.insertRefundEvent(
                        prepared.refund().refundId(),
                        paymentId,
                        "REFUND_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT",
                        "PROVIDER_PENDING",
                        "UNKNOWN",
                        correlationId
                );

                RefundResponse response =
                        RefundResponseMapper.unknown(prepared.refund(), correlationId);

                idempotencyService.markSucceeded(
                        "CREATE_REFUND",
                        idempotencyKey,
                        requestHash,
                        202,
                        response
                );

                return response;
            });

        } catch (GatewayDeclinedException | GatewayInvalidRequestException e) {
            return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                refundRepository.markFailed(prepared.refund().refundId(), e);
                refundRepository.insertRefundEvent(
                        prepared.refund().refundId(),
                        paymentId,
                        "REFUND_FAILED",
                        "PROVIDER_PENDING",
                        "FAILED",
                        correlationId
                );

                RefundResponse response =
                        RefundResponseMapper.failed(prepared.refund(), e, correlationId);

                idempotencyService.markSucceeded(
                        "CREATE_REFUND",
                        idempotencyKey,
                        requestHash,
                        200,
                        response
                );

                return response;
            });
        }
    }

    private void validateRefundAllowed(PaymentRecord payment, CreateRefundRequest request) {
        if (!payment.currency().equals(request.currency())) {
            throw PaymentErrors.currencyMismatch(payment.paymentId(), payment.currency(), request.currency());
        }

        if (payment.capturedAmountMinor() <= 0) {
            throw PaymentErrors.paymentNotCaptured(payment.paymentId());
        }

        if (payment.status().equals("REFUNDED")) {
            throw PaymentErrors.paymentAlreadyRefunded(payment.paymentId());
        }

        if (payment.status().equals("DISPUTED")) {
            /*
             * Domain-dependent:
             * Some orgs disallow refund during active dispute,
             * others allow it with special approval.
             */
            throw PaymentErrors.paymentInDispute(payment.paymentId());
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 15. Applying Refund Result

```java
package com.company.payment.application.refund;

public class RefundResultApplier {

    private final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;
    private final PaymentRepository paymentRepository;
    private final RefundRepository refundRepository;
    private final LedgerRepository ledgerRepository;
    private final OutboxRepository outboxRepository;
    private final IdempotencyService idempotencyService;

    public RefundResponse applyProviderRefundResult(
            PaymentRecord payment,
            RefundRecord refund,
            GatewayRefundResult result,
            String correlationId,
            String idempotencyKey,
            String requestHash
    ) {
        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            if (result.status() == GatewayRefundStatus.SUCCEEDED) {
                applyRefundSucceeded(payment, refund, result, correlationId);
                RefundResponse response = RefundResponseMapper.succeeded(refund, result, correlationId);

                idempotencyService.markSucceeded(
                        "CREATE_REFUND",
                        idempotencyKey,
                        requestHash,
                        200,
                        response
                );

                return response;
            }

            if (result.status() == GatewayRefundStatus.PENDING
                    || result.status() == GatewayRefundStatus.UNKNOWN) {
                refundRepository.markProviderPending(
                        refund.refundId(),
                        result.providerRefundId(),
                        result.rawProviderStatus()
                );

                RefundResponse response = RefundResponseMapper.pending(refund, result, correlationId);

                idempotencyService.markSucceeded(
                        "CREATE_REFUND",
                        idempotencyKey,
                        requestHash,
                        202,
                        response
                );

                return response;
            }

            if (result.status() == GatewayRefundStatus.FAILED) {
                refundRepository.markFailed(
                        refund.refundId(),
                        result.rawProviderStatus(),
                        "Provider refund failed"
                );

                outboxRepository.insertRefundFailed(payment.paymentId(), refund.refundId(), correlationId);

                RefundResponse response = RefundResponseMapper.failed(refund, result, correlationId);

                idempotencyService.markSucceeded(
                        "CREATE_REFUND",
                        idempotencyKey,
                        requestHash,
                        200,
                        response
                );

                return response;
            }

            throw new IllegalStateException("Unsupported refund result: " + result.status());
        });
    }

    private void applyRefundSucceeded(
            PaymentRecord payment,
            RefundRecord refund,
            GatewayRefundResult result,
            String correlationId
    ) {
        refundRepository.markSucceeded(
                refund.refundId(),
                result.providerRefundId(),
                result.refundedAmountMinor(),
                result.rawProviderStatus()
        );

        int updated = paymentRepository.addRefundedAmount(
                payment.paymentId(),
                result.refundedAmountMinor(),
                result.currency(),
                payment.version()
        );

        if (updated != 1) {
            throw new ConcurrentPaymentUpdateException(payment.paymentId());
        }

        ledgerRepository.insertRefundSucceededIfAbsent(
                payment.paymentId(),
                refund.refundId(),
                result.providerRefundId(),
                result.refundedAmountMinor(),
                result.currency()
        );

        outboxRepository.insertRefundSucceeded(
                payment.paymentId(),
                refund.refundId(),
                result.refundedAmountMinor(),
                result.currency(),
                correlationId
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 16. SQL Guard: Update Payment Refunded Amount

```xml
<update id="addRefundedAmount">
    UPDATE payments
    SET refunded_amount_minor = refunded_amount_minor + #{refundAmountMinor},
        status = CASE
            WHEN refunded_amount_minor + #{refundAmountMinor} = captured_amount_minor
                THEN 'REFUNDED'
            ELSE 'PARTIALLY_REFUNDED'
        END,
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
      AND status IN ('CAPTURED', 'PARTIALLY_REFUNDED', 'REFUND_PENDING', 'UNKNOWN')
      AND currency = #{currency}
      AND refunded_amount_minor + #{refundAmountMinor} <= captured_amount_minor
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

Important:

```text
Refund success webhook juga harus memakai SQL guard ini.
Jangan update refunded_amount langsung tanpa amount boundary.
```

---

# 17. Ledger Posting untuk Refund

Refund adalah reversal dari capture.

Contoh ledger sederhana:

```sql
INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    refund_id,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    account_code,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    description,
    created_at
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    #{paymentId},
    #{refundId},
    'REFUND_SUCCEEDED',
    'DEBIT',
    #{amountMinor},
    #{currency},
    'MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE',
    'PROVIDER_REFUND',
    #{providerRefundId},
    'Refund succeeded',
    now()
)
ON CONFLICT (reference_type, reference_id, entry_type)
DO NOTHING;
```

Why `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`?

```text
Refund webhook bisa duplicate.
Replay bisa terjadi.
Worker crash bisa reprocess.
Ledger tidak boleh double.
```

# 18. Provider Adapter: Refund Request

Provider-neutral request:

```java
package com.company.payment.gateway.model;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

public record GatewayRefundRequest(
        UUID paymentId,
        UUID refundId,

        String providerPaymentId,
        String providerCaptureId,

        long amountMinor,
        String currency,

        String reasonCode,
        String reasonDetail,

        String providerIdempotencyKey,
        String correlationId,

        Map<String, Object> metadata
) {}
```

Result:

```java
package com.company.payment.gateway.model;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;

public record GatewayRefundResult(
        String provider,
        String providerPaymentId,
        String providerCaptureId,
        String providerRefundId,

        GatewayRefundStatus status,

        long refundedAmountMinor,
        String currency,

        String rawProviderStatus,
        String providerRequestId,
        Map<String, Object> providerMetadata,

        Instant refundedAt
) {}
```

```java
public enum GatewayRefundStatus {
    PENDING,
    SUCCEEDED,
    FAILED,
    CANCELLED,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

---

# 19. Provider Behavior Examples

## 19.1 Stripe

Stripe refund can be created for a PaymentIntent or charge depending integration, and refund amount is optional; if amount is omitted, Stripe refunds the full charge, while specifying amount enables partial refund. Stripe docs also state partial refunds can be performed multiple times until fully refunded. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/refunds/create?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Internal mapping:

| Stripe Refund Status | Internal `GatewayRefundStatus` |
|---|---|
| `pending` | `PENDING` |
| `requires_action` if applicable/provider-specific | `PENDING` / `UNKNOWN` |
| `succeeded` | `SUCCEEDED` |
| `failed` | `FAILED` |
| `canceled` | `CANCELLED` |
| unknown/new | `UNKNOWN` |

## 19.2 Adyen

Adyen refund may not be final synchronously. Their docs say refund request processing can take several hours, and outcome can be signaled by webhook such as `REFUND_FAILED`; Terminal API refund docs also say refund outcome is usually asynchronous through webhook. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/online-payments/refund?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Internal mapping:

| Adyen Event | Internal |
|---|---|
| refund API accepted | `PROVIDER_PENDING` |
| `REFUND` success true | `SUCCEEDED` |
| `REFUND_FAILED` | `FAILED` |
| unknown outcome | `UNKNOWN` / retry status query |

## 19.3 PayPal

PayPal Payments API refund captured payment allows full refund with empty payload and partial refund with amount object. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/payments/v2/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Internal mapping:

| PayPal Refund Response | Internal |
|---|---|
| completed/succeeded | `SUCCEEDED` |
| pending | `PENDING` |
| failed/denied | `FAILED` |
| no clear response due timeout | `UNKNOWN` |

---

# 20. Refund Webhook Handling

Webhook event should not directly add refunded amount without guards.

Flow:

```text
1. Webhook inbox dedup by provider_event_id.
2. Map provider refund event to internal event.
3. Resolve refund by provider_refund_id or provider_payment_id + amount/reference.
4. Lock refund row.
5. If refund already SUCCEEDED -> ignore duplicate.
6. If event says success:
   a. mark refund SUCCEEDED
   b. update payments.refunded_amount_minor with SQL guard
   c. insert ledger if absent
   d. insert outbox RefundSucceeded
7. If event says failure:
   a. mark refund FAILED
   b. release pending refund hold
   c. insert outbox RefundFailed
```

Pseudo-code:

```java
public void applyRefundWebhook(ProviderWebhookEvent event) {
    RefundRecord refund = refundRepository.findByProviderRefundIdForUpdate(
            event.provider(),
            event.providerRefundId()
    );

    if (refund == null) {
        throw new RecoverableWebhookProcessingException(
                "REFUND_REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND",
                "Refund not found for provider refund id",
                nextAttemptAt()
        );
    }

    if (refund.status().equals("SUCCEEDED")) {
        refundEventRepository.insertIgnoredDuplicate(refund.refundId(), event);
        return;
    }

    PaymentRecord payment = paymentRepository.findByIdForUpdate(refund.paymentId());

    if (event.category() == ProviderWebhookEventCategory.REFUND_SUCCEEDED) {
        refundResultApplier.applyWebhookRefundSucceeded(payment, refund, event);
        return;
    }

    if (event.category() == ProviderWebhookEventCategory.REFUND_FAILED) {
        refundResultApplier.applyWebhookRefundFailed(payment, refund, event);
        return;
    }

    refundEventRepository.insertIgnoredUnknown(refund.refundId(), event);
}
```

---

# 21. Refund Pending Hold

A subtle but important issue:

```text
Payment captured = 1,000,000
No successful refund yet.

Two agents concurrently request refund:
A = 700,000
B = 700,000
```

If you check only `refunded_amount_minor`, both pass. Later both succeed → over-refund.

Solution:

```text
Available refundable amount =
captured_amount
- successful_refunded_amount
- pending_refund_amount
```

Statuses counted as pending:

```text
REQUESTED
APPROVAL_PENDING
APPROVED
PROVIDER_PENDING
UNKNOWN
RETRY_PENDING
```

When refund fails/rejected/cancelled:

```text
pending hold is released automatically because status no longer counted.
```

---

# 22. Approval Workflow

Refund approval is domain-dependent. Common policy:

| Condition | Approval Required? |
|---|---:|
| Low amount refund | No |
| High amount refund | Yes |
| Refund after X days | Yes |
| Refund for disputed payment | Yes |
| Goodwill refund | Yes |
| Automated order cancellation refund | Maybe no |
| Multiple refund exceeding threshold | Yes |

Example policy:

```java
public class RefundApprovalPolicy {

    public boolean requiresApproval(
            PaymentRecord payment,
            CreateRefundRequest request,
            long previousRefundAmountMinor
    ) {
        if (request.amountMinor() >= 5_000_000) {
            return true;
        }

        if ("GOODWILL".equals(request.reasonCode())) {
            return true;
        }

        if ("DISPUTED".equals(payment.status())) {
            return true;
        }

        long totalRefundAfterRequest =
                previousRefundAmountMinor + request.amountMinor();

        return totalRefundAfterRequest > payment.capturedAmountMinor() * 80 / 100;
    }
}
```

If approval required:

```text
1. Insert refund APPROVAL_PENDING.
2. Do not call provider yet.
3. Outbox RefundApprovalRequested.
4. Support/manager approves.
5. Then call provider refund API.
```

---

# 23. Admin Approval Endpoints

```http
POST /admin/refunds/{refundId}/approve
```

Request:

```json
{
  "approvedBy": "manager-001",
  "approvalNote": "Customer returned item and refund is valid."
}
```

```http
POST /admin/refunds/{refundId}/reject
```

Request:

```json
{
  "rejectedBy": "manager-001",
  "rejectionReason": "Refund request amount does not match returned item."
}
```

Implementation rule:

```text
Approval changes refund status only.
Provider call should be done by application service/job after APPROVED,
with idempotency and provider idempotency key.
```

---

# 24. Refund Retry Policy

Retry only safe if:

```text
1. same refund_id
2. same provider idempotency key
3. same amount/currency
4. previous provider result is UNKNOWN or retryable failure
5. refund has not already SUCCEEDED
```

Retry unsafe if:

```text
- provider might have accepted original refund but response lost
- provider idempotency retention expired
- provider refund id unknown and provider lacks status lookup
```

Retry matrix:

| Scenario | Retry? | Action |
|---|---:|---|
| Network timeout before provider request sent | Yes | Send original request. |
| Timeout after request sent | Not blindly | Query status / wait webhook. |
| Provider 502/503 | Yes, same provider idempotency key | Limited retry. |
| Provider hard failure invalid amount | No | Mark failed/config issue. |
| Provider says already refunded | Resolve | Query provider/reference, update internal if needed. |
| Refund unknown for too long | Manual investigation | Avoid duplicate refund. |

---

# 25. Reconciliation for Refund

Refund success from provider response/webhook is not the end. Finance may still need settlement/report confirmation.

Reconciliation matches:

```text
internal refund:
  provider
  provider_refund_id
  provider_payment_id
  amount
  currency

provider report item:
  refund reference
  original payment reference
  amount
  currency
  fee if any
  date
```

Possible statuses:

| Match Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `MATCHED` | Internal refund exists and provider report confirms. |
| `AMOUNT_MISMATCH` | Amount differs. |
| `CURRENCY_MISMATCH` | Currency differs. |
| `MISSING_PROVIDER` | Internal refund succeeded but not in provider report. |
| `MISSING_INTERNAL` | Provider report has refund not known internally. |
| `DUPLICATE` | Duplicate provider item/reference. |
| `STATUS_MISMATCH` | Internal status and provider status differ. |

SQL insert reconciliation item:

```sql
INSERT INTO reconciliation_items (
    reconciliation_item_id,
    reconciliation_run_id,
    payment_id,
    refund_id,
    provider,
    provider_payment_id,
    provider_refund_id,
    item_type,
    match_status,
    internal_amount_minor,
    provider_amount_minor,
    currency,
    difference_minor,
    details,
    created_at
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    #{reconciliationRunId},
    #{paymentId},
    #{refundId},
    #{provider},
    #{providerPaymentId},
    #{providerRefundId},
    'REFUND',
    #{matchStatus},
    #{internalAmountMinor},
    #{providerAmountMinor},
    #{currency},
    #{differenceMinor},
    CAST(#{detailsJson} AS jsonb),
    now()
);
```

---

# 26. Outbox Events for Refund

Recommended events:

| Event | When |
|---|---|
| `RefundRequested` | Refund request created. |
| `RefundApprovalRequested` | Approval needed. |
| `RefundApproved` | Manager approved. |
| `RefundRejected` | Manager rejected. |
| `RefundProviderPending` | Provider accepted but outcome pending. |
| `RefundSucceeded` | Provider confirmed success. |
| `RefundFailed` | Provider confirmed failure. |
| `RefundNeedsInvestigation` | Status unclear too long. |
| `RefundReconciled` | Refund matched in report. |
| `RefundReconciliationMismatch` | Refund mismatch found. |

Payload example:

```json
{
  "eventId": "d9579f92-d17f-40e0-bc78-812e2bf4a5fb",
  "eventType": "RefundSucceeded",
  "eventVersion": 1,
  "aggregateType": "REFUND",
  "aggregateId": "3ae3fa22-f72b-42a0-aac8-d8ebf2a6550b",
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-14T16:50:00Z",
  "producer": "payment-service",
  "correlationId": "corr-123",
  "payload": {
    "refundId": "3ae3fa22-f72b-42a0-aac8-d8ebf2a6550b",
    "paymentId": "de004e58-7e62-4a8c-8fc0-d5a0b5db1b7a",
    "orderId": "ORD-123",
    "amountMinor": 250000,
    "currency": "IDR",
    "provider": "ADYEN",
    "providerRefundId": "refund-psp-123",
    "reasonCode": "ITEM_UNAVAILABLE",
    "succeededAt": "2026-07-14T16:49:58Z"
  }
}
```

# 27. MyBatis Mapper Examples

## 27.1 Insert Refund Pending

```xml
<insert id="insertRefund">
    INSERT INTO refunds (
        refund_id,
        payment_id,
        refund_request_id,
        provider,
        provider_payment_id,
        provider_capture_id,
        amount_minor,
        currency,
        status,
        reason_code,
        reason_detail,
        requested_by,
        idempotency_key,
        provider_idempotency_key,
        requested_at,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    ) VALUES (
        #{refundId},
        #{paymentId},
        #{refundRequestId},
        #{provider},
        #{providerPaymentId},
        #{providerCaptureId},
        #{amountMinor},
        #{currency},
        #{status},
        #{reasonCode},
        #{reasonDetail},
        #{requestedBy},
        #{idempotencyKey},
        #{providerIdempotencyKey},
        now(),
        now(),
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

## 27.2 Find Refund for Update

```xml
<select id="findRefundByIdForUpdate" resultMap="RefundResultMap">
    SELECT
        refund_id,
        payment_id,
        refund_request_id,
        provider,
        provider_payment_id,
        provider_capture_id,
        provider_refund_id,
        amount_minor,
        currency,
        status,
        reason_code,
        reason_detail,
        requested_by,
        approved_by,
        idempotency_key,
        provider_idempotency_key,
        version,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    FROM refunds
    WHERE refund_id = #{refundId}
    FOR UPDATE
</select>
```

## 27.3 Mark Refund Succeeded

```xml
<update id="markRefundSucceeded">
    UPDATE refunds
    SET status = 'SUCCEEDED',
        provider_refund_id = #{providerRefundId},
        response_payload = CAST(#{responsePayloadJson} AS jsonb),
        succeeded_at = now(),
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE refund_id = #{refundId}
      AND status IN ('REQUESTED', 'APPROVED', 'PROVIDER_PENDING', 'UNKNOWN', 'RETRY_PENDING')
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

## 27.4 Mark Refund Failed

```xml
<update id="markRefundFailed">
    UPDATE refunds
    SET status = 'FAILED',
        failure_code = #{failureCode},
        failure_message = #{failureMessage},
        response_payload = CAST(#{responsePayloadJson} AS jsonb),
        failed_at = now(),
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE refund_id = #{refundId}
      AND status IN ('REQUESTED', 'APPROVED', 'PROVIDER_PENDING', 'UNKNOWN', 'RETRY_PENDING')
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

---

# 28. Error Codes

| Error Code | HTTP | Meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| `PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | Payment tidak ada. |
| `PAYMENT_NOT_CAPTURED` | 409 | Payment belum captured. |
| `PAYMENT_ALREADY_REFUNDED` | 409 | Tidak ada remaining refundable amount. |
| `REFUND_AMOUNT_EXCEEDS_CAPTURED` | 422 | Amount melebihi captured/refundable amount. |
| `REFUND_CURRENCY_MISMATCH` | 422 | Currency beda. |
| `REFUND_DUPLICATE_REQUEST` | 409 | `refundRequestId` duplicate. |
| `REFUND_IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT` | 409 | Key sama payload beda. |
| `REFUND_REQUIRES_APPROVAL` | 202 | Refund dibuat tapi menunggu approval. |
| `REFUND_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | 202 | Provider status unclear. |
| `REFUND_PROVIDER_FAILED` | 409/422 | Provider menolak refund. |
| `REFUND_IN_DISPUTE_NOT_ALLOWED` | 409 | Refund saat dispute tidak diizinkan policy. |
| `REFUND_NEEDS_INVESTIGATION` | 409 | Recovery manual diperlukan. |

Example:

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/refund-amount-exceeds-captured",
  "title": "Refund amount exceeds refundable amount",
  "status": 422,
  "detail": "Requested refund amount exceeds available refundable amount.",
  "errorCode": "REFUND_AMOUNT_EXCEEDS_CAPTURED",
  "paymentId": "de004e58-7e62-4a8c-8fc0-d5a0b5db1b7a",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

---

# 29. Refund and Dispute Interaction

Refund saat dispute aktif perlu policy jelas.

Possible policy:

| Payment/Dispute State | Refund Allowed? | Notes |
|---|---:|---|
| No dispute | Yes | Normal. |
| Dispute opened | Usually restricted | Bisa double loss jika refund + chargeback. |
| Chargeback lost | Usually no normal refund | Dana sudah reversed via chargeback. |
| Chargeback won | Maybe | Business decision. |
| Pre-dispute alert | Maybe yes | Refund might prevent dispute escalation. |

Rule praktis:

```text
Jika payment status DISPUTED,
jangan auto-refund tanpa approval/risk policy.
```

---

# 30. Support Console Requirements

Refund biasanya dipakai oleh support/ops.

Support UI harus menampilkan:

```text
1. captured amount
2. already refunded amount
3. pending refund amount
4. available refundable amount
5. refund history
6. provider refund status
7. refund reason
8. requested by / approved by
9. related order items
10. timeline webhook/provider response
11. reconciliation status
12. retry/replay actions
```

Admin actions:

| Action | Guard |
|---|---|
| Create refund | Permission + amount guard. |
| Approve refund | Manager role. |
| Reject refund | Manager role. |
| Retry unknown refund | Same provider idempotency key. |
| Replay refund webhook | Idempotent processing. |
| Mark needs investigation | Ops role. |
| Export refund report | Finance role. |

---

| `refund.reconciliation_mismatch.total` | Finance mismatch. |

Structured log:

```json
{
  "event": "refund_succeeded",
  "refundId": "3ae3fa22-f72b-42a0-aac8-d8ebf2a6550b",

}
```

Alerts:

```text
- refund unknown too long
- refund provider pending > SLA
- refund failure spike
- refund amount mismatch
- refund webhook reference not found
- refund approval queue aging
- refund duplicate spike
```

# 32. Testing Strategy

## 32.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Refund before capture | Rejected. |
| Refund amount zero | Validation failed. |
| Refund currency mismatch | Rejected. |
| Full refund success | Payment `REFUNDED`. |
| Partial refund success | Payment `PARTIALLY_REFUNDED`. |
| Multiple partial refunds | Total <= captured. |
| Refund exceeds captured | Rejected. |
| Concurrent refund overbooking | Only valid amount accepted. |
| Duplicate refundRequestId | No second refund. |
| Same idempotency key same payload | Cached response. |
| Same idempotency key different payload | Conflict. |
| Provider timeout | Refund `UNKNOWN`, not failed. |
| Duplicate refund webhook | Ledger once. |
| Refund failed webhook | Pending hold released. |

## 32.2 Integration Tests

```text
1. Create captured payment 1,000,000 IDR.
2. Create refund 250,000 IDR.
3. Mock provider returns pending.
4. Verify refund PROVIDER_PENDING.
5. Send refund succeeded webhook.
6. Verify refund SUCCEEDED.
7. Verify payments.refunded_amount_minor = 250,000.
8. Verify payment status PARTIALLY_REFUNDED.
9. Verify ledger REFUND_SUCCEEDED once.
10. Send duplicate webhook.
11. Verify ledger still once.
12. Create remaining refund 750,000.
13. Verify payment status REFUNDED.
```

## 32.3 Concurrency Test

```text
Payment captured = 1,000,000
Two concurrent refund requests = 700,000 each

Expected:
- one accepted
- one rejected because available refundable amount is reduced by pending refund
- total successful + pending refund never > captured amount
```

---

# 33. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Refund hanya update `payments.status` | Tidak bisa partial/multiple/audit. | Refund entity sendiri. |
| Hanya cek `refunded_amount`, abaikan pending refund | Concurrent over-refund. | Hitung pending hold. |
| Refund tanpa idempotency | Double refund saat retry. | `Idempotency-Key` + `refundRequestId`. |
| Timeout dianggap failed | Provider bisa sudah menerima refund. | `UNKNOWN` + query/webhook. |
| Webhook duplicate double ledger | Financial record dobel. | unique ledger reference. |
| Refund saat dispute tanpa policy | Bisa double loss. | Approval/risk policy. |
| Tidak ada approval untuk high-value refund | Abuse/human error. | Approval workflow. |
| Tidak simpan reason/actor | Audit lemah. | `reason_code`, `requested_by`, `approved_by`. |
| Provider status disimpan mentah sebagai internal status | Vendor coupling. | Status mapper. |
| Tidak reconcile refund | Finance mismatch terlambat. | Reconciliation job. |

# 35. Exercise Praktis

Buat module:

```text
payment-service/
  domain/refund/
    Refund.java
    RefundStatus.java
    RefundPolicy.java
    RefundApprovalPolicy.java

  application/refund/
    RefundApplicationService.java
    RefundResultApplier.java
    RefundWebhookHandler.java
    RefundReconciliationService.java

  api/
    RefundResource.java
    AdminRefundResource.java

  infrastructure/persistence/
    RefundMapper.java
    RefundMapper.xml
```

Target skenario:

```text
1. Payment captured 1,000,000 IDR.
2. Refund 250,000 IDR -> provider pending.
3. Refund webhook success -> PARTIALLY_REFUNDED.
4. Duplicate webhook -> no duplicate ledger.
5. Refund 750,000 IDR -> REFUNDED.
6. Try refund 1 IDR again -> rejected.
7. Concurrent refund 700,000 + 700,000 -> one rejected.
8. Provider timeout -> refund UNKNOWN.
9. Recovery query confirms success -> SUCCEEDED.
10. High-value refund -> APPROVAL_PENDING.
11. Manager approve -> provider call.
12. Reconciliation report confirms refund -> RECONCILED.
```

---

# Ringkasan

Refund adalah financial workflow tersendiri, bukan sekadar update status payment.

Prinsip utama:

```text
1. Refund harus punya entity dan lifecycle sendiri.
2. Full, partial, dan multiple refund harus didukung dengan invariant.
3. Total successful + pending refund tidak boleh melebihi captured amount.
4. Refund harus idempotent.
5. Timeout refund tidak berarti failed.
6. Provider outcome bisa async lewat webhook.
7. Refund success harus membuat ledger entry.
8. Refund event harus dipublish via outbox.
9. Refund harus masuk reconciliation.
10. High-value/risky refund perlu approval workflow.
```

Dengan desain ini, refund service tahan terhadap duplicate request, duplicate webhook, partial refund, provider timeout, audit, dan finance reconciliation.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 11/18 - Dispute, Chargeback, dan Evidence Workflow

Seri ini fokus pada **dispute/chargeback lifecycle** dalam payment service berbasis **Java Jersey/JAX-RS + PostgreSQL/MyBatis**.

Dispute bukan refund biasa. Refund adalah merchant mengembalikan dana secara sadar. Dispute/chargeback adalah customer/cardholder atau issuer menantang transaksi melalui payment network/provider. Jika merchant kalah, dana bisa ditarik balik, fee bisa dikenakan, dan ratio chargeback bisa memengaruhi risiko merchant.

Stripe mendukung pengelolaan dispute lewat API untuk upload evidence, respond to disputes, dan menerima dispute events lewat webhook. Dispute object Stripe juga memiliki `evidence_details` termasuk deadline `due_by`. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/disputes/api?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen menyediakan Disputes API untuk retrieve defense reasons, supply/delete defense documents, accept dispute, dan defend dispute; Adyen juga mengirim webhook saat payment disputed agar staff bisa menangani chargeback/pending chargeback. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/risk-management/disputes-api/disputes-api-reference?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PayPal Customer Disputes API menyediakan mekanisme provide evidence atau appeal dispute, termasuk proof of delivery/proof of refund atau notes/logs, dan PayPal mendokumentasikan lifecycle stage seperti Inquiry, Claim, dan Settlement. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/customer-disputes/v1/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Dispute harus dimodelkan sebagai workflow tersendiri:

```text
Payment CAPTURED
  -> Customer disputes transaction
  -> Provider sends dispute webhook
  -> Merchant decides accept or defend
  -> Evidence collected
  -> Evidence submitted before deadline
  -> Provider/network reviews
  -> Merchant wins or loses
  -> Ledger/accounting adjusted
  -> Finance reconciliation confirms
```

Jangan samakan dispute dengan refund.

| Konsep | Makna | Efek Finansial |
|---|---|---|
| Refund | Merchant mengembalikan dana secara sukarela. | Merchant debit/refund. |
| Dispute | Customer/issuer menantang transaksi. | Dana bisa ditahan/ditarik balik. |
| Chargeback | Reversal dana akibat dispute formal. | Merchant kehilangan captured amount + fee. |
| Representment/Defense | Merchant melawan dispute dengan evidence. | Bisa recover dana jika menang. |
| Pre-dispute/inquiry | Tahap awal sebelum chargeback formal. | Bisa diselesaikan dengan refund/evidence/resolution. |

Core rule:

```text
Dispute tidak boleh otomatis dianggap refund.
Refund saat dispute aktif harus policy-based karena bisa menyebabkan double loss.
```

---

# 2. Diagram Lifecycle

```mermaid
stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> OPENED

    OPENED --> NEEDS_REVIEW: webhook received
    NEEDS_REVIEW --> ACCEPTED: merchant accepts dispute
    NEEDS_REVIEW --> EVIDENCE_REQUIRED: merchant decides to defend

    EVIDENCE_REQUIRED --> EVIDENCE_COLLECTING
    EVIDENCE_COLLECTING --> EVIDENCE_READY
    EVIDENCE_READY --> EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED

    EVIDENCE_REQUIRED --> EXPIRED: deadline missed
    EVIDENCE_COLLECTING --> EXPIRED: deadline missed
    EVIDENCE_READY --> EXPIRED: deadline missed

    EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED --> UNDER_REVIEW

    UNDER_REVIEW --> WON: merchant wins
    UNDER_REVIEW --> LOST: merchant loses
    UNDER_REVIEW --> PARTIALLY_WON: partial outcome

    ACCEPTED --> LOST
    EXPIRED --> LOST

    WON --> RECONCILED
    LOST --> RECONCILED
    PARTIALLY_WON --> RECONCILED

    RECONCILED --> [*]
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Dispute Domain Model** | Dispute sebagai aggregate/entity sendiri. | `Dispute`, `DisputeStatus` |
| 2 | **Provider Dispute Webhook** | Terima event dispute dari PSP. | `DISPUTE_OPENED`, `DISPUTE_UPDATED` |
| 3 | **Payment Linkage** | Map dispute ke payment/capture/refund. | `provider_payment_id`, `provider_charge_id` |
| 4 | **Reason Code Mapping** | Normalize reason provider/network. | `DisputeReasonCode` |
| 5 | **Deadline Management** | Evidence due date, SLA, reminders. | `evidence_due_at`, scheduled job |
| 6 | **Accept vs Defend Decision** | Merchant/risk/support decision. | `acceptDispute()`, `defendDispute()` |
| 7 | **Evidence Collection** | Proof delivery, logs, invoice, customer comms. | `dispute_evidence_documents` |
| 8 | **Evidence Submission** | Submit evidence ke provider. | `GatewayDisputeClient` |
| 9 | **Evidence Versioning** | Evidence bisa draft/final/submitted. | `evidence_version` |
| 10 | **Chargeback Ledger** | Financial reversal dan fee. | `ledger_entries` |
| 11 | **Dispute Outcome** | Won/lost/partial. | `DISPUTE_WON`, `DISPUTE_LOST` |
| 12 | **Outbox Events** | Notify risk/support/billing/order. | `DisputeOpened`, `DisputeLost` |
| 13 | **Reconciliation** | Match dispute/chargeback/report. | `reconciliation_items` |
| 14 | **Support Workflow** | Case assignment, comments, due alerts. | support/admin API |
| 15 | **Risk Analytics** | Chargeback ratio, reason trends. | dashboard/metrics |

---

# 4. Dispute Status Model

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.dispute;

public enum DisputeStatus {
    OPENED,

    NEEDS_REVIEW,

    ACCEPTED,

    EVIDENCE_REQUIRED,
    EVIDENCE_COLLECTING,
    EVIDENCE_READY,
    EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED,

    UNDER_REVIEW,

    WON,
    LOST,
    PARTIALLY_WON,

    EXPIRED,

    CANCELLED,

    NEEDS_INVESTIGATION,

    RECONCILED,
    RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH
}
```

Practical note:

```text
Status dispute jangan dicampur langsung ke PaymentStatus.
Payment bisa tetap CAPTURED, tetapi dispute_status = OPENED/LOST/WON.
```

Untuk payment aggregate, bisa gunakan field ringkas:

```text
payments.dispute_status = NONE / OPENED / WON / LOST / PARTIALLY_WON
```

Atau lebih bersih: semua detail ada di table `disputes`.

---

# 5. Dispute Reason Normalization

Provider punya reason code berbeda. Internal system sebaiknya punya reason normalized.

```java
package com.company.payment.domain.dispute;

public enum DisputeReasonCategory {
    FRAUD,
    PRODUCT_NOT_RECEIVED,
    PRODUCT_UNACCEPTABLE,
    DUPLICATE_CHARGE,
    SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED,
    CREDIT_NOT_PROCESSED,
    AUTHORIZATION_ISSUE,
    PROCESSING_ERROR,
    CUSTOMER_RECOGNITION,
    OTHER,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

Mapping provider-specific:

| Provider Reason | Internal Category |
|---|---|
| Fraudulent / unauthorized | `FRAUD` |
| Product not received | `PRODUCT_NOT_RECEIVED` |
| Product unacceptable / not as described | `PRODUCT_UNACCEPTABLE` |
| Duplicate processing | `DUPLICATE_CHARGE` |
| Subscription canceled | `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED` |
| Credit not processed | `CREDIT_NOT_PROCESSED` |
| Authorization issue | `AUTHORIZATION_ISSUE` |
| Unknown/new provider reason | `UNKNOWN` |

Store both:

```text
raw_provider_reason_code
normalized_reason_category
```

Jangan hanya simpan normalized reason karena saat support bicara dengan PSP/acquirer, raw reason code tetap dibutuhkan.

---

# 6. Database Schema

## 6.1 `disputes`

```sql
CREATE TABLE disputes (
    dispute_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),
    capture_id UUID,
    refund_id UUID,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_dispute_id VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_charge_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_capture_id VARCHAR(200),

    status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    raw_reason_code VARCHAR(120),
    reason_category VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'UNKNOWN',

    disputed_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    chargeback_fee_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    chargeback_fee_currency CHAR(3),

    evidence_due_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    opened_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    outcome VARCHAR(40),

    assigned_to VARCHAR(100),
    priority VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NORMAL',

    accepted_by VARCHAR(100),
    accepted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    defense_decision_by VARCHAR(100),
    defense_decision_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    provider_payload JSONB,

    reconciliation_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'NOT_RECONCILED',

    version BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_disputes_amount_positive
        CHECK (disputed_amount_minor > 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_disputes_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency)),

    CONSTRAINT chk_disputes_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'OPENED',
            'NEEDS_REVIEW',
            'ACCEPTED',
            'EVIDENCE_REQUIRED',
            'EVIDENCE_COLLECTING',
            'EVIDENCE_READY',
            'EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED',
            'UNDER_REVIEW',
            'WON',
            'LOST',
            'PARTIALLY_WON',
            'EXPIRED',
            'CANCELLED',
            'NEEDS_INVESTIGATION',
            'RECONCILED',
            'RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_disputes_outcome
        CHECK (
            outcome IS NULL
            OR outcome IN ('WON', 'LOST', 'PARTIALLY_WON', 'CANCELLED', 'UNKNOWN')
        ),

    CONSTRAINT chk_disputes_reconciliation_status
        CHECK (reconciliation_status IN (
            'NOT_RECONCILED',
            'PENDING',
            'MATCHED',
            'MISMATCH',
            'MISSING_PROVIDER',
            'MISSING_INTERNAL'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_disputes_provider_dispute
ON disputes(provider, provider_dispute_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_disputes_payment_id
ON disputes(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_disputes_status
ON disputes(status);

CREATE INDEX ix_disputes_evidence_due_at
ON disputes(evidence_due_at);

CREATE INDEX ix_disputes_provider_payment
ON disputes(provider, provider_payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_disputes_opened_at
ON disputes(opened_at);

CREATE INDEX ix_disputes_assigned_to
ON disputes(assigned_to);
```

---

## 6.2 `dispute_events`

```sql
CREATE TABLE dispute_events (
    dispute_event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    dispute_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES disputes(dispute_id),
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    event_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    source VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    old_status VARCHAR(50),
    new_status VARCHAR(50),

    provider VARCHAR(50),
    provider_event_id VARCHAR(200),

    payload JSONB,
    raw_payload TEXT,

    created_by VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_dispute_events_source
        CHECK (source IN (
            'WEBHOOK',
            'API',
            'ADMIN',
            'SYSTEM_JOB',
            'PROVIDER_RESPONSE',
            'RECONCILIATION',
            'CAMUNDA'
        ))
);

CREATE INDEX ix_dispute_events_dispute_id
ON dispute_events(dispute_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_dispute_events_payment_id
ON dispute_events(payment_id);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_dispute_events_provider_event
ON dispute_events(provider, provider_event_id)
WHERE provider IS NOT NULL AND provider_event_id IS NOT NULL;
```

---

## 6.3 `dispute_evidence_documents`

Evidence bisa berupa file, URL, generated PDF, logs snapshot, delivery proof, invoice, communication transcript, refund proof, dan lain-lain.

```sql
CREATE TABLE dispute_evidence_documents (
    evidence_document_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    dispute_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES disputes(dispute_id),

    document_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,

    storage_provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    storage_key VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,

    file_name VARCHAR(255),
    content_type VARCHAR(120),
    size_bytes BIGINT,

    checksum_sha256 VARCHAR(128),

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',

    uploaded_by VARCHAR(100),
    uploaded_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    submitted_to_provider BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    provider_document_id VARCHAR(200),

    metadata JSONB,

    CONSTRAINT chk_evidence_documents_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'DRAFT',
            'READY',
            'SUBMITTED',
            'REJECTED',
            'DELETED'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql
CREATE INDEX ix_dispute_evidence_documents_dispute_id
ON dispute_evidence_documents(dispute_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_dispute_evidence_documents_status
ON dispute_evidence_documents(status);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_dispute_evidence_documents_provider_doc
ON dispute_evidence_documents(provider_document_id)
WHERE provider_document_id IS NOT NULL;
```

---

## 6.4 `dispute_evidence_submissions`

Satu dispute bisa punya draft evidence dan submission attempt.

```sql
CREATE TABLE dispute_evidence_submissions (
    evidence_submission_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    dispute_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES disputes(dispute_id),

    submission_version INTEGER NOT NULL,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    submitted_by VARCHAR(100),
    submitted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    provider_submission_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_response JSONB,

    failure_code VARCHAR(100),
    failure_message TEXT,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_evidence_submissions_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'DRAFT',
            'SUBMITTING',
            'SUBMITTED',
            'FAILED',
            'REJECTED',
            'EXPIRED'
        ))
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_dispute_evidence_submission_version
ON dispute_evidence_submissions(dispute_id, submission_version);
```

---

## 6.5 `dispute_tasks`

Untuk support/risk workflow.

```sql
CREATE TABLE dispute_tasks (
    dispute_task_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    dispute_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES disputes(dispute_id),

    task_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    assigned_to VARCHAR(100),
    due_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    title VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,

    completed_by VARCHAR(100),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_dispute_tasks_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'OPEN',
            'IN_PROGRESS',
            'COMPLETED',
            'CANCELLED',
            'OVERDUE'
        ))
);

CREATE INDEX ix_dispute_tasks_dispute_id
ON dispute_tasks(dispute_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_dispute_tasks_assigned_to_status
ON dispute_tasks(assigned_to, status);

CREATE INDEX ix_dispute_tasks_due_at
ON dispute_tasks(due_at);
```

---

# 7. Provider Webhook Handling

Dispute biasanya masuk lewat webhook.

Flow:

```text
1. Provider sends dispute webhook.
2. Webhook endpoint verifies signature.
3. Inbox dedup stores raw payload.
4. Worker maps provider dispute event.
5. Resolve payment/capture by provider refs.
6. Insert/update dispute.
7. Insert dispute_event.
8. Insert ledger hold/chargeback entry if financial impact occurred.
9. Insert outbox event DisputeOpened.
10. Create support/risk task.
```

Provider differences:

| Provider | Dispute Integration Notes |
|---|---|
| Stripe | Dispute API supports upload evidence, respond to disputes, and dispute webhook events; `evidence_details.due_by` provides deadline. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/disputes/api?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| Adyen | Disputes API can automate retrieval of defense reasons, supply/delete defense docs, and accept/defend disputes; dispute webhooks notify payment disputed events. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/risk-management/disputes-api/disputes-api-reference?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |
| PayPal | Customer Disputes API supports providing evidence/appeal, and PayPal documents dispute lifecycle stages such as Inquiry, Claim, and Settlement. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/customer-disputes/v1/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) |

---

# 8. Provider Event Mapping

Internal event model:

```java
package com.company.payment.dispute;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;

public record ProviderDisputeEvent(
        String provider,
        String providerEventId,
        String providerDisputeId,

        String providerPaymentId,
        String providerChargeId,
        String providerCaptureId,

        String rawEventType,
        String rawStatus,
        String rawReasonCode,
        DisputeReasonCategory reasonCategory,

        long disputedAmountMinor,
        String currency,

        Instant openedAt,
        Instant evidenceDueAt,
        Instant eventCreatedAt,

        ProviderDisputeEventCategory category,

        Map<String, Object> rawData
) {}
```

```java
package com.company.payment.dispute;

public enum ProviderDisputeEventCategory {
    DISPUTE_OPENED,
    DISPUTE_UPDATED,
    EVIDENCE_REQUIRED,
    EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED,
    DISPUTE_WON,
    DISPUTE_LOST,
    DISPUTE_CANCELLED,
    DISPUTE_EXPIRED,
    UNKNOWN
}
```

Mapping example:

| Provider Event | Internal Category |
|---|---|
| Stripe `charge.dispute.created` | `DISPUTE_OPENED` |
| Stripe `charge.dispute.updated` | `DISPUTE_UPDATED` |
| Stripe `charge.dispute.closed` | `DISPUTE_WON` / `DISPUTE_LOST` depending status/outcome |
| Adyen pending chargeback / chargeback event | `DISPUTE_OPENED` / `EVIDENCE_REQUIRED` |
| Adyen chargeback reversed | `DISPUTE_WON` |
| PayPal dispute created/updated | `DISPUTE_OPENED` / `DISPUTE_UPDATED` |
| Unknown provider event | `UNKNOWN` |

Practical rule:

```text
Unknown dispute event harus disimpan dan diberi alert.
Jangan drop karena provider bisa menambah status/event baru.
```

---

# 9. Dispute Reference Resolution

Urutan lookup:

```text
1. provider + provider_dispute_id -> disputes
2. provider + provider_charge_id -> payment_captures/payments
3. provider + provider_payment_id -> payments
4. provider + provider_capture_id -> payment_captures
5. metadata/payment_id jika provider payload menyimpan internal ref
6. kalau tidak ketemu -> NEEDS_INVESTIGATION
```

SQL examples:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM disputes
WHERE provider = #{provider}
  AND provider_dispute_id = #{providerDisputeId}
FOR UPDATE;
```

```sql
SELECT payment_id
FROM payments
WHERE provider = #{provider}
  AND provider_payment_id = #{providerPaymentId};
```

```sql
SELECT payment_id, capture_id
FROM payment_captures
WHERE provider = #{provider}
  AND provider_capture_id = #{providerCaptureId};
```

---

# 10. Dispute Webhook Processor Skeleton

```java
package com.company.payment.application.dispute;

public class DisputeWebhookHandler {

    private final ProviderDisputeEventMapperRegistry mapperRegistry;
    private final DisputeRepository disputeRepository;
    private final PaymentRepository paymentRepository;
    private final DisputeTaskRepository taskRepository;
    private final LedgerRepository ledgerRepository;
    private final OutboxRepository outboxRepository;

    public void handle(PaymentInboxEventRecord inboxEvent) {
        ProviderDisputeEvent event =
                mapperRegistry.map(inboxEvent.provider(), inboxEvent.parsedPayloadJson());

        if (event.category() == ProviderDisputeEventCategory.UNKNOWN) {
            markUnknownDisputeEvent(inboxEvent, event);
            return;
        }

        DisputeRecord existing =
                disputeRepository.findByProviderDisputeIdForUpdate(
                        event.provider(),
                        event.providerDisputeId()
                );

        if (existing == null) {
            createDisputeFromProviderEvent(inboxEvent, event);
            return;
        }

        applyDisputeUpdate(existing, inboxEvent, event);
    }

    private void createDisputeFromProviderEvent(
            PaymentInboxEventRecord inboxEvent,
            ProviderDisputeEvent event
    ) {
        PaymentRecord payment = paymentRepository
                .findByProviderRefs(event.provider(), event.providerPaymentId(), event.providerChargeId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RecoverableWebhookProcessingException(
                        "PAYMENT_REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND",
                        "Payment not found for dispute event",
                        RetrySchedule.inMinutes(5)
                ));

        DisputeRecord dispute = disputeRepository.insertOpened(
                payment.paymentId(),
                event
        );

        disputeRepository.insertDisputeEvent(
                dispute.disputeId(),
                payment.paymentId(),
                "DISPUTE_OPENED",
                "WEBHOOK",
                null,
                dispute.status(),
                inboxEvent.providerEventId(),
                inboxEvent.rawPayload()
        );

        paymentRepository.markDisputed(payment.paymentId());

        taskRepository.createEvidenceReviewTask(
                dispute.disputeId(),
                event.evidenceDueAt()
        );

        outboxRepository.insertDisputeOpened(
                payment.paymentId(),
                dispute.disputeId(),
                event
        );

        /*
         * Ledger impact depends on provider/network event:
         * some disputes immediately debit/hold amount,
         * others are inquiry/pre-dispute with no immediate financial impact.
         */
        if (shouldPostChargebackHold(event)) {
            ledgerRepository.insertChargebackHoldIfAbsent(
                    payment.paymentId(),
                    dispute.disputeId(),
                    event.providerDisputeId(),
                    event.disputedAmountMinor(),
                    event.currency()
            );
        }
    }

    private void applyDisputeUpdate(
            DisputeRecord dispute,
            PaymentInboxEventRecord inboxEvent,
            ProviderDisputeEvent event
    ) {
        switch (event.category()) {
            case DISPUTE_WON -> applyWon(dispute, inboxEvent, event);
            case DISPUTE_LOST -> applyLost(dispute, inboxEvent, event);
            case EVIDENCE_REQUIRED, DISPUTE_UPDATED -> applyUpdated(dispute, inboxEvent, event);
            case DISPUTE_CANCELLED -> applyCancelled(dispute, inboxEvent, event);
            default -> markUnknownDisputeEvent(inboxEvent, event);
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 11. Accept vs Defend Decision

Ketika dispute masuk, merchant biasanya punya pilihan:

```text
Accept:
  - merchant tidak melawan dispute
  - sering berarti merchant menerima loss

Defend:
  - merchant submit evidence
  - provider/network review
  - merchant bisa menang/kalah
```

Decision matrix:

| Kondisi | Rekomendasi |
|---|---|
| Amount kecil, evidence mahal | Accept mungkin lebih ekonomis. |
| Fraud jelas, liability shift tidak ada | Accept atau no contest. |
| Proof delivery kuat | Defend. |
| Digital goods punya usage logs kuat | Defend. |
| Customer sudah refunded | Submit refund proof / accept depending provider. |
| Deadline sangat dekat dan evidence belum siap | Escalate priority. |
| Dispute reason duplicate charge | Defend jika ada proof single charge/refund. |
| Subscription canceled | Defend hanya jika ada clear terms + usage + cancellation timeline. |

# 12. API Design

## 12.1 List Disputes

```http
GET /disputes?status=NEEDS_REVIEW&assignedTo=support-agent-001
```

Response:

```json
{
  "items": [
    {
      "disputeId": "6a464a65-2709-49f4-900b-37c6149438de",
      "paymentId": "b1806219-220c-4a25-ad4e-33b233143c0a",
      "provider": "STRIPE",
      "providerDisputeId": "dp_123",
      "status": "NEEDS_REVIEW",
      "reasonCategory": "PRODUCT_NOT_RECEIVED",
      "disputedAmountMinor": 500000,
      "currency": "IDR",
      "evidenceDueAt": "2026-07-20T23:59:59Z",
      "assignedTo": "support-agent-001"
    }
  ]
}
```

---

## 12.2 Get Dispute Detail

```http
GET /disputes/{disputeId}
```

Should include:

```text
- dispute summary
- payment summary
- order/customer refs
- reason
- deadline
- timeline
- evidence docs
- ledger impact
- provider raw refs
- recommended actions
```

---

## 12.3 Accept Dispute

```http
POST /disputes/{disputeId}/accept
Idempotency-Key: accept-dispute-123-v1
```

Request:

```json
{
  "acceptedBy": "risk-manager-001",
  "reason": "Evidence insufficient and amount below defense threshold."
}
```

Internal flow:

```text
1. Lock dispute.
2. Validate status defendable/needs review.
3. Call provider accept dispute API if applicable.
4. Mark ACCEPTED.
5. Insert dispute_event.
6. Insert ledger if provider outcome means financial loss.
7. Insert outbox DisputeAccepted.
```

---

## 12.4 Decide to Defend

```http
POST /disputes/{disputeId}/defend
Idempotency-Key: defend-dispute-123-v1
```

Request:

```json
{
  "defenseDecisionBy": "risk-manager-001",
  "strategy": "PROOF_OF_DELIVERY_AND_CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION",
  "note": "Delivery confirmed and customer used service."
}
```

Internal status:

```text
NEEDS_REVIEW -> EVIDENCE_REQUIRED / EVIDENCE_COLLECTING
```

---

## 12.5 Upload Evidence Metadata

Actual file upload can be separate storage flow.

```http
POST /disputes/{disputeId}/evidence-documents
```

Request:

```json
{
  "documentType": "PROOF_OF_DELIVERY",
  "storageProvider": "S3",
  "storageKey": "disputes/6a46/proof-delivery.pdf",
  "fileName": "proof-delivery.pdf",
  "contentType": "application/pdf",
  "checksumSha256": "sha256:abc..."
}
```

---

## 12.6 Submit Evidence

```http
POST /disputes/{disputeId}/submit-evidence
Idempotency-Key: submit-evidence-123-v1
```

Request:

```json
{
  "submittedBy": "risk-agent-001",
  "submissionNote": "Submitting proof of delivery, invoice, and customer communication."
}
```

Internal flow:

```text
1. Lock dispute.
2. Validate evidence due_at not passed.
3. Validate required evidence docs ready.
4. Build provider-specific evidence payload.
5. Upload/supply documents if provider requires.
6. Submit/respond via provider API.
7. Mark EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED / UNDER_REVIEW.
8. Insert event/outbox.
```

---

# 13. Evidence Types

Evidence required depends on reason/provider/network. Common types:

| Evidence Type | Example |
|---|---|
| `PROOF_OF_DELIVERY` | Shipping tracking, delivery confirmation. |
| `PROOF_OF_SERVICE` | Service usage logs, completion logs. |
| `CUSTOMER_COMMUNICATION` | Email/chat transcript. |
| `INVOICE` | Invoice/order detail. |
| `REFUND_PROOF` | Refund ID, date, amount. |
| `TERMS_ACCEPTANCE` | Terms and conditions accepted timestamp/IP/version. |
| `AUTHENTICATION_PROOF` | 3DS/frictionless/challenge result, device info. |
| `CUSTOMER_IDENTITY` | Account history, login/session evidence. |
| `DUPLICATE_CHARGE_PROOF` | Evidence charges were distinct or one reversed. |
| `CANCELLATION_POLICY` | Subscription terms and cancellation timestamp. |

Practical rule:

```text
Evidence package harus dibangun dari source internal yang bisa diaudit:
order, fulfillment, delivery, usage logs, customer support, refund records, auth/3DS logs.
```

---

# 14. Evidence Completeness Checklist

| Reason Category | Evidence yang Biasanya Dibutuhkan |
|---|---|
| `FRAUD` | 3DS/authentication proof, device/IP/account history, CVV/AVS result if available, customer usage. |
| `PRODUCT_NOT_RECEIVED` | Tracking number, delivery confirmation, address match, carrier proof. |
| `PRODUCT_UNACCEPTABLE` | Product description, communication, refund/return policy, photos/logs. |
| `DUPLICATE_CHARGE` | Order IDs, charge IDs, timestamps, refund proof if duplicate was reversed. |
| `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED` | Terms accepted, billing schedule, cancellation logs, usage after billing. |
| `CREDIT_NOT_PROCESSED` | Refund proof or explanation why refund not due. |
| `CUSTOMER_RECOGNITION` | Merchant descriptor explanation, invoice, customer account/order detail. |

---

# 15. Deadline Management

Stripe exposes evidence deadline via `evidence_details.due_by` on dispute data. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/disputes/api?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen dispute docs emphasize acting on dispute webhooks so staff can take appropriate action, and the Disputes API can automate dispute handling as soon as disputes are initiated. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/risk-management/disputes-api/disputes-api-reference?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Scheduled job:

```text
Every hour:
  1. Find open disputes with evidence_due_at within 72h/48h/24h/6h.
  2. Create/refresh tasks.
  3. Send reminders to assignee/team.
  4. Escalate high amount disputes.
  5. Mark EXPIRED if deadline passed and not submitted.
```

SQL:

```sql
SELECT *
FROM disputes
WHERE status IN (
    'NEEDS_REVIEW',
    'EVIDENCE_REQUIRED',
    'EVIDENCE_COLLECTING',
    'EVIDENCE_READY'
)
AND evidence_due_at IS NOT NULL
AND evidence_due_at <= now() + interval '24 hours'
ORDER BY evidence_due_at ASC;
```

Mark expired:

```sql
UPDATE disputes
SET status = 'EXPIRED',
    version = version + 1,
    updated_at = now()
WHERE dispute_id = #{disputeId}
  AND status IN (
      'NEEDS_REVIEW',
      'EVIDENCE_REQUIRED',
      'EVIDENCE_COLLECTING',
      'EVIDENCE_READY'
  )
  AND evidence_due_at < now()
  AND version = #{version};
```

---

# 16. Provider Dispute Adapter

Provider-neutral interface:

```java
package com.company.payment.gateway.dispute;

public interface PaymentDisputeGatewayClient {

    GatewayDisputeDetails getDispute(GatewayGetDisputeRequest request);

    GatewayAcceptDisputeResult acceptDispute(GatewayAcceptDisputeRequest request);

    GatewaySupplyEvidenceResult supplyEvidence(GatewaySupplyEvidenceRequest request);

    GatewaySubmitEvidenceResult submitEvidence(GatewaySubmitEvidenceRequest request);

    GatewayDisputeCapabilities capabilities();
}
```

Request models:

```java
package com.company.payment.gateway.dispute;

import java.util.UUID;

public record GatewaySubmitEvidenceRequest(
        UUID disputeId,
        String providerDisputeId,
        String providerPaymentId,
        String providerIdempotencyKey,
        String correlationId
) {}
```

```java
package com.company.payment.gateway.dispute;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

public record GatewaySupplyEvidenceRequest(
        UUID disputeId,
        String providerDisputeId,
        List<GatewayEvidenceDocument> documents,
        String notes,
        String providerIdempotencyKey,
        String correlationId
) {}
```

```java
package com.company.payment.gateway.dispute;

public record GatewayEvidenceDocument(
        String documentType,
        String fileName,
        String contentType,
        byte[] content,
        String checksumSha256
) {}
```

Result:

```java
package com.company.payment.gateway.dispute;

public record GatewaySubmitEvidenceResult(
        String provider,
        String providerDisputeId,
        String providerSubmissionId,
        String rawProviderStatus,
        boolean accepted,
        String providerRequestId
) {}
```
