# 17. Evidence Submission Service Skeleton

```java
package com.company.payment.application.dispute;

public class DisputeEvidenceSubmissionService {

    private final DisputeRepository disputeRepository;
    private final EvidenceDocumentRepository evidenceRepository;
    private final PaymentDisputeGatewayRegistry gatewayRegistry;
    private final OutboxRepository outboxRepository;
    private final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;

    public DisputeResponse submitEvidence(
            UUID disputeId,
            String idempotencyKey,
            String correlationId,
            SubmitEvidenceRequest request
    ) {
        DisputePreparation preparation = transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            DisputeRecord dispute = disputeRepository.findByIdForUpdate(disputeId);

            validateCanSubmitEvidence(dispute);

            List<EvidenceDocumentRecord> documents =
                    evidenceRepository.findReadyDocuments(disputeId);

            validateEvidenceCompleteness(dispute, documents);

            UUID submissionId = UUID.randomUUID();

            disputeRepository.insertEvidenceSubmissionDraft(
                    submissionId,
                    disputeId,
                    nextSubmissionVersion(disputeId),
                    request.submittedBy()
            );

            disputeRepository.markSubmittingEvidence(
                    disputeId,
                    dispute.version()
            );

            disputeRepository.insertDisputeEvent(
                    disputeId,
                    dispute.paymentId(),
                    "EVIDENCE_SUBMITTING",
                    "API",
                    dispute.status(),
                    "EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED",
                    request.submittedBy()
            );

            return new DisputePreparation(dispute, documents, submissionId);
        });

        PaymentDisputeGatewayClient gateway =
                gatewayRegistry.getRequired(preparation.dispute().provider());

        try {
            GatewaySupplyEvidenceResult supplyResult =
                    gateway.supplyEvidence(buildSupplyEvidenceRequest(preparation, idempotencyKey, correlationId));

            GatewaySubmitEvidenceResult submitResult =
                    gateway.submitEvidence(buildSubmitEvidenceRequest(preparation, idempotencyKey, correlationId));

            return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                disputeRepository.markEvidenceSubmitted(
                        disputeId,
                        preparation.submissionId(),
                        submitResult.providerSubmissionId(),
                        submitResult.rawProviderStatus()
                );

                evidenceRepository.markSubmitted(disputeId);

                outboxRepository.insertDisputeEvidenceSubmitted(
                        preparation.dispute().paymentId(),
                        disputeId,
                        correlationId
                );

                return disputeRepository.getResponse(disputeId);
            });

        } catch (GatewayTimeoutException e) {
            return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                disputeRepository.markNeedsInvestigation(
                        disputeId,
                        "EVIDENCE_SUBMISSION_TIMEOUT",
                        e.getMessage()
                );

                outboxRepository.insertDisputeNeedsInvestigation(
                        preparation.dispute().paymentId(),
                        disputeId,
                        correlationId
                );

                return disputeRepository.getResponse(disputeId);
            });
        }
    }

    private void validateCanSubmitEvidence(DisputeRecord dispute) {
        if (dispute.evidenceDueAt() != null && dispute.evidenceDueAt().isBefore(Instant.now())) {
            throw DisputeErrors.evidenceDeadlinePassed(dispute.disputeId());
        }

        if (!Set.of(
                "EVIDENCE_REQUIRED",
                "EVIDENCE_COLLECTING",
                "EVIDENCE_READY"
        ).contains(dispute.status())) {
            throw DisputeErrors.invalidDisputeState(dispute.disputeId(), dispute.status());
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 18. Accept Dispute Service Skeleton

```java
package com.company.payment.application.dispute;

public class AcceptDisputeService {

    private final DisputeRepository disputeRepository;
    private final PaymentDisputeGatewayRegistry gatewayRegistry;
    private final LedgerRepository ledgerRepository;
    private final OutboxRepository outboxRepository;
    private final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;

    public DisputeResponse acceptDispute(
            UUID disputeId,
            String idempotencyKey,
            String correlationId,
            AcceptDisputeRequest request
    ) {
        DisputeRecord dispute = disputeRepository.findByIdForUpdate(disputeId);

        validateCanAccept(dispute);

        PaymentDisputeGatewayClient gateway =
                gatewayRegistry.getRequired(dispute.provider());

        GatewayAcceptDisputeResult result = gateway.acceptDispute(
                new GatewayAcceptDisputeRequest(
                        dispute.disputeId(),
                        dispute.providerDisputeId(),
                        buildProviderIdempotencyKey(dispute.provider(), idempotencyKey),
                        correlationId
                )
        );

        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            DisputeRecord locked = disputeRepository.findByIdForUpdate(disputeId);

            disputeRepository.markAccepted(
                    disputeId,
                    request.acceptedBy(),
                    request.reason(),
                    locked.version()
            );

            disputeRepository.insertDisputeEvent(
                    disputeId,
                    locked.paymentId(),
                    "DISPUTE_ACCEPTED",
                    "API",
                    locked.status(),
                    "ACCEPTED",
                    request.acceptedBy()
            );

            /*
             * Financial ledger impact might already be posted when chargeback opened.
             * If not, post loss here depending provider semantics.
             */
            ledgerRepository.insertDisputeAcceptedIfNeeded(locked);

            outboxRepository.insertDisputeAccepted(
                    locked.paymentId(),
                    disputeId,
                    correlationId
            );

            return disputeRepository.getResponse(disputeId);
        });
    }

    private void validateCanAccept(DisputeRecord dispute) {
        if (!Set.of("NEEDS_REVIEW", "EVIDENCE_REQUIRED", "EVIDENCE_COLLECTING").contains(dispute.status())) {
            throw DisputeErrors.invalidDisputeState(dispute.disputeId(), dispute.status());
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 19. Ledger / Accounting Model

Dispute accounting depends on provider/network event timing. Common ledger events:

| Event | Ledger Entry | Direction | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Dispute opened with funds withdrawn/held | `CHARGEBACK_HOLD` or `CHARGEBACK_DEBIT` | DEBIT | Depends whether provider debits immediately. |
| Chargeback fee charged | `CHARGEBACK_FEE` | DEBIT | Fee may be separate line item. |
| Dispute won | `CHARGEBACK_REVERSAL` | CREDIT | Recover disputed amount. |
| Dispute lost | `CHARGEBACK_LOST` | DEBIT or finalize previous hold | Loss finalization. |
| Partial win | Credit/debit net adjustment | Mixed | Depends outcome. |
| Evidence submission fee | Optional | DEBIT | Provider-specific. |

Example ledger for chargeback loss:

```sql
INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    account_code,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    description,
    created_at
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    #{paymentId},
    'CHARGEBACK_LOST',
    'DEBIT',
    #{disputedAmountMinor},
    #{currency},
    'MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE',
    'PROVIDER_DISPUTE',
    #{providerDisputeId},
    'Chargeback lost',
    now()
)
ON CONFLICT (reference_type, reference_id, entry_type)
DO NOTHING;
```

Chargeback fee:

```sql
INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    account_code,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    description,
    created_at
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    #{paymentId},
    'CHARGEBACK_FEE',
    'DEBIT',
    #{feeAmountMinor},
    #{feeCurrency},
    'PAYMENT_PROVIDER_FEES',
    'PROVIDER_DISPUTE_FEE',
    #{providerDisputeId},
    'Chargeback fee',
    now()
)
ON CONFLICT (reference_type, reference_id, entry_type)
DO NOTHING;
```

Dispute won reversal:

```sql
INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    account_code,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    description,
    created_at
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    #{paymentId},
    'CHARGEBACK_WON_REVERSAL',
    'CREDIT',
    #{recoveredAmountMinor},
    #{currency},
    'MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE',
    'PROVIDER_DISPUTE',
    #{providerDisputeId},
    'Chargeback won reversal',
    now()
)
ON CONFLICT (reference_type, reference_id, entry_type)
DO NOTHING;
```

---

# 20. Payment State Interaction

Payment status and dispute outcome should be consistent but not over-simplified.

| Payment Status | Dispute Status | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `CAPTURED` | none | Normal captured payment. |
| `CAPTURED` | `OPENED` | Payment captured but disputed. |
| `DISPUTED` | `EVIDENCE_REQUIRED` | Active dispute workflow. |
| `DISPUTED` | `UNDER_REVIEW` | Evidence submitted; waiting outcome. |
| `CAPTURED` or `DISPUTED` | `WON` | Merchant won; payment can be considered retained. |
| `CHARGEBACK_LOST` | `LOST` | Funds lost. |
| `PARTIALLY_CHARGEBACK_LOST` | `PARTIALLY_WON` | Partial recovery/loss. |

Recommendation:

```text
Do not overwrite payment CAPTURED to REFUNDED when dispute opens.
Use dispute-specific state and ledger impact.
```

Possible `PaymentStatus` additions:

```java
public enum PaymentStatus {
    CAPTURED,
    PARTIALLY_REFUNDED,
    REFUNDED,

    DISPUTED,
    CHARGEBACK_WON,
    CHARGEBACK_LOST,
    PARTIALLY_CHARGEBACK_LOST
}
```

Or keep:

```text
payments.status = CAPTURED
payments.dispute_status = OPENED/LOST/WON
```

For clarity in enterprise systems, a separate `dispute_status` field is often cleaner.

---

# 21. Refund During Dispute Policy

Refund during active dispute is high-risk.

Policy examples:

| Condition | Allowed? | Reason |
|---|---:|---|
| Dispute not opened | Yes | Normal refund. |
| Inquiry/pre-dispute only | Maybe | Refund can resolve inquiry depending provider. |
| Formal chargeback opened | Usually restricted | Avoid refund + chargeback double loss. |
| Evidence already submitted | Usually no auto-refund | Could undermine defense. |
| Dispute lost | No normal refund for same amount | Funds already reversed. |
| Dispute won | Maybe | Business goodwill decision. |

Implementation:

```java
public class RefundDisputePolicy {

    public void validateRefundAllowed(PaymentRecord payment, Optional<DisputeRecord> activeDispute) {
        if (activeDispute.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }

        DisputeRecord dispute = activeDispute.get();

        if (Set.of(
                "OPENED",
                "NEEDS_REVIEW",
                "EVIDENCE_REQUIRED",
                "EVIDENCE_COLLECTING",
                "EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED",
                "UNDER_REVIEW"
        ).contains(dispute.status())) {
            throw PaymentErrors.refundBlockedByActiveDispute(payment.paymentId(), dispute.disputeId());
        }

        if ("LOST".equals(dispute.status())) {
            throw PaymentErrors.refundBlockedByChargebackLost(payment.paymentId(), dispute.disputeId());
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 22. Outbox Events

Recommended dispute outbox events:

| Event | Consumer |
|---|---|
| `DisputeOpened` | Risk, Support, Order, Billing |
| `DisputeNeedsEvidence` | Support/Risk |
| `DisputeEvidenceSubmitted` | Risk, Audit |
| `DisputeAccepted` | Billing, Finance |
| `DisputeWon` | Billing, Finance |
| `DisputeLost` | Billing, Finance, Risk |
| `DisputeExpired` | Risk, Support |
| `DisputeNeedsInvestigation` | Ops |
| `ChargebackFeePosted` | Finance |
| `DisputeReconciled` | Finance |

Example event:

```json
{
  "eventId": "6f59e3bb-6b96-4d97-a5e0-f96c5d2fa19d",
  "eventType": "DisputeOpened",
  "eventVersion": 1,
  "aggregateType": "DISPUTE",
  "aggregateId": "6a464a65-2709-49f4-900b-37c6149438de",
  "occurredAt": "2026-07-14T17:30:00Z",

  "payload": {
    "disputeId": "6a464a65-2709-49f4-900b-37c6149438de",

    "orderId": "ORD-123",

    "providerDisputeId": "dp_123",
    "reasonCategory": "PRODUCT_NOT_RECEIVED",

}
```

# 23. Reconciliation

Dispute reconciliation must match provider reports/balance transactions with internal dispute ledger.

Reconciliation keys:

```text
provider
provider_dispute_id
provider_payment_id
provider_charge_id
amount
currency
fee
event date
```

Statuses:

| Match Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `MATCHED` | Internal dispute matches provider report. |
| `AMOUNT_MISMATCH` | Disputed amount differs. |
| `FEE_MISMATCH` | Chargeback fee differs. |
| `MISSING_INTERNAL` | Provider reports dispute not known internally. |
| `MISSING_PROVIDER` | Internal dispute not found in provider report. |
| `STATUS_MISMATCH` | Internal won/lost differs from provider. |
| `DUPLICATE` | Duplicate dispute/report item. |

Insert item:

```sql
INSERT INTO reconciliation_items (
    reconciliation_item_id,
    reconciliation_run_id,
    payment_id,
    provider,
    provider_payment_id,
    item_type,
    match_status,
    internal_amount_minor,
    provider_amount_minor,
    currency,
    difference_minor,
    details,
    created_at
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    #{reconciliationRunId},
    #{paymentId},
    #{provider},
    #{providerPaymentId},
    'DISPUTE',
    #{matchStatus},
    #{internalAmountMinor},
    #{providerAmountMinor},
    #{currency},
    #{differenceMinor},
    CAST(#{detailsJson} AS jsonb),
    now()
);
```

---

# 24. Support/Risk Console Requirements

A usable dispute console should show:

```text
1. Dispute ID and provider dispute ID
2. Payment/order/customer summary
3. Disputed amount and currency
4. Reason category and raw reason code
5. Evidence deadline countdown
6. Current status
7. Assigned owner
8. Recommended evidence checklist
9. Uploaded evidence documents
10. Provider communication/status
11. Timeline of dispute events
12. Ledger impact
13. Refund history
14. Delivery/fulfillment proof
15. Customer communication
16. Submit/accept/assign/escalate actions
```

Actions:

| Action | Guard |
|---|---|
| Assign dispute | Support/risk role. |
| Accept dispute | Risk manager or policy. |
| Decide defend | Risk/support role. |
| Upload evidence | Assigned staff. |
| Submit evidence | Permission + before deadline. |
| Mark needs investigation | Ops/risk. |
| Replay webhook | Ops role. |
| Export evidence packet | Audit/risk role. |

---

# 25. Camunda Workflow Example

Dispute workflow is a good Camunda use case.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Dispute Opened Webhook] --> B[Create Dispute Case]
    B --> C[Assign Risk/Support Owner]
    C --> D{Auto Accept?}

    D -->|Yes| E[Accept Dispute]
    E --> F[Post Loss Ledger / Notify Finance]

    D -->|No| G[Collect Evidence]
    G --> H{Evidence Complete?}

    H -->|No| I[Reminder / Escalation]
    I --> G

    H -->|Yes| J[Submit Evidence]
    J --> K[Wait Provider Outcome]

    K --> L{Outcome}
    L -->|Won| M[Post Recovery Ledger]
    L -->|Lost| N[Finalize Chargeback Loss]
    L -->|Partial| O[Post Partial Adjustment]
    L -->|Timeout/Unknown| P[Needs Investigation]

    M --> Q[Reconcile]
    N --> Q
    O --> Q
```

Boundary:

```text
Camunda orchestrates support/risk workflow.
Payment service owns dispute state, ledger, provider integration, and invariants.
```

# 26. MyBatis Mapper Examples

## 26.1 Insert Dispute

```xml
<insert id="insertDispute">
    INSERT INTO disputes (
        dispute_id,
        payment_id,
        capture_id,
        provider,
        provider_dispute_id,
        provider_payment_id,
        provider_charge_id,
        provider_capture_id,
        status,
        raw_reason_code,
        reason_category,
        disputed_amount_minor,
        currency,
        chargeback_fee_amount_minor,
        chargeback_fee_currency,
        evidence_due_at,
        opened_at,
        provider_payload,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    ) VALUES (
        #{disputeId},
        #{paymentId},
        #{captureId},
        #{provider},
        #{providerDisputeId},
        #{providerPaymentId},
        #{providerChargeId},
        #{providerCaptureId},
        #{status},
        #{rawReasonCode},
        #{reasonCategory},
        #{disputedAmountMinor},
        #{currency},
        #{chargebackFeeAmountMinor},
        #{chargebackFeeCurrency},
        #{evidenceDueAt},
        #{openedAt},
        CAST(#{providerPayloadJson} AS jsonb),
        now(),
        now()
    )
</insert>
```

## 26.2 Mark Evidence Submitted

```xml
<update id="markEvidenceSubmitted">
    UPDATE disputes
    SET status = 'EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED',
        submitted_at = now(),
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE dispute_id = #{disputeId}
      AND status IN ('EVIDENCE_REQUIRED', 'EVIDENCE_COLLECTING', 'EVIDENCE_READY')
      AND (evidence_due_at IS NULL OR evidence_due_at > now())
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

## 26.3 Mark Dispute Won

```xml
<update id="markDisputeWon">
    UPDATE disputes
    SET status = 'WON',
        outcome = 'WON',
        resolved_at = now(),
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE dispute_id = #{disputeId}
      AND status IN ('EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED', 'UNDER_REVIEW', 'NEEDS_REVIEW')
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

## 26.4 Mark Dispute Lost

```xml
<update id="markDisputeLost">
    UPDATE disputes
    SET status = 'LOST',
        outcome = 'LOST',
        resolved_at = now(),
        version = version + 1,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE dispute_id = #{disputeId}
      AND status IN (
          'OPENED',
          'NEEDS_REVIEW',
          'ACCEPTED',
          'EVIDENCE_REQUIRED',
          'EVIDENCE_COLLECTING',
          'EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED',
          'UNDER_REVIEW',
          'EXPIRED'
      )
      AND version = #{version}
</update>
```

# 27. Error Codes

| Error Code | HTTP | Meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| `DISPUTE_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | Dispute tidak ditemukan. |
| `DISPUTE_INVALID_STATE` | 409 | Action tidak valid untuk status sekarang. |
| `DISPUTE_EVIDENCE_DEADLINE_PASSED` | 409 | Evidence sudah melewati deadline. |
| `DISPUTE_EVIDENCE_INCOMPLETE` | 422 | Evidence wajib belum lengkap. |
| `DISPUTE_ALREADY_ACCEPTED` | 409 | Dispute sudah accepted. |
| `DISPUTE_ALREADY_SUBMITTED` | 409 | Evidence sudah submitted. |
| `DISPUTE_NOT_DEFENDABLE` | 422 | Provider/dispute type tidak bisa defend. |
| `DISPUTE_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | 202/409 | Provider response tidak jelas. |
| `DISPUTE_REFERENCE_NOT_FOUND` | 409 | Payment/capture reference tidak ditemukan. |
| `REFUND_BLOCKED_BY_ACTIVE_DISPUTE` | 409 | Refund tidak boleh saat dispute aktif. |

Example:

```json
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/dispute-evidence-deadline-passed",
  "title": "Dispute evidence deadline passed",
  "status": 409,
  "detail": "Evidence can no longer be submitted because the provider deadline has passed.",
  "errorCode": "DISPUTE_EVIDENCE_DEADLINE_PASSED",
  "disputeId": "6a464a65-2709-49f4-900b-37c6149438de",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

```text
- dispute deadline within 24h with no evidence
- lost dispute spike
- chargeback ratio spike
- reference not found spike
- evidence submission failure
- chargeback fee spike
- provider dispute webhook lag
```

# 29. Testing Strategy

## 29.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Dispute webhook creates dispute | `OPENED` / `NEEDS_REVIEW`. |
| Duplicate dispute webhook | No duplicate dispute. |
| Dispute links to payment by provider refs | Correct `payment_id`. |
| Unknown provider dispute reason | `UNKNOWN`, not crash. |
| Accept dispute invalid state | Rejected. |
| Submit evidence after deadline | Rejected. |
| Evidence incomplete | Rejected. |
| Submit evidence success | `EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED`. |
| Dispute won webhook | `WON` + recovery ledger. |
| Dispute lost webhook | `LOST` + chargeback loss ledger. |
| Refund during active dispute | Blocked by policy. |
| Duplicate outcome webhook | Ledger once. |

## 29.2 Integration Tests

```text
1. Create captured payment.
2. Send provider dispute opened webhook.
3. Verify dispute row created.
4. Verify payment dispute status updated.
5. Verify task created.
6. Upload evidence documents.
7. Submit evidence via mock provider.
8. Verify dispute EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED.
9. Send dispute won webhook.
10. Verify dispute WON.
11. Verify outbox DisputeWon.
12. Verify ledger reversal posted once.
13. Replay won webhook.
14. Verify ledger still once.
```

## 29.3 Deadline Test

```text
1. Create dispute with evidence_due_at = now + 1 hour.
2. Run reminder job.
3. Verify escalation task.
4. Move clock past due_at.
5. Run expiry job.
6. Verify dispute EXPIRED.
7. Submit evidence should fail.
```

---

# 30. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Treat dispute as refund | Bisa salah accounting dan double loss. | Dispute entity sendiri. |
| Tidak simpan evidence deadline | Miss deadline, otomatis kalah. | `evidence_due_at` + reminders. |
| Tidak simpan raw reason/provider status | Sulit defend dengan PSP. | Store raw + normalized reason. |
| Refund otomatis saat dispute masuk | Bisa refund + chargeback. | Refund-dispute policy. |
| Late webhook overwrite outcome | State rusak. | Transition guard. |
| Duplicate outcome webhook double ledger | Accounting dobel. | Unique ledger reference. |
| Evidence file tidak versioned/audited | Sulit audit. | Evidence document/submission tables. |
| Tidak ada assigned owner | Dispute tidak tertangani. | Task/workflow. |
| Tidak ada outbox event | Risk/support/billing tidak tahu. | Outbox. |
| Tidak reconcile chargeback fee | Finance mismatch. | Reconciliation items. |

Target skenario:

```text
1. Payment CAPTURED.
2. Receive dispute opened webhook.
3. Create dispute NEEDS_REVIEW.
4. Create support task.
5. Upload evidence documents.
6. Decide defend.
7. Submit evidence before due date.
8. Provider returns under review.
9. Receive dispute won webhook.
10. Post recovery ledger.
11. Publish DisputeWon outbox.
12. Replay duplicate webhook; ledger remains once.
13. Create another dispute and miss deadline.
14. Mark EXPIRED then LOST.
15. Verify refund blocked while dispute active.
```

---

# Ringkasan

Dispute/chargeback adalah workflow finansial dan operasional yang berbeda dari refund.

Prinsip utama:

```text
1. Dispute harus punya entity dan lifecycle sendiri.
2. Provider webhook dispute harus dedup dan diproses async.
3. Reason code harus disimpan raw + normalized.
4. Evidence deadline adalah data kritikal.
5. Accept vs defend harus policy-based.
6. Evidence documents harus auditable.
7. Refund saat dispute aktif harus dibatasi.
8. Outcome won/lost harus memengaruhi ledger.
9. Dispute events harus dipublish via outbox.
10. Chargeback/fee/outcome harus direconcile.
```

Dengan desain ini, payment service tidak hanya “tahu ada chargeback”, tetapi bisa mengelola dispute secara operasional: assignment, evidence, deadline, provider submission, outcome, ledger, dan reconciliation.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 12/18 - Payment Ledger dan Financial Audit Trail

Seri ini fokus pada **payment ledger** dan **financial audit trail** untuk payment service berbasis **Java Jersey/JAX-RS + PostgreSQL/MyBatis**.

Core idea:

```text id="zneqhs"
Payment status menjawab: “apa state transaksi?”
Ledger menjawab: “apa dampak finansial dari transaksi?”
Audit trail menjawab: “siapa/apa yang mengubah apa, kapan, dan kenapa?”
```

Provider besar juga memodelkan pergerakan dana sebagai transaction/balance movement. Stripe, misalnya, mendokumentasikan **Balance Transactions** sebagai record dana yang masuk atau keluar dari Stripe account balance, dan menyebut balance transactions sebagai starting point untuk reporting balance activity. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/balance_transactions?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen menyediakan **Payment accounting report** untuk lifecycle status changes, events, modifications, cost/status matching, dan invoice reconciliation. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/reporting/invoice-reconciliation/payment-accounting-report?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PayPal menyediakan Transaction Search API untuk mengambil histori transaksi PayPal account. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/api/transaction-search/v1/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Payment service minimal punya 3 layer data finansial:

```text id="w9mo7s"
1. Operational State
   payments.status, refunds.status, disputes.status

2. Financial Ledger
   ledger_entries, account_code, debit/credit, amount, reference

3. Audit Trail
   payment_events, refund_events, dispute_events, immutable action/event history
```

Contoh:

```text id="bixg18"
Payment CAPTURED 100,000 IDR
  -> payment.status = CAPTURED
  -> ledger entry PAYMENT_CAPTURED CREDIT 100,000
  -> payment event PAYMENT_CAPTURED
  -> outbox PaymentCaptured
```

Refund:

```text id="dhb2b2"
Refund SUCCEEDED 25,000 IDR
  -> refund.status = SUCCEEDED
  -> payment.refunded_amount_minor += 25,000
  -> ledger entry REFUND_SUCCEEDED DEBIT 25,000
  -> refund event REFUND_SUCCEEDED
  -> outbox RefundSucceeded
```

Chargeback lost:

```text id="vaos8n"
Dispute LOST 100,000 IDR
  -> dispute.status = LOST
  -> ledger entry CHARGEBACK_LOST DEBIT 100,000
  -> optional ledger entry CHARGEBACK_FEE DEBIT fee
  -> dispute event DISPUTE_LOST
  -> outbox DisputeLost
```

---

# 2. Diagram

```mermaid id="vfdvt4"
flowchart TD
    A[Payment State Change] --> B[Application Service Transaction]

    B --> C[Update Aggregate State]
    C --> C1[payments]
    C --> C2[refunds]
    C --> C3[disputes]
    C --> C4[payment_captures]

    B --> D[Insert Audit Event]
    D --> D1[payment_events]
    D --> D2[refund_events]
    D --> D3[dispute_events]

    B --> E[Insert Ledger Entry]
    E --> E1[ledger_entries]
    E --> E2[ledger_batches optional]
    E --> E3[ledger_accounts]

    B --> F[Insert Outbox Event]
    F --> F1[payment_outbox_events]

    E1 --> G[Reconciliation]
    G --> G1[Stripe Balance Transactions]
    G --> G2[Adyen Settlement / Accounting Report]
    G --> G3[PayPal Transaction Report]
    G --> G4[Bank Statement]

    G --> H[Finance Reporting]
    H --> H1[Gross Volume]
    H --> H2[Refunds]
    H --> H3[Fees]
    H --> H4[Chargebacks]
    H --> H5[Net Settlement]
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Ledger Entry Model** | Catatan finansial append-only. | `ledger_entries` |
| 2 | **Ledger Account Model** | Kode akun internal untuk receivable, fees, refunds, settlement. | `ledger_accounts` |
| 3 | **Debit/Credit Direction** | Direction finansial internal. | `DEBIT`, `CREDIT` |
| 4 | **Reference Idempotency** | Cegah double ledger. | unique `reference_type + reference_id + entry_type` |
| 5 | **Payment Posting** | Ledger saat capture. | `PAYMENT_CAPTURED` |
| 6 | **Refund Posting** | Ledger saat refund sukses. | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED` |
| 7 | **Dispute Posting** | Ledger saat chargeback/fee/reversal. | `CHARGEBACK_LOST`, `CHARGEBACK_FEE` |
| 8 | **Settlement Posting** | Ledger saat settlement report masuk. | `SETTLEMENT_RECEIVED`, `FEE_CHARGED` |
| 9 | **Payout Posting** | Ledger saat provider payout ke bank. | `PAYOUT_PAID` |
| 10 | **Audit Event** | Timeline immutable domain event. | `payment_events`, `refund_events`, `dispute_events` |
| 11 | **Immutable Guard** | Mencegah update/delete ledger. | DB privilege/trigger/policy |
| 12 | **Reversal Pattern** | Koreksi pakai reversal, bukan update. | `REVERSAL` entries |
| 13 | **Reconciliation Support** | Cocokkan provider report dengan ledger. | `reconciliation_items` |
| 14 | **Reporting Views** | Finance dashboard/reporting. | `v_ledger_summary`, materialized view |
| 15 | **Operational Investigation** | Trace from ledger → payment/refund/dispute/event. | reference fields |

---

# 4. Ledger vs Event vs Outbox

| Data | Tujuan | Boleh Diubah? | Consumer |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `payments.status` | State operasional saat ini. | Ya, melalui valid transition. | API/service internal. |
| `payment_events` | Timeline audit state transition. | Tidak seharusnya. | Support/audit/debug. |
| `ledger_entries` | Dampak finansial append-only. | Tidak. | Finance/reconciliation/reporting. |
| `payment_outbox_events` | Event integration ke service lain. | Status publish boleh berubah. | Broker/consumer. |

Prinsip:

```text id="pu2jaj"
Event menjelaskan “apa yang terjadi”.
Ledger menjelaskan “dampak finansialnya”.
Outbox menjelaskan “apa yang harus diberitahu ke sistem lain”.
```

---

# 5. Ledger Model: Simple vs Double-Entry-ish

## 5.1 Simple Ledger

Satu event finansial = satu row ledger.

```text id="fbf8ys"
PAYMENT_CAPTURED CREDIT 100,000 MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE
REFUND_SUCCEEDED  DEBIT   25,000 MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE
FEE_CHARGED       DEBIT    2,500 PAYMENT_PROVIDER_FEES
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="s2bn7l"
Mudah dipahami.
Cukup untuk audit payment operational.
Cepat diimplementasikan.
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="to0oeb"
Tidak benar-benar balanced secara accounting.
Finance mungkin tetap perlu mapping ke GL/accounting system.
```

## 5.2 Double-Entry-ish Ledger

Satu event finansial menghasilkan minimal dua sisi.

Contoh capture:

```text id="xodnxt"
DEBIT  PSP_CLEARING_RECEIVABLE 100,000
CREDIT CUSTOMER_PAYMENT_REVENUE_OR_LIABILITY 100,000
```

Refund:

```text id="c2n2ed"
DEBIT  REFUND_EXPENSE_OR_REVENUE_REVERSAL 25,000
CREDIT PSP_CLEARING_RECEIVABLE             25,000
```

Fee:

```text id="q9bobv"
DEBIT  PAYMENT_PROVIDER_FEES 2,500
CREDIT PSP_CLEARING_RECEIVABLE 2,500
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="v743su"
Lebih dekat ke accounting.
Setiap batch bisa dicek balance debit = credit.
Lebih mudah masuk finance/GL.
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="a8wi05"
Butuh desain chart of accounts.
Butuh alignment dengan finance.
Lebih kompleks.
```

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text id="qsx4jk"
Untuk payment microservice:
mulai dengan simple append-only ledger yang punya account_code jelas.

Untuk finance-grade platform:
gunakan double-entry-ish ledger atau export ke accounting system yang melakukan double-entry final.
```

---

# 6. Recommended Ledger Accounts

Contoh `account_code` internal:

| Account Code | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE` | Piutang/receivable dari payment captured. |
| `PSP_CLEARING` | Balance/clearing di provider. |
| `PAYMENT_PROVIDER_FEES` | Fee PSP/acquirer. |
| `REFUND_PAYABLE` | Kewajiban refund. |
| `CHARGEBACK_LOSS` | Kerugian chargeback. |
| `CHARGEBACK_FEES` | Fee chargeback/dispute. |
| `SETTLEMENT_RECEIVED` | Dana settled/available. |
| `PAYOUT_TO_BANK` | Dana payout ke rekening bank. |
| `BANK_CASH` | Dana aktual di bank. |
| `FX_GAIN_LOSS` | Selisih kurs jika multi-currency. |
| `ADJUSTMENT` | Koreksi/adjustment manual terkontrol. |

Table:

```sql id="lvdm5q"
CREATE TABLE ledger_accounts (
    account_code VARCHAR(80) PRIMARY KEY,
    account_name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    account_type VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_account_type
        CHECK (account_type IN (
            'ASSET',
            'LIABILITY',
            'REVENUE',
            'EXPENSE',
            'CLEARING',
            'CONTRA_REVENUE',
            'ADJUSTMENT'
        ))
);
```

Seed:

```sql id="rfpyva"
INSERT INTO ledger_accounts (
    account_code,
    account_name,
    account_type
)
VALUES
    ('MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE', 'Merchant Receivable', 'ASSET'),
    ('PSP_CLEARING', 'Payment Provider Clearing', 'CLEARING'),
    ('PAYMENT_PROVIDER_FEES', 'Payment Provider Fees', 'EXPENSE'),
    ('REFUND_PAYABLE', 'Refund Payable', 'LIABILITY'),
    ('CHARGEBACK_LOSS', 'Chargeback Loss', 'EXPENSE'),
    ('CHARGEBACK_FEES', 'Chargeback Fees', 'EXPENSE'),
    ('SETTLEMENT_RECEIVED', 'Settlement Received', 'ASSET'),
    ('PAYOUT_TO_BANK', 'Payout To Bank', 'ASSET'),
    ('BANK_CASH', 'Bank Cash', 'ASSET'),
    ('FX_GAIN_LOSS', 'FX Gain Loss', 'ADJUSTMENT'),
    ('ADJUSTMENT', 'Manual Adjustment', 'ADJUSTMENT');
```

# 7. `ledger_entries` Schema

Versi simple tapi production-friendly:

```sql id="tnl873"
CREATE TABLE ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    ledger_batch_id UUID,

    payment_id UUID REFERENCES payments(payment_id),
    capture_id UUID,
    refund_id UUID REFERENCES refunds(refund_id),
    dispute_id UUID,

    provider VARCHAR(50),
    merchant_id VARCHAR(100),
    customer_id VARCHAR(100),
    order_id VARCHAR(100),

    entry_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    direction VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,

    account_code VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL REFERENCES ledger_accounts(account_code),

    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    exchange_rate NUMERIC(20, 10),
    functional_amount_minor BIGINT,
    functional_currency CHAR(3),

    reference_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    reference_id VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    provider_reference_id VARCHAR(200),

    description TEXT,

    event_source VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    event_id UUID,

    reversal_of_ledger_entry_id UUID REFERENCES ledger_entries(ledger_entry_id),
    reversed BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,

    metadata JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_entries_direction
        CHECK (direction IN ('DEBIT', 'CREDIT')),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_entries_amount_positive
        CHECK (amount_minor > 0),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_entries_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency)),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_entries_functional_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (
            functional_currency IS NULL
            OR functional_currency = upper(functional_currency)
        ),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_entries_event_source
        CHECK (event_source IN (
            'API',
            'PROVIDER_RESPONSE',
            'WEBHOOK',
            'RECONCILIATION',
            'SYSTEM_JOB',
            'ADMIN',
            'CAMUNDA'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql id="qymy8s"
CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_payment_id
ON ledger_entries(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_capture_id
ON ledger_entries(capture_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_refund_id
ON ledger_entries(refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_dispute_id
ON ledger_entries(dispute_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_order_id
ON ledger_entries(order_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_account_currency
ON ledger_entries(account_code, currency);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_entry_type
ON ledger_entries(entry_type);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_created_at
ON ledger_entries(created_at);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_provider_reference
ON ledger_entries(provider, provider_reference_id);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_ledger_entries_reference
ON ledger_entries(reference_type, reference_id, entry_type);

CREATE INDEX ix_ledger_entries_metadata_gin
ON ledger_entries USING GIN(metadata);
```

---

# 8. Immutable Ledger Guard

Ledger idealnya append-only.

```text id="t65fve"
Jangan UPDATE amount.
Jangan DELETE row.
Kalau salah, buat reversal entry.
```

PostgreSQL mendukung trigger yang dapat dieksekusi sebelum/sesudah `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE`, atau `TRUNCATE`; ini bisa dipakai sebagai defense-in-depth untuk mencegah perubahan ledger di luar flow resmi. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/trigger-definition.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Trigger untuk block update/delete:

```sql id="jvd5lz"
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION prevent_ledger_mutation()
RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'ledger_entries is append-only; update/delete is not allowed';
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

Apply:

```sql id="xtc1k1"
CREATE TRIGGER trg_prevent_ledger_update
BEFORE UPDATE ON ledger_entries
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_ledger_mutation();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_prevent_ledger_delete
BEFORE DELETE ON ledger_entries
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION prevent_ledger_mutation();
```

Catatan praktis:

```text id="99ybe4"
Trigger append-only bagus sebagai safety net.
Tetapi tetap atur DB privileges:
aplikasi normal hanya boleh INSERT/SELECT ledger,
bukan UPDATE/DELETE.
```

---

# 9. Reversal Pattern

Jika ledger salah, jangan update/delete.

Buat reversal.

Original:

```text id="4mpqjz"
ledger_entry_id = L1
entry_type = PAYMENT_CAPTURED
direction = CREDIT
amount = 100,000
```

Correction:

```text id="to3h02"
ledger_entry_id = L2
entry_type = PAYMENT_CAPTURED_REVERSAL
direction = DEBIT
amount = 100,000
reversal_of_ledger_entry_id = L1
```

SQL:

```sql id="217dnh"
INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    account_code,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    description,
    event_source,
    reversal_of_ledger_entry_id,
    created_by
)
SELECT
    gen_random_uuid(),
    payment_id,
    entry_type || '_REVERSAL',
    CASE
        WHEN direction = 'DEBIT' THEN 'CREDIT'
        ELSE 'DEBIT'
    END,
    account_code,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    'LEDGER_REVERSAL',
    ledger_entry_id::text,
    'Reversal of ledger entry ' || ledger_entry_id,
    'ADMIN',
    ledger_entry_id,
    #{createdBy}
FROM ledger_entries
WHERE ledger_entry_id = #{ledgerEntryId};
```

Mark original reversed? Since update is blocked, either:

1. allow limited update only for `reversed = true`, or  
2. infer reversed by querying reversal rows.

Rekomendasi untuk strict immutable:

```text id="0wgrco"
Jangan update original row.
Gunakan view untuk menentukan apakah ledger entry sudah reversed.
```

View:

```sql id="cqiiaj"
CREATE VIEW v_ledger_entries_with_reversal_status AS
SELECT
    le.*,
    EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM ledger_entries r
        WHERE r.reversal_of_ledger_entry_id = le.ledger_entry_id
    ) AS has_reversal
FROM ledger_entries le;
```

# 10. Ledger Posting Rules

## 10.1 Payment Captured

Simple ledger:

```sql id="au3f7b"
INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    capture_id,
    provider,
    merchant_id,
    customer_id,
    order_id,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    account_code,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    provider_reference_id,
    description,
    event_source,
    event_id,
    created_by
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    #{paymentId},
    #{captureId},
    #{provider},
    #{merchantId},
    #{customerId},
    #{orderId},
    'PAYMENT_CAPTURED',

ON CONFLICT (reference_type, reference_id, entry_type)
DO NOTHING;
```

# 11. Double-Entry-ish Batch Model

Jika ingin lebih accounting-ready, tambahkan batch/header.

## 11.1 `ledger_batches`

```sql id="cw64eb"
CREATE TABLE ledger_batches (
    ledger_batch_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    batch_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    source VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    reference_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    reference_id VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    payment_id UUID REFERENCES payments(payment_id),
    refund_id UUID REFERENCES refunds(refund_id),
    dispute_id UUID,

    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    total_debit_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    total_credit_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    balanced BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_batches_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency)),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_batches_source
        CHECK (source IN (
            'API',
            'PROVIDER_RESPONSE',
            'WEBHOOK',
            'RECONCILIATION',
            'SYSTEM_JOB',
            'ADMIN'
        ))
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_ledger_batches_reference
ON ledger_batches(reference_type, reference_id, batch_type);
```

## 11.2 Balanced Constraint via Application

Karena entries dimasukkan sebagai beberapa row, validasi balance lebih mudah di application transaction:

```text id="rarydj"
sum(DEBIT) == sum(CREDIT)
```

Pseudo-code:

```java id="nzyqq7"
public void postBatch(LedgerBatchCommand command) {
    long totalDebit = command.entries().stream()
            .filter(e -> e.direction() == Direction.DEBIT)
            .mapToLong(LedgerEntryCommand::amountMinor)
            .sum();

    long totalCredit = command.entries().stream()
            .filter(e -> e.direction() == Direction.CREDIT)
            .mapToLong(LedgerEntryCommand::amountMinor)
            .sum();

    if (totalDebit != totalCredit) {
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Ledger batch is not balanced");
    }

    transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
        ledgerBatchMapper.insert(command.batch());
        for (LedgerEntryCommand entry : command.entries()) {
            ledgerEntryMapper.insert(entry);
        }
        ledgerBatchMapper.markBalanced(command.batchId(), totalDebit, totalCredit);
        return null;
    });
}
```

---

# 12. Ledger Service Design

```java id="1u8m0q"
package com.company.payment.application.ledger;

public interface LedgerPostingService {

    void postPaymentCaptured(PaymentCapturedLedgerCommand command);

    void postRefundSucceeded(RefundSucceededLedgerCommand command);

    void postProviderFeeCharged(ProviderFeeLedgerCommand command);

    void postChargebackLost(ChargebackLostLedgerCommand command);

    void postChargebackWonReversal(ChargebackWonLedgerCommand command);

    void postSettlementReceived(SettlementLedgerCommand command);

    void postPayoutPaid(PayoutLedgerCommand command);

    void postManualAdjustment(ManualAdjustmentLedgerCommand command);
}
```

Implementation should be idempotent:

```java id="gcm4fo"
public class DefaultLedgerPostingService implements LedgerPostingService {

    private final LedgerEntryMapper ledgerEntryMapper;

    @Override
    public void postPaymentCaptured(PaymentCapturedLedgerCommand command) {
        LedgerEntryRecord entry = LedgerEntryRecord.builder()
                .ledgerEntryId(UUID.randomUUID())
                .paymentId(command.paymentId())
                .captureId(command.captureId())
                .provider(command.provider())
                .merchantId(command.merchantId())
                .customerId(command.customerId())
                .orderId(command.orderId())
                .entryType("PAYMENT_CAPTURED")
                .direction("CREDIT")
                .accountCode("MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE")
                .amountMinor(command.amountMinor())
                .currency(command.currency())
                .referenceType("PROVIDER_CAPTURE")
                .referenceId(command.providerCaptureId())
                .providerReferenceId(command.providerCaptureId())
                .description("Payment captured")
                .eventSource(command.eventSource())
                .eventId(command.eventId())
                .createdBy("payment-service")
                .build();

        ledgerEntryMapper.insertIfAbsent(entry);
    }
}
```

# 13. MyBatis Mapper

## 13.1 Interface

```java id="nlt00y"
package com.company.payment.infrastructure.persistence.ledger;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

public interface LedgerEntryMapper {

    int insertIfAbsent(LedgerEntryRecord record);

    List<LedgerEntryRecord> findByPaymentId(UUID paymentId);

    List<LedgerEntryRecord> findByRefundId(UUID refundId);

    List<LedgerEntryRecord> findByDisputeId(UUID disputeId);

    List<LedgerEntryRecord> findByReference(String referenceType, String referenceId);

    LedgerSummaryRecord summarizeByPaymentId(UUID paymentId);
}
```

## 13.2 Insert If Absent

```xml id="rr36a1"
<insert id="insertIfAbsent">
    INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
        ledger_entry_id,
        ledger_batch_id,
        payment_id,
        capture_id,
        refund_id,
        dispute_id,
        provider,
        merchant_id,
        customer_id,
        order_id,
        entry_type,
        direction,
        account_code,
        amount_minor,
        currency,
        exchange_rate,
        functional_amount_minor,
        functional_currency,
        reference_type,
        reference_id,
        provider_reference_id,
        description,
        event_source,
        event_id,
        metadata,
        created_at,
        created_by
    ) VALUES (
        #{ledgerEntryId},
        #{ledgerBatchId},
        #{paymentId},
        #{captureId},
        #{refundId},
        #{disputeId},
        #{provider},
        #{merchantId},
        #{customerId},
        #{orderId},
        #{entryType},
        #{direction},
        #{accountCode},
        #{amountMinor},
        #{currency},
        #{exchangeRate},
        #{functionalAmountMinor},
        #{functionalCurrency},
        #{referenceType},
        #{referenceId},
        #{providerReferenceId},
        #{description},
        #{eventSource},
        #{eventId},
        CAST(#{metadataJson} AS jsonb),
        now(),
        #{createdBy}
    )
    ON CONFLICT (reference_type, reference_id, entry_type)
    DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

## 13.3 Payment Ledger Timeline

```xml id="qd2yj1"
<select id="findByPaymentId" resultMap="LedgerEntryResultMap">
    SELECT
        ledger_entry_id,
        ledger_batch_id,
        payment_id,

        dispute_id,
        provider,
        merchant_id,
        customer_id,
        order_id,
        entry_type,
        direction,
        account_code,
        amount_minor,
        currency,
        reference_type,
        reference_id,
        provider_reference_id,
        description,
        event_source,
        event_id,
        created_at,
        created_by
    FROM ledger_entries
    WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
    ORDER BY created_at ASC, ledger_entry_id ASC
</select>
```

## 13.4 Summary by Payment

```xml id="uqb03t"
<select id="summarizeByPaymentId" resultMap="LedgerSummaryResultMap">
    SELECT
        payment_id,
        currency,
        SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'PAYMENT_CAPTURED' THEN amount_minor ELSE 0 END)
            AS captured_minor,
        SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'REFUND_SUCCEEDED' THEN amount_minor ELSE 0 END)
            AS refunded_minor,
        SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED' THEN amount_minor ELSE 0 END)
            AS fee_minor,
        SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'CHARGEBACK_LOST' THEN amount_minor ELSE 0 END)
            AS chargeback_lost_minor,
        SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'CHARGEBACK_WON_REVERSAL' THEN amount_minor ELSE 0 END)
            AS chargeback_recovered_minor
    FROM ledger_entries
    WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
    GROUP BY payment_id, currency
</select>
```

---

# 14. Audit Trail Design

Ledger is financial. Audit events are behavioral/operational.

`payment_events` should capture:

```text id="zbyjgv"
- state transition
- source
- old status
- new status
- provider event id
- raw/parsed metadata
- actor
- timestamp
```

Example:

```sql id="a1a273"
CREATE TABLE payment_events (
    event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    event_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    source VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    old_status VARCHAR(50),
    new_status VARCHAR(50),

    provider VARCHAR(50),
    provider_event_id VARCHAR(200),

    request_id VARCHAR(100),
    correlation_id VARCHAR(100),
    causation_id VARCHAR(100),

    payload JSONB,
    raw_payload TEXT,

    created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_events_source
        CHECK (source IN (
            'API',
            'PROVIDER_RESPONSE',
            'WEBHOOK',
            'RECONCILIATION',
            'SYSTEM_JOB',
            'ADMIN',
            'CAMUNDA'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql id="t7kamo"
CREATE INDEX ix_payment_events_payment_id
ON payment_events(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_events_type
ON payment_events(event_type);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_events_created_at
ON payment_events(created_at);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_events_provider_event
ON payment_events(provider, provider_event_id)
WHERE provider IS NOT NULL AND provider_event_id IS NOT NULL;
```

---

# 15. Audit Event Examples

| Event Type | Source | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `PAYMENT_INITIATED` | API | Payment dibuat. |
| `PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED` | PROVIDER_RESPONSE/WEBHOOK | Auth sukses. |
| `PAYMENT_CAPTURED` | PROVIDER_RESPONSE/WEBHOOK | Capture sukses. |
| `PAYMENT_CAPTURE_UNKNOWN` | PROVIDER_RESPONSE | Timeout/unclear. |
| `REFUND_REQUESTED` | API/ADMIN/CAMUNDA | Refund diminta. |
| `REFUND_SUCCEEDED` | PROVIDER_RESPONSE/WEBHOOK | Refund sukses. |
| `DISPUTE_OPENED` | WEBHOOK | Dispute masuk. |
| `DISPUTE_EVIDENCE_SUBMITTED` | API/PROVIDER_RESPONSE | Evidence submitted. |
| `RECONCILIATION_MATCHED` | RECONCILIATION | Provider report matched. |
| `MANUAL_ADJUSTMENT_CREATED` | ADMIN | Adjustment manual. |

Practical rule:

```text id="k926hk"
Ledger entries tidak menggantikan audit events.
Audit events menjelaskan konteks dan actor.
Ledger entries menjelaskan amount/account/reference.
```

---

# 16. Manual Adjustment

Manual adjustment harus sangat dibatasi.

Use case:

```text id="5zj8bh"
- provider report correction
- migration data repair
- finance-approved adjustment
- reconciliation mismatch resolution
```

Schema:

```sql id="2kntc2"
CREATE TABLE ledger_adjustments (
    ledger_adjustment_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    payment_id UUID REFERENCES payments(payment_id),
    refund_id UUID REFERENCES refunds(refund_id),
    dispute_id UUID,

    reason_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    reason_detail TEXT NOT NULL,

    requested_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    approved_by VARCHAR(100),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_ledger_adjustments_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'REQUESTED',
            'APPROVED',
            'REJECTED',
            'POSTED',
            'CANCELLED'
        ))
);
```

Rules:

```text id="594kuz"
1. No direct ledger update.
2. Adjustment requires reason.
3. High-value adjustment requires approval.
4. Adjustment posts new ledger entry.
5. Adjustment must be auditable.
```

---

# 17. Reconciliation Support

Ledger must reconcile against provider financial records.

Provider sources:

| Provider | Useful Source |
|---|---|
| Stripe | Balance Transactions / Balance reports. |
| Adyen | Settlement details report, Payment accounting report. |
| PayPal | Transaction Search API / Transaction Detail Report. |

Stripe Balance report returns complete transaction history for reconciliation, and Stripe Balance Transactions represent funds moving through the account. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/reports/report-types/balance?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen Settlement Details Report is transaction-level and includes settled/paid out payments and balance movements, while Adyen Payment Accounting Report includes lifecycle status changes, events, and modifications for transactions. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/reporting/settlement-reconciliation/transaction-level/settlement-details-report?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PayPal Transaction Detail Report provides detailed transaction data including debits, credits, and payments for a defined recent period. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/reports/sftp-reports/transaction-detail/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Reconciliation keys:

```text id="gks9gf"
provider
provider_payment_id
provider_capture_id
provider_refund_id
provider_dispute_id
provider_balance_transaction_id
amount
currency
event type
event date
```

---

# 18. Reconciliation Views

## 18.1 Ledger Summary by Payment

```sql id="hrah3t"
CREATE VIEW v_payment_ledger_summary AS
SELECT
    payment_id,
    currency,

    SUM(CASE
        WHEN entry_type = 'PAYMENT_CAPTURED' THEN amount_minor
        ELSE 0
    END) AS captured_minor,

    SUM(CASE
        WHEN entry_type = 'REFUND_SUCCEEDED' THEN amount_minor
        ELSE 0
    END) AS refunded_minor,

    SUM(CASE
        WHEN entry_type = 'PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED' THEN amount_minor
        ELSE 0
    END) AS provider_fee_minor,

    SUM(CASE
        WHEN entry_type = 'CHARGEBACK_LOST' THEN amount_minor
        ELSE 0
    END) AS chargeback_lost_minor,

    SUM(CASE
        WHEN entry_type = 'CHARGEBACK_WON_REVERSAL' THEN amount_minor
        ELSE 0
    END) AS chargeback_recovered_minor

FROM ledger_entries
GROUP BY payment_id, currency;
```

## 18.2 Payment vs Ledger Consistency

```sql id="pzsskp"
CREATE VIEW v_payment_ledger_consistency AS
SELECT
    p.payment_id,
    p.currency,

    p.captured_amount_minor AS payment_captured_minor,
    COALESCE(s.captured_minor, 0) AS ledger_captured_minor,

    p.refunded_amount_minor AS payment_refunded_minor,
    COALESCE(s.refunded_minor, 0) AS ledger_refunded_minor,

    CASE
        WHEN p.captured_amount_minor = COALESCE(s.captured_minor, 0)
         AND p.refunded_amount_minor = COALESCE(s.refunded_minor, 0)
        THEN 'MATCHED'
        ELSE 'MISMATCH'
    END AS consistency_status

FROM payments p
LEFT JOIN v_payment_ledger_summary s
  ON s.payment_id = p.payment_id
 AND s.currency = p.currency;
```

## 18.3 Daily Ledger Summary

```sql id="o5wkp6"
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_daily_ledger_summary AS
SELECT
    date_trunc('day', created_at)::date AS ledger_date,
    provider,
    merchant_id,
    account_code,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    currency,
    COUNT(*) AS entry_count,
    SUM(amount_minor) AS total_amount_minor
FROM ledger_entries
GROUP BY
    date_trunc('day', created_at)::date,
    provider,
    merchant_id,
    account_code,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    currency;
```

Refresh:

```sql id="2uisn9"
REFRESH MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_daily_ledger_summary;
```

---

# 19. Ledger Consistency Jobs

Scheduled checks:

```text id="o2x8il"
1. Payment captured amount = sum successful captures ledger.
2. Payment refunded amount = sum successful refund ledger.
3. Ledger entries have valid account_code.
4. Duplicate provider reference impossible.
5. Provider report item matched to ledger.
6. Settlement net = gross - fee - refund - dispute adjustments.
7. No pending reconciliation older than SLA.
```

Example query: captured mismatch.

```sql id="fvwc43"
SELECT *
FROM v_payment_ledger_consistency
WHERE consistency_status = 'MISMATCH';
```

Query duplicate financial references:

```sql id="e6c99n"
SELECT
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    entry_type,
    COUNT(*)
FROM ledger_entries
GROUP BY reference_type, reference_id, entry_type
HAVING COUNT(*) > 1;
```

---

# 20. Multi-Currency Handling

Never use `double` for money.

Use:

```text id="ab6ze1"
amount_minor BIGINT
currency CHAR(3)
```

For multi-currency reporting:

```text id="3f6kiw"
amount_minor             = original transaction currency
currency                 = original ISO-like currency code
exchange_rate            = provider/accounting FX rate
functional_amount_minor  = company reporting currency amount
functional_currency      = company reporting currency
```

Example:

```text id="g0sw5b"
Payment captured:
100.00 USD -> amount_minor = 10000, currency = USD

Functional reporting:
1,600,000 IDR -> functional_amount_minor = 1600000, functional_currency = IDR
```

Caution:

```text id="qcxa7n"
Provider FX amount, accounting FX amount, and bank FX amount can differ.
Store source and timestamp of exchange rate if you report functional currency.
```

---

# 21. Provider Balance Transaction Mapping

Use provider balance/settlement/report records to enrich ledger.

Example provider financial transaction table:

```sql id="714p3u"
CREATE TABLE provider_financial_transactions (
    provider_financial_transaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_transaction_id VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_capture_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_refund_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_dispute_id VARCHAR(200),

    transaction_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    gross_amount_minor BIGINT,
    fee_amount_minor BIGINT,
    net_amount_minor BIGINT,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    available_on DATE,
    reporting_category VARCHAR(100),

    raw_payload JSONB,

    imported_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_provider_financial_tx_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency))
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_provider_financial_transactions
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_transaction_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_financial_tx_payment
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_financial_tx_refund
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_financial_tx_dispute
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_dispute_id);
```

---

# 22. Ledger Posting from Reconciliation

When provider report arrives:

```text id="qysnyw"
1. Import provider financial transactions.
2. Match to payment/refund/dispute.
3. If fee appears, post PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED.
4. If settlement appears, post SETTLEMENT_RECEIVED.
5. If payout appears, post PAYOUT_PAID.
6. Create reconciliation item.
```

Important:

```text id="n0vi6i"
Do not use provider reports to blindly overwrite payment state.
Use reports to reconcile and post financial settlement/fee entries.
```

---

# 23. Audit Trail Query Patterns

## 23.1 Full Payment Investigation

```sql id="58dy1h"
SELECT 'payment_event' AS source,
       event_type,
       old_status,
       new_status,
       created_at,
       created_by
FROM payment_events
WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}

UNION ALL

SELECT 'ledger_entry' AS source,
       entry_type AS event_type,
       NULL AS old_status,
       direction || ' ' || account_code AS new_status,
       created_at,
       created_by
FROM ledger_entries
WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}

ORDER BY created_at ASC;
```

## 23.2 Finance View Per Order

```sql id="7rvsxv"
SELECT
    order_id,
    currency,
    SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'PAYMENT_CAPTURED' THEN amount_minor ELSE 0 END) AS captured,
    SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'REFUND_SUCCEEDED' THEN amount_minor ELSE 0 END) AS refunded,
    SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED' THEN amount_minor ELSE 0 END) AS fees,
    SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'CHARGEBACK_LOST' THEN amount_minor ELSE 0 END) AS chargeback_loss
FROM ledger_entries
WHERE order_id = #{orderId}
GROUP BY order_id, currency;
```

```http id="cvv50h"
GET /payments/{paymentId}/ledger
```

# 25. Security and Access Control

Ledger API harus restricted.

| Endpoint | Role |
|---|---|
| `GET /payments/{id}/ledger` | Support senior / finance / audit |
| `GET /finance/ledger-summary` | Finance |
| Manual adjustment | Finance approver + audit role |
| Reversal | Restricted admin + approval |
| Export ledger | Finance/audit only |
| Raw provider financial data | Finance/ops only |

Practical rule:

```text id="x75z46"
Support boleh melihat ringkasan finansial.
Finance/audit boleh melihat ledger detail.
Hanya role terbatas yang boleh membuat adjustment.
```

---

# 26. Data Retention

Ledger dan audit trail biasanya punya retention panjang.

Practical direction:

| Data | Retention Direction |
|---|---|
| `ledger_entries` | Long retention, archive carefully. |
| `payment_events` | Long retention for audit/debug. |
| `provider_financial_transactions` | Long enough for reconciliation/audit. |
| raw webhook payload | Retention depends sensitivity and compliance. |
| raw provider report files | Store with access control. |
| manual adjustments | Long retention. |
| evidence documents | Depends dispute/compliance/legal policy. |

---

# 27. Observability

Metrics:

| Metric | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `ledger.entries.created.total` | Jumlah ledger entry dibuat. |
| `ledger.entry.duplicate.total` | Duplicate prevented by unique ref. |
| `ledger.payment_captured.amount` | Captured amount by provider/currency. |
| `ledger.refund.amount` | Refund amount. |
| `ledger.fee.amount` | Provider fee amount. |
| `ledger.chargeback.loss.amount` | Chargeback loss amount. |
| `ledger.consistency.mismatch.total` | Payment vs ledger mismatch. |
| `ledger.reconciliation.unmatched.total` | Provider report unmatched. |
| `ledger.adjustment.created.total` | Manual adjustments. |
| `ledger.reversal.created.total` | Reversal count. |

Structured log:

```json id="v7idsn"
{
  "event": "ledger_entry_created",
  "ledgerEntryId": "85b6e967-5abd-4afd-af6a-8fe5f4d18cd0",
  "paymentId": "b1806219-220c-4a25-ad4e-33b233143c0a",
  "entryType": "PAYMENT_CAPTURED",
  "direction": "CREDIT",
  "accountCode": "MERCHANT_RECEIVABLE",
  "amountMinor": 1000000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "referenceType": "PROVIDER_CAPTURE",
  "referenceId": "cap_123",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

Alerts:

```text id="b689nm"
- ledger consistency mismatch
- duplicate ledger reference attempt spike
- reconciliation unmatched older than SLA
- manual adjustment created
- reversal created
- provider fee mismatch spike
- chargeback loss spike
```

---

# 28. Testing Strategy

## 28.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Payment captured posts ledger | `PAYMENT_CAPTURED CREDIT`. |
| Duplicate capture event | Only one ledger entry. |
| Refund succeeded posts ledger | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED DEBIT`. |
| Duplicate refund webhook | Ledger once. |
| Chargeback lost posts ledger | `CHARGEBACK_LOST DEBIT`. |
| Chargeback won posts reversal | `CHARGEBACK_WON_REVERSAL CREDIT`. |
| Provider fee report posts fee | `PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED DEBIT`. |
| Ledger reversal creates opposite direction | Original unchanged. |
| Direct update ledger blocked | Trigger/permission rejects. |
| Payment vs ledger mismatch detected | Consistency view flags mismatch. |

## 28.2 Integration Tests

```text id="jpt8j0"
1. Create payment captured 1,000,000 IDR.
2. Verify payment status CAPTURED.
3. Verify ledger PAYMENT_CAPTURED exists.
4. Send duplicate capture webhook.
5. Verify ledger still one PAYMENT_CAPTURED.
6. Create refund 250,000.
7. Verify ledger REFUND_SUCCEEDED.
8. Send provider fee report 25,000.
9. Verify ledger PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED.
10. Create dispute lost 1,000,000.
11. Verify CHARGEBACK_LOST.
12. Run consistency view.
13. Verify expected summaries.
```

## 28.3 Mutation Protection Test

```text id="t4dgxe"
1. Insert ledger entry.
2. Try UPDATE amount.
3. Expect exception.
4. Try DELETE row.
5. Expect exception.
6. Create reversal.
7. Verify original remains.
8. Verify reversal exists.
```

---

# 29. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Ledger pakai update/delete | Audit hilang. | Append-only + reversal. |
| Ledger dibuat dari status API saja | Financial impact bisa async dari webhook/report. | Post from confirmed financial events. |
| Duplicate webhook double ledger | Finance overstatement. | Unique financial reference. |
| Tidak simpan provider reference | Reconciliation sulit. | `reference_type`, `reference_id`, `provider_reference_id`. |
| Tidak bedakan captured vs settled vs payout | Finance salah baca cash. | Separate entry types. |
| Event audit dianggap ledger | Tidak cukup untuk finance. | Ledger entries terpisah. |
| Raw provider payload jadi ledger | Coupled dan sensitif. | Normalize fields + metadata terbatas. |
| Manual correction update row | Audit rusak. | Adjustment/reversal flow. |
| Tidak ada consistency check | Mismatch terlambat. | Views/jobs. |
| No access control | Data finansial terekspos. | Role-based API. |

---

# 31. Exercise Praktis

Buat module:

```text id="3k2b70"
payment-service/
  domain/ledger/
    LedgerEntry.java
    LedgerAccount.java
    LedgerEntryType.java
    LedgerDirection.java
    LedgerPostingPolicy.java

  application/ledger/
    LedgerPostingService.java
    LedgerReversalService.java
    LedgerConsistencyCheckJob.java
    LedgerReportingService.java

  infrastructure/persistence/ledger/
    LedgerEntryMapper.java
    LedgerEntryMapper.xml
    LedgerAccountMapper.java

  api/
    LedgerResource.java
    FinanceLedgerResource.java
```

Target skenario:

```text id="9a32lh"
1. Payment captured 1,000,000 IDR.
2. Ledger PAYMENT_CAPTURED CREDIT created.
3. Duplicate capture event does not create duplicate ledger.
4. Refund 250,000 IDR.
5. Ledger REFUND_SUCCEEDED DEBIT created.
6. Provider fee 25,000 IDR imported from report.
7. Ledger PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED DEBIT created.
8. Dispute lost 1,000,000 IDR.
9. Ledger CHARGEBACK_LOST DEBIT created.
10. Dispute won reversal.
11. Ledger CHARGEBACK_WON_REVERSAL CREDIT created.
12. Try update ledger amount; blocked.
13. Create manual approved adjustment.
14. Run payment ledger summary.
15. Run payment-vs-ledger consistency view.
```

---

# Ringkasan

Payment ledger adalah fondasi audit dan finance correctness.

Prinsip utama:

```text id="tgowh5"
1. Ledger harus append-only.
2. Koreksi memakai reversal/adjustment, bukan update/delete.
3. Setiap financial event harus punya unique reference.
4. Capture, refund, fee, chargeback, settlement, payout harus dibedakan.
5. Ledger tidak menggantikan audit events.
6. Audit events tidak menggantikan ledger.
7. Provider reports harus direconcile ke ledger.
8. Consumer/outbox boleh duplicate, ledger tidak boleh double-post.
9. Access ledger harus restricted.
10. Finance-grade system butuh consistency checks dan reporting views.
```

Dengan desain ini, payment service bisa menjawab pertanyaan penting:

```text id="s0l4ku"
- Uang masuk dari mana?
- Uang keluar karena apa?
- Fee provider berapa?
- Refund mana yang sudah sukses?
- Chargeback mana yang jadi loss?
- Settlement mana yang sudah cocok?
- Siapa/apa yang membuat adjustment?
- Apakah payment state cocok dengan ledger?
```
