Saya lanjut ke **Seri 13/18**: Settlement, Reconciliation, dan Provider Report Ingestion. Fokusnya import report provider, matching payment/refund/dispute/fee, mismatch handling, settlement/payout lifecycle, dan dashboard finance.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 13/18 - Settlement, Reconciliation, dan Provider Report Ingestion

Seri ini fokus pada **settlement dan reconciliation**: bagaimana payment service mengimpor report dari PSP/bank, mencocokkan payment/refund/dispute/fee internal dengan data provider, mendeteksi mismatch, dan menyiapkan dashboard finance.

Core idea:

```text id="6gkl4z"
CAPTURED bukan berarti uang sudah masuk bank.
SETTLED bukan berarti semua fee/refund/chargeback sudah cocok.
PAID_OUT bukan berarti bank statement sudah reconcile.

Karena itu payment service butuh settlement + reconciliation pipeline.
```

Stripe menyediakan payout reconciliation report untuk melihat transaksi yang termasuk dalam payout dan mengelompokkannya berdasarkan reporting category; Stripe Balance summary report juga menyediakan CSV itemized export dari transaction history untuk reconcile Stripe balance. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/reports/payout-reconciliation?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen Settlement details report adalah transaction-level report yang berisi payment yang sudah settled dan paid out, termasuk cost per transaction untuk reconciliation; Adyen Payment accounting report berisi lifecycle status changes, events, dan modifications untuk transaksi, termasuk cost/status matching dan invoice reconciliation. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/reporting/settlement-reconciliation/transaction-level/settlement-details-report?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PayPal Transaction Search API dipakai untuk mengambil histori transaksi PayPal account. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/transaction-search/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Ada 5 layer yang harus dibedakan:

```text id="0gvh2r"
1. Payment state
   AUTHORIZED / CAPTURED / REFUNDED / DISPUTED

2. Ledger
   PAYMENT_CAPTURED / REFUND_SUCCEEDED / FEE_CHARGED / CHARGEBACK_LOST

3. Provider financial transaction
   balance transaction / settlement report item / PayPal transaction

4. Settlement / payout batch
   batch provider yang menjelaskan dana settled / paid out

5. Bank reconciliation
   bukti dana masuk rekening bank
```

Contoh flow:

```text id="m7g21w"
Payment CAPTURED 100,000 IDR
  -> ledger PAYMENT_CAPTURED 100,000
  -> later provider report says:
       gross = 100,000
       fee   = 2,900
       net   = 97,100
       payout_batch = BATCH-001
  -> post ledger PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED 2,900
  -> mark settlement item MATCHED
  -> later bank statement shows 97,100 received
  -> mark payout/bank reconciliation MATCHED
```

---

# 2. Diagram

```mermaid id="q0hyuy"
flowchart TD
    A[Provider Report Source] --> A1[Stripe Balance / Payout Report]
    A --> A2[Adyen Settlement Details / Payment Accounting Report]
    A --> A3[PayPal Transaction Search / Report]
    A --> A4[Bank Statement]

    A1 --> B[Report Ingestion Job]
    A2 --> B
    A3 --> B
    A4 --> B

    B --> C[Raw Report Storage]
    B --> D[Staging Tables]

    D --> E[Normalize Provider Financial Transactions]
    E --> F[(provider_financial_transactions)]

    F --> G[Reconciliation Engine]

    G --> H[Match Payments]
    G --> I[Match Captures]
    G --> J[Match Refunds]
    G --> K[Match Disputes / Chargebacks]
    G --> L[Match Fees]
    G --> M[Match Payouts]

    H --> N[(reconciliation_items)]
    I --> N
    J --> N
    K --> N
    L --> N
    M --> N

    N --> O[Ledger Posting]
    O --> O1[PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED]
    O --> O2[SETTLEMENT_RECEIVED]
    O --> O3[PAYOUT_PAID]
    O --> O4[CHARGEBACK_FEE]
    O --> O5[ADJUSTMENT]

    N --> P[Finance Dashboard]
    P --> P1[Matched]
    P --> P2[Mismatched]
    P --> P3[Missing Internal]
    P --> P4[Missing Provider]
    P --> P5[Unreconciled Aging]
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Report Source Registry** | Definisi report provider dan tipe file/API. | `provider_report_sources` |
| 2 | **Report Run** | Satu proses import report. | `provider_report_import_runs` |
| 3 | **Raw Report Storage** | Simpan file mentah untuk audit/replay. | object storage + metadata |
| 4 | **Staging Import** | Load CSV/JSON ke staging table. | `provider_report_staging_*` |
| 5 | **Normalization** | Ubah report provider-specific menjadi canonical financial transaction. | `provider_financial_transactions` |
| 6 | **Payment Matching** | Cocokkan provider payment/capture dengan internal capture. | provider refs |
| 7 | **Refund Matching** | Cocokkan refund report dengan `refunds`. | provider_refund_id |
| 8 | **Dispute Matching** | Cocokkan chargeback/dispute/fee. | provider_dispute_id |
| 9 | **Fee Matching** | Post provider fee ke ledger. | `PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED` |
| 10 | **Settlement Batch** | Group by payout/settlement batch. | `settlements`, `settlement_items` |
| 11 | **Payout Matching** | Cocokkan provider payout dengan bank statement. | `payouts`, `bank_statement_lines` |
| 12 | **Mismatch Handling** | Amount/currency/status/ref mismatch. | `reconciliation_items` |
| 13 | **Ledger Posting** | Post fee/settlement/payout/adjustment. | `ledger_entries` |
| 14 | **Replay/Reprocess** | Re-run report import safely. | idempotent import |
| 15 | **Finance Dashboard** | Matched %, aging, fee, net settlement. | views/materialized views |

---

# 4. Key Distinctions

| Concept | Meaning | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `CAPTURED` | Provider/payment method says capture succeeded. | API response/webhook. |
| `SETTLED` | Provider report says funds are settled/available/included in settlement batch. | Provider settlement/balance report. |
| `PAYOUT_PAID` | Provider says payout to merchant bank was sent/completed. | Provider payout report/API. |
| `BANK_MATCHED` | Bank statement confirms money arrived. | Bank statement import. |
| `RECONCILED` | Internal record matches provider/bank evidence. | Reconciliation engine. |

Practical rule:

```text id="bw5124"
Jangan mengubah payment CAPTURED menjadi PAID_OUT hanya dari capture webhook.
Settlement dan payout harus berdasarkan provider report/payout/bank evidence.
```

---

# 5. Provider Report Sources

## 5.1 Stripe

Useful sources:

| Source | Use |
|---|---|
| Balance Transactions | Canonical movement of funds in Stripe balance. |
| Balance Summary Report | Monthly/end-period reconciliation. |
| Payout Reconciliation Report | Transactions included in a payout. |
| Fees Report | Detailed fee analysis. |

Stripe Balance Transactions merepresentasikan dana yang masuk/keluar dari Stripe account balance; payout reconciliation report menunjukkan transaksi yang termasuk dalam payout/batch. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/reports/payout-reconciliation?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.2 Adyen

Useful sources:

| Source | Use |
|---|---|
| Settlement Details Report | Transaction-level settled/paid out payments and costs. |
| Payment Accounting Report | Lifecycle status changes, events, modifications, invoice reconciliation. |
| Other finance reports | Depending Adyen setup/platform. |

Adyen Settlement Details Report mencakup payment yang sudah settled dan paid out, sedangkan Payment Accounting Report mencakup lifecycle changes/events/modifications serta cost/status matching. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/reporting/settlement-reconciliation/transaction-level/settlement-details-report?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.3 PayPal

Useful sources:

| Source | Use |
|---|---|
| Transaction Search API | Transaction history. |
| Transaction Detail Report | Detailed debits/credits/payments depending reporting setup. |
| Dispute/Refund APIs | Detailed operational follow-up. |

PayPal Transaction Search API dipakai untuk mendapatkan histori transaksi PayPal account. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/transaction-search/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 6. Canonical Data Model

Agar provider-specific report tidak bocor ke seluruh sistem, normalize ke canonical table:

```text id="4o3hgh"
provider report row
  -> provider_financial_transactions
  -> reconciliation_items
  -> ledger_entries if needed
```

Canonical financial transaction harus bisa mewakili:

```text id="bc1s71"
- capture/sale
- refund
- chargeback
- chargeback fee
- provider fee
- adjustment
- settlement
- payout
- reserve/hold/release
- FX conversion
```

---

# 7. Database Schema

## 7.1 `provider_report_sources`

```sql id="jyvpv1"
CREATE TABLE provider_report_sources (
    report_source_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    report_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    source_mode VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
    file_format VARCHAR(40),

    enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,

    schedule_cron VARCHAR(100),
    timezone VARCHAR(80),

    config JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_provider_report_source_mode
        CHECK (source_mode IN ('API', 'SFTP', 'OBJECT_STORAGE', 'MANUAL_UPLOAD')),

    CONSTRAINT chk_provider_report_file_format
        CHECK (
            file_format IS NULL
            OR file_format IN ('CSV', 'TSV', 'JSON', 'NDJSON', 'XML', 'XLSX')
        )
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_provider_report_source
ON provider_report_sources(provider, report_type, source_mode);
```

---

## 7.2 `provider_report_import_runs`

```sql id="vlca1s"
CREATE TABLE provider_report_import_runs (
    import_run_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    report_source_id UUID REFERENCES provider_report_sources(report_source_id),

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    report_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    report_period_start TIMESTAMPTZ,
    report_period_end TIMESTAMPTZ,

    source_file_name VARCHAR(500),
    source_file_uri TEXT,
    source_file_checksum_sha256 VARCHAR(128),

    import_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    total_rows INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    imported_rows INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    rejected_rows INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    duplicate_rows INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    finished_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    error_code VARCHAR(100),
    error_message TEXT,

    created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',

    CONSTRAINT chk_provider_report_import_status
        CHECK (import_status IN (
            'STARTED',
            'RAW_STORED',
            'STAGED',
            'NORMALIZED',
            'RECONCILED',
            'FAILED',
            'PARTIAL',
            'CANCELLED'
        ))
);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_report_import_runs_provider_type
ON provider_report_import_runs(provider, report_type);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_report_import_runs_status
ON provider_report_import_runs(import_status);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_provider_report_file_checksum
ON provider_report_import_runs(provider, report_type, source_file_checksum_sha256)
WHERE source_file_checksum_sha256 IS NOT NULL;
```

---

## 7.3 Generic staging table

Untuk fleksibilitas awal, simpan raw row sebagai JSONB.

```sql id="fgtbn4"
CREATE TABLE provider_report_staging_rows (
    staging_row_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    import_run_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES provider_report_import_runs(import_run_id),

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    report_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    row_number INTEGER NOT NULL,

    raw_row JSONB NOT NULL,

    normalized BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    rejected BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,

    rejection_code VARCHAR(100),
    rejection_message TEXT,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT ux_provider_report_staging_row UNIQUE (import_run_id, row_number)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_report_staging_import
ON provider_report_staging_rows(import_run_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_report_staging_raw_gin
ON provider_report_staging_rows USING GIN(raw_row);
```

Kelebihan staging generic:

```text id="98p2ap"
1. Bisa menerima banyak format provider.
2. Replay normalization mudah.
3. Debug row-level import lebih gampang.
4. Tidak perlu ubah schema setiap provider menambah kolom.
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="dlj0dw"
1. Query raw JSONB bisa lebih lambat.
2. Validasi tipe data harus di application.
3. Untuk volume tinggi, staging typed table bisa lebih efisien.
```

---

## 7.4 `provider_financial_transactions`

Ini canonical normalized table.

```sql id="bq597w"
CREATE TABLE provider_financial_transactions (
    provider_financial_transaction_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    import_run_id UUID REFERENCES provider_report_import_runs(import_run_id),
    staging_row_id UUID REFERENCES provider_report_staging_rows(staging_row_id),

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    provider_transaction_id VARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,

    provider_batch_id VARCHAR(250),
    provider_payout_id VARCHAR(250),

    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(250),
    provider_authorization_id VARCHAR(250),
    provider_capture_id VARCHAR(250),
    provider_refund_id VARCHAR(250),
    provider_dispute_id VARCHAR(250),
    provider_balance_transaction_id VARCHAR(250),

    merchant_id VARCHAR(100),
    provider_merchant_account VARCHAR(200),

    transaction_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    reporting_category VARCHAR(120),

    gross_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    fee_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    exchange_rate NUMERIC(20, 10),
    functional_gross_amount_minor BIGINT,
    functional_fee_amount_minor BIGINT,
    functional_net_amount_minor BIGINT,
    functional_currency CHAR(3),

    transaction_created_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    available_on DATE,
    settled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    paid_out_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    raw_status VARCHAR(100),
    raw_type VARCHAR(100),
    raw_category VARCHAR(100),

    raw_payload JSONB,

    reconciliation_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'UNRECONCILED',

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_provider_fin_tx_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency)),

    CONSTRAINT chk_provider_fin_tx_func_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (
            functional_currency IS NULL
            OR functional_currency = upper(functional_currency)
        ),

    CONSTRAINT chk_provider_fin_tx_recon_status
        CHECK (reconciliation_status IN (
            'UNRECONCILED',
            'MATCHED',
            'MISMATCH',
            'IGNORED',
            'NEEDS_INVESTIGATION'
        ))
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_provider_financial_transactions_provider_tx
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_transaction_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_fin_tx_payment
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_fin_tx_capture
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_capture_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_fin_tx_refund
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_fin_tx_dispute
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_dispute_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_fin_tx_batch
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_batch_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_fin_tx_payout
ON provider_financial_transactions(provider, provider_payout_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_fin_tx_recon_status
ON provider_financial_transactions(reconciliation_status);

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_fin_tx_created
ON provider_financial_transactions(transaction_created_at);
```

## 7.5 `settlements`

```sql id="m1o1s5"
CREATE TABLE settlements (
    settlement_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_batch_id VARCHAR(250) NOT NULL,

    merchant_id VARCHAR(100),
    provider_merchant_account VARCHAR(200),

    settlement_currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    gross_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    fee_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    refund_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    chargeback_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    adjustment_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    net_amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    settlement_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    settlement_date DATE,
    paid_out_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    provider_payout_id VARCHAR(250),

    import_run_id UUID REFERENCES provider_report_import_runs(import_run_id),

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_settlements_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (settlement_currency = upper(settlement_currency)),

    CONSTRAINT chk_settlements_status
        CHECK (settlement_status IN (
            'RECEIVED',
            'PROCESSING',
            'RECONCILED',
            'PARTIAL',
            'MISMATCH',
            'FAILED',
            'PAID_OUT',
            'BANK_MATCHED'
        ))
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_settlements_provider_batch
ON settlements(provider, provider_batch_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlements_provider_payout
ON settlements(provider, provider_payout_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlements_status
ON settlements(settlement_status);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlements_date
ON settlements(settlement_date);
```

## 7.6 `settlement_items`

```sql id="4obxgy"
            'IGNORED',
            'NEEDS_INVESTIGATION'

);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlement_items_settlement

ON settlement_items(payment_id);

ON settlement_items(refund_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlement_items_dispute
ON settlement_items(dispute_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlement_items_provider_tx
ON settlement_items(provider, provider_transaction_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_settlement_items_match_status
ON settlement_items(match_status);
```

## 7.7 `reconciliation_runs`

```sql id="6rjzda"
CREATE TABLE reconciliation_runs (
    reconciliation_run_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    period_start TIMESTAMPTZ,
    period_end TIMESTAMPTZ,

    import_run_id UUID REFERENCES provider_report_import_runs(import_run_id),

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    total_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    matched_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    mismatch_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    missing_internal_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    missing_provider_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    ignored_items INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    finished_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    error_code VARCHAR(100),
    error_message TEXT,

    created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',

    CONSTRAINT chk_reconciliation_run_type
        CHECK (reconciliation_type IN (
            'PAYMENT_CAPTURE',
            'REFUND',
            'DISPUTE',
            'FEE',
            'SETTLEMENT',
            'PAYOUT',
            'BANK',
            'FULL_PROVIDER_REPORT'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_reconciliation_run_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'STARTED',
            'PROCESSING',
            'COMPLETED',
            'COMPLETED_WITH_MISMATCH',
            'FAILED',
            'CANCELLED'
        ))
);

CREATE INDEX ix_reconciliation_runs_provider_type
ON reconciliation_runs(provider, reconciliation_type);

CREATE INDEX ix_reconciliation_runs_status
ON reconciliation_runs(status);
```

# 8. Import Pipeline

```mermaid id="l7l6ko"
sequenceDiagram
    participant Job as Report Import Job
    participant PSP as Provider/API/SFTP
    participant Store as Object Storage
    participant DB as PostgreSQL
    participant Recon as Reconciliation Engine
    participant Ledger as Ledger Service

    Job->>PSP: download/fetch report
    Job->>Store: store raw file
    Job->>DB: create import_run STARTED/RAW_STORED
    Job->>DB: load rows into staging
    Job->>DB: mark import_run STAGED

    Job->>DB: normalize staging -> provider_financial_transactions
    Job->>DB: mark import_run NORMALIZED

    Recon->>DB: create reconciliation_run
    Recon->>DB: match provider tx to internal payments/refunds/disputes
    Recon->>DB: insert reconciliation_items
    Recon->>Ledger: post fees/settlement/payout if matched
    Recon->>DB: update statuses
    Recon->>DB: mark reconciliation completed
```

---

# 9. CSV Import Strategy

Untuk report CSV besar, gunakan staging approach.

PostgreSQL `COPY` memindahkan data antara table dan file; `COPY FROM` membaca file ke table, dan `COPY TO` menulis table/query ke file. ([PostgreSQL](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-copy.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Untuk RDS atau environment tanpa server-side file access, `psql \copy` bisa memuat CSV dari client workstation ke database setelah table dibuat. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonRDS/latest/UserGuide/PostgreSQL.Procedural.Importing.Copy.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical approach:

```text id="xsy6ob"
Small/medium report:
  Java parser -> batch insert staging rows

Large report:
  load file to staging using COPY/\copy or bulk-loader process

Always:
  raw file stored first
  staging rows inserted
  normalization idempotent
```

---

# 12. Provider-Specific Normalization Examples

## 12.1 Stripe

Stripe balance/payout rows often need mapping by:

```text id="i8ui1d"
- balance transaction id
- source id
- reporting category
- amount
- fee
- net
- currency
- available_on
- payout id
```

Canonical mapping example:

| Stripe-like Field | Canonical Field |
|---|---|
| `balance_transaction_id` | `provider_balance_transaction_id`, `provider_transaction_id` |
| `source_id` | `provider_payment_id` / `provider_refund_id` depending category |
| `reporting_category` | `reporting_category` |
| `gross` / `amount` | `gross_amount_minor` |
| `fee` | `fee_amount_minor` |
| `net` | `net_amount_minor` |
| `currency` | `currency` |
| `available_on` | `available_on` |
| `payout_id` | `provider_payout_id` |

Stripe docs say payout reconciliation report shows transactions included in payout batches and groups them by reporting category. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/reports/payout-reconciliation?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 12.2 Adyen

Adyen Settlement Details Report is transaction-level and includes settled/paid out payments plus cost details, useful for transaction-level reconciliation. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/reporting/settlement-reconciliation/transaction-level/settlement-details-report?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Canonical mapping example:

| Adyen-like Field | Canonical Field |
|---|---|
| PSP reference | `provider_payment_id` / `provider_transaction_id` |
| Merchant reference | internal lookup fallback |
| Record type | `transaction_type` |
| Gross debit/credit | `gross_amount_minor` |
| Commission/markup/scheme fees | `fee_amount_minor` |
| Net debit/credit | `net_amount_minor` |
| Batch number | `provider_batch_id` |
| Booking date / timezone date | `settled_at` / `transaction_created_at` |

## 12.3 PayPal

PayPal Transaction Search API returns PayPal account transaction history; depending fields, normalize transaction ID, event code/type, gross/fee/net, currency, and related transaction info. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/docs/transaction-search/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Canonical mapping example:

| PayPal-like Field | Canonical Field |
|---|---|
| Transaction ID | `provider_transaction_id` |
| Transaction event code/type | `transaction_type`, `raw_type` |
| Gross amount | `gross_amount_minor` |
| Fee amount | `fee_amount_minor` |
| Net amount | `net_amount_minor` |
| Currency | `currency` |
| Transaction updated date | `transaction_created_at` |
| Payer/payee refs | metadata |

---

# 13. Matching Algorithm

## 13.1 Matching Priority

```text id="4qaf8g"
1. Exact provider specific ID
   provider_refund_id, provider_capture_id, provider_dispute_id

2. Provider payment/charge ID
   provider_payment_id / provider_charge_id

3. Merchant/internal reference
   payment_id in metadata, merchantReference, orderId

4. Composite fallback
   provider + amount + currency + approximate date + merchant account

5. Manual investigation
```

Never rely on amount/date alone unless marked low-confidence.

---

## 13.2 Capture Match

```text id="n10ocr"
Provider item type = CAPTURE / SALE / CHARGE

Match:
  provider + provider_capture_id -> payment_captures
  OR provider + provider_payment_id -> payments
  AND amount/currency must match
```

SQL example:

```sql id="v9qi6t"
SELECT
    p.payment_id,
    c.capture_id,
    c.amount_minor,
    c.currency,
    c.status
FROM payment_captures c
JOIN payments p ON p.payment_id = c.payment_id
WHERE c.provider = #{provider}
  AND (
        c.provider_capture_id = #{providerCaptureId}
        OR p.provider_payment_id = #{providerPaymentId}
      )
LIMIT 1;
```

Match status rules:

| Internal | Provider | Result |
|---|---|---|
| capture exists, amount/currency equal | capture row | `MATCHED` |
| capture exists, amount differs | provider row | `AMOUNT_MISMATCH` |
| capture exists, currency differs | provider row | `CURRENCY_MISMATCH` |
| provider capture exists, no internal | provider row | `MISSING_INTERNAL` |
| internal capture no provider report after SLA | internal row | `MISSING_PROVIDER` |

---

## 13.3 Refund Match

```text id="ssdesy"
Provider item type = REFUND

Match:
  provider + provider_refund_id -> refunds
  fallback provider_payment_id + amount + date
```

Important:

```text id="ggtukf"
Fallback refund matching by amount/date is risky if multiple partial refunds have same amount.
Prefer provider_refund_id or explicit reference stored in provider metadata.
```

---

## 13.4 Fee Match

Fee can be attached to:

```text id="ktpc1y"
- payment/capture
- refund
- dispute/chargeback
- payout
- account-level adjustment
```

Fee posting rule:

```text id="89fmw1"
If provider report has fee and fee ledger does not exist:
  post PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED
  reference_type = PROVIDER_FINANCIAL_TRANSACTION
  reference_id = provider_transaction_id
```

---

## 13.5 Dispute/Chargeback Match

```text id="9uxurh"
Provider item type = CHARGEBACK / DISPUTE / CHARGEBACK_FEE

Match:
  provider + provider_dispute_id -> disputes
  provider + provider_payment_id/charge_id -> payment
```

Rules:

| Provider Item | Ledger |
|---|---|
| chargeback debit | `CHARGEBACK_LOST` or `CHARGEBACK_HOLD` |
| chargeback fee | `CHARGEBACK_FEE` |
| chargeback reversal/won | `CHARGEBACK_WON_REVERSAL` |

---

# 14. Reconciliation Engine Skeleton

```java id="m8ym5r"
package com.company.payment.reconciliation;

public interface ReconciliationEngine {

    ReconciliationResult reconcile(ReconciliationCommand command);
}
```

```java id="uhkbaw"
package com.company.payment.reconciliation;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

public record ReconciliationCommand(
        UUID importRunId,
        String provider,
        String reconciliationType,
        Instant periodStart,
        Instant periodEnd,
        String requestedBy
) {}
```

```java id="hpm9rc"
package com.company.payment.reconciliation;

import java.util.UUID;

public record ReconciliationResult(
        UUID reconciliationRunId,
        int totalItems,
        int matchedItems,
        int mismatchItems,
        int missingInternalItems,
        int missingProviderItems,
        String status
) {}
```

Implementation:

```java id="czk6to"
package com.company.payment.reconciliation;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.UUID;

public class DefaultReconciliationEngine implements ReconciliationEngine {

    private final ProviderFinancialTransactionRepository providerTxRepository;
    private final InternalPaymentMatcher paymentMatcher;
    private final InternalRefundMatcher refundMatcher;
    private final InternalDisputeMatcher disputeMatcher;
    private final ReconciliationItemRepository reconciliationItemRepository;
    private final LedgerPostingService ledgerPostingService;
    private final SettlementService settlementService;
    private final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;

    @Override
    public ReconciliationResult reconcile(ReconciliationCommand command) {
        UUID runId = UUID.randomUUID();

        transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            reconciliationItemRepository.createRun(runId, command);
            return null;
        });

        List<ProviderFinancialTransaction> transactions =
                providerTxRepository.findUnreconciledByImportRun(command.importRunId());

        ReconciliationCounters counters = new ReconciliationCounters();

        for (ProviderFinancialTransaction tx : transactions) {
            transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                ReconciliationDecision decision = reconcileSingle(runId, tx);

                reconciliationItemRepository.insert(decision.toItem());
                providerTxRepository.updateReconciliationStatus(
                        tx.providerFinancialTransactionId(),
                        decision.providerTxStatus()
                );

                if (decision.shouldPostLedger()) {
                    ledgerPostingService.postFromReconciliation(decision);
                }

                if (decision.shouldUpdateSettlement()) {
                    settlementService.applyTransaction(decision);
                }

                counters.increment(decision.matchStatus());

                return null;
            });
        }

        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            reconciliationItemRepository.completeRun(runId, counters);
            return counters.toResult(runId);
        });
    }

    private ReconciliationDecision reconcileSingle(
            UUID runId,
            ProviderFinancialTransaction tx
    ) {
        return switch (tx.transactionType()) {
            case CAPTURE -> paymentMatcher.matchCapture(runId, tx);
            case REFUND -> refundMatcher.matchRefund(runId, tx);
            case CHARGEBACK, CHARGEBACK_FEE, CHARGEBACK_REVERSAL ->
                    disputeMatcher.matchDispute(runId, tx);
            case FEE -> paymentMatcher.matchFee(runId, tx);
            case PAYOUT -> paymentMatcher.matchPayout(runId, tx);
            case ADJUSTMENT, RESERVE, RESERVE_RELEASE, FX, UNKNOWN ->
                    ReconciliationDecision.needsInvestigation(runId, tx, "Unsupported or unknown transaction type");
        };
    }
}
```

---

# 15. Reconciliation Decision

```java id="ptko3t"
package com.company.payment.reconciliation;

import java.util.UUID;

public record ReconciliationDecision(
        UUID reconciliationRunId,
        UUID providerFinancialTransactionId,

        UUID paymentId,
        UUID captureId,
        UUID refundId,
        UUID disputeId,

        String provider,
        String providerTransactionId,

        String itemType,
        String matchStatus,

        Long internalAmountMinor,
        Long providerAmountMinor,
        Long differenceMinor,

        String internalCurrency,
        String providerCurrency,

        String internalStatus,
        String providerStatus,

        String reasonCode,
        String reasonDetail,

        boolean shouldPostLedger,
        boolean shouldUpdateSettlement
) {
    public String providerTxStatus() {
        return switch (matchStatus) {
            case "MATCHED" -> "MATCHED";
            case "IGNORED" -> "IGNORED";
            default -> "MISMATCH";
        };
    }
}
```

---

# 16. Matching Implementation Example: Refund

```java id="821uf4"
package com.company.payment.reconciliation.matching;

public class InternalRefundMatcher {

    private final RefundRepository refundRepository;

    public ReconciliationDecision matchRefund(
            UUID reconciliationRunId,
            ProviderFinancialTransaction tx
    ) {
        RefundRecord refund = refundRepository
                .findByProviderRefundId(tx.provider(), tx.providerRefundId())
                .orElse(null);

        if (refund == null) {
            return ReconciliationDecisionFactory.missingInternal(
                    reconciliationRunId,
                    tx,
                    "REFUND",
                    "Provider refund not found internally"
            );
        }

        if (!refund.currency().equals(tx.currency())) {
            return ReconciliationDecisionFactory.currencyMismatch(
                    reconciliationRunId,
                    tx,
                    refund.paymentId(),
                    null,
                    refund.refundId(),
                    null,
                    refund.amountMinor(),
                    tx.grossAmountMinor(),
                    refund.currency(),
                    tx.currency()
            );
        }

        long providerRefundAmount = Math.abs(tx.grossAmountMinor());

        if (refund.amountMinor() != providerRefundAmount) {
            return ReconciliationDecisionFactory.amountMismatch(
                    reconciliationRunId,
                    tx,
                    refund.paymentId(),
                    null,
                    refund.refundId(),
                    null,
                    refund.amountMinor(),
                    providerRefundAmount,
                    refund.currency()
            );
        }

        if (!"SUCCEEDED".equals(refund.status())) {
            return ReconciliationDecisionFactory.statusMismatch(
                    reconciliationRunId,
                    tx,
                    refund.paymentId(),
                    null,
                    refund.refundId(),
                    null,
                    refund.status(),
                    tx.rawStatus()
            );
        }

        return ReconciliationDecisionFactory.matched(
                reconciliationRunId,
                tx,
                refund.paymentId(),
                null,
                refund.refundId(),
                null,
                "REFUND",
                refund.amountMinor(),
                providerRefundAmount,
                refund.currency(),
                true
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 17. Missing Provider Detection

Provider report import only detects rows that exist in provider report. You also need internal records that should appear but do not.

Example:

```text id="3q6ehy"
Internal capture succeeded 3 days ago.
Provider settlement report for that period is imported.
No provider row found.
=> MISSING_PROVIDER
```

SQL:

```sql id="2q8fln"
SELECT
    c.capture_id,
    c.payment_id,
    c.provider,
    c.provider_capture_id,
    c.amount_minor,
    c.currency,
    c.captured_at
FROM payment_captures c
LEFT JOIN provider_financial_transactions pft
  ON pft.provider = c.provider
 AND (
      pft.provider_capture_id = c.provider_capture_id
      OR pft.provider_payment_id = c.provider_payment_id
 )
WHERE c.status = 'SUCCEEDED'
  AND c.captured_at < now() - interval '2 days'
  AND pft.provider_financial_transaction_id IS NULL;
```

Caution:

```text id="rh7k3p"
Settlement delay berbeda per provider/payment method/country.
SLA missing provider harus configurable per provider/currency/payment method.
```

---

# 18. Ledger Posting from Reconciliation

## 18.1 Provider Fee

```java id="38aw7q"
public void postProviderFeeIfNeeded(ProviderFinancialTransaction tx, UUID paymentId) {
    if (tx.feeAmountMinor() == 0) {
        return;
    }

    ledgerPostingService.postProviderFeeCharged(new ProviderFeeLedgerCommand(
            paymentId,
            tx.provider(),
            tx.providerTransactionId(),
            Math.abs(tx.feeAmountMinor()),
            tx.currency(),
            "PROVIDER_FINANCIAL_TRANSACTION",
            tx.providerTransactionId(),
            "RECONCILIATION"
    ));
}
```

SQL:

```sql id="c7t2ds"
INSERT INTO ledger_entries (
    ledger_entry_id,
    payment_id,
    provider,
    entry_type,
    direction,
    account_code,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    reference_type,
    reference_id,
    provider_reference_id,
    description,
    event_source,
    created_by
)
VALUES (
    gen_random_uuid(),
    #{paymentId},
    #{provider},
    'PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED',
    'DEBIT',
    'PAYMENT_PROVIDER_FEES',
    #{feeAmountMinor},

    'Provider fee charged from reconciliation report',
    'RECONCILIATION',
    'payment-service'
)
ON CONFLICT (reference_type, reference_id, entry_type)
DO NOTHING;
```

# 19. Settlement Aggregation

After normalizing provider transactions, aggregate by provider batch/payout.

```sql id="5sgl8g"
INSERT INTO settlements (
    settlement_id,
    provider,
    provider_batch_id,
    merchant_id,
    provider_merchant_account,
    settlement_currency,
    gross_amount_minor,
    fee_amount_minor,
    refund_amount_minor,
    chargeback_amount_minor,
    adjustment_amount_minor,
    net_amount_minor,
    settlement_status,
    settlement_date,
    provider_payout_id,
    import_run_id
)
SELECT
    gen_random_uuid(),
    provider,
    provider_batch_id,
    merchant_id,
    provider_merchant_account,
    currency,

    SUM(CASE WHEN transaction_type = 'CAPTURE' THEN gross_amount_minor ELSE 0 END),
    SUM(ABS(fee_amount_minor)),
    SUM(CASE WHEN transaction_type = 'REFUND' THEN ABS(gross_amount_minor) ELSE 0 END),
    SUM(CASE WHEN transaction_type IN ('CHARGEBACK', 'CHARGEBACK_FEE') THEN ABS(gross_amount_minor) ELSE 0 END),
    SUM(CASE WHEN transaction_type = 'ADJUSTMENT' THEN net_amount_minor ELSE 0 END),
    SUM(net_amount_minor),

    'RECEIVED',
    MAX(available_on),
    MAX(provider_payout_id),
    #{importRunId}
FROM provider_financial_transactions
WHERE import_run_id = #{importRunId}
  AND provider_batch_id IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY
    provider,
    provider_batch_id,
    merchant_id,
    provider_merchant_account,
    currency
ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_batch_id)
DO NOTHING;
```

---

# 20. Bank Statement Matching

Provider payout says:

```text id="biysrx"
provider_payout_id = po_123
net_amount = 97,100 IDR
paid_out_at = 2026-07-15
```

Bank statement says:

```text id="c92uny"
date = 2026-07-16
amount = 97,100 IDR
description contains provider payout reference
```

Bank statement table:

```sql id="h6srh6"
CREATE TABLE bank_statement_lines (
    bank_statement_line_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    bank_account_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    statement_date DATE NOT NULL,
    transaction_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,
    currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL,

    direction VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,

    bank_reference_id VARCHAR(250),
    counterparty_name VARCHAR(250),

    raw_payload JSONB,

    reconciliation_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'UNRECONCILED',

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_bank_statement_direction
        CHECK (direction IN ('DEBIT', 'CREDIT')),

    CONSTRAINT chk_bank_statement_currency_uppercase
        CHECK (currency = upper(currency))
);

CREATE INDEX ix_bank_statement_lines_amount_date
ON bank_statement_lines(currency, amount_minor, statement_date);

CREATE INDEX ix_bank_statement_lines_reference
ON bank_statement_lines(bank_reference_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_bank_statement_lines_recon
ON bank_statement_lines(reconciliation_status);
```

Payout matching:

```text id="5cxw9h"
1. Exact provider payout reference in bank description/reference
2. Amount/currency equal
3. Date within expected window
4. If multiple candidates -> manual review
```

```json id="bg8rhj"
      "providerAmountMinor": 249000,
      "differenceMinor": -1000,
      "currency": "IDR",
      "reasonDetail": "Provider refund amount differs from internal refund amount."
    }
  ]
}
```

## 22.3 Find Unreconciled Provider Transactions

```xml id="vm3gkt"
<select id="findUnreconciledByImportRun" resultMap="ProviderFinancialTransactionResultMap">
    SELECT
        provider_financial_transaction_id,
        import_run_id,
        provider,
        provider_transaction_id,
        provider_batch_id,
        provider_payout_id,

        currency,

        available_on,
        raw_status,
        raw_type,
        raw_category,
        raw_payload
    FROM provider_financial_transactions
    WHERE import_run_id = #{importRunId}
      AND reconciliation_status = 'UNRECONCILED'
    ORDER BY transaction_created_at ASC NULLS LAST,
             provider_financial_transaction_id ASC
</select>
```

# 23. Mismatch Types and Actions

| Mismatch | Meaning | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `AMOUNT_MISMATCH` | Internal amount differs from provider. | Check fee/net/gross interpretation, partial capture/refund, FX. |
| `CURRENCY_MISMATCH` | Currency differs. | Check provider settlement currency vs transaction currency. |
| `STATUS_MISMATCH` | Internal says pending/failed, provider says settled/succeeded. | Update internal via recovery if provider is source of truth. |
| `MISSING_INTERNAL` | Provider has transaction, internal does not. | Investigate missing webhook/API record, possible manual/provider-side operation. |
| `MISSING_PROVIDER` | Internal success not found in provider report after SLA. | Wait more, query provider, investigate settlement delay. |
| `DUPLICATE` | Duplicate provider ref or duplicate internal record. | Fix data/model; ensure unique constraints. |
| `IGNORED` | Non-financial or irrelevant row. | Document ignore rule. |
| `NEEDS_INVESTIGATION` | Ambiguous. | Finance/Ops manual case. |

---

# 24. Reconciliation Resolution Workflow

```mermaid id="9v8nnv"
flowchart TD
    A[Mismatch Item Created] --> B[Finance Review Queue]
    B --> C{Mismatch Type}

    C -->|Amount Mismatch| D[Check Gross/Fee/Net/FX]
    C -->|Missing Internal| E[Search Provider Reference / Webhook Inbox]
    C -->|Missing Provider| F[Query Provider API / Wait SLA]
    C -->|Status Mismatch| G[Run Recovery / Status Query]
    C -->|Currency Mismatch| H[Check Settlement Currency / FX]

    D --> I{Resolved?}
    E --> I
    F --> I
    G --> I
    H --> I

    I -->|Yes| J[Mark Resolved]
    I -->|Requires Adjustment| K[Create Ledger Adjustment]
    I -->|Provider Issue| L[Escalate to PSP]
    I -->|Internal Bug| M[Bug / Data Fix]
```

---

# 25. Dashboard Views

## 25.1 Reconciliation Summary

```sql id="jk5k5t"
CREATE VIEW v_reconciliation_run_summary AS
SELECT
    reconciliation_run_id,
    provider,
    reconciliation_type,
    status,
    total_items,
    matched_items,
    mismatch_items,
    missing_internal_items,
    missing_provider_items,
    ignored_items,
    started_at,
    finished_at,
    CASE
        WHEN total_items = 0 THEN 0
        ELSE ROUND((matched_items::numeric / total_items::numeric) * 100, 2)
    END AS match_rate_percent
FROM reconciliation_runs;
```

## 25.2 Open Mismatch Aging

```sql id="c47jvm"
CREATE VIEW v_open_reconciliation_mismatch_aging AS
SELECT
    provider,
    item_type,
    match_status,
    COUNT(*) AS item_count,
    MIN(created_at) AS oldest_created_at,
    MAX(now() - created_at) AS max_age
FROM reconciliation_items
WHERE resolved = false
  AND match_status <> 'MATCHED'
GROUP BY provider, item_type, match_status;
```

## 25.3 Daily Net Settlement

```sql id="f99f8l"
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW mv_daily_provider_settlement AS
SELECT
    settlement_date,
    provider,
    merchant_id,
    settlement_currency AS currency,
    COUNT(*) AS settlement_count,
    SUM(gross_amount_minor) AS gross_amount_minor,
    SUM(fee_amount_minor) AS fee_amount_minor,
    SUM(refund_amount_minor) AS refund_amount_minor,
    SUM(chargeback_amount_minor) AS chargeback_amount_minor,
    SUM(adjustment_amount_minor) AS adjustment_amount_minor,
    SUM(net_amount_minor) AS net_amount_minor
FROM settlements
GROUP BY
    settlement_date,
    provider,
    merchant_id,
    settlement_currency;
```

---

# 26. Operational Jobs

| Job | Frequency | Function |
|---|---:|---|
| Report discovery/download | daily/hourly | Fetch provider reports/API. |
| Raw storage checksum job | per import | Ensure file not duplicate/corrupt. |
| Staging import | per report | Load rows. |
| Normalization job | per import | Convert provider rows to canonical tx. |
| Reconciliation job | per import or schedule | Match provider tx to internal. |
| Missing provider detector | daily | Internal success not found in provider report. |
| Ledger fee posting job | during reconciliation | Post fee entries. |
| Settlement aggregation job | per import | Create settlement batches/items. |
| Payout-bank matching job | daily | Match payouts to bank statement. |
| Mismatch aging alert job | hourly/daily | Alert unresolved mismatch. |

---

# 27. Idempotency and Replay

Report import must be idempotent.

Mechanisms:

```text id="r32kgy"
1. raw file checksum unique per provider/report type
2. unique provider_transaction_id
3. staging unique import_run + row_number
4. ledger unique reference
5. reconciliation item can be re-created per run, but provider tx status must be handled carefully
```

Replay options:

| Replay Type | Description |
|---|---|
| Re-run normalization | Same staging rows → canonical tx upsert/no duplicate. |
| Re-run reconciliation | Create new reconciliation run, compare again. |
| Re-post ledger | Safe due unique ledger reference. |
| Re-import same file | Should be rejected as duplicate unless force allowed. |
| Force re-import | New run with same checksum allowed only with admin flag and reason. |

---

# 28. Error Codes

| Error Code | HTTP / Status | Meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| `REPORT_SOURCE_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | Report source config missing. |
| `REPORT_FILE_DUPLICATE` | 409 | Same checksum already imported. |
| `REPORT_FILE_UNSUPPORTED_FORMAT` | 422 | Format unsupported. |
| `REPORT_PARSE_FAILED` | FAILED | CSV/JSON parse error. |
| `REPORT_NORMALIZATION_FAILED` | PARTIAL/FAILED | Row cannot normalize. |
| `RECONCILIATION_MATCH_FAILED` | PARTIAL | Matching engine error. |
| `RECONCILIATION_AMOUNT_MISMATCH` | item | Amount mismatch. |
| `RECONCILIATION_MISSING_INTERNAL` | item | Provider tx not found internally. |
| `RECONCILIATION_MISSING_PROVIDER` | item | Internal tx missing provider evidence. |
| `BANK_PAYOUT_MATCH_FAILED` | item | Provider payout not matched to bank line. |

# 30. Testing Strategy

## 30.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Normalize Stripe capture row | canonical `CAPTURE`. |
| Normalize Adyen refund row | canonical `REFUND`. |
| Normalize PayPal transaction row | canonical tx with amount/currency. |
| Duplicate provider transaction | no duplicate. |
| Capture amount matched | `MATCHED`. |
| Refund amount mismatch | `AMOUNT_MISMATCH`. |
| Currency mismatch | `CURRENCY_MISMATCH`. |
| Provider refund no internal record | `MISSING_INTERNAL`. |
| Internal capture not in report after SLA | `MISSING_PROVIDER`. |
| Fee item posts ledger once | one `PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED`. |
| Re-run reconciliation | no duplicate ledger. |

## 30.2 Integration Tests

```text id="m57dor"
1. Create payment CAPTURED 1,000,000 IDR.
2. Create refund SUCCEEDED 250,000 IDR.
3. Import provider report:
   - capture 1,000,000
   - fee 29,000
   - refund 250,000
   - net 721,000
4. Normalize report rows.
5. Run reconciliation.
6. Verify capture MATCHED.
7. Verify refund MATCHED.
8. Verify fee ledger posted once.
9. Verify settlement net amount.
10. Re-run same import/reconciliation.
11. Verify no duplicate provider tx/ledger.
```

## 30.3 Mismatch Tests

```text id="kmrrrh"
1. Provider amount differs -> AMOUNT_MISMATCH.
2. Provider currency differs -> CURRENCY_MISMATCH.
3. Provider has refund unknown internally -> MISSING_INTERNAL.
4. Internal capture older than SLA not in provider report -> MISSING_PROVIDER.
5. Duplicate provider transaction ID -> duplicate ignored/rejected.
6. Bad CSV row -> rejected row, import PARTIAL.
```

# 31. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Menganggap capture webhook cukup untuk finance | Fee/settlement/payout belum terbukti. | Provider report reconciliation. |
| Menggabungkan gross/net/fee tanpa jelas | Finance salah hitung. | Simpan gross, fee, net terpisah. |
| Tidak simpan raw report | Tidak bisa audit/replay. | Store raw file + checksum. |
| Import langsung ke final tanpa staging | Sulit debug bad rows. | Staging table. |
| Matching pakai amount/date saja | False match tinggi. | Provider refs first. |
| Tidak idempotent import | Duplicate report double ledger. | checksum + unique provider tx. |
| Fee ledger dipost tiap run | Fee dobel. | unique ledger reference. |
| Mismatch tidak punya workflow | Finance backlog tidak selesai. | reconciliation_items + resolve. |
| Tidak monitor match rate | Masalah provider/config terlambat. | Dashboard + alert. |
| Bank payout tidak direconcile | Cash belum terbukti masuk. | bank statement matching. |

# Ringkasan

Settlement dan reconciliation adalah jembatan antara **payment operational state** dan **finance truth**.

Prinsip utama:

```text id="5qrpbx"
1. CAPTURED bukan SETTLED.
2. SETTLED bukan BANK_MATCHED.
3. Provider report harus diimport, distage, dinormalisasi, lalu direconcile.
4. Matching harus mengutamakan provider reference, bukan amount/date saja.
5. Gross, fee, dan net harus disimpan terpisah.
6. Ledger posting dari report harus idempotent.
7. Mismatch harus menjadi workflow, bukan hanya log error.
8. Raw report wajib disimpan untuk audit/replay.
9. Reconciliation harus bisa diulang tanpa double ledger.
10. Finance dashboard harus menunjukkan match rate, open mismatch, fee, settlement, dan payout-bank match.
```

Dengan desain ini, payment service bisa menjawab:

```text id="xbjx85"
- Payment mana yang sudah capture tapi belum muncul di provider report?
- Refund mana yang provider amount-nya beda?
- Fee provider berapa per merchant/currency?
- Settlement batch mana yang belum cocok?
- Payout mana yang belum masuk bank?
- Mismatch mana yang belum diselesaikan finance?
```
