## Response:
7/14/2026, 10:55:55 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 14/18**: Camunda Saga untuk payment-oriented workflow. Fokusnya orchestration order-payment-fulfillment, compensation, wait webhook, timeout boundary event, retry, human task, dan sinkronisasi dengan payment state machine.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 14/18 - Camunda Saga untuk Payment-Oriented Workflow

Seri ini fokus pada **Camunda Saga** untuk workflow payment yang melibatkan banyak domain: **Order → Payment → Fraud/Risk → Inventory/Fulfillment → Billing → Refund/Void/Dispute**.

Core idea:

```text id="8xk7zz"
Payment service tetap pemilik state machine dan financial invariant.
Camunda hanya mengorkestrasi business process lintas service.
```

Dalam payment-oriented workflow, Camunda cocok untuk:

```text id="v52he9"
1. Mengatur urutan step bisnis lintas service.
2. Menunggu webhook / event async dari payment provider.
3. Menangani timeout order/payment/fulfillment.
4. Menjalankan compensation seperti void authorization atau refund.
5. Membuka human task untuk fraud review, refund approval, dispute evidence.
6. Memonitor incident workflow yang butuh intervensi manual.
```

Di Camunda 8, message correlation memakai **message name + correlation key** untuk mencocokkan message ke process instance yang sedang menunggu; jika tidak ada subscription yang cocok, message dapat discard. Ini penting untuk payment webhook karena webhook bisa datang sebelum process mencapai waiting state. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Timer event di Camunda 8 mendukung timer start event, intermediate timer catch event, dan interrupting/non-interrupting boundary timer event; ini sangat berguna untuk auth expiry, payment timeout, SLA fraud review, dan dispute deadline. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/timer-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Compensation event digunakan untuk “undo” step yang sudah berhasil tetapi hasilnya tidak lagi diinginkan, misalnya void auth setelah inventory gagal atau refund setelah fulfillment gagal. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/compensation-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Jangan membuat Camunda sebagai pemilik financial truth.

Desain yang sehat:

```text id="8n61nm"
Camunda Process:
  - tahu business flow
  - tahu step mana berikutnya
  - tahu kapan wait/timeout/compensate
  - tahu kapan human task perlu dibuat

Payment Service:
  - tahu payment state machine
  - tahu idempotency
  - tahu provider adapter
  - tahu ledger
  - tahu refund/dispute/reconciliation
```

Contoh:

```text id="cm6zju"
Camunda: "Capture payment sekarang karena order siap fulfill."
Payment Service: "Capture valid atau tidak? Amount tersisa cukup? Provider original apa? Ledger/outbox apa yang harus dibuat?"
```

---

# 2. Payment-Oriented Saga Diagram

```mermaid id="oo933f"
flowchart TD
    A[Order Created] --> B[Start Payment-Oriented Saga]

    B --> C[Create / Authorize Payment]
    C --> D{Payment Result}

    D -->|Requires Action| E[Wait Customer Action / Webhook]
    E --> F{Action Completed?}
    F -->|Yes| C
    F -->|Timeout| G[Cancel Order / Expire Payment]

    D -->|Authorized| H[Reserve Inventory]
    D -->|Captured| I[Proceed to Fulfillment]
    D -->|Failed| J[Mark Order Payment Failed]

    H --> K{Inventory Reserved?}
    K -->|Yes| L[Fraud / Risk Review]
    K -->|No| M[Void Authorization]
    M --> N[Cancel Order]

    L --> O{Fraud Approved?}
    O -->|Yes| P[Capture Payment]
    O -->|Manual Review| Q[Human Task: Fraud Review]
    O -->|Rejected| M

    Q --> O

    P --> R{Capture Result}
    R -->|Captured| I
    R -->|Unknown| S[Wait Payment Status Webhook / Query]
    R -->|Failed| T[Manual Investigation]

    S --> U{Final Status?}
    U -->|Captured| I
    U -->|Failed| T
    U -->|Timeout| T

    I --> V[Fulfill Order]
    V --> W{Fulfillment Result}

    W -->|Success| X[Order Completed]
    W -->|Failure After Capture| Y[Create Refund]
    Y --> Z[Order Compensation Completed]
```

---

# 3. Practical Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Saga Boundary** | Apa yang diorkestrasi Camunda vs Payment Service. | process contract |
| 2 | **Process Variables** | Data minimal untuk routing workflow. | `paymentId`, `orderId`, `paymentStatus` |
| 3 | **Start Process** | Mulai saga dari Order Service / Payment API. | `startPaymentSaga()` |
| 4 | **Payment Worker** | Worker memanggil Payment Service. | `AuthorizePaymentWorker`, `CapturePaymentWorker` |
| 5 | **Message Correlation** | Webhook/payment event melanjutkan process. | `PaymentCapturedMessage` |
| 6 | **Wait State** | Menunggu 3DS, capture webhook, refund webhook. | message catch / receive task |
| 7 | **Timeout Boundary** | Timer untuk auth/payment/customer action. | boundary timer |
| 8 | **Compensation** | Void/refund/release inventory. | compensation task |
| 9 | **Human Task** | Fraud review, refund approval, dispute evidence. | user task / tasklist |
| 10 | **Retry Semantics** | Bedakan technical retry vs business retry. | job retries + BPMN error |
| 11 | **Incident Handling** | Worker retries habis → incident. | Operate/Cockpit playbook |
| 12 | **Idempotent Workers** | Worker boleh dipanggil ulang. | operation idempotency key |
| 13 | **Outbox Integration** | Payment events memicu process correlation. | outbox → Camunda message |
| 14 | **Process Versioning** | Running instance harus aman saat BPMN berubah. | version strategy |
| 15 | **Observability** | Trace order/payment/process. | correlation IDs, metrics |

---

# 4. Saga Boundary: Apa yang Tidak Boleh Dipindahkan ke Camunda

Jangan menaruh business invariant payment di BPMN.

| Logic | Tempat yang Benar |
|---|---|
| `captured_amount <= authorized_amount` | Payment domain/service |
| idempotency key validation | Payment service |
| provider routing | Payment service |
| provider retry/timeout mapping | Payment gateway adapter/application |
| ledger posting | Payment service |
| refund amount guard | Payment service |
| webhook signature verification | Payment service |
| dispute/chargeback ledger | Payment service |
| order-payment-fulfillment orchestration | Camunda |
| fraud review wait/approval | Camunda + Risk/Support service |
| timeout waiting customer action | Camunda |
| compensation sequence | Camunda orchestration, action executed by services |

Rule:

```text id="h97z2x"
BPMN boleh menentukan “kapan capture/refund/void dipanggil”.
BPMN tidak boleh menentukan “apakah amount capture/refund valid secara finansial”.
```

---

# 5. Recommended Process Variables

Jangan menyimpan raw provider payload besar di Camunda variables. Simpan reference dan summary.

```json id="boqtv8"
{
  "sagaId": "saga-ord-123",
  "orderId": "ORD-123",
  "paymentId": "b1806219-220c-4a25-ad4e-33b233143c0a",
  "merchantId": "MRC-001",
  "customerId": "CUST-001",

  "amountMinor": 1000000,
  "currency": "IDR",

  "captureMode": "MANUAL",
  "paymentStatus": "AUTHORIZED",

  "inventoryReserved": false,
  "fraudReviewRequired": false,
  "fraudDecision": null,

  "correlationKey": "ORD-123",
  "correlationId": "corr-123",

  "paymentAttemptCount": 1,
  "captureAttemptCount": 0,

  "requiresAction": false,
  "customerActionExpiresAt": null,

  "compensationReason": null
}
```

Practical rule:

```text id="1iqk1r"
Camunda variables harus cukup untuk menggerakkan process,
bukan menjadi database kedua untuk payment/order.
```

---

# 6. Process Key dan Correlation Key

Recommended correlation key:

```text id="46n6gi"
orderId untuk order-level saga
paymentId untuk payment-level wait
refundId untuk refund wait
disputeId untuk dispute workflow
```

Untuk payment saga order-level:

```text id="ve6y49"
process business key / correlation key = orderId
```

Untuk menunggu payment webhook:

```text id="2m1yyj"
messageName = PaymentCaptured
correlationKey = paymentId
```

Camunda 8 message subscription dibuka ketika process instance mencapai message catch event, dan correlation key dievaluasi saat message catch event aktif; subscription tersebut tidak otomatis berubah jika variable yang direferensikan berubah setelahnya. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Important:

```text id="dod1e2"
Pastikan variable paymentId sudah ada sebelum process masuk message catch event yang memakai paymentId sebagai correlation key.
```

---

# 7. BPMN Pattern 1 - Manual Capture Order Flow

```mermaid id="bs7sxe"
flowchart TD
    A((Start: OrderCreated)) --> B[Authorize Payment]
    B --> C{Payment Authorized?}

    C -->|No: Failed| D[Mark Order Payment Failed]
    C -->|Requires Action| E[Wait Customer Action]
    E --> F{Action Completed?}
    F -->|Yes| B
    F -->|Timeout| G[Cancel Payment / Cancel Order]

    C -->|Yes| H[Reserve Inventory]
    H --> I{Inventory Reserved?}

    I -->|No| J[Void Authorization]
    J --> K[Cancel Order]

    I -->|Yes| L[Risk Review Decision]
    L --> M{Risk Approved?}

    M -->|No| J
    M -->|Manual Review| N[Human Task: Fraud Review]
    N --> L

    M -->|Yes| O[Capture Payment]
    O --> P{Capture Result}

    P -->|Captured| Q[Fulfill Order]
    P -->|Unknown| R[Wait Payment Captured/Failed Message]
    P -->|Failed| S[Manual Investigation]

    R --> T{Final?}
    T -->|Captured| Q
    T -->|Failed| S
    T -->|Timeout| S

    Q --> U{Fulfillment Success?}
    U -->|Yes| V((End: Completed))
    U -->|No| W[Refund Payment]
    W --> X((End: Compensated))
```

---

# 8. BPMN Pattern 2 - Auto-Capture Checkout Flow

```mermaid id="nad9p2"
flowchart TD
    A((Start Checkout)) --> B[Create Payment Auto Capture]
    B --> C{Payment Status}

    C -->|Captured| D[Confirm Order]
    C -->|Requires Action| E[Wait Customer Action]
    C -->|Failed| F[Reject Checkout]
    C -->|Unknown| G[Wait Provider Webhook]

    E --> H{Action Result}
    H -->|Captured| D
    H -->|Failed| F
    H -->|Timeout| I[Expire Checkout]

    G --> J{Webhook Result}
    J -->|Captured| D
    J -->|Failed| F
    J -->|Timeout| K[Manual Review]

    D --> L[Publish Order Confirmed]
    L --> M((End))
```

Auto-capture flow biasanya lebih pendek, tetapi tetap perlu wait state untuk `REQUIRES_ACTION` dan `UNKNOWN`.

---

# 9. BPMN Pattern 3 - Compensation Flow

Compensation dipakai saat step sebelumnya berhasil, tetapi step berikutnya gagal.

Contoh:

```text id="k28lr4"
1. Authorization berhasil.
2. Inventory reservation gagal.
3. Saga harus void authorization.

Atau:

1. Capture berhasil.
2. Fulfillment gagal permanen.
3. Saga harus refund payment.
```

Camunda compensation event memang digunakan untuk undo step yang sudah berhasil, dan compensation handler bertugas mengembalikan efek dari activity yang dikompensasi. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/compensation-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```mermaid id="sezmsk"
flowchart TD
    A[Authorize Payment] --> B[Reserve Inventory]
    B --> C{Inventory Result}

    C -->|Success| D[Capture Payment]
    C -->|Failure| E[Compensate: Void Authorization]

    D --> F[Fulfill Order]
    F --> G{Fulfillment Result}

    G -->|Success| H((Completed))
    G -->|Failure| I[Compensate: Refund Payment]
```

Practical warning:

```text id="sqc6qa"
Compensation action harus tetap idempotent.
Camunda bisa retry worker; worker crash bisa menyebabkan action dipanggil ulang.
Payment service harus memastikan duplicate void/refund tidak membuat efek dobel.
```

---

# 10. BPMN Pattern 4 - Wait Webhook / Message Correlation

Payment webhook tidak boleh langsung mengubah Camunda process tanpa melalui payment service.

Recommended flow:

```text id="jrrs9v"
Provider Webhook
  -> Payment Webhook Endpoint
  -> Verify signature
  -> Inbox dedup
  -> Update payment state
  -> Insert outbox PaymentCaptured / PaymentFailed
  -> Outbox publisher publishes internal event
  -> Camunda correlation worker publishes message to process
```

Diagram:

```mermaid id="kbmhnd"
flowchart TD
    A[Provider Webhook] --> B[Payment Webhook Endpoint]
    B --> C[Inbox Dedup]
    C --> D[Payment State Transition]
    D --> E[Outbox PaymentCaptured]
    E --> F[Internal Event Broker]
    F --> G[Camunda Correlation Worker]
    G --> H[Publish Message: PaymentCaptured]
    H --> I[Camunda Process Waiting]
```

Why not direct provider webhook → Camunda?

```text id="9m95pe"
Karena provider webhook perlu signature verification, dedup, state guard, ledger, dan financial audit.
Camunda tidak boleh menjadi webhook trust boundary untuk PSP.
```

---

# 11. Camunda 8 Message Correlation Worker

Gunakan wrapper agar Payment Service tidak tergantung langsung pada client API di semua tempat.

```java id="gg5dd2"
package com.company.payment.workflow;

import java.util.Map;

public interface WorkflowMessagePublisher {

    void publishMessage(
            String messageName,
            String correlationKey,
            Map<String, Object> variables,
            String messageId,
            long timeToLiveMillis
    );
}
```

Camunda 8-style implementation konseptual:

```java id="dkv5lx"
package com.company.payment.workflow.camunda8;

import com.company.payment.workflow.WorkflowMessagePublisher;
import io.camunda.zeebe.client.ZeebeClient;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

public class Camunda8WorkflowMessagePublisher implements WorkflowMessagePublisher {

    private final ZeebeClient client;

    public Camunda8WorkflowMessagePublisher(ZeebeClient client) {
        this.client = client;
    }

    @Override
    public void publishMessage(
            String messageName,
            String correlationKey,
            Map<String, Object> variables,
            String messageId,
            long timeToLiveMillis
    ) {
        client.newPublishMessageCommand()
                .messageName(messageName)
                .correlationKey(correlationKey)
                .messageId(messageId)
                .timeToLive(Duration.ofMillis(timeToLiveMillis))
                .variables(variables)
                .send()
                .join();
    }
}
```

Practical rules:

```text id="nkheeg"
1. messageName harus sama dengan BPMN message.
2. correlationKey harus sama dengan process variable yang dipakai di catch event.
3. messageId sebaiknya eventId/outboxEventId untuk dedup.
4. timeToLive harus disesuaikan dengan race condition: message bisa datang sebelum subscription aktif.
```

Camunda 8 documentation menyatakan message akan correlated jika message name dan correlation key cocok dengan subscription; jika tidak ada subscription yang proper, message bisa discard. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/messages/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 12. Race Condition: Webhook Datang Sebelum Process Wait

Problem:

```text id="go3akr"
1. Process call Create Payment.
2. Provider langsung kirim webhook PaymentCaptured.
3. Payment service publish Camunda message.
4. Process belum sampai message catch event.
5. Message tidak correlated / hilang.
6. Process menunggu selamanya.
```

Mitigasi:

| Strategi | Penjelasan |
|---|---|
| Message TTL | Publish message dengan TTL cukup panjang jika engine mendukung. |
| Event buffering | Internal outbox/event store tetap sumber kebenaran. |
| Status check before wait | Sebelum masuk wait state, worker query payment status. |
| Timeout boundary | Jika message tidak datang, process query status. |
| Idempotent correlation | Duplicate message aman. |
| Process variable readiness | Pastikan `paymentId`/correlation key sudah set sebelum wait. |

Recommended BPMN pattern:

```text id="y6pbhv"
After Create Payment:
  if result is final -> continue
  if pending/unknown -> wait message
  before wait, set paymentId correlation variable
  boundary timer -> query payment status
```

---

# 13. Timer Boundary for Payment Timeout

Boundary timer berguna untuk:

```text id="223e34"
- customer action timeout
- authorization expiry
- provider webhook timeout
- fraud review SLA
- refund pending too long
- dispute evidence due soon
```

Camunda 8 timer event docs mencakup intermediate timer catch event dan interrupting/non-interrupting boundary timer event, sehingga timer bisa dipakai baik sebagai waiting step maupun SLA branch pada activity. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/timer-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Example:

```mermaid id="i0u63r"
flowchart TD
    A[Wait Payment Captured Message] --> B{Message Received?}
    B -->|PaymentCaptured| C[Continue Fulfillment]
    B -->|PaymentFailed| D[Manual Review / Cancel]

    A -. boundary timer 15m .-> E[Query Payment Status]
    E --> F{Status?}
    F -->|Captured| C
    F -->|Failed| D
    F -->|Still Unknown| G[Create Incident / Human Task]
```

Practical rule:

```text id="xl98np"
Timer should not assume payment failed.
Timer should trigger status query or manual investigation.
```

---

# 14. Worker Design

Workers should call services through stable APIs, not directly mutate database tables unless they are inside the same bounded context and intentionally designed that way.

Worker categories:

| Worker | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `AuthorizePaymentWorker` | Call Payment Service create/authorize endpoint/use case. |
| `CapturePaymentWorker` | Call capture endpoint/use case. |
| `VoidAuthorizationWorker` | Compensation for authorized but unfulfilled order. |
| `RefundPaymentWorker` | Compensation after captured payment. |
| `ReserveInventoryWorker` | Call inventory service. |
| `ReleaseInventoryWorker` | Compensation inventory. |
| `FraudDecisionWorker` | Call risk engine. |
| `CreateSupportTaskWorker` | Create manual review/support task. |
| `QueryPaymentStatusWorker` | Recovery when message/webhook timeout. |
| `CorrelatePaymentEventWorker` | Publish Camunda message from payment outbox/broker event. |

---

# 15. Idempotent Worker Rule

Every worker that calls side-effecting service must have stable operation id.

Example operation IDs:

```text id="ygnkkv"
authorizePayment:
  operationId = "authorize:" + orderId

capturePayment:
  operationId = "capture:" + paymentId + ":" + fulfillmentId

voidAuthorization:
  operationId = "void:" + paymentId + ":" + sagaId

refundPayment:
  operationId = "refund:" + paymentId + ":" + compensationReason + ":" + sagaId

reserveInventory:
  operationId = "reserve:" + orderId

releaseInventory:
  operationId = "release:" + orderId
```

Use operation ID as:

```text id="7s9imh"
- HTTP Idempotency-Key
- payment refundRequestId / captureRequestId
- worker log key
- audit causationId
```

---

# 16. Camunda 8 Job Worker Retry

Camunda 8 job workers can fail jobs with retry backoff; retry backoff is useful when an external system is down because immediate retries can exhaust retries and create incidents. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/job-workers/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Camunda incidents are created when a job fails and no retries remain; resolving job-related incidents can involve fixing variables, increasing retries, and marking the incident as resolved. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/concepts/incidents/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Worker pseudo-code:

```java id="wedu18"
public class CapturePaymentJobHandler {

    private final PaymentClient paymentClient;

    public Map<String, Object> handle(JobContext job) {
        String paymentId = job.getVariable("paymentId");
        String orderId = job.getVariable("orderId");
        String fulfillmentId = job.getVariable("fulfillmentId");
        long amountMinor = job.getVariable("amountMinor");
        String currency = job.getVariable("currency");

        String idempotencyKey =
                "capture:" + paymentId + ":" + fulfillmentId;

        try {
            PaymentResponse response = paymentClient.capture(
                    paymentId,
                    amountMinor,
                    currency,
                    idempotencyKey
            );

            return Map.of(
                    "paymentStatus", response.status(),
                    "captureId", response.captureId(),
                    "paymentCaptured", "CAPTURED".equals(response.status())
            );

        } catch (PaymentBusinessException e) {
            /*
             * Business error should be modeled in BPMN,
             * not retried forever as technical incident.
             */
            throw new BpmnBusinessError(
                    e.errorCode(),
                    e.getMessage()
            );

        } catch (TemporaryInfrastructureException e) {
            /*
             * Technical error:
             * fail job with retries/backoff.
             */
            throw new RetryableWorkerException(
                    "PAYMENT_CAPTURE_TEMPORARY_FAILURE",
                    e
            );
        }
    }
}
```

Practical mapping:

| Error Type | Camunda Treatment |
|---|---|
| Payment declined | BPMN business path: `PaymentFailed`. |
| Capture amount invalid | BPMN error / incident depending cause. |
| Payment service 503 | technical retry with backoff. |
| Provider timeout but Payment Service returns `UNKNOWN` | continue to wait/query path, not worker retry forever. |
| Bug/null variable | incident. |
| Fraud rejected | business path. |

---

# 17. Camunda 7 External Task Retry

Jika masih memakai Camunda 7 external tasks, worker bertanggung jawab report failure dengan jumlah retries dan retry timeout. Camunda 7 ExternalTaskService `handleFailure` menerima retries dan retry timeout; jika retries diset ke 0, incident dibuat. ([Camunda Documentation](https://docs.camunda.org/javadoc/camunda-bpm-platform/7.15/org/camunda/bpm/client/task/ExternalTaskService.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Camunda 7-style pseudo-code:

```java id="vb4yo4"
client.subscribe("capture-payment")
        .lockDuration(30_000)
        .handler((externalTask, externalTaskService) -> {
            String paymentId = externalTask.getVariable("paymentId");
            String fulfillmentId = externalTask.getVariable("fulfillmentId");

            String idempotencyKey = "capture:" + paymentId + ":" + fulfillmentId;

            try {
                PaymentResponse response = paymentClient.capture(
                        paymentId,
                        externalTask.getVariable("amountMinor"),
                        externalTask.getVariable("currency"),
                        idempotencyKey
                );

                Map<String, Object> variables = Map.of(
                        "paymentStatus", response.status(),
                        "captureId", response.captureId()
                );

                externalTaskService.complete(externalTask, variables);

            } catch (TemporaryInfrastructureException e) {
                Integer currentRetries = externalTask.getRetries();
                int retriesLeft = currentRetries == null ? 3 : currentRetries - 1;
                long retryTimeoutMillis = 60_000;

                externalTaskService.handleFailure(
                        externalTask,
                        "Temporary payment capture failure",
                        e.getMessage(),
                        retriesLeft,
                        retryTimeoutMillis
                );

            } catch (PaymentBusinessException e) {
                externalTaskService.handleBpmnError(
                        externalTask,
                        e.errorCode(),
                        e.getMessage()
                );
            }
        })
        .open();
```

---

# 18. Business Error vs Technical Error

Ini sangat penting.

| Scenario | Type | BPMN Handling |
|---|---|---|
| Card declined | Business result | Gateway path to payment failed. |
| Fraud rejected | Business result | Route to cancel/void. |
| Inventory unavailable | Business result | Compensation void authorization. |
| Customer action timeout | Business timeout | Timer branch. |
| Payment service unavailable | Technical error | Job retry/backoff. |
| DB unavailable | Technical error | Job retry/incident. |
| Worker code bug | Technical error | Incident. |
| Invalid process variable | Model/data error | Incident or validation task. |
| Provider timeout mapped as `UNKNOWN` | Business uncertainty | Wait message/query status path. |

Camunda 8 error events are for deviations from the default scenario that the process model should react to; this fits business exceptions, while technical failures usually use job retries/incidents. ([docs.camunda.io](https://docs.camunda.io/docs/components/modeler/bpmn/error-events/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical rule:

```text id="3r8b82"
Jangan model semua hal sebagai incident.
Jangan juga model semua hal sebagai BPMN error.
Business result masuk BPMN path.
Technical failure masuk retry/incident.
```

---

# 19. Process State vs Payment State

Do not duplicate full payment state machine in BPMN.

Use summary status:

```text id="d2re1t"
paymentStatus:
  INITIATED
  REQUIRES_ACTION
  AUTHORIZED
  CAPTURED
  FAILED
  UNKNOWN
  REFUNDED
```

When detailed decision needed, query Payment Service:

```http id="gi40y6"
GET /payments/{paymentId}
```

Example process decision:

```text id="gyv0d6"
if paymentStatus == AUTHORIZED:
  continue inventory/fraud/manual capture

if paymentStatus == CAPTURED:
  continue fulfillment

if paymentStatus == UNKNOWN:
  wait webhook or query status

if paymentStatus == FAILED:
  mark order payment failed
```

---

# 20. Payment Saga Tables

Selain Camunda runtime, simpan mapping internal agar gampang trace.

## 20.1 `payment_saga_instances`

```sql id="ssqmnb"
CREATE TABLE payment_saga_instances (
    saga_instance_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    process_engine VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    process_definition_key VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    process_instance_key VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    order_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    payment_id UUID,
    refund_id UUID,
    dispute_id UUID,

    saga_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    saga_status VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    correlation_key VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
    correlation_id VARCHAR(100),

    started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    completed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    last_known_activity VARCHAR(200),
    last_error_code VARCHAR(100),
    last_error_message TEXT,

    variables_snapshot JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_saga_engine
        CHECK (process_engine IN ('CAMUNDA_7', 'CAMUNDA_8')),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_saga_status
        CHECK (saga_status IN (
            'STARTED',
            'RUNNING',
            'WAITING',
            'COMPLETED',
            'COMPENSATED',
            'FAILED',
            'CANCELLED',
            'INCIDENT',
            'NEEDS_INVESTIGATION'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql id="ksetc9"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_saga_process_instance
ON payment_saga_instances(process_engine, process_instance_key);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_saga_order
ON payment_saga_instances(order_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_saga_payment
ON payment_saga_instances(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_saga_status
ON payment_saga_instances(saga_status);
```

## 20.2 `payment_saga_events`

```sql id="9jpye6"
CREATE TABLE payment_saga_events (
    saga_event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    saga_instance_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payment_saga_instances(saga_instance_id),

    event_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    activity_id VARCHAR(200),
    activity_name VARCHAR(200),

    old_status VARCHAR(50),
    new_status VARCHAR(50),

    payload JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system'
);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_saga_events_instance
ON payment_saga_events(saga_instance_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_saga_events_created
ON payment_saga_events(created_at);
```

---

# 21. Starting a Saga

Interface:

```java id="5wufz0"
package com.company.payment.workflow;

import java.util.Map;

public interface WorkflowProcessStarter {

    WorkflowProcessInstance startProcess(
            String processDefinitionKey,
            String businessKey,
            Map<String, Object> variables
    );
}
```

Camunda 8-style conceptual implementation:

```java id="t7dch5"
package com.company.payment.workflow.camunda8;

import com.company.payment.workflow.WorkflowProcessInstance;
import com.company.payment.workflow.WorkflowProcessStarter;
import io.camunda.zeebe.client.ZeebeClient;

import java.util.Map;

public class Camunda8ProcessStarter implements WorkflowProcessStarter {

    private final ZeebeClient client;

    public Camunda8ProcessStarter(ZeebeClient client) {
        this.client = client;
    }

    @Override
    public WorkflowProcessInstance startProcess(
            String processDefinitionKey,
            String businessKey,
            Map<String, Object> variables
    ) {
        var event = client.newCreateInstanceCommand()
                .bpmnProcessId(processDefinitionKey)
                .latestVersion()
                .variables(variables)
                .send()
                .join();

        return new WorkflowProcessInstance(
                "CAMUNDA_8",
                processDefinitionKey,
                String.valueOf(event.getProcessInstanceKey()),
                businessKey
        );
    }
}
```

Service:

```java id="9q6tsd"
public StartPaymentSagaResponse startOrderPaymentSaga(StartOrderPaymentSagaCommand command) {
    String businessKey = command.orderId();

    Map<String, Object> variables = Map.of(
            "sagaId", UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
            "orderId", command.orderId(),
            "merchantId", command.merchantId(),
            "customerId", command.customerId(),
            "amountMinor", command.amountMinor(),
            "currency", command.currency(),
            "captureMode", command.captureMode(),
            "correlationKey", command.orderId(),
            "correlationId", command.correlationId()
    );

    WorkflowProcessInstance instance =
            workflowProcessStarter.startProcess(
                    "order-payment-saga",
                    businessKey,
                    variables
            );

    sagaRepository.insertStarted(instance, command);

    return new StartPaymentSagaResponse(
            instance.processInstanceKey(),
            businessKey,
            "STARTED"
    );
}
```

---

# 22. Worker: Authorize Payment

```java id="6c7x2e"
package com.company.payment.workflow.worker;

public class AuthorizePaymentWorker {

    private final PaymentClient paymentClient;

    public Map<String, Object> execute(Map<String, Object> variables) {
        String orderId = (String) variables.get("orderId");
        String merchantId = (String) variables.get("merchantId");
        long amountMinor = ((Number) variables.get("amountMinor")).longValue();
        String currency = (String) variables.get("currency");
        String captureMode = (String) variables.get("captureMode");
        String correlationId = (String) variables.get("correlationId");

        String idempotencyKey = "authorize:" + orderId;

        PaymentResponse response = paymentClient.createPayment(
                new CreatePaymentRequest(
                        orderId,
                        merchantId,
                        amountMinor,
                        currency,
                        captureMode
                ),
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId
        );

        return Map.of(
                "paymentId", response.paymentId().toString(),
                "paymentStatus", response.status(),
                "requiresAction", response.requiresAction(),
                "nextActionUrl", response.nextActionUrl(),
                "customerActionExpiresAt", response.customerActionExpiresAt()
        );
    }
}
```

Worker output branches:

| Payment Response | BPMN Next |
|---|---|
| `AUTHORIZED` | Reserve inventory. |
| `CAPTURED` | Fulfill order. |
| `REQUIRES_ACTION` | Wait customer action. |
| `FAILED` | Mark order payment failed. |
| `UNKNOWN` | Wait webhook/query status. |

---

# 23. Worker: Capture Payment

```java id="cimzlb"
package com.company.payment.workflow.worker;

public class CapturePaymentWorker {

    private final PaymentClient paymentClient;

    public Map<String, Object> execute(Map<String, Object> variables) {
        String paymentId = (String) variables.get("paymentId");
        String orderId = (String) variables.get("orderId");
        String fulfillmentId = (String) variables.getOrDefault("fulfillmentId", orderId);
        long amountMinor = ((Number) variables.get("amountMinor")).longValue();
        String currency = (String) variables.get("currency");
        String correlationId = (String) variables.get("correlationId");

        String idempotencyKey = "capture:" + paymentId + ":" + fulfillmentId;

        PaymentResponse response = paymentClient.capturePayment(
                paymentId,
                amountMinor,
                currency,
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId
        );

        return Map.of(
                "paymentStatus", response.status(),
                "captureId", response.captureId(),
                "capturedAmountMinor", response.capturedAmountMinor(),
                "captureResultKnown", !"UNKNOWN".equals(response.status())
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 24. Worker: Void Authorization Compensation

```java id="yljnzl"
package com.company.payment.workflow.worker;

public class VoidAuthorizationWorker {

    private final PaymentClient paymentClient;

    public Map<String, Object> execute(Map<String, Object> variables) {
        String paymentId = (String) variables.get("paymentId");
        String sagaId = (String) variables.get("sagaId");
        String correlationId = (String) variables.get("correlationId");

        String idempotencyKey = "void:" + paymentId + ":" + sagaId;

        PaymentResponse response = paymentClient.voidAuthorization(
                paymentId,
                "ORDER_COMPENSATION",
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId
        );

        return Map.of(
                "paymentStatus", response.status(),
                "voidCompleted", "VOIDED".equals(response.status())
        );
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="h3xrc3"
Void worker tidak boleh mencoba void jika payment sudah captured.
Payment Service akan reject dan BPMN harus route ke refund/manual investigation sesuai status.
```

---

# 25. Worker: Refund Compensation

```java id="m28lo9"
package com.company.payment.workflow.worker;

public class RefundPaymentWorker {

    private final PaymentClient paymentClient;

    public Map<String, Object> execute(Map<String, Object> variables) {
        String paymentId = (String) variables.get("paymentId");
        String orderId = (String) variables.get("orderId");
        String sagaId = (String) variables.get("sagaId");
        long amountMinor = ((Number) variables.get("amountMinor")).longValue();
        String currency = (String) variables.get("currency");
        String correlationId = (String) variables.get("correlationId");

        String refundRequestId = "compensation:" + orderId + ":" + sagaId;
        String idempotencyKey = "refund:" + paymentId + ":" + refundRequestId;

        RefundResponse response = paymentClient.createRefund(
                paymentId,
                new CreateRefundRequest(
                        refundRequestId,
                        amountMinor,
                        currency,
                        "FULFILLMENT_FAILED",
                        "Refund due to fulfillment failure",
                        "camunda-saga"
                ),
                idempotencyKey,
                correlationId
        );

        return Map.of(
                "refundId", response.refundId().toString(),
                "refundStatus", response.status()
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 26. Message Correlation from Payment Outbox

Consumer listens to payment events and correlates Camunda process.

```java id="kfvbwe"
package com.company.payment.workflow.correlation;

public class PaymentEventToWorkflowCorrelationHandler {

    private final WorkflowMessagePublisher workflowMessagePublisher;

    public void handle(DomainEventEnvelope<PaymentEventPayload> event) {
        PaymentEventPayload payload = event.payload();

        String messageName = switch (event.eventType()) {
            case "PaymentCaptured" -> "PaymentCaptured";
            case "PaymentFailed" -> "PaymentFailed";
            case "PaymentAuthorized" -> "PaymentAuthorized";
            case "RefundSucceeded" -> "RefundSucceeded";
            case "RefundFailed" -> "RefundFailed";
            default -> null;
        };

        if (messageName == null) {
            return;
        }

        String correlationKey = payload.paymentId().toString();

        workflowMessagePublisher.publishMessage(
                messageName,
                correlationKey,
                Map.of(
                        "paymentId", payload.paymentId().toString(),
                        "paymentStatus", payload.status(),
                        "eventId", event.eventId().toString(),
                        "occurredAt", event.occurredAt().toString()
                ),
                event.eventId().toString(),
                15 * 60 * 1000L
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 27. Idempotency for Correlation

Correlation can be duplicate.

Use `messageId = eventId` or outbox event ID.

Store correlation attempts:

```sql id="acdlwh"
CREATE TABLE workflow_message_correlations (
    workflow_message_correlation_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    event_id UUID NOT NULL,
    event_type VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,

    message_name VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    correlation_key VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    process_engine VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,

    correlation_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

    last_error_code VARCHAR(100),
    last_error_message TEXT,

    correlated_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_workflow_msg_corr_status
        CHECK (correlation_status IN (
            'PENDING',
            'CORRELATED',
            'FAILED',
            'IGNORED',
            'DEAD_LETTER'
        ))
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_workflow_message_correlations_event
ON workflow_message_correlations(event_id, message_name, correlation_key);
```

---

# 28. Human Task Patterns

Payment workflows often need human tasks:

| Human Task | Trigger |
|---|---|
| Fraud review | Risk engine says manual review. |
| Payment unknown investigation | Provider status unclear after timeout. |
| Refund approval | Amount high / reason risky. |
| Dispute evidence collection | Dispute opened. |
| Reconciliation mismatch review | Amount/status mismatch. |
| Manual adjustment approval | Finance correction. |

BPMN example:

```mermaid id="q7vu7z"
flowchart TD
    A[Risk Decision] --> B{Risk Result}
    B -->|Approved| C[Capture Payment]
    B -->|Rejected| D[Void Authorization]
    B -->|Manual Review| E[User Task: Fraud Review]
    E --> F{Reviewer Decision}
    F -->|Approve| C
    F -->|Reject| D
```

Practical rule:

```text id="x27yfi"
Human task result harus ditulis kembali ke domain service jika memengaruhi financial action.
Jangan hanya update Camunda variable tanpa audit domain.
```

---

# 29. Timeout and Recovery Patterns

## 29.1 Customer Action Timeout

```text id="pkf2zk"
Payment requires 3DS/customer action.
Process waits for PaymentAuthorized/PaymentCaptured.
Boundary timer expires.
Process calls payment service expire/cancel.
Order is cancelled.
```

## 29.2 Capture Unknown Timeout

```text id="vctbfu"
Capture call returns UNKNOWN.
Process waits PaymentCaptured/PaymentFailed.
Boundary timer expires.
Process calls QueryPaymentStatus.
If still unknown -> human investigation.
```

## 29.3 Refund Pending Timeout

```text id="jba40c"
Refund provider pending.
Process waits RefundSucceeded/RefundFailed.
Timer expires.
Query refund status.
If still pending -> support task.
```

Important:

```text id="o0kubq"
Timeout path should query source of truth.
Timeout path should not assume failure.
```

---

# 30. Recommended BPMN Error Codes

| Error Code | Meaning | BPMN Route |
|---|---|---|
| `PAYMENT_FAILED` | Payment business failure. | Cancel/failed order. |
| `PAYMENT_REQUIRES_ACTION` | Customer action needed. | Wait action. |
| `PAYMENT_UNKNOWN` | Provider result unclear. | Wait/query/manual review. |
| `CAPTURE_FAILED` | Capture rejected/failed. | Manual review/cancel. |
| `INVENTORY_UNAVAILABLE` | Cannot reserve inventory. | Void authorization. |
| `FRAUD_REJECTED` | Risk rejected. | Void authorization. |
| `REFUND_REQUIRES_APPROVAL` | Refund needs approval. | Human task. |
| `REFUND_UNKNOWN` | Refund status unclear. | Wait/query. |
| `DISPUTE_EVIDENCE_DEADLINE_PASSED` | Cannot submit evidence. | Escalation/loss handling. |

---

# 31. Process Versioning Strategy

Payment saga process can change, but running instances may continue with old model.

Practical strategy:

| Change | Strategy |
|---|---|
| Add optional variable | Usually safe. |
| Rename variable | Risky; version process. |
| Change correlation key | Very risky; version process carefully. |
| Change message name | Risky; support old + new during migration. |
| Add compensation branch | Validate active instances. |
| Change worker topic/type | Deploy workers before process. |
| Remove task used by active instances | Avoid unless no active instances. |
| Change SLA timers | New version; decide whether migrate existing. |

Recommended:

```text id="7eqj5n"
Use explicit processDefinitionKey version naming for major changes:
order-payment-saga-v1
order-payment-saga-v2
```

---

# 32. Deployment Architecture

```mermaid id="fajife"
flowchart TD
    A[Payment Service - Jersey/JAX-RS] --> B[PostgreSQL]
    A --> C[Outbox Publisher]
    A --> D[Payment Workers]

    D --> E[Camunda 8 / Zeebe]
    F[Order Service] --> E
    G[Inventory Service] --> E
    H[Risk Service] --> E
    I[Fulfillment Service] --> E

    C --> J[Event Broker]
    J --> K[Workflow Correlation Worker]
    K --> E

    E --> L[Operate / Tasklist]
    M[Support/Risk Users] --> L
```

Runtime components:

| Component | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| Payment Service | State/ledger/idempotency/provider integration. |
| Camunda Engine | Workflow orchestration. |
| Workers | Execute service tasks. |
| Correlation Worker | Convert domain events to Camunda messages. |
| Outbox Publisher | Publish domain events. |
| Tasklist/Operate/Cockpit | Human tasks/incidents. |
| PostgreSQL | Payment state + audit + ledger + saga mapping. |

---

# 33. Kubernetes Considerations

| Concern | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Worker replicas | Multiple replicas okay if workers idempotent. |
| Job timeout | Longer than expected execution; avoid premature retry. |
| Graceful shutdown | Stop accepting new jobs, complete/fail current jobs. |
| Readiness | Worker ready only if dependencies available. |
| Secrets | Camunda credentials via secret manager/Kubernetes Secret. |
| Network | Payment workers need outbound to Camunda and service APIs. |
| Observability | Include processInstanceKey, paymentId, orderId in logs. |
| Scaling | Scale workers by job backlog and external service capacity. |

Camunda 8 performance guidance notes job timeout should be longer than average worker execution time; otherwise workers may time out while still working. ([Camunda](https://camunda.com/blog/2025/01/performance-tuning-camunda-8/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 34.1 Metrics

| Metric | Fungsi |

| `payment_saga.started.total` | Saga started. |
| `payment_saga.completed.total` | Saga completed. |
| `payment_saga.compensated.total` | Saga compensated. |

| `payment_saga.incident.total` | Incidents. |

| `payment_saga.timeout.total` | Timer timeout branches. |
| `payment_worker.execution.duration` | Worker duration. |
| `payment_worker.failure.total` | Worker failure by type. |
| `workflow_message.correlation.failed.total` | Message correlation failures. |
| `workflow_message.correlation.lag.seconds` | Event occurred → process correlated. |

## 34.2 Structured Log

```json id="7m2w7k"
{
  "event": "payment_saga_activity_completed",
  "sagaId": "saga-ord-123",
  "processInstanceKey": "2251799813685250",
  "activityId": "capture-payment",
  "orderId": "ORD-123",
  "paymentId": "b1806219-220c-4a25-ad4e-33b233143c0a",
  "paymentStatus": "CAPTURED",
  "correlationId": "corr-123"
}
```

## 34.3 Alerts

```text id="3n6x9r"
- saga incident count > threshold
- payment wait timeout spike
- capture worker failure spike
- correlation failures > 0
- human task SLA overdue
- compensation failure
- stuck process instance older than SLA
```

# 35. Testing Strategy

## 35.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Authorize worker with `AUTHORIZED` | Variables include `paymentId`, `paymentStatus=AUTHORIZED`. |
| Authorize worker with `REQUIRES_ACTION` | Routes to wait customer action. |
| Capture worker duplicate execution | Same idempotency key; no double capture. |
| Void compensation duplicate execution | No duplicate void effect. |
| Refund compensation duplicate execution | No duplicate refund. |
| Payment event correlation | Publishes message with paymentId correlation key. |
| Unknown payment event | Ignored or routed to investigation. |
| Business error | BPMN error path, not technical retry. |
| Temporary infra error | Job retry/backoff. |
| Timer timeout | Queries Payment Service, not auto-failed. |

## 35.2 Integration Tests

```text id="r9s8rx"
1. Start saga for order ORD-123.
2. Authorize payment returns AUTHORIZED.
3. Inventory reserved.
4. Fraud approved.
5. Capture payment returns UNKNOWN.
6. Process waits PaymentCaptured.
7. Send PaymentCaptured internal event.
8. Correlation worker publishes Camunda message.
9. Process continues to fulfillment.
10. Fulfillment success.
11. Saga completes.
```

## 35.3 Compensation Test

```text id="ghtfn7"
1. Start saga.
2. Authorize payment returns AUTHORIZED.
3. Inventory reservation fails.
4. Saga invokes VoidAuthorizationWorker.
5. Payment becomes VOIDED.
6. Order cancelled.
7. Saga ends COMPENSATED.
8. Re-run void worker.
9. Payment remains VOIDED; no duplicate side effect.
```

## 35.4 Race Condition Test

```text id="l9fvvf"
1. PaymentCaptured event published before process reaches wait state.
2. Message TTL or event buffer prevents loss.
3. Process reaches wait state.
4. Message correlates or timer query detects CAPTURED.
5. Saga continues.
```

---

# 36. Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Camunda langsung menerima PSP webhook | Bypass signature/dedup/ledger. | Webhook → Payment Service → Outbox → Camunda message. |
| BPMN menyimpan raw payment provider payload | Variable bloat/sensitive data. | Store refs only. |
| BPMN jadi payment state machine penuh | Duplikasi logic dan invariant bocor. | Payment service owns state. |
| Worker tidak idempotent | Retry bisa double capture/refund. | Stable idempotency key. |
| Timeout dianggap gagal | Payment provider bisa sudah sukses. | Query status/wait webhook. |
| Semua error jadi incident | Business flow macet. | Business error path. |
| Semua error jadi BPMN branch | Technical bug tersembunyi. | Technical retry/incident. |
| Compensation tidak idempotent | Duplicate side effects. | Payment service guards. |
| Correlation key berubah setelah wait aktif | Message tidak match. | Set key before wait. |
| Tidak ada saga mapping table | Sulit trace/debug. | `payment_saga_instances`. |
| Deploy BPMN sebelum worker siap | Incidents. | Deploy workers first or backward compatible. |
| Tidak monitor stuck wait state | Process menggantung. | timer + SLA alert. |

# 38. Exercise Praktis

Buat module:

```text id="htffh6"
payment-service/
  workflow/
    WorkflowProcessStarter.java
    WorkflowMessagePublisher.java
    WorkflowProcessInstance.java

  workflow/camunda8/
    Camunda8ProcessStarter.java
    Camunda8WorkflowMessagePublisher.java

  workflow/worker/
    AuthorizePaymentWorker.java
    CapturePaymentWorker.java
    VoidAuthorizationWorker.java
    RefundPaymentWorker.java
    QueryPaymentStatusWorker.java

  workflow/correlation/
    PaymentEventToWorkflowCorrelationHandler.java

  infrastructure/persistence/workflow/
    PaymentSagaMapper.java
    WorkflowMessageCorrelationMapper.java

  api/admin/
    PaymentSagaAdminResource.java
```

Target BPMN:

```text id="625joc"
order-payment-saga:
  Start OrderCreated
  Authorize Payment
  Gateway payment result
  Wait Customer Action if required
  Reserve Inventory
  Fraud Review / Human Task if needed
  Capture Payment
  Wait PaymentCaptured if UNKNOWN
  Fulfill Order
  Refund compensation if fulfillment fails
  Void compensation if inventory/fraud fails before capture
  End Completed / Compensated / Failed
```

Target test:

```text id="efm0hh"
1. AUTHORIZED -> inventory success -> fraud approved -> CAPTURED -> fulfillment success.
2. AUTHORIZED -> inventory failed -> VOIDED.
3. AUTHORIZED -> fraud rejected -> VOIDED.
4. CAPTURED -> fulfillment failed -> refund created.
5. Capture UNKNOWN -> wait message -> PaymentCaptured continues.
6. Customer action timeout -> cancel/expire payment.
7. Duplicate capture worker execution -> only one capture.
8. Duplicate refund compensation -> only one refund.
9. PaymentCaptured event before wait -> handled via TTL/status query.
10. Technical worker failure -> retry/incident.
```

---

# Ringkasan

Camunda Saga untuk payment bukan pengganti payment state machine. Camunda adalah **orchestrator** untuk proses bisnis lintas service.

Prinsip utama:

```text id="78f6rl"
1. Payment Service tetap source of truth untuk state, ledger, idempotency, provider integration.
2. Camunda mengatur order-payment-fulfillment workflow.
3. Webhook provider harus masuk Payment Service dulu, bukan langsung Camunda.
4. Payment outbox event bisa dikorelasikan ke Camunda message.
5. Wait state harus punya timeout dan recovery query.
6. Compensation harus idempotent: void/refund/release inventory.
7. Business error masuk BPMN path.
8. Technical error masuk retry/incident.
9. Workers wajib idempotent.
10. Correlation key harus stabil dan sudah ada sebelum wait state.
```

Dengan desain ini, saga bisa tahan terhadap provider async webhook, worker retry, duplicate event, timeout, compensation, human review, dan incident recovery.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 15/18 - Error Mapping, Timeout, Unknown State, dan Recovery Pattern

Seri ini fokus pada bagian yang paling sering membuat payment system **salah secara finansial**: error handling.

Core idea:

```text id="7sgpp6"
Dalam payment system:
timeout ≠ failed
HTTP 500 ≠ failed
provider unavailable ≠ payment failed
webhook terlambat ≠ payment gagal
unknown state ≠ boleh retry sembarangan
```

Payment operation adalah side-effect finansial. Jika request ke provider timeout, bisa saja provider **sudah menerima request dan memproses charge/capture/refund**, tetapi response tidak sampai ke service kita. Karena itu Stripe, PayPal, dan Adyen sama-sama menekankan idempotency untuk retry aman: Stripe mendukung idempotency untuk retry request tanpa menjalankan operasi yang sama dua kali, PayPal menyebut idempotent call dapat diretry saat network timeout atau HTTP 5xx selama server masih menyimpan ID, dan Adyen menyatakan idempotency memungkinkan retry beberapa kali sambil memastikan action hanya dilakukan sekali. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/api/idempotent_requests?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Untuk API error contract internal, gunakan format yang stabil dan machine-readable. RFC 9457 mendefinisikan Problem Details untuk membawa detail error machine-readable dalam response HTTP API dan menggantikan RFC 7807. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc9457/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Payment error handling harus menjawab 4 pertanyaan:

```text id="v1iqkv"
1. Apakah provider menerima request?
2. Apakah provider mengeksekusi side effect?
3. Apakah hasilnya final?
4. Apakah aman untuk retry?
```

Jangan mulai dari:

```text id="07y10o"
HTTP status apa?
```

Mulai dari:

```text id="ndy3x4"
Financial side effect-nya known atau unknown?
```

Contoh:

```text id="t8tpip"
POST /capture ke PSP
  -> client timeout after 10 seconds

Kemungkinan:
  1. PSP tidak pernah menerima request.
  2. PSP menerima request, tetapi belum memproses.
  3. PSP menerima request dan capture sukses.
  4. PSP menerima request dan capture gagal.
  5. PSP memproses tetapi response hilang.
```

Karena itu status internal yang benar biasanya:

```text id="esppzv"
CAPTURE_PENDING / UNKNOWN / PENDING_CONFIRMATION
```

Bukan langsung:

```text id="osmq7g"
FAILED
```

---

# 2. Diagram Recovery Flow

```mermaid id="cckd7a"
flowchart TD
    A[Payment Operation: authorize/capture/refund/void] --> B[Call Provider with Idempotency Key]

    B --> C{Provider Response}

    C -->|Success Final| D[Apply Success State]
    C -->|Business Failure / Decline| E[Apply Failed State]
    C -->|Requires Action| F[Apply Requires Action]
    C -->|Accepted / Pending| G[Apply Pending State]
    C -->|Timeout / Network Error| H[Apply UNKNOWN / Pending Confirmation]
    C -->|5xx / Provider Unavailable| I[Retry or UNKNOWN]
    C -->|Invalid Request / Config Error| J[Incident / Failed Attempt]

    H --> K[Recovery Scheduler]
    I --> K
    G --> K

    K --> L[Query Provider Status]
    L --> M{Status Result}

    M -->|Captured / Authorized / Refunded| N[Apply Success Idempotently]
    M -->|Failed / Declined| O[Apply Failure Idempotently]
    M -->|Still Pending| P[Retry Later]
    M -->|Not Found| Q[Investigate / Retry Original If Safe]
    M -->|Ambiguous| R[Needs Investigation]

    N --> S[Ledger + Outbox]
    O --> T[Outbox Failure Event]
    R --> U[Manual Investigation Queue]
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Error Taxonomy** | Klasifikasi error provider/internal. | `PaymentErrorCategory` |
| 2 | **Provider Error Mapping** | Map Stripe/Adyen/PayPal error ke internal code. | `GatewayErrorMapper` |
| 3 | **Problem Details Contract** | Error API stabil. | `application/problem+json` |
| 4 | **Timeout Handling** | Timeout menjadi unknown/pending, bukan failed. | `PaymentStatus.UNKNOWN` |
| 5 | **Safe Retry** | Retry hanya dengan idempotency key sama. | retry policy |
| 6 | **Provider Idempotency** | Propagate provider-specific idempotency header. | `ProviderIdempotencyKeyFactory` |
| 7 | **Unknown State Recovery** | Query provider/webhook/reconciliation. | `PaymentRecoveryJob` |
| 8 | **Status Query Pattern** | Query status by provider reference. | `GatewayStatusQueryClient` |
| 9 | **Attempt Tracking** | Semua provider call dicatat. | `payment_attempts` |
| 10 | **Error Persistence** | Simpan failure code/message/retryability. | `payment_operation_failures` |
| 11 | **Manual Investigation** | Case untuk ambiguous state. | `payment_investigation_cases` |
| 12 | **Retry Backoff** | Exponential/fixed backoff dengan cap. | `next_attempt_at` |
| 13 | **Circuit Breaker Awareness** | Provider down tidak bikin double charge. | pre-call only |
| 14 | **Incident Playbook** | Runbook untuk stuck/unknown/mismatch. | ops procedure |
| 15 | **Observability** | Metric unknown, timeout, recovery success. | dashboard/alert |

---

# 4. Error Taxonomy

Jangan hanya punya `Exception`.

Gunakan taxonomy yang memisahkan **business result**, **technical failure**, dan **financial uncertainty**.

```java id="1wj4h2"
package com.company.payment.error;

public enum PaymentErrorCategory {
    VALIDATION_ERROR,

    BUSINESS_DECLINE,
    BUSINESS_RULE_VIOLATION,
    INVALID_STATE,

    PROVIDER_DECLINE,
    PROVIDER_INVALID_REQUEST,
    PROVIDER_AUTHENTICATION_ERROR,
    PROVIDER_AUTHORIZATION_ERROR,
    PROVIDER_RATE_LIMIT,
    PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE,
    PROVIDER_TIMEOUT,
    PROVIDER_UNKNOWN_RESPONSE,
    PROVIDER_SCHEMA_ERROR,

    NETWORK_ERROR,
    INTERNAL_ERROR,
    DATABASE_ERROR,
    CONFIGURATION_ERROR,

    IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT,
    CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION,

    UNKNOWN_FINANCIAL_STATE,
    NEEDS_INVESTIGATION
}
```

Mapping konseptual:

| Category | Retry? | Payment State | Notes |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `VALIDATION_ERROR` | Tidak | unchanged | Request client salah. |
| `BUSINESS_DECLINE` | Tidak langsung | `FAILED` | Customer/card/payment method declined. |
| `INVALID_STATE` | Tidak | unchanged | Capture before auth, refund before capture. |
| `PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | Tidak blindly | `UNKNOWN` / pending | Query provider/wait webhook. |
| `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` | Mungkin | pending/unknown | Retry jika side effect belum terjadi atau idempotent. |
| `PROVIDER_RATE_LIMIT` | Ya delayed | pending | Backoff. |
| `PROVIDER_INVALID_REQUEST` | Tidak | attempt failed / incident | Bug/config/mapping. |
| `PROVIDER_AUTHENTICATION_ERROR` | Tidak | incident | Secret/API key issue. |
| `NETWORK_ERROR` | Depends | unknown | Jika request mungkin terkirim, treat unknown. |
| `IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT` | Tidak | unchanged | Same key different payload. |
| `CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION` | Mungkin | unchanged | Reload state and retry command logic. |
| `UNKNOWN_FINANCIAL_STATE` | Recovery | `UNKNOWN` | Status query / investigation. |

---

# 5. Payment Status for Uncertainty

Tambahkan state yang eksplisit.

```java id="9fzay0"
public enum PaymentStatus {
    INITIATED,

    AUTHORIZATION_PENDING,
    REQUIRES_ACTION,
    AUTHORIZED,

    CAPTURE_PENDING,
    CAPTURED,
    PARTIALLY_CAPTURED,

    REFUND_PENDING,
    PARTIALLY_REFUNDED,
    REFUNDED,

    VOID_PENDING,
    VOIDED,

    FAILED,
    CANCELLED,

    UNKNOWN,
    NEEDS_INVESTIGATION
}
```

Untuk operation-level status:

```java id="fig3s9"
public enum PaymentOperationStatus {
    REQUESTED,
    SENT_TO_PROVIDER,
    PROVIDER_ACCEPTED,
    SUCCEEDED,
    FAILED,
    TIMEOUT,
    UNKNOWN,
    RETRY_SCHEDULED,
    NEEDS_INVESTIGATION
}
```

Rule:

```text id="woj0i3"
Payment status menunjukkan state aggregate.
Operation status menunjukkan state attempt/call/recovery.
```

Contoh:

```text id="dqo3xr"
payment.status = UNKNOWN
payment_attempt.status = TIMEOUT
payment_recovery.status = SCHEDULED
```

---

# 6. Error Mapping Table

## 6.1 Internal API Error Codes

| Error Code | HTTP | Category | Retry? | Notes |
|---|---:|---|---:|---|
| `PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND` | 404 | validation/domain | No | Payment ID tidak ada. |
| `PAYMENT_INVALID_STATE` | 409 | invalid state | No | Operation tidak valid untuk current state. |
| `PAYMENT_AMOUNT_EXCEEDS_AUTHORIZED` | 422 | business rule | No | Capture > authorized. |
| `PAYMENT_REFUND_EXCEEDS_CAPTURED` | 422 | business rule | No | Refund > captured. |
| `PAYMENT_CURRENCY_MISMATCH` | 422 | validation | No | Currency beda. |
| `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED` | 400 | validation | No | Mutating endpoint tanpa key. |
| `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_CONFLICT` | 409 | idempotency | No | Key sama, payload beda. |
| `PAYMENT_PROVIDER_DECLINED` | 402/422 | provider decline | No | Business decline. |
| `PAYMENT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT` | 202/504 | financial uncertainty | Recovery | Jangan mark failed. |
| `PAYMENT_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` | 503 | technical | Yes | Retry/backoff. |
| `PAYMENT_PROVIDER_RATE_LIMITED` | 429/503 | technical | Yes delayed | Backoff. |
| `PAYMENT_PROVIDER_INVALID_REQUEST` | 502/500 | mapping/config | No | Internal bug/config. |
| `PAYMENT_STATUS_UNKNOWN` | 202/409 | uncertainty | Recovery | Query provider. |
| `PAYMENT_NEEDS_INVESTIGATION` | 409 | investigation | Manual | Ambiguous/contradictory. |
| `PAYMENT_CONCURRENT_MODIFICATION` | 409 | concurrency | Maybe | Reload/retry. |

Note:

```text id="shfm8e"
HTTP status bukan satu-satunya contract.
errorCode adalah contract utama untuk consumer logic.
```

---

# 7. Problem Details Response

Gunakan RFC 9457 style response.

```json id="278zq4"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/payment-provider-timeout",
  "title": "Payment provider timeout",
  "status": 202,
  "detail": "The provider result is unknown. The payment has been moved to pending confirmation and will be recovered asynchronously.",
  "instance": "/payments/6e3a9e6f-2505-4d58-a8a4-9f552c061d4f",
  "errorCode": "PAYMENT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT",
  "paymentId": "6e3a9e6f-2505-4d58-a8a4-9f552c061d4f",
  "paymentStatus": "UNKNOWN",
  "retryable": false,
  "recoveryScheduled": true,
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

RFC 9457 mendefinisikan problem detail sebagai format machine-readable untuk error HTTP API sehingga API tidak perlu membuat format error baru untuk setiap error. ([RFC Editor](https://www.rfc-editor.org/info/rfc9457/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Java record:

```java id="pbwjtc"
package com.company.payment.api.error;

import java.util.Map;

public record ProblemDetails(
        String type,
        String title,
        int status,
        String detail,
        String instance,
        String errorCode,
        String traceId,
        Map<String, Object> context
) {}
```

Exception mapper:

```java id="nvovmn"
package com.company.payment.api.error;

import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;

public class PaymentExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<PaymentException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(PaymentException exception) {
        ProblemDetails problem = new ProblemDetails(
                exception.type(),
                exception.title(),
                exception.httpStatus(),
                exception.getMessage(),
                exception.instance(),
                exception.errorCode(),
                exception.traceId(),
                exception.context()
        );

        return Response.status(exception.httpStatus())
                .type("application/problem+json")
                .entity(problem)
                .build();
    }
}
```

# 8. Provider Exception Hierarchy

```java id="ph44xh"
package com.company.payment.gateway.error;

public abstract class GatewayException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String provider;
    private final String providerErrorCode;
    private final String providerRequestId;
    private final boolean retryable;
    private final boolean financialStateUnknown;

    protected GatewayException(
            String message,
            String provider,
            String providerErrorCode,
            String providerRequestId,
            boolean retryable,
            boolean financialStateUnknown
    ) {
        super(message);
        this.provider = provider;
        this.providerErrorCode = providerErrorCode;
        this.providerRequestId = providerRequestId;
        this.retryable = retryable;
        this.financialStateUnknown = financialStateUnknown;
    }

    public String provider() {
        return provider;
    }

    public String providerErrorCode() {
        return providerErrorCode;
    }

    public String providerRequestId() {
        return providerRequestId;
    }

    public boolean retryable() {
        return retryable;
    }

    public boolean financialStateUnknown() {
        return financialStateUnknown;
    }
}
```

Specific exceptions:


# 9. Timeout Handling Pattern

## 9.1 Bad Pattern

```java id="x1spug"
try {
    gateway.capture(request);
} catch (Exception e) {
    paymentRepository.markFailed(paymentId);
}
```

Kenapa salah?

```text id="34lnu7"
Karena exception bisa terjadi setelah provider menerima request.
Jika capture sebenarnya sukses, kita baru saja menandai payment FAILED padahal customer sudah ter-charge.
```

## 9.2 Correct Pattern

```java id="rcp9il"
try {
    GatewayCaptureResult result = gateway.capture(request);
    applyCaptureResult(paymentId, result);

} catch (GatewayTimeoutException e) {
    markCaptureUnknownAndScheduleRecovery(paymentId, captureId, e);

} catch (GatewayUnavailableException e) {
    markCaptureUnknownOrRetrySafely(paymentId, captureId, e);

} catch (GatewayDeclinedException e) {
    markCaptureFailed(paymentId, captureId, e);

} catch (GatewayInvalidRequestException e) {
    markAttemptFailedAndCreateIncident(paymentId, captureId, e);
}
```

## 9.3 Application Service Example

```java id="fecqif"
public PaymentResponse capturePayment(CapturePaymentCommand command) {
    CapturePreparation preparation = transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
        PaymentRecord payment = paymentRepository.findByIdForUpdate(command.paymentId());

        validateCaptureAllowed(payment, command);

        CaptureRecord capture = captureRepository.insertCaptureRequested(
                command.captureId(),
                payment,
                command
        );

        paymentRepository.markCapturePending(payment.paymentId(), payment.version());

        paymentEventRepository.insertPaymentEvent(
                payment.paymentId(),
                "CAPTURE_REQUESTED",
                payment.status(),
                "CAPTURE_PENDING",
                command.correlationId()
        );

        return new CapturePreparation(payment, capture);
    });

    GatewayCaptureRequest gatewayRequest =
            gatewayRequestFactory.createCaptureRequest(
                    preparation.payment(),
                    preparation.capture(),
                    command
            );

    try {
        GatewayCaptureResult result =
                gatewayRegistry.getRequired(preparation.payment().provider())
                        .capture(gatewayRequest);

        return captureResultApplier.applySuccessOrPending(
                preparation,
                result,
                command.correlationId()
        );

    } catch (GatewayTimeoutException e) {
        return transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
            captureRepository.markUnknown(preparation.capture().captureId(), e);
            paymentRepository.markUnknown(preparation.payment().paymentId());
            recoveryRepository.scheduleRecovery(
                    PaymentRecoveryRequest.forCapture(
                            preparation.payment().paymentId(),
                            preparation.capture().captureId(),
                            e.provider(),
                            e.providerRequestId()
                    )
            );

            paymentEventRepository.insertPaymentEvent(
                    preparation.payment().paymentId(),
                    "CAPTURE_TIMEOUT_UNKNOWN",
                    "CAPTURE_PENDING",
                    "UNKNOWN",
                    command.correlationId()
            );

            outboxRepository.insertPaymentPendingConfirmation(
                    preparation.payment().paymentId(),
                    "CAPTURE_TIMEOUT",
                    command.correlationId()
            );

            return paymentResponseMapper.pendingConfirmation(
                    preparation.payment().paymentId(),
                    command.correlationId()
            );
        });
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Safe Retry Rules

Provider retry harus mengikuti aturan ini:

```text id="fvypw1"
1. Mutating operation harus punya idempotency key.
2. Retry mutating operation harus memakai key yang sama.
3. Retry tidak boleh mengganti amount/currency/provider reference.
4. Retry tidak boleh pindah provider setelah request mungkin terkirim.
5. Jika result unknown, prioritaskan status query/wait webhook.
6. Jika provider idempotency retention expired, escalate investigation.
```

PayPal secara eksplisit menyatakan idempotent calls dapat diretry saat network timeout atau HTTP 5xx selama server menyimpan ID; Adyen menyatakan idempotency membantu menghindari unwanted duplication saat failure dan retry; Stripe mendukung idempotency untuk safely retry write requests. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/api/rest/reference/idempotency/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 11. Provider Idempotency Key Factory

Internal idempotency key jangan selalu dikirim mentah ke provider. Buat derived key yang stabil dan ter-scope.

```java id="wnsuwy"
package com.company.payment.idempotency;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.util.HexFormat;

public class ProviderIdempotencyKeyFactory {

    public String create(
            String environment,
            String provider,
            String operationType,
            String internalIdempotencyKey
    ) {
        String raw = environment + ":" + provider + ":" + operationType + ":" + internalIdempotencyKey;
        return sha256(raw).substring(0, 48);
    }

    private String sha256(String value) {
        try {
            MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
            byte[] hash = digest.digest(value.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            return HexFormat.of().formatHex(hash);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to build provider idempotency key", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Example:

```text id="uw2h8p"
internal idempotency key:
  capture:payment-123:shipment-1

provider idempotency key:
  sha256(PROD:ADYEN:CAPTURE:capture:payment-123:shipment-1)
```

Header mapping:

| Provider | Header |
|---|---|
| Stripe | `Idempotency-Key` |
| Adyen | `idempotency-key` |
| PayPal | `PayPal-Request-Id` |

PayPal documents `PayPal-Request-Id` as a unique user-generated ID for REST API POST calls to enforce idempotency; Adyen and Stripe document provider-specific idempotency headers/behavior for safe retries. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/api/rest/requests/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 12. Recovery Database Schema

## 12.1 `payment_recovery_requests`

```sql id="50nx6s"
CREATE TABLE payment_recovery_requests (
    recovery_request_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    payment_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(payment_id),
    capture_id UUID,
    refund_id UUID REFERENCES refunds(refund_id),
    dispute_id UUID,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    recovery_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,

    provider_payment_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_authorization_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_capture_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_refund_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_request_id VARCHAR(200),

    original_operation_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    original_idempotency_key VARCHAR(200),
    provider_idempotency_key VARCHAR(200),

    status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    attempt_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    max_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 10,

    next_attempt_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    locked_until TIMESTAMPTZ,

    last_error_code VARCHAR(100),
    last_error_message TEXT,

    resolved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    resolution_status VARCHAR(80),
    resolution_detail TEXT,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_recovery_type
        CHECK (recovery_type IN (
            'AUTHORIZE_STATUS_QUERY',
            'CAPTURE_STATUS_QUERY',
            'REFUND_STATUS_QUERY',
            'VOID_STATUS_QUERY',
            'PAYMENT_STATUS_QUERY',
            'WEBHOOK_WAIT',
            'RECONCILIATION_WAIT',
            'MANUAL_INVESTIGATION'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_recovery_status
        CHECK (status IN (
            'SCHEDULED',
            'PROCESSING',
            'RETRY_WAIT',
            'RESOLVED',
            'FAILED',
            'NEEDS_INVESTIGATION',
            'CANCELLED'
        ))
);
```

Indexes:

```sql id="jqk0i5"
CREATE INDEX ix_payment_recovery_due
```

```sql id="u7w6lj"
ON payment_recovery_requests(payment_id, original_operation_type, original_idempotency_key)
WHERE status IN ('SCHEDULED', 'PROCESSING', 'RETRY_WAIT');
```

```sql id="z81ife"
        )),
```
