# 13. Recovery Job Pattern

## 13.1 Claim Due Recovery Requests

```sql id="b8fo8r"
SELECT *
FROM payment_recovery_requests
WHERE status IN ('SCHEDULED', 'RETRY_WAIT')
  AND next_attempt_at <= now()
ORDER BY next_attempt_at ASC, created_at ASC
LIMIT #{limit}
FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;
```

Mark processing:

```sql id="ko4mrp"
UPDATE payment_recovery_requests
SET status = 'PROCESSING',
    attempt_count = attempt_count + 1,
    locked_until = now() + interval '5 minutes',
    updated_at = now()
WHERE recovery_request_id = #{recoveryRequestId}
  AND status IN ('SCHEDULED', 'RETRY_WAIT');
```

## 13.2 Recovery Worker Skeleton

```java id="wkeejp"
package com.company.payment.recovery;

import java.util.List;

public class PaymentRecoveryWorker {

    private final PaymentRecoveryRepository recoveryRepository;
    private final PaymentRecoveryProcessor recoveryProcessor;
    private final TransactionTemplate transactionTemplate;

    public void processBatch(int limit) {
        List<PaymentRecoveryRequest> requests =
                transactionTemplate.execute(() -> recoveryRepository.claimDue(limit));

        for (PaymentRecoveryRequest request : requests) {
            processSingle(request);
        }
    }

    private void processSingle(PaymentRecoveryRequest request) {
        try {
            RecoveryResult result = recoveryProcessor.process(request);

            transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                recoveryRepository.markResolved(
                        request.recoveryRequestId(),
                        result.resolutionStatus(),
                        result.resolutionDetail()
                );
                return null;
            });

        } catch (RecoverableRecoveryException e) {
            transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                recoveryRepository.markRetryWait(
                        request.recoveryRequestId(),
                        e.errorCode(),
                        e.getMessage(),
                        e.nextAttemptAt()
                );
                return null;
            });

        } catch (NeedsInvestigationException e) {
            transactionTemplate.execute(() -> {
                recoveryRepository.markNeedsInvestigation(
                        request.recoveryRequestId(),
                        e.errorCode(),
                        e.getMessage()
                );

                investigationRepository.openIfAbsent(
                        e.toInvestigationCase()
                );

                return null;
            });
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 14. Recovery Processor

```java id="wmcrmv"
package com.company.payment.recovery;

public class PaymentRecoveryProcessor {

    private final PaymentGatewayRegistry gatewayRegistry;
    private final PaymentRepository paymentRepository;
    private final CaptureRepository captureRepository;
    private final RefundRepository refundRepository;
    private final LedgerPostingService ledgerPostingService;
    private final OutboxRepository outboxRepository;
    private final PaymentEventRepository paymentEventRepository;

    public RecoveryResult process(PaymentRecoveryRequest request) {
        return switch (request.recoveryType()) {
            case "CAPTURE_STATUS_QUERY" -> recoverCapture(request);
            case "REFUND_STATUS_QUERY" -> recoverRefund(request);
            case "AUTHORIZE_STATUS_QUERY" -> recoverAuthorization(request);
            case "VOID_STATUS_QUERY" -> recoverVoid(request);
            case "PAYMENT_STATUS_QUERY" -> recoverPayment(request);
            default -> throw new NeedsInvestigationException(
                    "UNSUPPORTED_RECOVERY_TYPE",
                    "Unsupported recovery type: " + request.recoveryType(),
                    request
            );
        };
    }

    private RecoveryResult recoverCapture(PaymentRecoveryRequest request) {
        PaymentGatewayClient gateway =
                gatewayRegistry.getRequired(request.provider());

        GatewayPaymentStatusResult status =
                gateway.getPaymentStatus(new GatewayPaymentStatusRequest(
                        request.paymentId(),
                        request.providerPaymentId(),
                        request.providerCaptureId(),
                        request.providerRequestId()
                ));

        if (status.status() == GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED) {
            applyCaptureRecovered(request, status);
            return RecoveryResult.resolved("CAPTURED", "Provider confirmed capture");
        }

        if (status.status() == GatewayPaymentStatus.FAILED) {
            applyCaptureFailedRecovered(request, status);
            return RecoveryResult.resolved("FAILED", "Provider confirmed capture failed");
        }

        if (status.status() == GatewayPaymentStatus.AUTHORIZATION_PENDING
                || status.status() == GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURE_PENDING
                || status.status() == GatewayPaymentStatus.UNKNOWN) {
            throw RecoverableRecoveryException.retryLater(
                    "PROVIDER_STATUS_STILL_PENDING",
                    "Provider status is still pending/unknown",
                    RecoveryBackoff.next(request.attemptCount())
            );
        }

        throw new NeedsInvestigationException(
                "UNEXPECTED_PROVIDER_STATUS",
                "Unexpected provider status during capture recovery: " + status.status(),
                request
        );
    }

    private void applyCaptureRecovered(
            PaymentRecoveryRequest request,
            GatewayPaymentStatusResult status
    ) {
        PaymentRecord payment = paymentRepository.findByIdForUpdate(request.paymentId());

        int updated = paymentRepository.addCapturedAmount(
                request.paymentId(),
                status.capturedAmountMinor(),
                status.currency(),
                payment.version()
        );

        if (updated != 1) {
            throw new NeedsInvestigationException(
                    "CAPTURE_RECOVERY_UPDATE_FAILED",
                    "Could not apply recovered capture due to state/version/amount guard",
                    request
            );
        }

        captureRepository.markRecoveredSucceeded(
                request.captureId(),
                status.providerCaptureId(),
                status.capturedAmountMinor(),
                status.currency()
        );

        ledgerPostingService.postPaymentCaptured(
                PaymentCapturedLedgerCommand.fromRecovery(request, status)
        );

        paymentEventRepository.insertPaymentEvent(
                request.paymentId(),
                "CAPTURE_RECOVERED_SUCCEEDED",
                payment.status(),
                "CAPTURED",
                "recovery-job"
        );

        outboxRepository.insertPaymentCaptured(
                request.paymentId(),
                request.captureId(),
                "recovery-job"
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 15. Recovery Result Matrix

| Original Operation | Unknown Cause | First Recovery Action | If Provider Success | If Provider Failed | If Still Unknown |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Authorize | timeout | query payment/auth status | `AUTHORIZED` / `CAPTURED` | `FAILED` | retry then investigation |
| Capture | timeout | query payment/capture status | `CAPTURED` | `CAPTURE_FAILED` | retry then investigation |
| Refund | timeout | query refund status | `REFUND_SUCCEEDED` | `REFUND_FAILED` | retry then investigation |
| Void | timeout | query auth/void status | `VOIDED` | `VOID_FAILED` | retry then investigation |
| Webhook missing | no event | query provider + reconciliation wait | apply final | apply final | wait/report/investigate |
| Reconciliation missing | report absent | wait settlement SLA | reconcile | mismatch | investigation |

---

# 16. Retry Policy

```java id="0z5ehm"
package com.company.payment.recovery;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;

public class RecoveryBackoff {

    public static Instant next(int attemptCount) {
        Duration delay = switch (attemptCount) {
            case 0, 1 -> Duration.ofMinutes(1);
            case 2 -> Duration.ofMinutes(5);
            case 3 -> Duration.ofMinutes(15);
            case 4 -> Duration.ofMinutes(30);
            case 5 -> Duration.ofHours(1);
            case 6 -> Duration.ofHours(3);
            default -> Duration.ofHours(6);
        };

        return Instant.now().plus(delay);
    }

    public static boolean shouldEscalate(int attemptCount) {
        return attemptCount >= 10;
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="0de719"
Recovery retry adalah status query retry.
Bukan selalu original mutating operation retry.
```

---

# 17. When to Retry Original Mutating Request

Retry original mutating request hanya aman jika:

```text id="aehhqa"
1. Provider supports idempotency for that operation.
2. Same provider idempotency key is available.
3. Same payload is guaranteed.
4. Same provider is used.
5. Provider retention window for idempotency key is still valid.
6. There is no provider reference indicating operation already succeeded differently.
```

Prefer:

```text id="eq1c8o"
query status first
```

Before:

```text id="87jdfk"
send original mutating request again
```

Practical decision table:

| Condition | Retry Original Call? | Better Action |
|---|---:|---|
| Connection failed before bytes sent | Maybe | Retry same idempotency key. |
| Timeout after request sent | Not first | Query status/wait webhook. |
| HTTP 500 with provider request id | Usually query/retry same key | Provider-specific. |
| HTTP 429 | Delayed retry same key | Respect backoff. |
| Provider says duplicate/idempotency conflict | No | Fetch original result/status. |
| Idempotency key expired | No blindly | Investigation. |
| Payload changed | No | Reject conflict. |
| Provider changed | No | Stick original provider. |

---

# 18. Circuit Breaker Interaction

Circuit breaker hanya boleh memengaruhi **pre-call decision**.

```text id="wbp5yb"
Before sending request:
  circuit breaker open -> do not call provider, route/fail before side effect.

After request sent and timeout:
  circuit breaker state does not prove payment failed.
  mark UNKNOWN and recover.
```

Bad:

```text id="ko28w9"
Provider call timeout.
Circuit breaker opens.
System marks all pending operations failed.
```

Correct:

```text id="6y3x0d"
Provider call timeout.
Mark affected operation UNKNOWN.
Stop new calls while circuit open.
Recover existing unknown operations via query/webhook/reconciliation.
```

---

# 19. Provider Error Mapper Example

```java id="57fi6t"
package com.company.payment.gateway.error;

public interface GatewayErrorMapper {

    GatewayException toException(GatewayHttpResponse response);

    GatewayException toNetworkException(Exception exception, String provider);
}
```

Stripe-like mapper:

```java id="vj179b"
public class StripeErrorMapper implements GatewayErrorMapper {

    @Override
    public GatewayException toException(GatewayHttpResponse response) {
        int status = response.statusCode();

        String providerRequestId = response.headers().get("Request-Id");
        String errorCode = extractErrorCode(response.body());
        String message = extractMessage(response.body());

        if (status == 402) {
            return new GatewayDeclinedException(
                    "STRIPE",
                    errorCode,
                    providerRequestId,
                    message
            );
        }

        if (status == 400 || status == 404) {
            return new GatewayInvalidRequestException(
                    "STRIPE",
                    errorCode,
                    providerRequestId,
                    message
            );
        }

        if (status == 401 || status == 403) {
            return new GatewayInvalidRequestException(
                    "STRIPE",
                    "AUTHENTICATION_OR_AUTHORIZATION_ERROR",
                    providerRequestId,
                    message
            );
        }

        if (status == 409 || status == 429 || status >= 500) {
            return new GatewayUnavailableException(
                    "STRIPE",
                    errorCode,
                    providerRequestId
            );
        }

        return new GatewayUnknownResponseException(
                "STRIPE",
                errorCode,
                providerRequestId,
                "Unexpected provider response: " + status
        );
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayException toNetworkException(Exception exception, String provider) {
        if (exception instanceof java.net.SocketTimeoutException) {
            return new GatewayTimeoutException(provider, null);
        }

        return new GatewayUnavailableException(
                provider,
                "NETWORK_ERROR",
                null
        );
    }
}
```

Important:

```text id="f1wtrv"
HTTP 409/429/5xx treatment must be provider-specific.
Do not blindly generalize all providers.
```

---

# 20. API Response Semantics for Unknown State

When operation result unknown, API can return:

```http id="q4ev8h"
202 Accepted
```

With body:

```json id="0aqeca"
{
  "paymentId": "6e3a9e6f-2505-4d58-a8a4-9f552c061d4f",
  "status": "UNKNOWN",
  "operationStatus": "PENDING_CONFIRMATION",
  "message": "The provider result is unknown. The system will recover using provider status query, webhook, or reconciliation.",
  "recovery": {
    "scheduled": true,
    "nextAttemptAt": "2026-07-14T19:05:00Z"
  },
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

Why not `500`?

```text id="wlsz7x"
Because the payment service accepted the command and persisted a durable recovery plan.
The provider result is unknown, but internal service did not simply fail.
```

When to use `504`?

```text id="djy93k"
If the service could not persist operation/recovery state,
or API contract says synchronous operation failed before durable acceptance.
```

Practical recommendation:

```text id="79ut4e"
For payment command:
if internal DB persisted operation + recovery scheduled,
return 202 with UNKNOWN/PENDING_CONFIRMATION.
```

---

# 21. Handling Provider `Not Found` During Recovery

Provider status query returns not found.

Interpretation depends on provider and reference.

| Scenario | Meaning | Action |
|---|---|---|
| Query by provider payment ID, not found | Request may not have reached provider. | If safe, retry original idempotent request or investigate. |
| Query by provider request ID, not found | Provider may not expose request lookup. | Retry/wait. |
| Query by idempotency key, not supported | Cannot conclude. | Use payment ref/webhook/reconciliation. |
| Internal has provider payment ID but provider says not found | Serious mismatch. | Needs investigation. |
| Provider environment mismatch | Config issue. | Incident. |

Rule:

```text id="a477b6"
Provider not found does not automatically mean internal failed.
Check environment, provider account, reference type, and timing.
```

---

# 22. Handling Contradictory Events

Example:

```text id="7pjnm0"
Internal payment status = FAILED
Webhook says CAPTURED
```

Do not blindly overwrite if it violates model.

Process:

```text id="go8k1f"
1. Insert webhook/audit event.
2. Check if transition is allowed.
3. If financial success after failed:
   - mark NEEDS_INVESTIGATION
   - optionally move to CAPTURED only through recovery policy
   - post ledger if provider evidence is trusted and amount valid
4. Alert ops/finance.
```

State guard table:

| Current | Incoming | Action |
|---|---|---|
| `UNKNOWN` | `CAPTURED` | Apply captured if amount valid. |
| `CAPTURE_PENDING` | `CAPTURED` | Apply captured. |
| `FAILED` | `CAPTURED` | Investigation / controlled recovery. |
| `CAPTURED` | `FAILED` | Ignore late failure / investigation. |
| `REFUNDED` | `CAPTURED` | Ignore/investigate. |
| `VOIDED` | `CAPTURED` | Critical investigation. |

---

# 23. Manual Investigation Case Workflow

Create case when:

```text id="zkwnpp"
1. Unknown state persists beyond max attempts.
2. Provider reports success but internal state is failed/cancelled.
3. Provider reports amount/currency mismatch.
4. Provider reference not found after SLA.
5. Duplicate provider references appear.
6. Recovery query returns contradictory statuses.
7. Idempotency retention expired before status resolved.
```

Case severity:

| Severity | Example |
|---|---|
| LOW | Status pending under normal SLA. |
| MEDIUM | Refund pending longer than expected. |
| HIGH | Capture unknown for high-value order. |
| CRITICAL | Possible double charge / internal failed but provider captured. |

Admin actions:

```text id="sr93yl"
- Query provider manually
- Attach provider evidence
- Mark recovered as captured/refunded/failed
- Create ledger adjustment/reversal if approved
- Retry correlation/outbox/webhook processing
- Close case with resolution note
```

# 25. MyBatis Mapper Examples

## 25.1 Insert Recovery Request

```xml id="7w2xfn"
<insert id="insertRecoveryRequest">
    INSERT INTO payment_recovery_requests (
        recovery_request_id,
        payment_id,
        capture_id,
        refund_id,
        provider,
        recovery_type,
        provider_payment_id,
        provider_authorization_id,
        provider_capture_id,
        provider_refund_id,
        provider_request_id,
        original_operation_type,
        original_idempotency_key,
        provider_idempotency_key,
        status,
        next_attempt_at,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    ) VALUES (
        #{recoveryRequestId},
        #{paymentId},
        #{captureId},
        #{refundId},
        #{provider},
        #{recoveryType},
        #{providerPaymentId},
        #{providerAuthorizationId},
        #{providerCaptureId},
        #{providerRefundId},
        #{providerRequestId},
        #{originalOperationType},
        #{originalIdempotencyKey},
        #{providerIdempotencyKey},
        'SCHEDULED',
        #{nextAttemptAt},
        now(),
        now()
    )
    ON CONFLICT ON CONSTRAINT ux_payment_recovery_active_operation
    DO NOTHING
</insert>
```

PostgreSQL syntax note: partial unique indexes are not constraints, so in real PostgreSQL use explicit `ON CONFLICT` columns only if supported by matching unique constraint/index inference; otherwise perform guarded insert with `WHERE NOT EXISTS`.

Safer pattern:

```xml id="95h2l6"
<insert id="insertRecoveryRequestIfAbsent">
    INSERT INTO payment_recovery_requests (
        recovery_request_id,
        payment_id,
        capture_id,
        refund_id,
        provider,
        recovery_type,
        provider_payment_id,
        provider_capture_id,
        provider_refund_id,
        provider_request_id,
        original_operation_type,
        original_idempotency_key,
        provider_idempotency_key,
        status,
        next_attempt_at,
        created_at,
        updated_at
    )
    SELECT
        #{recoveryRequestId},
        #{paymentId},
        #{captureId},
        #{refundId},
        #{provider},
        #{recoveryType},
        #{providerPaymentId},
        #{providerCaptureId},
        #{providerRefundId},
        #{providerRequestId},
        #{originalOperationType},
        #{originalIdempotencyKey},
        #{providerIdempotencyKey},
        'SCHEDULED',
        #{nextAttemptAt},
        now(),
        now()
    WHERE NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT 1
        FROM payment_recovery_requests
        WHERE payment_id = #{paymentId}
          AND original_operation_type = #{originalOperationType}
          AND original_idempotency_key = #{originalIdempotencyKey}
          AND status IN ('SCHEDULED', 'PROCESSING', 'RETRY_WAIT')
    )
</insert>
```

## 25.2 Claim Recovery Batch

```xml id="nl7toe"
<select id="claimDueRecoveryRequests" resultMap="PaymentRecoveryRequestResultMap">
    SELECT
        recovery_request_id,
        payment_id,
        capture_id,
        refund_id,
        provider,
        recovery_type,
        provider_payment_id,
        provider_capture_id,
        provider_refund_id,
        provider_request_id,
        original_operation_type,
        original_idempotency_key,
        provider_idempotency_key,
        status,
        attempt_count,
        max_attempts,
        next_attempt_at,
        locked_until
    FROM payment_recovery_requests
    WHERE status IN ('SCHEDULED', 'RETRY_WAIT')
      AND next_attempt_at <= now()
    ORDER BY next_attempt_at ASC, created_at ASC
    LIMIT #{limit}
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
</select>
```

## 25.3 Mark Retry

```xml id="tpsk6a"
<update id="markRetryWait">
    UPDATE payment_recovery_requests
    SET status = 'RETRY_WAIT',
        next_attempt_at = #{nextAttemptAt},
        locked_until = NULL,
        last_error_code = #{errorCode},
        last_error_message = #{errorMessage},
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE recovery_request_id = #{recoveryRequestId}
      AND status = 'PROCESSING'
</update>
```

## 25.4 Mark Resolved

```xml id="lc23mt"
<update id="markResolved">
    UPDATE payment_recovery_requests
    SET status = 'RESOLVED',
        resolved_at = now(),
        resolution_status = #{resolutionStatus},
        resolution_detail = #{resolutionDetail},
        locked_until = NULL,
        updated_at = now()
    WHERE recovery_request_id = #{recoveryRequestId}
      AND status = 'PROCESSING'
</update>
```

# 27. Incident Playbook

## 27.1 Provider Timeout Spike

```text id="ief07c"
Symptoms:
  - payment.provider.timeout.total spike
  - payment.unknown.total rising
  - outbox PaymentPendingConfirmation rising

Immediate action:
  1. Stop unsafe fallback to other providers.
  2. Check provider status dashboard.
  3. Confirm circuit breaker pre-call behavior.
  4. Scale recovery workers carefully.
  5. Monitor duplicate provider operations.
  6. Communicate pending payment status to Order/Support.
```

## 27.2 Unknown Payments Older Than SLA

```text id="k0rtv2"
Action:
  1. Query provider by payment/capture/refund refs.
  2. Check webhook inbox for unprocessed events.
  3. Check outbox publishing lag.
  4. Check reconciliation/provider report.
  5. Open investigation case if still ambiguous.
```

## 27.3 Internal Failed but Provider Captured

```text id="0idj4c"
Severity:
  CRITICAL

Action:
  1. Stop automatic refund/void for that payment.
  2. Lock payment investigation.
  3. Verify provider dashboard/report.
  4. If captured is confirmed:
       - apply controlled recovery to CAPTURED
       - post ledger if absent
       - publish PaymentCaptured
       - notify order/billing/support
  5. Create root cause ticket.
```

## 27.4 Possible Double Capture

```text id="gn7ypo"
Action:
  1. Search provider captures by payment/order/idempotency keys.
  2. Compare internal payment_captures.
  3. Check duplicate idempotency keys/payload mismatch.
  4. If duplicate provider capture confirmed:
       - create refund/adjustment workflow
       - notify support
       - reconcile ledger
```

---

# 28. Testing Strategy

## 28.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Provider timeout during capture | Payment `UNKNOWN`, recovery scheduled. |
| Provider decline during authorize | Payment `FAILED`. |
| Provider invalid request | Attempt failed, incident/investigation. |
| Provider 5xx | Retry/backoff or unknown based on operation phase. |
| Same idempotency key same payload | Cached response. |
| Same idempotency key different payload | Conflict. |
| Recovery query returns captured | Payment `CAPTURED`, ledger/outbox once. |
| Recovery query returns failed | Capture failed, outbox failure. |
| Recovery query still pending | retry scheduled. |
| Max recovery attempts exceeded | investigation case opened. |
| Contradictory captured after failed | needs investigation / controlled recovery. |
| Duplicate recovery worker run | no duplicate ledger/outbox. |

## 28.2 Integration Tests

```text id="t2zezl"
1. Create AUTHORIZED payment.
2. Capture call to mock provider times out.
3. Verify payment UNKNOWN.
4. Verify payment_recovery_requests row.
5. Recovery query mock provider returns CAPTURED.
6. Verify payment CAPTURED.
7. Verify ledger PAYMENT_CAPTURED once.
8. Verify outbox PaymentCaptured once.
9. Re-run recovery.
10. Verify no duplicate ledger/outbox.
```

## 28.3 Chaos Tests

```text id="luu8rc"
1. Provider timeout after receiving request.
2. Provider returns 500 then later webhook success.
3. Provider webhook arrives before API response.
4. Recovery worker crashes after applying ledger but before mark resolved.
5. Outbox publisher delayed.
6. DB deadlock during state transition.
7. Duplicate capture command with same idempotency key.
8. Duplicate capture command with different idempotency key.
```

---

# 29. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Timeout → FAILED | Bisa salah karena provider mungkin sukses. | `UNKNOWN` + recovery. |
| Retry mutating call dengan key baru | Bisa double charge/refund. | Same provider idempotency key. |
| Fallback provider setelah timeout | Bisa double charge di provider A dan B. | Stick provider, query status. |
| Semua provider error jadi 500 | Consumer tidak tahu action. | Stable errorCode + Problem Details. |
| Tidak simpan provider request ID | Support/recovery sulit. | Store in attempt/recovery. |
| Tidak ada recovery job | UNKNOWN menumpuk. | Scheduled recovery. |
| Recovery langsung update tanpa guard | State/ledger rusak. | Domain + SQL guards. |
| Duplicate recovery double ledger | Finance dobel. | unique ledger reference. |
| Unknown state tidak terlihat ops | Incident terlambat. | metrics + investigation cases. |
| Manual fix tanpa audit | Compliance/audit gagal. | investigation + adjustment workflow. |

---

# 30. Implementation Checklist

## Error Contract

- [ ] `PaymentErrorCategory`.
- [ ] Stable internal `errorCode`.
- [ ] RFC 9457-style Problem Details.
- [ ] Exception mapper.
- [ ] Provider error mapper per PSP.
- [ ] Retryable vs non-retryable flag.
- [ ] `financialStateUnknown` flag.

## Timeout Handling

- [ ] Timeout does not mark payment failed.
- [ ] Unknown/pending-confirmation status.
- [ ] Recovery request inserted.
- [ ] Payment event inserted.
- [ ] Outbox pending confirmation inserted.
- [ ] Consumer/order gets clear pending status.

## Retry Safety

- [ ] Idempotency key required for mutating API.
- [ ] Provider idempotency key derived and stored.
- [ ] Same payload enforced.
- [ ] Same provider enforced.
- [ ] No fallback after post-call timeout.
- [ ] Retry original call only if safe.

## Recovery

- [ ] `payment_recovery_requests`.
- [ ] Recovery worker with backoff.
- [ ] Status query adapters.
- [ ] Webhook + reconciliation as recovery sources.
- [ ] Max attempts escalation.
- [ ] `payment_investigation_cases`.

## Financial Integrity

- [ ] Recovery applies state through domain guards.
- [ ] Ledger insert idempotent.
- [ ] Outbox insert idempotent.
- [ ] Contradictory event handling.
- [ ] Manual resolution audit.

## Operations

- [ ] Unknown dashboard.
- [ ] Recovery lag dashboard.
- [ ] Provider timeout dashboard.
- [ ] Investigation queue.
- [ ] Incident playbooks.
- [ ] Admin recovery APIs.

Target skenario:

```text id="mfsieb"
1. Authorize timeout -> payment UNKNOWN + recovery scheduled.
2. Recovery query returns AUTHORIZED -> payment AUTHORIZED.
3. Capture timeout -> payment UNKNOWN + capture recovery.
4. Recovery query returns CAPTURED -> payment CAPTURED + ledger/outbox once.
5. Refund timeout -> refund UNKNOWN + recovery.
6. Recovery query returns refund SUCCEEDED -> refund SUCCEEDED + payment PARTIALLY_REFUNDED.
7. Provider declined -> payment FAILED, no recovery.
8. Provider invalid request -> incident/investigation.
9. Same idempotency key different payload -> 409.
10. Provider captured after internal failed -> investigation case.
11. Recovery attempts exceed max -> NEEDS_INVESTIGATION.
12. Manual resolution applies controlled correction with audit.
```

# Ringkasan

Error handling payment harus financial-aware.

Prinsip utama:

```text id="6w73xm"
1. Timeout bukan failed.
2. 5xx bukan failed.
3. Unknown state harus dimodelkan eksplisit.
4. Mutating retry harus memakai idempotency key yang sama.
5. Jangan fallback provider setelah request mungkin terkirim.
6. Provider error harus dimapping ke taxonomy internal.
7. API error contract harus stabil dan machine-readable.
8. Recovery harus memakai status query, webhook, dan reconciliation.
9. Ledger/outbox saat recovery harus idempotent.
10. Unknown yang tidak resolve harus masuk manual investigation.
```

Dengan desain ini, payment service tidak panik saat provider timeout. Ia menyimpan state yang jujur:

```text id="d3ew6u"
Saya belum tahu hasil finansialnya.
Saya sudah jadwalkan recovery.
Saya tidak akan menandai failed/success tanpa bukti.
```

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 16/18 - Security, Tokenization, Secrets, PCI Boundary, dan Sensitive Data Handling

Seri ini fokus pada **security boundary** payment service: bagaimana service Java Jersey/JAX-RS tidak berubah menjadi sistem penyimpan data kartu, tidak membocorkan token/secret, tetap aman terhadap webhook spoofing, dan punya desain operasional yang sesuai untuk lingkungan **PostgreSQL, Docker, Kubernetes, On-Prem/AWS/Azure**.

Core idea:

```text id="63bw5e"
Payment service yang sehat tidak menyimpan PAN/CVV.
Payment service menyimpan token/reference, state, ledger, audit, dan provider reference.
```

PCI DSS berlaku untuk entitas yang menyimpan, memproses, atau mentransmisikan cardholder data atau sensitive authentication data, dan PCI DSS v4.0.1 adalah revisi terbatas dari v4.0 yang memperbaiki/menjelaskan requirement tanpa menambah atau menghapus requirement. ([PCI Security Standards Council](https://www.pcisecuritystandards.org/standards/pci-dss/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Security boundary payment service harus jelas:

```text id="uyxwq4"
Customer Browser / Mobile App
  -> PSP-hosted page / SDK / tokenization component
  -> returns payment_method_token
  -> Backend Payment Service receives token only
  -> Payment Service calls PSP using token
  -> Payment Service stores provider refs, not raw card data
```

Yang boleh masuk payment service:

```text id="p5i0cq"
paymentMethodToken
providerPaymentMethodId
providerCustomerId
last4
brand
expiryMonth/year jika allowed
billing country
fingerprint/reference
```

Yang tidak boleh masuk payment service biasa:

```text id="a9n9d3"
PAN / full card number
CVV / CVC
magnetic stripe data
PIN / PIN block
raw 3DS cryptogram jika tidak perlu
raw sensitive authentication data
```

PCI SSC secara eksplisit menyatakan CVC/CVV tidak boleh disimpan untuk card-on-file atau recurring transaction setelah authorization; nilai tersebut tidak dibutuhkan untuk recurring dan penyimpanannya dilarang. ([blog.pcisecuritystandards.org](https://blog.pcisecuritystandards.org/faq-can-cvc-be-stored-for-card-on-file-or-recurring-transactions?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Security Boundary Diagram

```mermaid id="ai29xf"
flowchart TD
    A[Customer Browser/Mobile] --> B[PSP Hosted Page / SDK / Tokenization]
    B --> C[Payment Method Token]

    C --> D[Backend Payment Service - Jersey/JAX-RS]

    D --> E[Payment Domain State]
    D --> F[Provider Adapter]
    D --> G[PostgreSQL]

    G --> G1[payments]
    G --> G2[payment_attempts]
    G --> G3[payment_events]
    G --> G4[ledger_entries]
    G --> G5[payment_method_tokens]
    G --> G6[secret_references]

    F --> H[PSP API]

    I[Secrets Manager / Vault / KMS] --> D
    J[Webhook Provider] --> K[Webhook Endpoint]
    K --> L[Signature Verification]
    L --> M[Inbox Dedup]

    D -. never stores .-> N[PAN/CVV/SAD]
```

---

# 3. Practical Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **PCI Boundary** | Tentukan service mana yang masuk/keluar CDE. | data-flow diagram |
| 2 | **Tokenization** | Backend hanya menerima token/reference. | `PaymentMethodToken` |
| 3 | **Sensitive Data Classification** | Pisahkan PAN/CVV/token/reference/PII. | data classification table |
| 4 | **No PAN/CVV Storage** | Guard agar field sensitif tidak masuk DB/log. | validation + scanner |
| 5 | **Provider Vault** | Simpan kartu di vault PSP, bukan DB internal. | provider customer/payment method refs |
| 6 | **Secrets Management** | API key, webhook secret, DB password. | Vault/Secrets Manager/K8s integration |
| 7 | **KMS / Envelope Encryption** | Enkripsi field sensitif yang masih perlu disimpan. | encryption service |
| 8 | **Webhook Security** | Signature, timestamp, secret rotation. | verifier per provider |
| 9 | **Log Masking** | Jangan log token/secret/card/PII. | logging filter |
| 10 | **Database Security** | Least privilege, encryption, audit, RLS jika perlu. | roles + grants |
| 11 | **Kubernetes Secret Hardening** | Encryption at rest, RBAC, external secrets. | secret policy |
| 12 | **Access Control** | Admin/support/finance role separation. | RBAC/ABAC |
| 13 | **Operational Breakglass** | Controlled emergency access. | approval + audit |
| 14 | **Secret Rotation** | Rotate PSP keys/webhook secrets/DB creds. | rotation runbook/job |
| 15 | **Security Testing** | SAST/DAST/secret scan/log scan. | CI policy |

---

# 4. Data Classification

| Data | Example | Sensitivity | Store di Payment Service? | Notes |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| PAN / full card number | `4111111111111111` | Critical | Tidak | Hindari masuk backend. |
| CVV/CVC | `123` | Critical/SAD | Tidak | Tidak boleh disimpan post-auth. |
| Magnetic stripe / PIN | track data/PIN block | Critical/SAD | Tidak | Jangan masuk service. |
| PSP payment token | `pm_xxx`, `tok_xxx` | Sensitive | Ya, terbatas | Treat as sensitive credential-like reference. |
| Provider payment ID | `pi_xxx`, `pspReference` | Sensitive operational | Ya | Needed for reconciliation/webhook. |
| Last4 | `4242` | Low/medium | Ya jika allowed | Masked display only. |
| Brand | `VISA` | Low | Ya | Non-sensitive-ish. |
| Expiry month/year | `12/2030` | Medium | Ya jika allowed | Avoid unnecessary exposure. |
| Customer email/name | PII | Medium/high | Minimal | Follow privacy/data retention. |
| Billing address | PII | Medium/high | Minimal | Store only if needed. |
| API keys | PSP secret key | Secret | Secret manager only | Not DB/configmap/plain env if avoidable. |
| Webhook secret | `whsec_xxx` | Secret | Secret manager only | Support rotation. |
| Raw provider payload | JSON webhook/API response | Mixed | Limited retention | Redact/tokenize sensitive fields. |

Practical rule:

```text id="8xhmlo"
Kalau data tidak diperlukan untuk state, ledger, reconciliation, audit, atau support,
jangan simpan.
```

---

# 5. PCI Boundary Strategy

## 5.1 Target Boundary yang Disarankan

Untuk microservice backend biasa:

```text id="1by9yf"
Browser/Mobile collects card data using PSP SDK/hosted page.
Backend receives only token.
Backend never sees PAN/CVV.
```

Stripe mendokumentasikan pendekatan vault/tokenization untuk menyimpan card details di Stripe PCI-compliant vault lalu menggunakan token/reference; PayPal Payment Method Tokens API juga memakai permanent token untuk payment method yang disimpan di vault, walaupun PayPal mencatat penggunaan card payment method token API dapat membutuhkan SAQ D PCI compliance tergantung flow. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/payments/vault-and-forward?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.2 Boundary Table

| Component | Should See PAN/CVV? | CDE Impact |
|---|---:|---|
| Browser/mobile PSP SDK | Ya, controlled by PSP integration | Depends integration model |
| Payment Service backend | Tidak | Ideally reduced scope |
| Order Service | Tidak | Out of payment sensitive boundary |
| Billing Service | Tidak | Receives payment events, not card data |
| Fulfillment Service | Tidak | No payment method data |
| Support UI | Tidak | Only masked last4/provider refs |
| PostgreSQL payment DB | Tidak | Stores token/reference/ledger |
| Logs/Tracing/APM | Tidak | Must be masked |
| Data warehouse | Tidak | No raw token/secrets/PAN |

---

# 6. Tokenization Model

## 6.1 Payment Method Token Table

```sql id="m3dvew"
CREATE TABLE payment_method_tokens (
    payment_method_token_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    customer_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    merchant_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    provider_customer_id VARCHAR(200),
    provider_payment_method_id VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    payment_method_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,

    card_brand VARCHAR(40),
    card_last4 CHAR(4),
    card_exp_month INTEGER,
    card_exp_year INTEGER,
    card_country CHAR(2),

    token_status VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',

    reusable BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    off_session_allowed BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,

    fingerprint_hash VARCHAR(128),

    metadata JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_method_type
        CHECK (payment_method_type IN (
            'CARD_TOKEN',
            'PAYPAL',
            'BANK_ACCOUNT_TOKEN',
            'EWALLET',
            'VIRTUAL_ACCOUNT',
            'QRIS',
            'DIRECT_DEBIT',
            'OTHER'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_method_token_status
        CHECK (token_status IN (
            'ACTIVE',
            'EXPIRED',
            'REVOKED',
            'FAILED',
            'DELETED'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_card_exp_month
        CHECK (card_exp_month IS NULL OR card_exp_month BETWEEN 1 AND 12)
);
```

Indexes:

```sql id="tq6v8t"
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_method_provider_method
ON payment_method_tokens(provider, provider_payment_method_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_method_customer
ON payment_method_tokens(customer_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_method_merchant
ON payment_method_tokens(merchant_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_method_status
ON payment_method_tokens(token_status);
```

Important:

```text id="tqhvqp"
provider_payment_method_id masih sensitive.
Jangan expose penuh ke frontend/support kalau tidak perlu.
```

```java id="hsus0w"
package com.company.payment.api.dto;
```

```java id="0jz4p3"
        String expiryMonth,
        String expiryYear
```

Rule:

```text id="nvnv5b"
Jangan punya DTO backend yang menerima cardNumber/cvv kecuali service memang dirancang sebagai PCI-scoped card collection service.
```

# 7. PAN/CVV Guard

Tambahkan defense-in-depth untuk menolak payload yang mengandung field berbahaya.

```java id="jrd3hx"
package com.company.payment.security;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

import java.util.Set;

public class SensitivePaymentFieldGuard {

    private static final Set<String> FORBIDDEN_FIELD_NAMES = Set.of(
            "cardNumber",
            "pan",
            "primaryAccountNumber",
            "cvv",
            "cvc",
            "securityCode",
            "cardVerificationValue",
            "trackData",
            "pin",
            "pinBlock"
    );

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public SensitivePaymentFieldGuard(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }

    public void rejectForbiddenFields(byte[] rawBody) {
        try {
            JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(rawBody);
            scan(root, "$");
        } catch (ForbiddenSensitiveFieldException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            /*
             * Let normal JSON validation handle malformed body.
             */
        }
    }

    private void scan(JsonNode node, String path) {
        if (node == null) {
            return;
        }

        if (node.isObject()) {
            node.fields().forEachRemaining(entry -> {
                String fieldName = entry.getKey();

                if (FORBIDDEN_FIELD_NAMES.contains(fieldName)) {
                    throw new ForbiddenSensitiveFieldException(
                            "Forbidden sensitive payment field detected at " + path + "." + fieldName
                    );
                }

                scan(entry.getValue(), path + "." + fieldName);
            });
        }

        if (node.isArray()) {
            for (int i = 0; i < node.size(); i++) {
                scan(node.get(i), path + "[" + i + "]");
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Jersey filter:

```java id="fn038v"
package com.company.payment.api.filter;

import com.company.payment.security.SensitivePaymentFieldGuard;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;

public class SensitivePaymentFieldFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private final SensitivePaymentFieldGuard guard;

    public SensitivePaymentFieldFilter(SensitivePaymentFieldGuard guard) {
        this.guard = guard;
    }

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) throws java.io.IOException {
        if (!shouldInspect(requestContext)) {
            return;
        }

        byte[] body = requestContext.getEntityStream().readAllBytes();

        guard.rejectForbiddenFields(body);

        requestContext.setEntityStream(new ByteArrayInputStream(body));
    }

    private boolean shouldInspect(ContainerRequestContext requestContext) {
        String path = requestContext.getUriInfo().getPath();
        return path.startsWith("payments")
                || path.startsWith("payment-methods")
                || path.startsWith("refunds");
    }
}
```

---

# 8. Log Masking

OWASP Logging Cheat Sheet memberi panduan logging aplikasi, terutama security logging, dan pada payment service logging harus didesain agar tidak mengeluarkan secret/card/PII ke log, APM, traces, atau exception. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Logging_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 8.1 Sensitive Key Masker

```java id="nsfdt3"
package com.company.payment.logging;

import java.util.Set;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

public class SensitiveLogMasker {

    private static final Set<String> SENSITIVE_KEYS = Set.of(
            "authorization",
            "apiKey",
            "secretKey",
            "webhookSecret",
            "idempotencyKey",
            "paymentMethodToken",
            "providerPaymentMethodId",
            "cardNumber",
            "pan",
            "cvv",
            "cvc",
            "accessToken",
            "refreshToken",
            "password"
    );

    private static final Pattern POSSIBLE_PAN =
            Pattern.compile("\\b\\d{13,19}\\b");

    public String mask(String input) {
        if (input == null) {
            return null;
        }

        String masked = input;

        for (String key : SENSITIVE_KEYS) {
            masked = masked.replaceAll(
                    "(?i)(\"" + key + "\"\\s*:\\s*\")[^\"]+(\")",
                    "$1***MASKED***$2"
            );
        }

        masked = POSSIBLE_PAN.matcher(masked).replaceAll("***POSSIBLE_PAN_MASKED***");

        return masked;
    }
}
```

## 8.2 Logging Policy

| Data | Log? | Format |
|---|---:|---|
| `paymentId` | Ya | full UUID |
| `orderId` | Ya | full/internal |
| `provider` | Ya | full |
| `providerPaymentId` | Terbatas | mask middle |
| `paymentMethodToken` | Tidak | fingerprint/hash only |
| `Idempotency-Key` | Tidak full | hash/fingerprint |
| PSP API key | Tidak | never |
| Webhook secret | Tidak | never |
| PAN/CVV | Tidak | reject + alert |
| Raw provider payload | Tidak full | redacted snapshot |
| Customer email | Terbatas | hash/masked |
| Customer name/address | Tidak by default | only secured audit view |

# 9. Secret Management

Secrets yang umum:

```text id="qc8ney"
- PSP API secret key
- PSP webhook signing secret / HMAC key
- database password
- broker password
- Camunda client credentials
- KMS key alias/reference
- mTLS private key
- OAuth client secret
```

Jangan simpan secret di:

```text id="ob8n1i"
- GitHub repository
- Maven resource file
- Docker image layer
- plain ConfigMap
- application.properties committed to Git
- logs/error message
- database table biasa
```

AWS Secrets Manager mendukung automatic rotation; rotation berarti memperbarui credential di secret dan di database/service target. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/secretsmanager/latest/userguide/rotating-secrets.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 10. Secret Reference Pattern

Di DB, simpan reference, bukan secret value.

```sql id="f0azmv"
CREATE TABLE payment_provider_secret_refs (
    secret_ref_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    provider VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    environment VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,

    secret_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,

    secret_manager VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    secret_name VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    secret_version VARCHAR(100),

    active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_payment_provider_secret_type
        CHECK (secret_type IN (
            'API_KEY',
            'WEBHOOK_SECRET',
            'HMAC_KEY',
            'OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET',
            'MTLS_PRIVATE_KEY',
            'BASIC_AUTH_PASSWORD'
        )),

    CONSTRAINT chk_secret_manager
        CHECK (secret_manager IN (
            'AWS_SECRETS_MANAGER',
            'AZURE_KEY_VAULT',
            'HASHICORP_VAULT',
            'KUBERNETES_SECRET',
            'ON_PREM_HSM'
        ))
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_provider_secret_active
ON payment_provider_secret_refs(provider, environment, secret_type)
WHERE active = true;
```

Java resolver:

```java id="7wc7qc"
package com.company.payment.security.secret;

public interface SecretResolver {

    SecretValue resolve(SecretReference reference);

    java.util.List<SecretValue> resolveActiveSecrets(
            String provider,
            String environment,
            String secretType
    );
}
```

```java id="2ez5w2"
package com.company.payment.security.secret;

import java.time.Instant;

public record SecretValue(
        String value,
        String version,
        String fingerprint,
        Instant loadedAt
) {}
```

Fingerprint:

```text id="y0qfvq"
fingerprint = sha256(secret value)
```

Log only:

```text id="grkbgx"
fingerprint prefix
secret version
secret name/reference
```

Never log:

```text id="fn8d40"
secret value
```

# 11. Kubernetes Secret Hardening

Kubernetes documentation warns that Secrets are stored unencrypted in etcd by default and recommends enabling encryption at rest; anyone with API access, etcd access, or permission to create pods in a namespace can potentially access secrets in that namespace. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/configuration/secret/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical Kubernetes policy:

| Area | Recommendation |
|---|---|
| Secret storage | Enable encryption at rest for Kubernetes Secrets. |
| RBAC | Least privilege; app service account reads only required secrets. |
| Namespace | Separate payment namespace from generic workloads. |
| Pod creation | Restrict who can create pods in payment namespace. |
| Env vars | Prefer mounted files or external secret injection where practical. |
| Rotation | Use external secrets operator / CSI driver / secret manager integration. |
| Logging | Never dump env vars. |
| Image | Do not bake secrets into Docker images. |
| CI/CD | Use masked secrets and OIDC/short-lived credentials. |

---

# 12. KMS / Envelope Encryption

Jika harus menyimpan field sensitif non-card, gunakan application-level encryption dengan KMS/envelope encryption.

AWS KMS docs menjelaskan envelope encryption sebagai praktik mengenkripsi plaintext data dengan data key, lalu mengenkripsi data key tersebut di bawah key lain. ([AWS Documentation](https://docs.aws.amazon.com/kms/latest/developerguide/kms-cryptography.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 12.1 Use Case

Boleh dipertimbangkan untuk:

```text id="j18o4n"
- provider access token cache
- bank account reference metadata
- sensitive customer payment metadata
- uploaded dispute evidence metadata
- raw provider payload subset yang harus disimpan
```

Tetap jangan gunakan ini sebagai alasan menyimpan PAN/CVV jika tidak perlu.

## 12.2 Encrypted Field Table Pattern

```sql id="gx9bqa"
CREATE TABLE secure_payment_metadata (
    secure_metadata_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    payment_id UUID REFERENCES payments(payment_id),

    data_classification VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,

    ciphertext BYTEA NOT NULL,
    encrypted_data_key BYTEA NOT NULL,

    encryption_key_ref VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL,
    encryption_algorithm VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,

    aad_context JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'system',

    CONSTRAINT chk_secure_metadata_classification
        CHECK (data_classification IN (
            'SENSITIVE_PROVIDER_METADATA',
            'SENSITIVE_CUSTOMER_METADATA',
            'DISPUTE_EVIDENCE_METADATA',
            'BANK_METADATA'
        ))
);
```

Encryption interface:

```java id="j2c0cg"
package com.company.payment.security.crypto;

public interface FieldEncryptionService {

    EncryptedValue encrypt(
            String plaintext,
            EncryptionContext context
    );

    String decrypt(
            EncryptedValue encryptedValue,
            EncryptionContext context
    );
}
```

```java id="v2wfjh"
package com.company.payment.security.crypto;

import java.util.Map;

public record EncryptionContext(
        String keyRef,
        Map<String, String> aad
) {}
```

```java id="v99yc6"
package com.company.payment.security.crypto;

public record EncryptedValue(
        byte[] ciphertext,
        byte[] encryptedDataKey,
        String keyRef,
        String algorithm
```

AAD examples:

```text id="i2z1lr"
paymentId
merchantId
fieldName

```

# 13. Webhook Secret Rotation

Webhook verifier harus support multiple active secrets.

Reason:

```text id="txzg6w"
Saat rotation, provider bisa mengirim event yang ditandatangani dengan secret lama atau baru selama overlap window.
```

Pattern:

```java id="pv610n"
public class RotatingWebhookSecretVerifier {

    private final WebhookSignatureAlgorithm algorithm;
    private final SecretResolver secretResolver;

    public VerificationResult verify(
            String provider,
            String environment,
            byte[] rawBody,
            String signatureHeader
    ) {
        java.util.List<SecretValue> activeSecrets =
                secretResolver.resolveActiveSecrets(
                        provider,
                        environment,
                        "WEBHOOK_SECRET"
                );

        for (SecretValue secret : activeSecrets) {
            if (algorithm.verify(rawBody, signatureHeader, secret.value())) {
                return VerificationResult.valid(secret.version(), secret.fingerprint());
            }
        }

        return VerificationResult.invalid("No active webhook secret matched");
    }
}
```

Rotation runbook:

```text id="mn90ux"
1. Add new secret version as active-next.
2. Configure provider to sign with new secret.
3. Keep old secret active during grace window.
4. Monitor invalid signature metric.
5. Disable old secret after grace window.
6. Audit rotation event.
```

---

# 14. API Key Rotation

Provider API key rotation pattern:

```text id="u5r4dc"
1. Store new key in secret manager.
2. Deploy config with new secret version reference.
3. Support dual-key if provider allows.
4. Health check provider API using new key.
5. Shift traffic.
6. Revoke old key.
7. Audit.
```

If provider does not support dual active keys:

```text id="eft8d1"
Use maintenance window or rapid coordinated cutover.
Prepare rollback if new key fails.
```

Application caching rule:

```text id="i5qjoq"
Cache secrets briefly.
Refresh on version change or TTL.
Do not require service restart for every rotation if possible.
```

---

# 15. Database Security

PostgreSQL should use least privilege roles.

Example roles:

```text id="njux94"
payment_app_rw
payment_app_readonly
payment_migration
payment_finance_read
payment_support_read
payment_admin_ops
```

Grant style:

```sql id="k5vn6v"
CREATE ROLE payment_app_rw LOGIN PASSWORD '...';

GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO payment_app_rw;

GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON payments TO payment_app_rw;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON refunds TO payment_app_rw;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON disputes TO payment_app_rw;

GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON ledger_entries TO payment_app_rw;
REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE ON ledger_entries FROM payment_app_rw;

GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON payment_outbox_events TO payment_app_rw;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON payment_inbox_events TO payment_app_rw;
```

For migration:

```text id="fg61xd"
Migration role may create/alter schema.
Runtime app role should not own schema.
```

For ledger:

```text id="z17ts4"
Runtime app can INSERT ledger.
Runtime app cannot UPDATE/DELETE ledger.
```

---

# 16. Row-Level and Column-Level Access

Support UI tidak harus melihat semua kolom.

Views:

```sql id="2q180h"
CREATE VIEW v_support_payment_summary AS
SELECT
    payment_id,
    order_id,
    customer_id,
    merchant_id,
    status,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    provider,
    provider_payment_id,
    created_at,
    updated_at
FROM payments;
```

Masked payment method view:

```sql id="jlfpsx"
CREATE VIEW v_support_payment_methods AS
SELECT
    payment_method_token_id,
    customer_id,
    merchant_id,
    provider,
    payment_method_type,
    card_brand,
    CASE
        WHEN card_last4 IS NULL THEN NULL
        ELSE '****' || card_last4
    END AS masked_card,
    token_status,
    reusable,
    created_at
FROM payment_method_tokens;
```

Do not expose:

```text id="fsxw2f"
provider_payment_method_id full
token value
secret ref internals
raw payload
encrypted metadata
```

---

# 17. Secure Jersey Filters

## 17.1 Security Headers Filter

```java id="2c5gag"
package com.company.payment.api.filter;

import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseFilter;

public class SecurityHeadersFilter implements ContainerResponseFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(
            ContainerRequestContext requestContext,
            ContainerResponseContext responseContext
    ) {
        responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
        responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");
        responseContext.getHeaders().putSingle("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    }
}
```

## 17.2 No-Store for Payment Responses

```text id="3gqrfp"
Payment API responses should not be cached by proxies/browser unless explicitly safe.
```

---

# 18. Authorization Model

Role separation:

| Role | Can Do |
|---|---|
| `PAYMENT_READ` | View payment summary. |
| `PAYMENT_OPERATE` | Retry safe recovery/status query. |
| `REFUND_CREATE` | Create low-risk refund. |
| `REFUND_APPROVE` | Approve high-value refund. |
| `DISPUTE_MANAGE` | Upload/submit evidence. |
| `FINANCE_READ` | Read ledger/reconciliation. |
| `FINANCE_ADJUST` | Request adjustment. |
| `FINANCE_APPROVE` | Approve adjustment/reversal. |
| `SECRET_ADMIN` | Rotate secret references, not read values. |
| `BREAKGLASS_ADMIN` | Emergency access with audit. |

Authorization guard example:

```java id="4s3zfg"
public class PaymentAuthorizationService {

    public void requirePermission(UserPrincipal user, String permission) {
        if (!user.permissions().contains(permission)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Missing permission: " + permission);
        }
    }

    public void requireMerchantAccess(UserPrincipal user, String merchantId) {
        if (!user.allowedMerchantIds().contains(merchantId)) {
            throw new ForbiddenException("Merchant access denied");
        }
    }
}
```

---

# 19. Secure Admin Actions

Admin actions with financial impact:

```text id="7y75xj"
- manual mark captured
- manual refund retry
- manual recovery resolution
- ledger adjustment
- ledger reversal
- secret reference update
- provider disable/enable
- webhook replay
```

Must have:

```text id="9ne7ap"
1. role check
2. reason code
3. reason detail
4. actor
5. approval if high risk
6. audit event
7. immutable log
8. correlation ID
```

Audit table:

```sql id="mt2sxs"
CREATE TABLE security_audit_events (
    security_audit_event_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    actor_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    actor_type VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    action VARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
    resource_type VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL,
    resource_id VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,

    permission VARCHAR(120),
    decision VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,

    reason_code VARCHAR(100),
    reason_detail TEXT,

    ip_address INET,
    user_agent TEXT,

    correlation_id VARCHAR(100),

    metadata JSONB,

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),

    CONSTRAINT chk_security_audit_actor_type
        CHECK (actor_type IN ('USER', 'SERVICE', 'SYSTEM')),

    CONSTRAINT chk_security_audit_decision
        CHECK (decision IN ('ALLOWED', 'DENIED', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED'))
);
```

---

# 20. Webhook Security Recap

Webhook endpoint must:

```text id="pd5jq0"
1. read raw body
2. verify signature before trust
3. support secret rotation
4. validate timestamp/replay tolerance if provider supports timestamp
5. dedup provider_event_id
6. store raw payload only after valid signature
7. return fast after durable persistence
8. never trust amount/status without signature
```

Stripe requires raw request body for webhook signature verification; Adyen supports HMAC signatures for webhook events; PayPal webhook verification can use signature/certificate headers or verify endpoint. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/security?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 21. Data Retention and Deletion

Retention policy:

| Data | Suggested Direction |
|---|---|
| PAN/CVV | Never store. |
| PSP token/reference | Keep while active and needed. |
| Expired token | Revoke/delete/anonymize after policy window. |
| Payment state | Keep for business/audit/legal retention. |
| Ledger | Long retention; append-only. |
| Raw webhook payload | Shorter retention; redact if possible. |
| Provider report files | Finance/audit retention. |
| Support audit | Long retention. |
| Dispute evidence | Legal/compliance retention; access restricted. |
| Logs | Short/medium; no sensitive data. |

Deletion approach:

```text id="8wj6xs"
Delete or anonymize PII fields where legally required.
Do not break financial ledger integrity.
Use reference-preserving anonymization if ledger/reporting must remain.
```

---

# 22. Secure Outbox/Event Payload

Payment events must not leak sensitive data.

Allowed:

```json id="sl72sb"
{
  "paymentId": "uuid",
  "orderId": "ORD-123",
  "merchantId": "MRC-001",
  "amountMinor": 100000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "status": "CAPTURED",
  "provider": "ADYEN",
  "providerPaymentIdMasked": "psp_***123",
  "card": {
    "brand": "VISA",
    "last4": "4242"
  }
}
```

Forbidden:

```json id="jbzelm"
{
  "cardNumber": "4111111111111111",
  "cvv": "123",
  "paymentMethodToken": "full-token-value",
  "providerSecretKey": "sk_live_xxx",
  "rawProviderPayload": {}
}
```

Practical rule:

```text id="t6vq9x"
Outbox event is a data distribution mechanism.
Treat it as a public internal contract, not a secure vault.
```

---

# 23. CI/CD Security Gates

Recommended gates for GitHub/GitHub Actions:

| Gate | Purpose |
|---|---|
| Secret scanning | Prevent API keys committed. |
| Dependency scanning | Detect vulnerable libraries. |
| SAST | Detect code-level security issues. |
| IaC scanning | K8s/Terraform secret/RBAC issues. |
| Container image scan | Base image/dependency CVEs. |
| License scan | Compliance. |
| Test log scan | Prevent PAN/CVV/token in test logs. |
| Migration review | Prevent sensitive columns. |

Example forbidden migration check:

```text id="vw8aoz"
Reject migration if new column name matches:
card_number
pan
cvv
cvc
track_data
pin_block
secret_key
```

---

# 24. Secure Configuration Files

Bad:

```properties id="315ndn"
stripe.secretKey=sk_live_...
adyen.apiKey=...
db.password=...
```

Better:

```properties id="50z3kb"
stripe.secretRef=aws-secrets-manager:/prod/payment/stripe/api-key
adyen.secretRef=aws-secrets-manager:/prod/payment/adyen/api-key
db.secretRef=aws-secrets-manager:/prod/payment/db
```

Or environment reference:

```yaml id="c61414"
payment:
  providers:
    stripe:
      api-key-secret-ref: prod/payment/stripe/api-key
      webhook-secret-ref: prod/payment/stripe/webhook-secret
```

---

# 25. Docker Image Security

Checklist:

```text id="r7fitn"
1. Do not bake secrets into image.
2. Use minimal base image.
3. Run as non-root user.
4. Read-only root filesystem if possible.
5. Drop Linux capabilities unless needed.
6. Pin base image digest for high assurance.
7. Scan image in CI.
8. Do not include debug tools in prod image unless controlled.
9. Do not include test fixtures containing fake-but-realistic PAN in prod image.
```

Dockerfile example:

```dockerfile id="w35390"
FROM eclipse-temurin:21-jre-alpine

RUN addgroup -S app && adduser -S app -G app

WORKDIR /app

COPY target/payment-service.jar /app/payment-service.jar

USER app

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "/app/payment-service.jar"]
```

---

# 26. Kubernetes Deployment Security

Example hardening:

```yaml id="ctohxv"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: payment-service
spec:
  template:
    spec:
      serviceAccountName: payment-service
      containers:
        - name: payment-service
          image: registry.example.com/payment-service:1.0.0
          securityContext:
            runAsNonRoot: true
            allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
            readOnlyRootFilesystem: true
            capabilities:
              drop: ["ALL"]
          env:
            - name: PAYMENT_CONFIG_PROFILE
              value: "prod"
          volumeMounts:
            - name: tmp
              mountPath: /tmp
      volumes:
        - name: tmp
          emptyDir: {}
```

Network policy direction:

```text id="li0f75"
Payment service should only talk to:
- PostgreSQL
- broker
- PSP endpoints
- secret manager / vault
- Camunda if used
- observability endpoints
```

---

# 27. Security Error Handling

Do not expose sensitive details.

Bad:

```json id="ysqk49"
{
  "error": "Stripe key sk_live_xxx invalid"
}
```

Better:

```json id="zmu3uy"
{
  "type": "https://api.company.internal/problems/provider-authentication-failed",
  "title": "Payment provider authentication failed",
  "status": 503,
  "detail": "Payment provider authentication failed. The incident has been recorded.",
  "errorCode": "PAYMENT_PROVIDER_AUTHENTICATION_FAILED",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

Internal log:

```json id="0sm1ef"
{
  "event": "provider_authentication_failed",
  "provider": "STRIPE",
  "secretRef": "prod/payment/stripe/api-key",
  "secretVersion": "v12",
  "secretFingerprintPrefix": "ab12cd",
  "traceId": "corr-123"
}
```

# 28. Security Testing

## 28.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Request contains `cardNumber` | Rejected. |
| Request contains `cvv` | Rejected. |
| Log masker sees PAN-like digits | Masked. |
| Token in DTO logging | Masked. |
| ProblemDetails does not include secret | Safe. |
| Webhook verifies with current secret | Accepted. |
| Webhook verifies with previous active secret | Accepted during rotation. |
| Webhook invalid signature | Rejected. |
| Unauthorized refund approval | 403. |
| Support view hides token/provider method ID | Masked. |
| Ledger update attempt | Rejected. |

## 28.2 Integration Tests

```text id="lz8r3m"
1. POST /payments with paymentMethodToken -> accepted.
2. POST /payments with cardNumber/cvv -> rejected.
3. Verify DB has no cardNumber/cvv columns.
4. Verify logs contain no raw token/PAN.
5. Rotate webhook secret current+previous.
6. Send webhook signed with old secret -> accepted.
7. Disable old secret.
8. Send webhook signed with old secret -> rejected.
9. Access ledger as support role -> restricted.
10. Access ledger as finance role -> allowed.
```

## 28.3 CI Scans

```text id="4rqm6e"
1. Secret scan repository.
2. Scan application.properties/yaml for secret-like values.
3. Scan SQL migrations for forbidden sensitive column names.
4. Scan test logs for PAN-like patterns.
5. Scan Docker image.
6. Scan Kubernetes manifests for insecure securityContext/RBAC.
```

---

# 29. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Backend menerima card number/CVV | PCI scope besar dan risiko tinggi. | PSP SDK/hosted tokenization. |
| Simpan PSP token seperti data biasa | Token bisa disalahgunakan. | Treat as sensitive, mask/encrypt where needed. |
| Secret di application.properties | Bocor via Git/image/log. | Secret manager/ref only. |
| Kubernetes Secret dianggap aman karena base64 | Base64 bukan encryption; etcd default unencrypted. | Encryption at rest + RBAC + external secrets. |
| Log raw request/response provider | Bisa bocor token/PII/secret. | Redaction/masking. |
| Webhook tanpa signature verification | Bisa spoof payment status. | Verify raw body signature. |
| Rotation butuh restart/manual deploy | Risky saat incident. | Secret resolver + version/TTL refresh. |
| Support bisa lihat raw provider payload | PII/token leakage. | Masked views + role control. |
| Manual recovery tanpa approval/audit | Compliance gap. | approval + immutable audit. |
| Outbox membawa raw provider payload | Data tersebar ke banyak consumer. | Contract payload minimal. |
| Runtime DB user bisa update ledger | Audit bisa dirusak. | Revoke update/delete + trigger. |
| Semua admin punya akses secret value | Insider risk. | Secret reference admin, not secret read. |

Buat module:

```text id="2ke99g"
payment-service/
  security/
    SensitivePaymentFieldGuard.java
    SensitiveLogMasker.java
    PaymentDataClassifier.java

  security/secret/
    SecretResolver.java
    SecretReference.java

  security/crypto/
    FieldEncryptionService.java

    PaymentMethodTokenMapper.java
    PaymentMethodTokenMapper.xml

```

Target skenario:

```text id="1g2f08"
1. POST /payments dengan paymentMethodToken berhasil.
2. POST /payments dengan cardNumber/cvv ditolak.
3. Simpan provider payment method token/reference.
4. Support API hanya menampilkan brand + ****last4.
5. Log masker menyamarkan token, idempotency key, possible PAN.
6. Webhook verifier menerima current secret.
7. Webhook verifier menerima previous secret selama rotation.
8. Setelah old secret disabled, webhook lama ditolak.
9. Secret resolver mengambil secret dari secret manager via reference.
10. Payment event/outbox tidak membawa token/raw provider payload.
11. Finance role bisa baca ledger; support role hanya summary.
12. SQL migration dengan column `cvv` gagal di CI scanner.
```

---

# Ringkasan

Security payment service dimulai dari boundary yang benar:

```text id="fkbge1"
Jangan biarkan backend melihat PAN/CVV.
Jangan simpan secret di config/repo/image.
Jangan log token/secret/raw payload.
Jangan percaya webhook tanpa signature.
Jangan expose ledger/adjustment/admin action tanpa role dan audit.
```

Prinsip utama:

```text id="3rffqp"
1. Gunakan PSP tokenization/hosted SDK agar backend hanya menerima token.
2. Jangan simpan PAN/CVV/SAD.
3. Treat payment token/provider reference as sensitive.
4. Secret value tinggal di secret manager, DB hanya menyimpan reference.
5. Support secret rotation tanpa downtime besar.
6. Kubernetes Secrets perlu encryption-at-rest dan RBAC ketat.
7. Gunakan KMS/envelope encryption untuk sensitive non-card metadata jika perlu.
8. Mask log, trace, error response, and outbox payload.
9. Webhook harus signature-verified dengan raw body.
10. Semua admin/finance/security action harus auditable.
```

Dengan desain ini, payment service tetap bisa menjalankan payment lifecycle penuh-authorization, capture, refund, dispute, ledger, reconciliation-tanpa berubah menjadi penyimpan data kartu yang berisiko tinggi.

## Response:
7/14/2026, 11:04:36 PM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 17/18**: Observability, Monitoring, Alerting, Support Console, dan Runbook. Fokusnya membuat payment operation bisa dipantau, ditelusuri, di-alert, dan ditangani support/ops tanpa langsung masuk database.

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 17/18 - Observability, Monitoring, Alerting, Support Console, dan Runbook

Seri ini fokus pada membuat payment service **mudah dipantau, ditelusuri, di-alert, dan dioperasikan** ketika terjadi masalah seperti provider timeout, webhook delay, refund pending, outbox stuck, settlement mismatch, atau possible double charge.

Core idea:

```text
Payment operation harus observable dari 5 sudut:

1. Customer impact
2. Payment state
3. Provider interaction
4. Financial correctness
5. Operational recovery
```

OpenTelemetry adalah framework observability vendor-neutral untuk menghasilkan, mengumpulkan, memproses, dan mengekspor telemetry seperti **traces, metrics, dan logs**. Ini cocok sebagai standar instrumentation untuk Java/JAX-RS microservice modern. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Prometheus alerting memakai alerting rules berbasis PromQL, lalu Alertmanager mengelola routing, silencing, inhibition, aggregation, dan notifikasi. ([Prometheus](https://prometheus.io/docs/prometheus/latest/configuration/alerting_rules/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Google SRE menekankan monitoring yang actionable dan SLO-based alerting agar alert benar-benar mewakili ancaman terhadap reliability/user impact, bukan sekadar noise internal. ([Google SRE](https://sre.google/sre-book/monitoring-distributed-systems/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model Observability Payment

Payment system tidak cukup hanya punya log error.

Harus bisa menjawab:

```text
1. Customer terkena dampak atau tidak?
2. Payment stuck di state apa?
3. Provider mana yang bermasalah?
4. Operation mana yang timeout?
5. Webhook masuk atau belum?
6. Inbox/outbox stuck atau tidak?
7. Ledger sudah terposting atau belum?
8. Refund/dispute/settlement mismatch atau tidak?
9. Apakah ada risiko double charge/refund?
10. Apa action support/ops berikutnya?
```

Dalam payment domain, observability harus menggabungkan:

```text
metrics  -> apa yang sedang naik/turun/anomali?
logs     -> event spesifik apa yang terjadi?
traces   -> request ini lewat service/provider mana saja?
audit    -> siapa/apa mengubah state?
ledger   -> dampak finansialnya apa?
runbook  -> apa tindakan manusia/ops?
```

---

# 2. Diagram Observability Architecture

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A[Payment Service - Jersey/JAX-RS] --> B[OpenTelemetry SDK/Agent]
    A --> C[Structured Logs]
    A --> D[Business Metrics]
    A --> E[Audit Events]
    A --> F[Ledger Entries]

    B --> G[OpenTelemetry Collector]
    C --> G
    D --> G

    G --> H[Metrics Backend: Prometheus]
    G --> I[Tracing Backend: Tempo/Jaeger/etc]
    G --> J[Logs Backend: Loki/ELK/etc]

    H --> K[Grafana Dashboards]
    I --> K
    J --> K

    H --> L[Alertmanager]
    L --> M[On-call / Slack / Pager / Email]

    A --> N[PostgreSQL]
    N --> O[Support Console]
    N --> P[Finance Console]
    N --> Q[Admin Recovery Console]

    O --> R[Runbooks]
    P --> R
    Q --> R
```

Grafana exemplars dapat menghubungkan tren metric latency/error dengan trace spesifik yang menyebabkan latency tinggi, sehingga investigasi bisa bergerak dari dashboard metric ke trace konkret lebih cepat. ([Grafana Labs](https://grafana.com/docs/grafana/latest/fundamentals/exemplars/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Business Metrics** | Status payment/refund/dispute/settlement. | Prometheus counters/gauges/histograms |
| 2 | **Provider Metrics** | Latency/error/rate-limit per PSP. | `payment_provider_*` metrics |
| 3 | **State Metrics** | Stuck/unknown/pending states. | gauges by status/age |
| 4 | **Webhook Metrics** | received/dedup/invalid signature/lag. | webhook dashboard |
| 5 | **Inbox/Outbox Metrics** | backlog, oldest pending, dead letter. | worker dashboard |
| 6 | **Ledger Metrics** | captured/refunded/fee/chargeback totals. | finance metrics |
| 7 | **Reconciliation Metrics** | match rate, mismatch aging. | finance dashboard |
| 8 | **Tracing** | API → DB → provider → outbox flow. | OpenTelemetry spans |
| 9 | **Structured Logging** | Searchable payment lifecycle logs. | JSON logs |
| 10 | **Correlation IDs** | Trace across Order/Payment/Provider/Camunda. | `traceId`, `correlationId`, `paymentId` |
| 11 | **Alerting** | User-impacting and finance-risk alerts. | Prometheus rules |
| 12 | **SLO/SLA** | Target availability/latency/recovery. | SLO dashboard |
| 13 | **Support Console** | Safe read/action UI for support. | payment timeline/retry/replay |
| 14 | **Runbooks** | Actionable incident handling. | provider timeout, webhook stuck, mismatch |
| 15 | **Operational Audit** | Who did what and why. | admin action audit |

---

# 4. Golden Signals untuk Payment Service

Untuk generic service, golden signals biasanya latency, traffic, errors, saturation. Untuk payment, tambahkan **financial correctness** dan **recovery health**.

| Signal | Payment Interpretation |
|---|---|
| Traffic | Jumlah create/authorize/capture/refund/webhook/reconciliation request. |
| Latency | API latency, provider latency, webhook processing lag. |
| Errors | Provider decline, provider timeout, invalid state, idempotency conflict. |
| Saturation | DB pool, worker backlog, inbox/outbox queue, thread pool. |
| Financial correctness | Ledger mismatch, duplicate prevention, reconciliation mismatch. |
| Recovery health | Unknown payments, recovery lag, investigation cases. |

---

# 5. Metric Naming Convention

Gunakan naming yang konsisten:

```text
payment_<domain>_<measurement>_<unit>
```

Contoh:

```text
payment_api_requests_total
payment_provider_requests_total
payment_provider_request_duration_seconds
payment_webhook_received_total
payment_outbox_pending_count
payment_recovery_lag_seconds
payment_reconciliation_match_rate
```

Label yang disarankan:

```text
provider
operation
status
error_code
payment_method_type
currency
merchant_id
environment
```

Hati-hati cardinality:

```text
Jangan gunakan paymentId, orderId, refundId, providerRequestId sebagai metric label.
Gunakan data itu di logs/traces, bukan metrics.
```

---

# 6. Core Metrics - API Layer

| Metric | Type | Labels | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|---|
| `payment_api_requests_total` | Counter | `method`, `path`, `status` | Traffic API. |
| `payment_api_request_duration_seconds` | Histogram | `method`, `path` | Latency API. |
| `payment_api_errors_total` | Counter | `error_code`, `path` | API errors. |
| `payment_idempotency_conflicts_total` | Counter | `operation` | Same key different payload. |
| `payment_invalid_state_total` | Counter | `operation`, `current_status` | Operation invalid. |
| `payment_concurrent_modification_total` | Counter | `operation` | Optimistic lock/concurrency. |

Prometheus histogram cocok untuk distribusi latency karena dapat dihitung dalam quantile/rate di PromQL, sedangkan counter cocok untuk jumlah event monoton seperti request/error count. Alerting rules Prometheus dapat memakai ekspresi PromQL atas metric seperti ini. ([Prometheus](https://prometheus.io/docs/prometheus/latest/configuration/alerting_rules/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 7. Core Metrics - Payment Lifecycle

| Metric | Type | Labels | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|---|
| `payment_created_total` | Counter | `provider`, `currency` | Payment created. |
| `payment_authorized_total` | Counter | `provider`, `currency` | Auth success. |
| `payment_authorization_failed_total` | Counter | `provider`, `error_code` | Auth failed. |
| `payment_capture_requested_total` | Counter | `provider`, `currency` | Capture requested. |
| `payment_captured_total` | Counter | `provider`, `currency` | Capture success. |
| `payment_capture_failed_total` | Counter | `provider`, `error_code` | Capture failed. |
| `payment_unknown_total` | Counter | `provider`, `operation` | Moved to UNKNOWN. |
| `payment_status_current_count` | Gauge | `status`, `provider` | Current status distribution. |
| `payment_stuck_count` | Gauge | `status`, `age_bucket` | Stuck payment count. |

Important dashboard questions:

```text
1. Apakah UNKNOWN naik?
2. Apakah CAPTURE_FAILED naik?
3. Apakah REQUIRES_ACTION completion turun?
4. Apakah AUTHORIZED tapi belum captured menumpuk?
5. Apakah provider tertentu punya failure spike?
```

# 8. Core Metrics - Provider Adapter

| Metric | Type | Labels | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|---|
| `payment_provider_requests_total` | Counter | `provider`, `operation`, `result` | Provider call volume/result. |
| `payment_provider_request_duration_seconds` | Histogram | `provider`, `operation` | Provider latency. |
| `payment_provider_timeout_total` | Counter | `provider`, `operation` | Timeout. |
| `payment_provider_unavailable_total` | Counter | `provider`, `operation` | 5xx/network unavailable. |
| `payment_provider_decline_total` | Counter | `provider`, `decline_code` | Business decline. |
| `payment_provider_rate_limited_total` | Counter | `provider` | Rate limit. |
| `payment_provider_circuit_open` | Gauge | `provider`, `operation` | Circuit breaker open/closed. |
| `payment_provider_unknown_response_total` | Counter | `provider`, `operation` | Unknown schema/status. |

Alert yang berguna:

```text
Provider timeout spike
Provider latency p95/p99 naik
Provider 5xx naik
Provider decline spike abnormal
Rate limit spike
```

# 9. Core Metrics - Webhook

| Metric | Type | Labels | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|---|
| `payment_webhook_received_total` | Counter | `provider`, `event_type` | Webhook received. |
| `payment_webhook_signature_invalid_total` | Counter | `provider` | Possible spoof/misconfig. |
| `payment_webhook_duplicate_total` | Counter | `provider`, `event_type` | Duplicate provider webhook. |
| `payment_webhook_ingestion_failed_total` | Counter | `provider`, `error_code` | Failed before inbox. |
| `payment_webhook_processing_success_total` | Counter | `provider`, `event_type` | Processed success. |
| `payment_webhook_processing_failed_total` | Counter | `provider`, `error_code` | Processing failed. |
| `payment_webhook_dead_letter_total` | Counter | `provider`, `event_type` | DLQ/dead letter. |
| `payment_webhook_processing_lag_seconds` | Histogram/Gauge | `provider`, `event_type` | Received → processed. |
| `payment_webhook_event_age_seconds` | Histogram | `provider`, `event_type` | Provider event time → received. |

Critical alerts:

```text
1. Invalid signature spike.
2. Webhook dead letter > 0.
3. Webhook processing lag > SLA.
4. Reference not found spike.
5. No webhook received for provider while payment volume exists.
```

| `payment_inbox_dead_letter_count` | Gauge | `provider` | Dead letter backlog. |

| `payment_outbox_dead_letter_count` | Gauge | `event_type` | DLQ. |

Runbook-first alerts:

```text
Outbox pending age > 5 minutes
Inbox pending age > 2 minutes
Outbox dead letter > 0
Inbox dead letter > 0
```
