# 12. OpenTelemetry Tracing Design

Trace harus menjawab:

```text
Request payment ini lewat mana saja?
DB query mana yang lambat?
Provider call mana yang timeout?
Outbox event dibuat atau tidak?
Webhook event ini mengubah payment apa?
```

OpenTelemetry mendukung telemetry signals seperti traces, metrics, dan logs; tracing merepresentasikan perjalanan request/event melalui distributed system. ([OpenTelemetry](https://opentelemetry.io/docs/concepts/signals/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Recommended spans:

```text
HTTP POST /payments
  PaymentApplicationService.createPayment
    IdempotencyService.begin
    PaymentRepository.insert
    PaymentGatewayRouter.route
    StripeGatewayClient.authorize
      HTTP POST provider
    PaymentResultApplier.apply
    LedgerPostingService.post
    OutboxRepository.insert
```

Webhook trace:

```text
HTTP POST /webhooks/{provider}
  WebhookSignatureVerifier.verify
  InboxRepository.insertIfAbsent
  WebhookProcessor.process
    ProviderWebhookMapper.map
    PaymentReferenceResolver.resolve
    PaymentStateTransitionService.apply
    LedgerPostingService.post
    OutboxRepository.insert
```

---

# 13. Span Attributes

Gunakan attributes yang aman dan rendah cardinality.

Recommended:

```text
payment.provider
payment.operation
payment.status
payment.error_code
payment.currency
payment.capture_mode
payment.method_type
payment.webhook.event_type
payment.recovery.type
```

Avoid:

```text
card number
CVV
full token
secret
raw provider payload
customer full address
```

For IDs, acceptable in traces if access restricted:

```text
payment.id
order.id
refund.id
dispute.id
correlation.id
provider.request_id
```

But jangan jadikan ID unik sebagai metric label.

---

# 14. Structured Logging Standard

Gunakan JSON logs.

```json
{
  "timestamp": "2026-07-14T20:10:00Z",
  "level": "INFO",
  "service": "payment-service",
  "event": "payment_capture_succeeded",
  "paymentId": "6e3a9e6f-2505-4d58-a8a4-9f552c061d4f",
  "captureId": "e7d0edc5-7f9a-466a-a7e6-d7c9b5078a50",
  "orderId": "ORD-123",
  "provider": "ADYEN",
  "operation": "CAPTURE",
  "amountMinor": 1000000,
  "currency": "IDR",
  "oldStatus": "CAPTURE_PENDING",
  "newStatus": "CAPTURED",
  "correlationId": "corr-123",
  "traceId": "trace-abc",
  "spanId": "span-def"
}
```

Standard fields:

| Field | Notes |
|---|---|
| `event` | Machine-readable event name. |
| `paymentId` | Trace domain object. |
| `orderId` | Connect to order domain. |
| `provider` | PSP. |
| `operation` | AUTHORIZE/CAPTURE/REFUND/etc. |
| `oldStatus`, `newStatus` | State transition. |
| `errorCode` | Stable internal error. |
| `providerRequestId` | Helpful for PSP support. |
| `correlationId` | Cross-service tracking. |
| `traceId`, `spanId` | Distributed tracing. |

OWASP Logging Cheat Sheet provides practical guidance for application/security logging; for payment service, logs must be useful for investigation without exposing secrets, PAN/CVV, or sensitive payloads. ([Google SRE](https://sre.google/sre-book/monitoring-distributed-systems/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 15. Correlation ID Strategy

Headers:

```text
X-Correlation-Id
X-Request-Id
Idempotency-Key
traceparent
```

Propagation:

```text
Order Service
  -> Payment Service
  -> Provider Adapter
  -> DB audit event
  -> Outbox event
  -> Order/Billing/Fulfillment consumer
  -> Camunda process
```

Rules:

```text
1. If inbound X-Correlation-Id exists, propagate it.
2. If missing, generate one.
3. Store correlationId in payment_events/refund_events/dispute_events.
4. Include correlationId in outbox event envelope.
5. Include provider request ID when provider returns it.
6. Do not log full Idempotency-Key; log fingerprint/hash.
```

---

# 16. Prometheus Alert Rule Examples

## 16.1 Provider Timeout Spike

```yaml
groups:
  - name: payment-provider-alerts
    rules:
      - alert: PaymentProviderTimeoutSpike
        expr: |
          sum by (provider, operation) (
            rate(payment_provider_timeout_total[5m])
          ) > 0.5
        for: 10m
        labels:
          severity: warning
          service: payment-service
        annotations:
          summary: "Payment provider timeout spike"
          description: "Provider {{ $labels.provider }} operation {{ $labels.operation }} has high timeout rate."
```

## 16.2 Unknown Payment Aging

```yaml
groups:
  - name: payment-state-alerts
    rules:
      - alert: PaymentUnknownOlderThanSla
        expr: |
          payment_stuck_count{status="UNKNOWN",age_bucket=">15m"} > 0
        for: 5m
        labels:
          severity: critical
          service: payment-service
        annotations:
          summary: "Payment stuck in UNKNOWN"
          description: "There are payments stuck in UNKNOWN for more than 15 minutes."
```

## 16.3 Outbox Stuck

```yaml
groups:
  - name: payment-outbox-alerts
    rules:
      - alert: PaymentOutboxOldestPendingTooOld
        expr: |
          payment_outbox_oldest_pending_age_seconds > 300
        for: 5m
        labels:
          severity: critical
          service: payment-service
        annotations:
          summary: "Payment outbox pending event too old"
          description: "Oldest pending outbox event is older than 5 minutes."
```

## 16.4 Webhook Invalid Signature Spike

```yaml
groups:
  - name: payment-webhook-alerts
    rules:
      - alert: PaymentWebhookInvalidSignatureSpike
        expr: |
          sum by (provider) (
            rate(payment_webhook_signature_invalid_total[5m])
          ) > 0.1
        for: 5m
        labels:
          severity: warning
          service: payment-service
        annotations:
          summary: "Invalid payment webhook signatures"
          description: "Invalid webhook signatures increased for provider {{ $labels.provider }}."
```

Prometheus alert rules define alert conditions in PromQL, while Alertmanager handles deduplication/routing/silencing/grouping of firing alerts. ([Prometheus](https://prometheus.io/docs/prometheus/latest/configuration/alerting_rules/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 17. SLO/SLA untuk Payment

SLO harus dipisahkan antara **API availability**, **provider dependency**, dan **financial recovery**.

| SLO | Example Target |
|---|---|
| Payment API availability | 99.9% monthly successful non-provider-failure responses. |
| Create payment API latency | p95 < 500ms excluding provider call, or p95 < 3s including provider. |
| Provider capture latency | p95 < provider-specific threshold. |
| Webhook ingestion durability | 99.99% valid webhooks persisted. |
| Webhook processing lag | p95 processed < 60s. |
| Outbox publish lag | p95 published < 60s. |
| Unknown recovery | 95% unknown payments resolved < 15 minutes. |
| Reconciliation completion | Daily provider report reconciled by T+1. |
| Support investigation | Critical possible double charge triaged < 15 minutes. |

Google SRE workbook explains alerting on SLOs as a way to turn SLOs into actionable alerts when error budget is threatened; SLI metrics should be prominent in service dashboards. ([Google SRE](https://sre.google/workbook/alerting-on-slos/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

```text
Provider: Stripe / Adyen / PayPal / Bank

```

## 18.3 Payment Operations Dashboard

```text
- UNKNOWN by age
- CAPTURE_PENDING by age
- REFUND_PENDING by age
- webhook dead letter
- recovery scheduled/failed
- investigation cases
- manual resolutions
- duplicate idempotency conflicts
```

## 18.4 Finance Dashboard

```text
- captured amount
- refunded amount
- provider fees
- chargeback loss
- settlement net amount
- reconciliation match rate
- open mismatches by age
- payouts not bank matched
```

# 19. Support Console Design

Support console harus **safe**, bukan UI untuk langsung edit database.

## 19.1 Payment Summary Page

Display:

```text
paymentId
orderId
customerId masked/limited
merchantId
status
amount/currency
authorized/captured/refunded amount
provider
providerPaymentId masked
capture mode
created/updated timestamps
current risk flags
```

## 19.2 Timeline

Gabungkan:

```text
payment_events
refund_events
dispute_events
webhook inbox refs
outbox event refs
ledger entries summary
recovery attempts
admin actions
```

Example timeline:

```text
20:00 PAYMENT_INITIATED by API
20:00 AUTHORIZATION_PENDING
20:00 PROVIDER_TIMEOUT -> UNKNOWN
20:01 RECOVERY_SCHEDULED
20:02 WEBHOOK_RECEIVED payment_intent.succeeded
20:02 PAYMENT_CAPTURED by WEBHOOK
20:02 LEDGER PAYMENT_CAPTURED posted
20:02 OUTBOX PaymentCaptured created
20:03 OUTBOX published
```

## 19.3 Safe Actions

| Action | Role | Guard |
|---|---|---|
| Query provider status | Payment ops | No financial mutation unless confirmed. |
| Replay webhook | Ops | Idempotent inbox processing. |
| Replay outbox | Ops | Idempotent consumer expected. |
| Create refund | Support/Refund role | Amount guard + idempotency. |
| Approve refund | Manager | Approval policy. |
| Open investigation | Ops/support | Audit reason required. |
| Resolve investigation | Finance/Ops elevated | Approval + audit + ledger guard. |
| View ledger | Finance/support senior | Read-only. |
| Export timeline | Support/finance | Redacted data. |

## 19.4 Dangerous Actions Requiring Approval

```text
manual mark captured
manual mark refunded
ledger adjustment
ledger reversal
force close reconciliation mismatch
disable provider
rotate secret reference
```

---

# 21. Runbook 1 - Provider Timeout Spike

## Symptoms

```text
payment_provider_timeout_total naik
payment_unknown_total naik
payment_provider_request_duration_seconds p95/p99 naik
provider circuit breaker mulai open
support reports payment pending
```

## Immediate Action

```text
1. Check provider status page / PSP dashboard.
2. Check provider-specific timeout by operation.
3. Stop unsafe provider fallback after post-call timeout.
4. Confirm new payment calls are using idempotency key.
5. Confirm UNKNOWN payments have recovery requests.
6. Check webhook inbox lag.
7. Check outbox lag to Order/Billing.
8. Communicate “pending confirmation” status to support/order.
```

## Do Not

```text
- Do not mark timeout payments as FAILED.
- Do not retry capture/refund with new idempotency key.
- Do not route same payment to another provider after unknown.
- Do not bulk refund unknown payments.
```

## Recovery

```text
1. Run recovery query job.
2. Wait for webhook if provider says async.
3. Reconcile provider report if still ambiguous.
4. Open high-value cases manually.
5. Resolve with audit/ledger/outbox if confirmed.
```

---

# 22. Runbook 2 - Webhook Processing Stuck

## Symptoms

```text
payment_inbox_pending_count rising
payment_inbox_oldest_pending_age_seconds > SLA
payment_webhook_processing_failed_total rising
payment_webhook_dead_letter_count > 0
```

## Action

```text
1. Check DB connectivity and worker pods.
2. Check inbox worker logs by provider/event_type.
3. Inspect first failed/dead-letter event.
4. Verify payload parser/mapping version.
5. Check reference resolution: provider_payment_id/provider_refund_id.
6. Replay after fix.
7. Confirm ledger/outbox not duplicated.
```

## Common Causes

```text
provider added new event schema
reference not found due missing provider ID
DB lock/contention
bug in state transition guard
invalid payload parser
```

---

# 23. Runbook 3 - Outbox Backlog

## Symptoms

```text
payment_outbox_pending_count rising
payment_outbox_oldest_pending_age_seconds high
Order/Billing/Fulfillment not receiving payment events
```

## Action

```text
1. Check broker availability.
2. Check outbox publisher worker health.
3. Check destination topic/exchange config.
4. Check publish error code.
5. Scale workers carefully if broker healthy.
6. Replay dead-letter events after fix.
7. Verify consumers are idempotent.
```

## Risk

```text
Payment state may be correct internally,
but downstream Order/Billing/Fulfillment may be stale.
```

---

# 24. Runbook 4 - Possible Double Charge

## Symptoms

```text
customer complaint double charge
provider report has two captures
ledger duplicate attempt alert
same order has multiple payment refs
idempotency conflicts or missing keys
```

## Action

```text
1. Search by orderId, customerId, amount, provider refs.
2. Check idempotency keys and payment_attempts.
3. Check provider dashboard for capture IDs.
4. Check ledger entries.
5. Check if both captures are internal-known or one is missing internal.
6. Open CRITICAL investigation case.
7. If duplicate confirmed:
   - create controlled refund
   - post ledger correctly
   - notify support/order/billing
8. Create RCA ticket.
```

## Do Not

```text
Do not manually delete one payment.
Do not edit ledger.
Do not refund until provider evidence is clear.
```

---

# 25. Runbook 5 - Reconciliation Mismatch Spike

## Symptoms

```text
payment_reconciliation_match_rate drops
payment_reconciliation_mismatch_count rises
missing_internal spike
amount_mismatch spike
fee mismatch spike
```

## Action

```text
1. Check provider report format changed.
2. Check normalizer mapping.
3. Check gross/fee/net interpretation.
4. Check currency/minor unit conversion.
5. Check import duplicate/rejected rows.
6. Check provider account/merchant config.
7. Pause auto-adjustments.
8. Reprocess report after mapper fix.
9. Create finance communication.
```

---

# 26. Java Instrumentation Sketch

## 26.1 Metrics Interface

```java
package com.company.payment.observability;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

public interface PaymentMetrics {

    void incrementCounter(String name, Map<String, String> labels);

    void recordDuration(String name, Duration duration, Map<String, String> labels);

    void setGauge(String name, double value, Map<String, String> labels);
}
```

## 26.2 Provider Call Instrumentation

```java
package com.company.payment.gateway;

import com.company.payment.observability.PaymentMetrics;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;

public class InstrumentedPaymentGatewayClient implements PaymentGatewayClient {

    private final PaymentGatewayClient delegate;
    private final PaymentMetrics metrics;

    public InstrumentedPaymentGatewayClient(
            PaymentGatewayClient delegate,
            PaymentMetrics metrics
    ) {
        this.delegate = delegate;
        this.metrics = metrics;
    }

    @Override
    public GatewayCaptureResult capture(GatewayCaptureRequest request) {
        Instant start = Instant.now();

        Map<String, String> labels = Map.of(
                "provider", request.provider(),
                "operation", "CAPTURE"
        );

        metrics.incrementCounter(
                "payment_provider_requests_total",
                with(labels, "result", "started")
        );

        try {
            GatewayCaptureResult result = delegate.capture(request);

            metrics.incrementCounter(
                    "payment_provider_requests_total",
                    with(labels, "result", result.status().name())
            );

            return result;

        } catch (GatewayTimeoutException e) {
            metrics.incrementCounter(
                    "payment_provider_timeout_total",
                    labels
            );
            throw e;

        } catch (GatewayException e) {
            metrics.incrementCounter(
                    "payment_provider_requests_total",
                    with(labels, "result", e.getClass().getSimpleName())
            );
            throw e;

        } finally {
            metrics.recordDuration(
                    "payment_provider_request_duration_seconds",
                    Duration.between(start, Instant.now()),
                    labels
            );
        }
    }

    private Map<String, String> with(
            Map<String, String> labels,
            String key,
            String value
    ) {
        java.util.HashMap<String, String> copy = new java.util.HashMap<>(labels);
        copy.put(key, value);
        return copy;
    }
}
```

---

# 27. Support Views / SQL

## 27.1 Stuck Payments

```sql
CREATE VIEW v_stuck_payments AS
SELECT
    payment_id,
    order_id,
    provider,
    status,
    amount_minor,
    currency,
    created_at,
    updated_at,
    now() - updated_at AS age
FROM payments
WHERE status IN (
    'UNKNOWN',
    'AUTHORIZATION_PENDING',
    'CAPTURE_PENDING',
    'REFUND_PENDING'
)
AND updated_at < now() - interval '10 minutes';
```

## 27.2 Outbox Health

```sql
CREATE VIEW v_payment_outbox_health AS
SELECT
    publish_status,
    event_type,
    destination_topic,
    COUNT(*) AS event_count,
    MIN(created_at) AS oldest_created_at,
    MAX(now() - created_at) AS max_age
FROM payment_outbox_events
WHERE publish_status IN ('PENDING', 'FAILED', 'DEAD_LETTER')
GROUP BY publish_status, event_type, destination_topic;
```

## 27.3 Inbox Health

```sql
CREATE VIEW v_payment_inbox_health AS
SELECT
    provider,
    processing_status,
    event_type,
    COUNT(*) AS event_count,
    MIN(received_at) AS oldest_received_at,
    MAX(now() - received_at) AS max_age
FROM payment_inbox_events
WHERE processing_status IN ('RECEIVED', 'FAILED', 'DEAD_LETTER', 'NEEDS_INVESTIGATION')
GROUP BY provider, processing_status, event_type;
```

## 27.4 Open Reconciliation Mismatch

```sql
CREATE VIEW v_open_reconciliation_mismatches AS
SELECT
    provider,
    item_type,
    match_status,
    COUNT(*) AS item_count,
    MIN(created_at) AS oldest_created_at,
    MAX(now() - created_at) AS max_age
FROM reconciliation_items
WHERE resolved = false
  AND match_status <> 'MATCHED'
GROUP BY provider, item_type, match_status;
```

# 28. Operational Audit

Admin/support actions need immutable audit.

```sql
CREATE TABLE payment_admin_actions (
    admin_action_id UUID PRIMARY KEY,

    actor_id VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    actor_role VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    action_type VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,

    payment_id UUID,
    refund_id UUID,
    dispute_id UUID,
    reconciliation_item_id UUID,

    reason_code VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    reason_detail TEXT NOT NULL,

    old_value JSONB,
    new_value JSONB,

    approval_required BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
    approved_by VARCHAR(100),
    approved_at TIMESTAMPTZ,

    correlation_id VARCHAR(100),

    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_admin_actions_payment
ON payment_admin_actions(payment_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_admin_actions_actor
ON payment_admin_actions(actor_id);

CREATE INDEX ix_payment_admin_actions_created
ON payment_admin_actions(created_at);
```

Actions to audit:

```text
QUERY_PROVIDER_STATUS
REPLAY_WEBHOOK
REPLAY_OUTBOX
CREATE_REFUND
APPROVE_REFUND
RESOLVE_INVESTIGATION
CREATE_LEDGER_ADJUSTMENT
APPROVE_LEDGER_ADJUSTMENT
RESOLVE_RECONCILIATION_ITEM
DISABLE_PROVIDER
ENABLE_PROVIDER
```

---

# 29. Alert Design: Good vs Bad

## Bad Alerts

```text
CPU > 80%
DB connection > 80%
Provider latency high
Webhook failures exist
```

These can be useful as dashboard signals, but often not enough as paging alerts.

## Better Alerts

```text
Payment API success rate below SLO
UNKNOWN payments older than 15 minutes
Outbox events older than 5 minutes
Webhook dead letter > 0
Possible double charge detected
Reconciliation match rate below threshold
Critical provider timeout spike affecting captures
Refund pending older than provider SLA
```

Google SRE emphasizes alerting that is actionable and tied to meaningful reliability impact; SLO-based alerting helps reduce noise and focus on significant events. ([Google SRE](https://sre.google/sre-book/monitoring-distributed-systems/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 30. Testing Strategy

## 30.1 Unit Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Provider timeout increments metric | `payment_provider_timeout_total`. |
| Capture success logs structured event | Contains paymentId/status/provider, no token. |
| Webhook duplicate increments metric | `payment_webhook_duplicate_total`. |
| Outbox pending view calculates age | Correct max age. |
| Error response includes traceId | ProblemDetails has traceId. |
| Metrics labels do not include paymentId | Cardinality safe. |
| Log masker removes sensitive fields | Token/PAN/CVV masked. |
| Recovery creates admin-visible timeline | Timeline includes recovery event. |

## 30.2 Integration Tests

```text
1. Simulate capture timeout.
2. Verify payment UNKNOWN.
3. Verify recovery metric/log/event.
4. Run recovery to success.
5. Verify trace/log correlation.
6. Verify ledger/outbox entries visible in support timeline.
7. Simulate outbox publisher down.
8. Verify outbox backlog metric and alert condition.
9. Restore publisher.
10. Verify backlog clears.
```

## 30.3 Observability Regression Tests

```text
1. No metric with paymentId/orderId/refundId labels.
2. No logs contain PAN/CVV/token.
3. Every payment state transition emits audit event.
4. Every provider call has provider/operation/result metric.
5. Every webhook ingestion has received/dedup/processing metrics.
6. Every alert has runbook link.
```

---

# 31. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Monitoring hanya HTTP 500 | Payment timeout/unknown bisa tidak 500. | Domain metrics. |
| Metrics label pakai paymentId | Cardinality meledak. | IDs di logs/traces. |
| Alert provider latency saja | Bisa noisy. | Alert user/financial impact. |
| No webhook lag metric | Async stuck tidak terlihat. | webhook processing lag. |
| No outbox backlog alert | Downstream tidak tahu payment captured. | outbox pending age. |
| Support langsung query/edit DB | Risky dan tidak auditable. | support console. |
| Runbook tidak jelas | On-call bingung. | alert links to runbook. |
| Logs berisi token/raw payload | Security incident. | masking/redaction. |
| UNKNOWN tidak punya dashboard | Payment stuck terlambat diketahui. | unknown aging dashboard. |
| Reconciliation hanya batch manual | Finance issue terlambat. | automated metrics + mismatch workflow. |
| Semua alert page on-call | Alert fatigue. | severity/actionability tiers. |

---

# 32. Implementation Checklist

## Metrics

- [ ] API request/latency/error.
- [ ] Provider latency/error/timeout/rate-limit.
- [ ] Payment status current count.
- [ ] UNKNOWN/stuck payment count.
- [ ] Webhook received/invalid/duplicate/lag.
- [ ] Inbox/outbox pending/dead-letter/oldest age.
- [ ] Refund pending/success/failure.
- [ ] Dispute opened/won/lost/due soon.
- [ ] Reconciliation match rate/mismatch aging.
- [ ] Ledger/fee/chargeback amount metrics.

## Logs/Tracing

- [ ] JSON structured logs.
- [ ] Correlation ID propagation.
- [ ] Trace ID/span ID in logs.
- [ ] Provider request ID captured.
- [ ] Sensitive data masked.
- [ ] OpenTelemetry instrumentation.
- [ ] Trace important spans: API, DB, provider, outbox, webhook.

## Alerts

- [ ] Provider timeout spike.
- [ ] Payment UNKNOWN older than SLA.
- [ ] Webhook dead letter.
- [ ] Inbox lag.
- [ ] Outbox lag.
- [ ] Recovery failure.
- [ ] Possible double charge.
- [ ] Reconciliation match rate drop.
- [ ] Refund pending too long.
- [ ] Dispute evidence due soon.

## Support Console

- [ ] Payment summary.
- [ ] Payment timeline.
- [ ] Ledger summary.
- [ ] Refund/dispute history.
- [ ] Webhook inbox view.
- [ ] Outbox view.
- [ ] Recovery attempts.
- [ ] Safe actions with audit.
- [ ] Role-based access.

## Runbooks

- [ ] Provider timeout spike.
- [ ] Webhook stuck.
- [ ] Outbox backlog.
- [ ] Unknown payment aging.
- [ ] Possible double charge.
- [ ] Refund pending.
- [ ] Dispute deadline.
- [ ] Reconciliation mismatch.
- [ ] Secret/key incident.

# 33. Exercise Praktis

Buat module:

```text
payment-service/
  observability/
    PaymentMetrics.java
    PaymentTracing.java
    PaymentStructuredLogger.java
    CorrelationIdFilter.java
    ProviderCallMetricsDecorator.java

  support/
    PaymentInvestigationService.java
    PaymentTimelineService.java
    SupportActionService.java
    RunbookLinkResolver.java

  api/admin/
    PaymentSupportResource.java
    PaymentOutboxAdminResource.java
    PaymentInboxAdminResource.java
    PaymentRecoveryAdminResource.java
    PaymentReconciliationAdminResource.java

  infrastructure/persistence/support/
    PaymentTimelineMapper.java
    PaymentAdminActionMapper.java
```

Target skenario:

```text
1. Capture timeout emits metric/log/trace and schedules recovery.
2. Payment UNKNOWN appears in support console.
3. Recovery query succeeds and timeline shows full path.
4. Webhook duplicate increments duplicate metric but does not duplicate ledger.
5. Outbox publisher down creates backlog metric and alert.
6. Support replays outbox with audit reason.
7. Reconciliation mismatch appears in finance dashboard.
8. Possible double charge opens critical investigation case.
9. Logs contain no PAN/CVV/token.
10. Dashboard shows provider latency, unknown count, outbox lag, and match rate.
```

---

# Ringkasan

Payment observability harus memantau bukan hanya aplikasi, tetapi juga **financial workflow**.

Prinsip utama:

```text
1. Metrics untuk trend dan alert.
2. Logs untuk investigasi event spesifik.
3. Traces untuk flow lintas service/provider.
4. Audit events untuk state transition dan admin action.
5. Ledger untuk financial truth.
6. Dashboard harus domain-specific: payment, webhook, outbox, refund, dispute, reconciliation.
7. Alert harus actionable dan punya runbook.
8. Support console harus menggantikan direct DB access.
9. UNKNOWN, dead letter, mismatch, dan possible double charge harus terlihat cepat.
10. Jangan pernah mengorbankan security observability: no PAN/CVV/token/secret in logs/events.
```

Dengan desain ini, saat payment incident terjadi, tim bisa bergerak dari:

```text
Alert -> Dashboard -> Trace/Logs -> Support Console -> Runbook -> Safe Action -> Audit Trail
```

bukan dari:

```text
Customer complaint -> grep logs manual -> query DB langsung -> tebak provider state
```

# Payment Management & Integration Pattern  
## Seri 18/18 - Testing Strategy, Sandbox, Mock PSP, Testcontainers, dan Production Readiness

Seri terakhir ini fokus pada **testing dan production readiness** untuk payment service berbasis **Java Jersey/JAX-RS + PostgreSQL/MyBatis**.

Core idea:

```text id="uy7dvm"
Payment testing tidak cukup hanya unit test.
Payment testing harus membuktikan:

1. Tidak double charge.
2. Tidak double refund.
3. Timeout tidak salah dianggap failed.
4. Webhook duplicate/out-of-order aman.
5. Ledger tidak double-post.
6. Outbox tidak kehilangan event.
7. Reconciliation bisa menemukan mismatch.
8. Production go-live punya rollback dan runbook.
```

Stripe menyediakan test mode/sandbox dan Stripe CLI untuk testing webhook secara lokal; Stripe CLI dapat menjalankan trigger event sandbox dan membantu menguji webhook integration. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/webhooks?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Adyen menyediakan test cards dan merekomendasikan pengujian full integration, termasuk client-side dan server-side, sebelum menerima live payment. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/development-resources/test-cards-and-credentials/test-card-numbers?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) PayPal sandbox adalah virtual testing environment yang mensimulasikan production tanpa menyentuh live account. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/tools/sandbox/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Testcontainers Java mendukung JUnit tests dengan lightweight throwaway instances untuk dependency seperti PostgreSQL, sehingga integration test bisa memakai dependency yang mirip production. ([java.testcontainers.org](https://java.testcontainers.org/modules/databases/postgres/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model Testing Payment

Payment test harus dibagi berdasarkan risiko:

```text id="wk562i"
1. Domain correctness
   State transition, amount guard, invariant.

2. Persistence correctness
   SQL guard, locking, unique constraint, transaction boundary.

3. Integration correctness
   Provider adapter mapping, webhook, outbox, reconciliation.

4. Operational correctness
   Retry, timeout, recovery, dead letter, replay.

5. Financial correctness
   Ledger, settlement, refund, chargeback, reconciliation.

6. Security correctness
   No PAN/CVV, signature verification, secret masking.

7. Production readiness
   Go-live checklist, rollback, monitoring, runbook.
```

Payment bug yang paling mahal biasanya bukan `NullPointerException`, tapi:

```text id="ueey5u"
- duplicate charge
- refund lebih dari captured amount
- payment captured tapi order tidak tahu
- provider webhook invalid diterima
- ledger dobel
- settlement mismatch tidak terdeteksi
- unknown state dibiarkan berhari-hari
```

---

# 2. Testing Pyramid untuk Payment

```mermaid id="e3hgc2"
flowchart TD
    A[Production Readiness / Go-Live Simulation] --> B[E2E Sandbox Tests]
    B --> C[Provider Contract / Adapter Tests]
    C --> D[Integration Tests with Testcontainers]
    D --> E[Component Tests with Mock PSP]
    E --> F[Domain Unit Tests]
    F --> G[Static/Security/Schema Tests]
```

Rekomendasi proporsi:

| Layer | Banyaknya | Kecepatan | Tujuan |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Domain unit tests | Banyak | Cepat | Invariant/state machine. |
| Mapper/adapter tests | Banyak | Cepat-menengah | Provider mapping. |
| DB integration tests | Sedang | Menengah | PostgreSQL/MyBatis/locking. |
| Component tests | Sedang | Menengah | API + mock PSP + webhook. |
| E2E sandbox tests | Sedikit | Lambat | Validate real PSP assumptions. |
| Chaos/recovery tests | Sedikit-terarah | Lambat | Timeout/crash/duplicate safety. |
| Production readiness drills | Release-based | Manual/automated | Go-live confidence. |

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fokus Praktis | Output Implementasi |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Domain Unit Test** | State machine, invariant, amount guard. | JUnit tests |
| 2 | **MyBatis/PostgreSQL Test** | SQL guard, unique index, locking. | Testcontainers PostgreSQL |
| 3 | **Jersey API Test** | Request/response, validation, error contract. | API component test |
| 4 | **Mock PSP** | Simulasi provider success/decline/timeout/webhook. | WireMock/custom mock server |
| 5 | **Provider Adapter Contract Test** | Mapping payload/status/error provider. | fixtures |
| 6 | **Webhook Test** | Signature, dedup, out-of-order, replay. | webhook simulator |
| 7 | **Idempotency Test** | Same key same result, same key diff payload conflict. | concurrent test |
| 8 | **Outbox Test** | Durable event, duplicate publish, worker crash. | broker/mock test |
| 9 | **Ledger Test** | No double-post, reversal, consistency. | DB + domain test |
| 10 | **Recovery Test** | Timeout → UNKNOWN → status query recovery. | mock PSP + job |
| 11 | **Refund/Dispute Test** | Partial/multiple refund, chargeback outcome. | flow tests |
| 12 | **Reconciliation Test** | Import report, normalize, match/mismatch. | CSV fixture tests |
| 13 | **Security Test** | No PAN/CVV, log masking, webhook secret rotation. | security regression |
| 14 | **Performance/Load Test** | Throughput, p95 latency, worker backlog. | k6/JMeter/Gatling |
| 15 | **Go-Live Checklist** | Production readiness evidence. | release gate |

---

# 4. Test Categories and Target Coverage

| Category | Critical Scenarios |
|---|---|
| Authorization | approved, declined, requires action, timeout, provider 5xx. |
| Capture | full capture, partial capture, duplicate capture, timeout, amount exceeds auth. |
| Void | void authorized, void after capture rejected, timeout recovery. |
| Refund | full refund, partial refund, multiple refund, over-refund rejected, duplicate refund. |
| Webhook | valid signature, invalid signature, duplicate, out-of-order, unknown event type. |
| Ledger | capture/refund/dispute/fee posting once, duplicate ignored, reversal. |
| Outbox | event inserted with state change, publisher retry, duplicate delivery. |
| Recovery | unknown state query, max attempts, manual investigation. |
| Reconciliation | matched, amount mismatch, missing internal, missing provider, fee posting. |
| Security | PAN/CVV rejected, token masked, secret not logged, authz enforced. |
| Operational | stuck inbox/outbox, dead letter replay, provider timeout spike. |

# 5. Domain Unit Tests

## 5.1 Payment State Machine

```java id="mmsv1l"
class PaymentStateMachineTest {

    @Test
    void authorizedPaymentCanBeCaptured() {
        Payment payment = Payment.authorized(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                Money.ofMinor(100_000, "IDR")
        );

        payment.applyCaptureSucceeded(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                Money.ofMinor(100_000, "IDR")
        );

        assertEquals(PaymentStatus.CAPTURED, payment.status());
        assertEquals(100_000, payment.capturedAmount().amountMinor());
    }

    @Test
    void failedPaymentCannotBeCaptured() {
        Payment payment = Payment.failed(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                Money.ofMinor(100_000, "IDR")
        );

        assertThrows(InvalidPaymentStateException.class, () ->
                payment.applyCaptureSucceeded(
                        UUID.randomUUID(),
                        Money.ofMinor(100_000, "IDR")
                )
        );
    }

    @Test
    void captureCannotExceedAuthorizedAmount() {
        Payment payment = Payment.authorized(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                Money.ofMinor(100_000, "IDR")
        );

        assertThrows(AmountExceededException.class, () ->
                payment.applyCaptureSucceeded(
                        UUID.randomUUID(),
                        Money.ofMinor(120_000, "IDR")
                )
        );
    }
}
```

## 5.2 Refund Invariants

```java id="k7br6z"
class RefundPolicyTest {

    @Test
    void partialRefundAllowedUpToCapturedAmount() {
        Payment payment = Payment.captured(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                Money.ofMinor(1_000_000, "IDR")
        );

        RefundPolicy.validateRefundAllowed(
                payment,
                Money.ofMinor(250_000, "IDR"),
                0
        );

        assertTrue(true);
    }

    @Test
    void refundCannotExceedAvailableRefundableAmount() {
        Payment payment = Payment.captured(
                UUID.randomUUID(),
                Money.ofMinor(1_000_000, "IDR")
        );

        assertThrows(RefundAmountExceededException.class, () ->
                RefundPolicy.validateRefundAllowed(
                        payment,
                        Money.ofMinor(800_000, "IDR"),
                        300_000
                )
        );
    }
}
```

Domain tests harus sangat cepat dan tidak bergantung database/provider.

# 6. PostgreSQL/MyBatis Integration Tests with Testcontainers

Testcontainers Postgres module memungkinkan test Java menjalankan PostgreSQL container instance secara programmatic, cocok untuk menguji SQL asli, constraint, index, transaction, dan MyBatis mapper. ([java.testcontainers.org](https://java.testcontainers.org/modules/databases/postgres/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 6.1 Maven Dependencies

```xml id="hc0qyv"
<dependencies>
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.testcontainers</groupId>
        <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
        <version>${testcontainers.version}</version>
        <scope>test</scope>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.testcontainers</groupId>
        <artifactId>junit-jupiter</artifactId>
        <version>${testcontainers.version}</version>
        <scope>test</scope>
    </dependency>

    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.junit.jupiter</groupId>
        <artifactId>junit-jupiter</artifactId>
        <version>${junit.jupiter.version}</version>
        <scope>test</scope>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

## 6.2 Base Test

```java id="b6i8p9"
@Testcontainers
abstract class PostgreSqlIntegrationTest {

    @Container
    static PostgreSQLContainer<?> postgres =
            new PostgreSQLContainer<>("postgres:16-alpine")
                    .withDatabaseName("payment_test")
                    .withUsername("payment")
                    .withPassword("payment");

    @BeforeAll
    static void migrate() {
        String jdbcUrl = postgres.getJdbcUrl();
        String username = postgres.getUsername();
        String password = postgres.getPassword();

        MigrationRunner.run(jdbcUrl, username, password);
        MyBatisTestSessionFactory.init(jdbcUrl, username, password);
    }
}
```

## 6.3 SQL Guard Test

```java id="4f8d9o"
class PaymentCaptureMapperTest extends PostgreSqlIntegrationTest {

    @Test
    void concurrentCapturesCannotExceedAuthorizedAmount() throws Exception {
        UUID paymentId = fixture.insertAuthorizedPayment(100_000, "IDR");

        ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);

        Callable<Integer> capture70k = () ->
                transactionTemplate.execute(() ->
                        paymentMapper.addCapturedAmount(
                                paymentId,
                                70_000,
                                "IDR",
                                paymentMapper.findVersion(paymentId)
                        )
                );

        Future<Integer> first = executor.submit(capture70k);
        Future<Integer> second = executor.submit(capture70k);

        int updatedRows = first.get() + second.get();

        PaymentRecord payment = paymentMapper.findById(paymentId);

        assertEquals(1, updatedRows);
        assertEquals(70_000, payment.capturedAmountMinor());
    }
}
```

Critical DB tests:

```text id="044umu"
1. Unique provider refs.
2. Unique idempotency key.
3. Unique ledger reference.
4. Refund pending hold.
5. Outbox claim with SKIP LOCKED.
6. Inbox duplicate ON CONFLICT.
7. Ledger update/delete trigger blocked.
8. Optimistic lock version guard.
9. Transaction rollback removes outbox event.
10. Recovery request active uniqueness.
```

# 7. Jersey API Component Tests

Test API contract:

```text id="ynpim3"
- validation error
- Problem Details response
- idempotency header required
- duplicate idempotency returns cached response
- conflict key same key different payload
- sensitive field rejected
- correlation ID returned/propagated
```

Example:

```java id="dvfzcf"
class PaymentResourceTest {

    @Test
    void createPaymentRequiresIdempotencyKey() {
        Response response = target("/payments")
                .request()
                .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "orderId": "ORD-123",
                      "amountMinor": 100000,
                      "currency": "IDR",
                      "paymentMethod": {
                        "type": "CARD_TOKEN",
                        "provider": "STRIPE",
                        "paymentMethodToken": "pm_test_123"
                      }
                    }
                """));

        assertEquals(400, response.getStatus());

        ProblemDetails problem = response.readEntity(ProblemDetails.class);

        assertEquals("IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REQUIRED", problem.errorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void createPaymentRejectsCardNumberAndCvv() {
        Response response = target("/payments")
                .request()
                .header("Idempotency-Key", "test-key-1")
                .post(Entity.json("""
                    {
                      "orderId": "ORD-123",
                      "amountMinor": 100000,
                      "currency": "IDR",
                      "cardNumber": "4111111111111111",
                      "cvv": "123"
                    }
                """));

        assertEquals(400, response.getStatus());

        ProblemDetails problem = response.readEntity(ProblemDetails.class);

        assertEquals("SENSITIVE_PAYMENT_FIELD_FORBIDDEN", problem.errorCode());
    }
}
```

---

# 8. Mock PSP Strategy

Mock PSP harus bisa mensimulasikan behavior buruk provider, bukan hanya success.

## 8.1 Mock PSP Scenarios

| Scenario | Use |
|---|---|
| `AUTHORIZED` | Normal manual capture auth. |
| `CAPTURED` | Auto-capture success. |
| `REQUIRES_ACTION` | 3DS/customer action. |
| `DECLINED` | Business decline. |
| `TIMEOUT_BEFORE_ACCEPT` | Request not accepted. |
| `TIMEOUT_AFTER_ACCEPT` | Provider accepts side-effect but response lost. |
| `PENDING_THEN_WEBHOOK_SUCCESS` | Async final success. |
| `PENDING_THEN_WEBHOOK_FAILED` | Async final failure. |
| `DUPLICATE_WEBHOOK` | Provider retries webhook. |
| `OUT_OF_ORDER_WEBHOOK` | Captured before authorized event. |
| `UNKNOWN_STATUS` | New provider status. |
| `INVALID_SIGNATURE` | Security test. |
| `RATE_LIMIT` | Backoff test. |
| `PROVIDER_5XX` | Retry/recovery test. |

## 8.2 Mock PSP API

```http id="wqu5qq"
POST /mock-psp/config/scenario
```

```json id="vxwpmx"
{
  "provider": "MOCK",
  "operation": "CAPTURE",
  "paymentId": "payment-123",
  "scenario": "TIMEOUT_AFTER_ACCEPT",
  "finalStatus": "CAPTURED",
  "webhookDelayMillis": 5000
}
```

## 8.3 Mock PSP Response Example

```java id="bipujl"
public class MockPaymentProviderServer {

    private final Map<String, MockScenario> scenarios = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public GatewayCaptureResult capture(GatewayCaptureRequest request) {
        MockScenario scenario = scenarios.getOrDefault(
                request.paymentId().toString(),
                MockScenario.captured()
        );

        return switch (scenario.type()) {
            case CAPTURED -> GatewayCaptureResult.succeeded(
                    "MOCK",
                    request.providerPaymentId(),
                    "mock_cap_" + request.captureId(),
                    request.amountMinor(),
                    request.currency()
            );

            case DECLINED -> throw new GatewayDeclinedException(
                    "MOCK",
                    "card_declined",
                    "mock_req_123",
                    "Mock card declined"
            );

            case TIMEOUT_AFTER_ACCEPT -> {
                scheduleWebhook(
                        request.paymentId(),
                        "payment.captured",
                        scenario.webhookDelayMillis()
                );
                throw new GatewayTimeoutException("MOCK", "mock_req_timeout");
            }

            case PROVIDER_5XX -> throw new GatewayUnavailableException(
                    "MOCK",
                    "provider_5xx",
                    "mock_req_5xx"
            );
        };
    }
}
```

---

# 9. Provider Sandbox Strategy

## 9.1 Stripe

Use cases:

```text id="vrd902"
1. Test mode payments.
2. Test cards.
3. Stripe CLI webhook forwarding.
4. Stripe CLI trigger events.
5. Idempotency retry behavior.
```

Stripe CLI can be used to test webhook integrations and trigger sandbox events locally. ([Stripe Docs](https://docs.stripe.com/stripe-cli?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Example local webhook flow:

```bash id="wf5v2l"
stripe listen --forward-to localhost:8080/webhooks/stripe
stripe trigger payment_intent.succeeded
```

## 9.2 Adyen

Use cases:

```text id="4pua03"
1. Test cards.
2. Payment result mapping.
3. Manual/auto capture.
4. Refund/cancel modification.
5. Webhook/HMAC verification.
```

Adyen docs provide test card numbers and recommend testing full client-side and server-side integration before going live. ([Adyen Docs](https://docs.adyen.com/development-resources/test-cards-and-credentials/test-card-numbers?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 9.3 PayPal

Use cases:

```text id="ezmuxu"
1. Sandbox personal and business accounts.
2. Checkout/order/capture.
3. Refund capture.
4. Webhook verification.
5. Transaction history/report behavior.
```

PayPal sandbox simulates production-like transactions without touching live accounts, and PayPal developer accounts can include personal and business sandbox accounts for buyer/seller flows. ([PayPal Developer](https://developer.paypal.com/tools/sandbox/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 10. Provider Contract Tests

Contract tests validate adapter mapping against fixture payloads.

Directory:

```text id="7q2gr3"
src/test/resources/provider-fixtures/
  stripe/
    payment_intent_requires_capture.json
    payment_intent_succeeded.json
    payment_intent_payment_failed.json
    refund_succeeded.json
    dispute_created.json
    error_card_declined.json
    error_rate_limit.json

  adyen/
    authorisation_success.json
    authorisation_refused.json
    capture_success_webhook.json
    refund_failed_webhook.json
    settlement_report_row.json

  paypal/
    capture_completed.json
    capture_pending.json
    refund_completed.json
    dispute_created.json
    verify_webhook_signature_success.json
```

Example:

```java id="ug8qzd"
class StripeResponseMapperTest {

    private final StripeResponseMapper mapper = new StripeResponseMapper();

    @Test
    void mapsRequiresCaptureToAuthorized() {
        String json = fixture("stripe/payment_intent_requires_capture.json");

        GatewayAuthorizeResult result = mapper.mapAuthorize(json);

        assertEquals(GatewayPaymentStatus.AUTHORIZED, result.status());
        assertEquals("requires_capture", result.rawProviderStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void mapsSucceededToCaptured() {
        String json = fixture("stripe/payment_intent_succeeded.json");

        GatewayAuthorizeResult result = mapper.mapAuthorize(json);

        assertEquals(GatewayPaymentStatus.CAPTURED, result.status());
    }
}
```

Contract test principle:

```text id="gxg92p"
Provider fixture harus disimpan.
Mapping provider status baru harus menambah fixture/test.
Unknown status harus fail safe ke UNKNOWN, bukan captured/failed sembarangan.
```

---

# 11. Webhook Testing

## 11.1 Required Tests

| Test | Expected |
|---|---|
| Valid signature | Inbox row inserted. |
| Invalid signature | Rejected; no valid inbox processing. |
| Body mutated after signing | Rejected. |
| Duplicate provider event | One inbox row / duplicate ack. |
| Duplicate processing | No duplicate state/ledger/outbox. |
| Out-of-order captured then authorized | State remains correct. |
| Unknown event type | Stored and ignored/investigated. |
| Reference not found | Retry/pending correlation. |
| Dead letter | Event visible and replayable. |
| Replay | Idempotent processing. |

## 11.2 Webhook Simulator

```java id="wfbp95"
public class WebhookSimulator {

    public void sendStripePaymentCaptured(
            URI webhookEndpoint,
            String providerEventId,
            String providerPaymentId,
            String signingSecret
    ) {
        String payload = """
            {
              "id": "%s",
              "type": "payment_intent.succeeded",
              "data": {
                "object": {
                  "id": "%s",
                  "status": "succeeded",
                  "amount": 100000,
                  "currency": "idr"
                }
              }
            }
            """.formatted(providerEventId, providerPaymentId);

        String signature = StripeTestSignature.sign(payload, signingSecret);

        httpClient.post(
                webhookEndpoint,
                payload,
                Map.of("Stripe-Signature", signature)
        );
    }
}
```

---

# 12. Idempotency and Concurrency Tests

Critical scenarios:

```text id="fjdyo9"
1. Same idempotency key + same payload -> same response.
2. Same idempotency key + different payload -> 409 conflict.
3. Concurrent same key -> only one executes provider call.
4. Concurrent different keys same order -> business duplicate prevention.
5. Concurrent partial capture -> sum <= authorized.
6. Concurrent refund -> successful + pending <= captured.
7. Worker retry after crash -> no duplicate ledger/outbox.
```

Example:

```java id="2q6ywi"
@Test
void concurrentSameIdempotencyKeyOnlyCallsProviderOnce() throws Exception {
    mockProvider.resetCallCount();

    String key = "create-payment-ORD-123";

    Callable<PaymentResponse> call = () ->
            paymentClient.createPayment(createPaymentRequest(), key);

    ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(5);

    List<Future<PaymentResponse>> futures = IntStream.range(0, 5)
            .mapToObj(i -> executor.submit(call))
            .toList();

    for (Future<PaymentResponse> future : futures) {
        assertEquals("CAPTURED", future.get().status());
    }

    assertEquals(1, mockProvider.authorizeCallCount());
}
```

---

# 13. Outbox Reliability Tests

## 13.1 Transaction Rollback

```text id="gzdmpl"
If payment update rolls back, outbox event must not exist.
```

## 13.2 Publisher Crash

```text id="p4azjw"
1. Claim outbox event.
2. Publish to broker.
3. Crash before mark PUBLISHED.
4. Release expired lock.
5. Re-publish same event.
6. Consumer receives duplicate.
7. Consumer applies once.
```

## 13.3 Test

```java id="3sxcid"
@Test
void publisherCrashCanDuplicateDeliveryButConsumerIsIdempotent() {
    UUID outboxEventId = fixture.insertPaymentCapturedOutbox();

    outboxWorker.claimAndPublishButCrashBeforeMarkPublished(outboxEventId);

    assertEquals(1, broker.publishedMessages().size());

    outboxRepository.releaseExpiredProcessingLocks();

    outboxWorker.publishBatch(10);

    assertEquals(2, broker.publishedMessages().size());

    orderConsumer.consumeAll(broker.publishedMessages());

    assertEquals(1, orderRepository.paymentConfirmedCount("ORD-123"));
}
```

---

# 14. Ledger Tests

Ledger tests wajib membuktikan:

```text id="sf1t89"
1. Capture posts ledger once.
2. Refund posts ledger once.
3. Dispute lost posts ledger once.
4. Provider fee posts ledger once.
5. Duplicate webhook/recovery does not double-post.
6. Ledger update/delete blocked.
7. Reversal creates opposite entry.
8. Payment vs ledger consistency view catches mismatch.
```

Example:

```java id="i2cl6a"
@Test
void duplicateRefundWebhookDoesNotDoublePostLedger() {
    UUID paymentId = fixture.insertCapturedPayment(1_000_000, "IDR");
    UUID refundId = fixture.insertRefundProviderPending(paymentId, 250_000, "IDR", "refund_123");

    webhookSimulator.sendRefundSucceeded("evt_1", "refund_123");
    webhookSimulator.sendRefundSucceeded("evt_1", "refund_123");

    List<LedgerEntryRecord> entries =
            ledgerMapper.findByReference("PROVIDER_REFUND", "refund_123");

    assertEquals(1, entries.size());
    assertEquals("REFUND_SUCCEEDED", entries.get(0).entryType());
}
```

---

# 15. Recovery and Chaos Tests

## 15.1 Timeout After Accept

```text id="1o5cad"
Mock PSP:
  capture request accepted internally
  response times out
  webhook arrives later as captured

Expected:
  payment UNKNOWN first
  recovery scheduled
  webhook/recovery marks CAPTURED
  ledger/outbox once
```

## 15.2 Recovery Worker Crash

```text id="6b9hn3"
1. Recovery query confirms captured.
2. Payment update succeeds.
3. Ledger insert succeeds.
4. Worker crashes before recovery marked resolved.
5. Worker retries.
6. No duplicate ledger/outbox.
7. Recovery eventually resolved.
```

## 15.3 Provider 5xx Spike

```text id="w1k7v5"
1. Mock provider returns 5xx for capture.
2. Circuit breaker opens for new calls.
3. In-flight unknown operations recover via query/webhook.
4. No fallback to other provider after timeout.
```

Chaos test result should be judged by invariant:

```text id="49bv57"
No double financial side-effect.
No lost durable event.
No incorrect final state without evidence.
```

---

# 16. Reconciliation Tests

Use CSV/JSON report fixtures.

```text id="9ccx5m"
src/test/resources/reconciliation/
  provider-report-matched.csv
  provider-report-amount-mismatch.csv
  provider-report-missing-internal.csv
  provider-report-fee.csv
  provider-report-payout.csv
```

Test cases:

| Scenario | Expected |
|---|---|
| Capture report matches internal | `MATCHED`. |
| Refund report matches internal | `MATCHED`. |
| Provider amount differs | `AMOUNT_MISMATCH`. |
| Provider currency differs | `CURRENCY_MISMATCH`. |
| Provider tx unknown internally | `MISSING_INTERNAL`. |
| Internal tx absent from report after SLA | `MISSING_PROVIDER`. |
| Fee row exists | ledger `PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED` once. |
| Re-import same report | no duplicate tx/ledger. |
| Bad row | rejected row + partial import. |

Example:

```java id="52dy82"
@Test
void providerFeePostsLedgerOnlyOnceAcrossReconciliationReplay() {
    UUID paymentId = fixture.insertCapturedPaymentWithProviderRef(
            "STRIPE",
            "pi_123",
            1_000_000,
            "IDR"
    );

    UUID importRunId = reportImporter.importCsv("reconciliation/provider-report-fee.csv");

    reconciliationEngine.reconcile(importRunId);
    reconciliationEngine.reconcile(importRunId);

    List<LedgerEntryRecord> feeEntries =
            ledgerMapper.findByReference(
                    "PROVIDER_FINANCIAL_TRANSACTION",
                    "txn_fee_123"
            );

    assertEquals(1, feeEntries.size());
    assertEquals("PROVIDER_FEE_CHARGED", feeEntries.get(0).entryType());
}
```

# 17. Security Regression Tests

## 17.1 Forbidden Fields

```java id="1sivnb"
@Test
void paymentApiRejectsCvvAndPan() {
    Response response = paymentApi.postPayment("""
        {
          "orderId": "ORD-123",
          "amountMinor": 100000,
          "currency": "IDR",
          "pan": "4111111111111111",
          "cvv": "123"
        }
        """);

    assertEquals(400, response.status());
    assertEquals("SENSITIVE_PAYMENT_FIELD_FORBIDDEN", response.errorCode());
}
```

## 17.2 Log Scan

```java id="eorczf"
@Test
void logsDoNotContainPanCvvOrToken() {
    paymentApi.postPayment(validTokenPaymentRequest());

    String logs = testLogCollector.readAll();

    assertFalse(logs.contains("4111111111111111"));
    assertFalse(logs.contains("\"cvv\""));
    assertFalse(logs.contains("pm_test_full_token_value"));
}
```

## 17.3 Webhook Secret Rotation

```java id="ol0a12"
@Test
void webhookAcceptsOldAndNewSecretDuringRotation() {
    secretStore.activate("stripe_webhook_current", "whsec_new");
    secretStore.activate("stripe_webhook_previous", "whsec_old");

    assertEquals(200, webhookClient.sendStripeWebhookSignedWith("whsec_old").status());
    assertEquals(200, webhookClient.sendStripeWebhookSignedWith("whsec_new").status());

    secretStore.deactivate("stripe_webhook_previous");

    assertEquals(401, webhookClient.sendStripeWebhookSignedWith("whsec_old").status());
}
```

# 18. Performance and Load Testing

Payment load test harus realistis.

## 18.1 What to Load Test

| Area | Target |
|---|---|
| Payment create API | Throughput and latency. |
| Capture API | DB lock/SQL guard. |
| Webhook ingestion | Burst from provider retry. |
| Webhook processing | Inbox worker throughput. |
| Outbox publishing | Event backlog clearance. |
| Recovery worker | Unknown payment batch. |
| Reconciliation import | Large CSV/report. |
| Support search | Investigation dashboard queries. |

## 18.2 Avoid Dangerous Live Provider Load

```text id="5qjv2g"
Do not load test against live PSP with real money.
Use mock PSP or official sandbox within provider rate limits.
```

## 18.3 Load Test Acceptance Example

```text id="za9g4k"
Payment API:
  p95 < 500ms excluding provider
  p99 < 1s excluding provider
  error rate < 0.1%

Webhook ingestion:
  500 events/sec burst
  p95 persist < 200ms
  no duplicate state/ledger

Outbox:
  backlog clears < 2 minutes after broker recovery

Recovery:
  10,000 unknown payments processed with no duplicate ledger
```

---

# 19. Test Data Strategy

Use deterministic test IDs:

```text id="5vcrtz"
orderId = ORD-TST-0001
paymentId = fixed UUID when possible
refundRequestId = refund-test-001
idempotencyKey = test:create-payment:ORD-TST-0001
providerPaymentId = psp_test_0001
```

Test data matrix:

| Dimension | Values |
|---|---|
| Currency | IDR, USD, EUR, JPY |
| Amount | small, normal, high, zero invalid, overflow guard |
| Capture mode | AUTO, MANUAL |
| Provider | MOCK, STRIPE, ADYEN, PAYPAL |
| Payment method | CARD_TOKEN, PAYPAL, EWALLET, QR, BANK |
| Outcome | success, declined, pending, timeout, unknown |
| Webhook | normal, duplicate, out-of-order, late, invalid |
| Refund | full, partial, multiple, over-refund |
| Dispute | opened, evidence submitted, won, lost |
| Reconciliation | matched, missing, mismatch |

# 20. CI Pipeline

Recommended stages:

```mermaid id="khnv1t"
flowchart LR
    A[Compile] --> B[Unit Tests]
    B --> C[Static Analysis / SAST]
    C --> D[Secret Scan]
    D --> E[DB Migration Validate]
    E --> F[Integration Tests - Testcontainers]
    F --> G[Component Tests - Mock PSP]
    G --> H[Contract Fixture Tests]
    H --> I[Container Build]
    I --> J[Container Scan]
    J --> K[Deploy to Test Env]
    K --> L[E2E Sandbox Smoke]
    L --> M[Release Candidate]
```

Gates:

```text id="5drta0"
1. Unit tests pass.
2. Integration tests pass.
3. Migration validates against clean DB and previous DB.
4. No forbidden sensitive column names.
5. No secrets in repo.
6. Contract fixtures pass.
7. Container vulnerability threshold accepted.
8. Sandbox smoke pass for providers enabled.
```

---

# 21. Database Migration Testing

Must test:

```text id="mmwtbw"
1. Clean install migration.
2. Upgrade from previous version.
3. Rollback strategy if supported.
4. Expand-contract compatibility.
5. Large table migration performance.
6. Index creation impact.
7. Backfill idempotency.
8. Constraint validation.
```

Example migration test:

```java id="jgwx5m"
@Test
void migrationFromPreviousVersionPreservesLedgerImmutability() {
    Database db = fixture.startDatabaseAtVersion("V017");

    fixture.insertPaymentAndLedgerRows(db);

    MigrationRunner.migrateToLatest(db);

    assertThrows(SQLException.class, () ->
            db.execute("UPDATE ledger_entries SET amount_minor = 1")
    );
}
```

# 22. Production Readiness Checklist

## 22.1 Functional

- [ ] Create payment works.
- [ ] Authorization works.
- [ ] Capture works.
- [ ] Void works.
- [ ] Refund full/partial works.
- [ ] Webhook valid/duplicate/out-of-order works.
- [ ] Outbox publishes events.
- [ ] Ledger posts once.
- [ ] Recovery handles timeout.
- [ ] Reconciliation import works.
- [ ] Support console basic flow works.

## 22.2 Provider

- [ ] Sandbox credentials validated.
- [ ] Live credentials stored in secret manager.
- [ ] Webhook endpoints configured.
- [ ] Webhook signing secrets configured.
- [ ] Provider idempotency headers implemented.
- [ ] Provider account/merchant config validated.
- [ ] Provider routing rules validated.
- [ ] Provider rate limits understood.

## 22.3 Security

- [ ] No PAN/CVV backend fields.
- [ ] Sensitive field guard enabled.
- [ ] Logs redacted.
- [ ] Secrets not in repo/image/configmap.
- [ ] K8s secrets/RBAC hardened.
- [ ] Webhook signature verification enabled.
- [ ] Admin endpoints RBAC-protected.
- [ ] Ledger update/delete blocked.
- [ ] Security audit events enabled.
- [ ] Breakglass process documented.

## 22.4 Observability

- [ ] Metrics emitted.
- [ ] Logs structured.
- [ ] Trace correlation working.
- [ ] Dashboards created.
- [ ] Alerts configured.
- [ ] Runbook links included.
- [ ] On-call notified/tested.
- [ ] Unknown payment dashboard.
- [ ] Inbox/outbox dashboard.
- [ ] Reconciliation dashboard.

## 22.5 Reliability

- [ ] Idempotency tested.
- [ ] DB transaction boundaries reviewed.
- [ ] Outbox retry/dead-letter tested.
- [ ] Inbox retry/dead-letter tested.
- [ ] Recovery worker tested.
- [ ] Circuit breaker behavior tested.
- [ ] Provider timeout tested.
- [ ] Worker crash tested.
- [ ] Backpressure limits configured.
- [ ] Rollback plan ready.

# 23. Go-Live Playbook

## 23.1 Before Go-Live

```text id="p0r59l"
1. Freeze provider config.
2. Validate live secret references.
3. Confirm webhook endpoints reachable from provider.
4. Confirm allowlist/network/TLS if applicable.
5. Run live zero-value/non-financial health check if provider supports.
6. Run sandbox final smoke.
7. Enable dashboard and alerts.
8. Confirm support console access.
9. Confirm finance reconciliation owner.
10. Confirm rollback and provider disable switch.
```

## 23.2 Go-Live Day

```text id="bjke5r"
1. Enable provider for small merchant/cohort.
2. Route low percentage traffic.
3. Watch provider latency/timeout.
4. Watch unknown payment count.
5. Watch webhook ingestion and processing.
6. Watch outbox publishing.
7. Watch support complaints.
8. Check first captured payment ledger.
9. Check first refund if applicable.
10. Check first settlement/reconciliation when report arrives.
```

## 23.3 Ramp-Up

```text id="qhjs9a"
1% traffic -> 5% -> 10% -> 25% -> 50% -> 100%

At each step:
  - no unknown spike
  - no duplicate charge
  - webhook lag healthy
  - outbox lag healthy
  - provider errors acceptable
  - support/finance no blocker
```

## 23.4 Rollback

Rollback options:

| Problem | Rollback |
|---|---|
| Provider timeout spike | Disable new routing to provider; recover unknowns. |
| Webhook bug | Pause processing, keep ingestion, deploy fix, replay. |
| Outbox bug | Pause publisher, fix, replay. |
| Ledger bug | Stop financial operations if severe, create adjustment/reversal plan. |
| Refund bug | Disable refund endpoint except admin/manual. |
| Security leak | Rotate keys, disable affected endpoint, incident process. |
| Reconciliation parser bug | Stop auto-resolution/fee posting, reprocess reports after fix. |

---

# 24. Release Readiness Evidence

For senior/team lead level, do not just say “tested”. Produce evidence:

```text id="3v9p72"
1. Test run report.
2. Coverage report for critical domain.
3. Provider sandbox smoke results.
4. Migration test result.
5. Security scan result.
6. Load test summary.
7. Chaos/recovery test summary.
8. Dashboard screenshots/links.
9. Alert rule list.
10. Runbook links.
11. Known limitations.
12. Rollback plan.
```

---

# 25. Production Smoke Tests

After deployment:

```text id="5ihjgh"
1. Health endpoint.
2. DB connectivity.
3. Secret resolver can load provider refs.
4. Provider auth lightweight check.
5. Webhook endpoint signature test if provider/tool supports.
6. Outbox worker healthy.
7. Inbox worker healthy.
8. Recovery worker healthy.
9. Metrics scraping works.
10. Logs/traces visible.
```

Do not perform real-money charge unless approved.

---

# 26. Known Limitations Template

Before production, document known gaps.

```text id="vp5ev6"
Known limitations:
1. PayPal dispute automation not enabled in v1.
2. Reconciliation supports capture/refund/fee, but not reserve release yet.
3. Bank statement matching manual for first month.
4. Multi-currency FX reporting uses provider FX only.
5. Refund approval workflow enabled only for manual refunds > threshold.
6. Provider failover disabled after payment creation by design.
```

This avoids hidden assumptions.

---

# 27. Final Production Readiness Scorecard

| Area | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Domain invariants | PASS/FAIL | Unit test report |
| DB constraints | PASS/FAIL | Testcontainers report |
| Provider adapter | PASS/FAIL | Fixture contract tests |
| Webhook | PASS/FAIL | Signature/dedup tests |
| Idempotency | PASS/FAIL | Concurrency tests |
| Ledger | PASS/FAIL | Ledger tests |
| Outbox | PASS/FAIL | Crash/replay tests |
| Recovery | PASS/FAIL | Timeout chaos tests |
| Reconciliation | PASS/FAIL | Report fixture tests |
| Security | PASS/FAIL | Secret/PAN/log scan |
| Observability | PASS/FAIL | Dashboard/alert validation |
| Runbook | PASS/FAIL | On-call review |
| Rollback | PASS/FAIL | Rollback procedure tested |

Release rule:

```text id="yl6v8p"
No go-live if:
- idempotency tests fail
- ledger duplicate test fails
- webhook signature test fails
- timeout recovery test fails
- outbox durability test fails
- PAN/CVV rejection/log masking fails
```

---

# 28. Common Anti-Patterns

| Anti-Pattern | Kenapa Berbahaya | Solusi |
|---|---|---|
| Hanya unit test happy path | Duplicate/timeout bugs tidak terlihat. | Add failure/chaos tests. |
| Test pakai H2 untuk PostgreSQL logic | SQL behavior beda. | Testcontainers PostgreSQL. |
| Mock provider selalu success | Tidak melatih recovery. | Mock PSP scenario engine. |
| Sandbox dianggap production-like penuh | Sandbox tidak mencakup semua edge cases. | Combine sandbox + mock chaos. |
| Tidak simpan provider fixtures | Provider mapping regressions. | Fixture contract tests. |
| Tidak test duplicate webhook | Double ledger/refund. | Webhook dedup tests. |
| Tidak test concurrent refund/capture | Over-capture/over-refund. | Concurrent DB tests. |
| Tidak test outbox crash | Event loss/duplicate. | Crash simulation. |
| Tidak test log masking | Security incident. | Log scan tests. |
| Go-live tanpa runbook | Incident lambat. | Readiness gate. |

```text id="4z1mp3"
      InboxMapperIT.java

    api/
      PaymentResourceTest.java
      RefundResourceTest.java
      WebhookResourceTest.java

    provider/
      StripeResponseMapperTest.java
      AdyenResponseMapperTest.java
      PayPalResponseMapperTest.java
      MockPspScenarioTest.java

    workflow/
      OutboxPublisherCrashTest.java
      PaymentRecoveryWorkerTest.java
      WebhookReplayTest.java

    reconciliation/
      ProviderReportImportTest.java
      ReconciliationEngineTest.java

    security/
      SensitiveFieldGuardTest.java
      LogMaskingTest.java
      WebhookSecretRotationTest.java
```

Target akhir:

```text id="v4rl7p"
1. Semua critical tests pass di CI.
2. Testcontainers memakai PostgreSQL asli.
3. Mock PSP punya scenario timeout, duplicate webhook, out-of-order.
4. Sandbox smoke untuk provider yang enabled.
5. Reconciliation fixture tests pass.
6. Security/log scan pass.
7. Load smoke pass.
8. Dashboard dan alert tervalidasi.
9. Runbook tersedia.
10. Go-live scorecard PASS.
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 18

Testing payment service harus membuktikan **financial safety**, bukan sekadar code coverage.

Prinsip utama:

```text id="b297sq"
1. Test state machine dan financial invariant.
2. Test PostgreSQL constraint/locking dengan Testcontainers.
3. Test provider adapter dengan fixture contract.
4. Test webhook signature, duplicate, out-of-order, replay.
5. Test idempotency dan concurrency.
6. Test outbox crash dan duplicate delivery.
7. Test ledger no double-post.
8. Test timeout → UNKNOWN → recovery.
9. Test reconciliation matched/mismatch.
10. Test security: no PAN/CVV, no secret/token in logs.
11. Sandbox test provider asli, tetapi jangan hanya bergantung pada sandbox.
12. Go-live harus punya readiness scorecard, dashboard, alert, runbook, dan rollback.
```

Dengan strategi ini, tim tidak hanya yakin bahwa payment API “jalan”, tetapi juga yakin bahwa sistem aman saat menghadapi kondisi production nyata:

```text id="3eb3zo"
provider timeout,
webhook duplicate,
worker crash,
broker down,
concurrent refund,
settlement mismatch,
dan customer complaint double charge.
```

---

## Seri 18/18 selesai

Seluruh seri **Payment Management & Integration Pattern in Java Jersey/JAX-RS based microservice application** sudah selesai.

Urutan seri yang sudah selesai:

```text id="gva4ew"
1. Payment Domain Model, State Machine, dan Invariant
2. Database Schema Design: payments, attempts, events, refunds, ledger
3. Jersey/JAX-RS Payment API Design
4. Idempotency Key, Duplicate Prevention, dan Safe Retry
5. Payment Gateway Adapter Pattern
6. Multi-Provider Routing dan Provider Abstraction
7. Authorization, Capture, Void, dan Settlement Flow
8. Webhook Receiver, Signature Verification, dan Inbox Dedup
9. Outbox Pattern dan Event Publishing ke Order/Billing/Fulfillment
10. Refund Management: Full, Partial, Async Refund
11. Dispute, Chargeback, dan Evidence Workflow
12. Payment Ledger dan Financial Audit Trail
13. Reconciliation dengan PSP/Bank/Settlement Report
14. Camunda Saga untuk Payment-Oriented Workflow
15. Error Mapping, Timeout, Unknown State, dan Recovery Pattern
16. Security, Tokenization, Secrets, PCI Boundary, dan Sensitive Data Handling
17. Observability, Monitoring, Alerting, Support Console, dan Runbook
18. Testing Strategy, Sandbox, Mock PSP, Testcontainers, dan Production Readiness
```

Rekomendasi materi lanjutan setelah seri ini:

**buat end-to-end blueprint mini-project Payment Service**, berisi package structure, database migration order, core API, worker, mock PSP, Testcontainers setup, dan production readiness checklist yang bisa langsung dijadikan referensi onboarding/practice project.
